### [1] [A Matryoshka Hierarchical RAG for Efficient Multi-Hop Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01767)
*Gianluca Bonifazi,Christopher Buratti,Michele Marchetti,Federica Parlapiano,Giulia Quaglieri,Davide Traini,Domenico Ursino,Luca Virgili*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MatRAG是一个结合Matryoshka表示学习的分层RAG框架，通过聚类DAG和维度对齐的多级索引，降低了索引和查询成本，并在多跳QA基准上取得优于基线的检索质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳问答RAG系统中索引成本（KG构建、LLM摘要）与查询成本（迭代检索）过高，需要在不损失检索质量的前提下降低计算开销。

Method: MatRAG将文档语料组织成具有渐进粗粒度的聚类DAG。每个层级使用更低的Matryoshka维度进行索引，使得粗粒度层级用低维表示、细粒度层级用高维表示，实现维度感知的相似度计算。查询时，采用迭代的自顶向下DAG遍历：从粗粒度层开始，逐步向下到细粒度层检索候选，同时一个实体驱动机制控制跳数预算（hop budget）并对候选进行重排序。训练目标未明确披露，但利用MRL的嵌套特性，无需额外训练即可在不同维度下获得有意义的表示。推理时通过低维向量快速筛选粗粒度簇，再在高维下精细检索，从而降低查询延迟与计算量。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "检索质量", "value": "优于最强基线", "setting": "在三个标准多跳QA基准上，对比七种代表性基线"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将Matryoshka表示学习（MRL）的嵌套维度结构与聚类语义层次对齐，形成分层DAG索引；使用迭代的自顶向下DAG遍历进行多跳检索，并用实体驱动机制控制跳数和重排序候选

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems for multi-hop Question Answering (QA) must balance retrieval quality with computational cost. This cost is incurred during indexing time, through the use of expensive Knowledge Graphs (KGs) or Large Language Models (LLMs) to generate summaries, or during querying, through iterative LLM-driven retrieval. To reduce it while maintaining retrieval quality, we present MatRAG, a hierarchical framework that combines RAG systems with Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL). MatRAG addresses both kinds of cost by aligning the semantic hierarchy of a clustering structure with the nested structure of MRL. Specifically, it organizes the corpus of documents into a Directed Acyclic Graph (DAG) of clusters with progressively coarser granularity. Each level is indexed by a lower Matryoshka dimension. MatRAG pairs an iterative, top-down traversal of the DAG with an entity-driven mechanism that controls the hop budget and re-ranks candidates. We evaluated MatRAG on three standard multi-hop QA benchmarks against seven representative baselines. MatRAG outperforms its strongest competitors in terms of retrieval quality; furthermore, it reduces indexing costs by avoiding KG construction and LLM-based summarization, and lowers query-time costs through dimension-aware similarity.

</details>


### [2] [Madeleine: Learning Involuntary Recall for Conversational Memory from Simulated Lives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01118)
*Zhiyun Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Madeleine提出用离线模拟生活训练查询编码器来学习联想记忆检索，在线零LLM调用即可接入向量记忆系统。在LoCoMo-Plus官方协议上达到66.6分，单独使用接近HyperMem发布版得分（52.4 vs 52.9）且仅用1/21上下文，并将T-Mem提升26.2点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程对话助手需要在正确时刻召回正确记忆，但当前系统依赖LLM在写入或读取时进行推理，导致每个记忆库需数百至上千次LLM调用，查询时上下文可达数千token，成本高且不可扩展。

Method: Madeleine旨在学习可摊销的联想记忆检索。离线阶段，使用LLM生活模拟器生成模拟生活，从中提取cue-trigger对（线索-触发对）。这些对用于训练一个查询编码器，该编码器在冻结的相似度表征之上学习残差关联。训练目标是让查询编码器捕捉记忆在人类生活展开方式下的点互信息（PMI），从而学会联想相关性。在线阶段，训练好的查询编码器被插入到任意向量记忆系统，仅替换原有的查询编码器，不调用任何LLM。检索时，查询编码器将用户当前话语映射为带联想关联的查询向量，与记忆库中的向量进行相似度匹配，实现记忆召回。整个流程不依赖LLM在线推理，大幅降低计算成本与上下文长度。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "LoCoMo-Plus官方协议得分", "value": "66.6", "setting": "Madeleine (I) 接入HyperMem，在所有评估系统中最高"}, {"metric": "LoCoMo-Plus得分", "value": "52.4 vs 52.9", "setting": "Madeleine (II) 单独使用 vs HyperMem发布版本，零LLM调用，约1/21的答案上下文"}, {"metric": "T-Mem提升", "value": "26.2点", "setting": "Madeleine接入T-Mem后的改进"}, {"metric": "普通QA任务", "value": "未指定具体数值，报告称保持完好", "setting": "在4B骨干模型上，未损害普通QA性能"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将联想定义为可学习的相关度：记忆在人类生活展开方式下的点互信息（PMI）。；提出Madeleine，学习摊销联想：离线用LLM生活模拟器生成模拟生活，从其中的cue-trigger对训练查询编码器，学习在冻结的相似度之上的残差关联。；在线阶段无需任何LLM调用，通过仅替换查询编码器即可接入任意向量记忆系统。

Abstract: A long-term conversational assistant must recall the right memory at the right moment, yet the memory that matters most is often not similar to what the user says now. Current systems recover such associations by letting an LLM reason at write or read time, at a cost of hundreds to over a thousand LLM calls per memory bank and up to several thousand context tokens per query. We argue that association is a learnable relevance: the pointwise mutual information of memories under how human lives unfold. We introduce Madeleine, which learns amortized association: offline, an LLM life simulator writes simulated lives, whose cue-trigger pairs teach a query encoder a residual association on top of frozen similarity; online, it calls no LLM and plugs into any vector memory by replacing only the query encoder. On LoCoMo-Plus under the official protocol, Madeleine (I) reaches 66.6 when plugged into HyperMem, the highest among all systems evaluated under this protocol; (II) used alone, reaches the score of HyperMem as released (52.4 vs. 52.9) with zero LLM calls and about 1/21 of its answer context; and (III) lifts T-Mem by 26.2 points, significantly outperforms the same untrained backbone inside both systems, and leaves ordinary QA intact on the 4B backbone.

</details>


### [3] [Effective Synthetic Data Curation Requires Group-Level Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00779)
*Cathy Jiao,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文指出单一数据样本的效用估计不足以筛选合成数据，提出考虑样本交互的组级别信号能更有效筛选合成数据，提升下游生成能力，并给出降低计算成本的诊断方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成数据筛选方法依赖个体级训练效用估计，无法捕捉样本间交互效应，导致在高度合成化的训练流程中数据筛选失效。

Method: 摘要未披露具体模型架构。所述方法为数据筛选流程：输入为合成数据集及其候选训练效用估计；采用个体级信号（每个样本孤立效用估计）对比组级别信号（考虑样本间交互的效用估计）；通过组级别信号筛选数据用于LLM训练（涵盖预训练与后训练）；进一步提出一种廉价诊断方法，识别需要组级别估计的合成数据组，以在计算预算内近似完整组级别评分的收益。训练目标为提升下游生成能力等任务表现。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "下游生成能力", "value": "组级别信号筛选优于个体级别信号筛选", "setting": "未披露具体数值，基于合成数据集筛选实验"}, {"metric": "相对基线改进", "value": "仅含组级别信号的方法优于基线，且样本间关系权重增强时收益增加", "setting": "广泛使用的数据筛选方法对比"}, {"metric": "计算成本", "value": "廉价诊断可恢复大部分完整组级别评分的收益，且计算成本仅为一部分", "setting": "预算受限的模型开发者"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出并论证合成数据筛选需要组级别信号：考虑样本间交互对下游任务效果的共同影响。；发现组级别信号在训练流程合成数据占比提升时愈发重要；只有包含该信号的方法能超越基线。；提出一种廉价诊断方法，可优先找出最需要组级别估计的合成数据组，以较低计算代价恢复大部分完整组级别评分的收益。

Abstract: Synthetic data now is essential to LLM training, used to strengthen advanced capabilities such as autonomous and long-horizon task execution. Yet recent work shows that training on it at scale can degrade model generation, making it important to decide what synthetic data is worth training on. While current data curation practices do so with individual-level signals (i.e., estimates of each data sample's training utility in isolation), across pre-training and post-training settings we show that this is insufficient for synthetic data, and that group-level signals (i.e., estimates of utility that account for interactions among data samples) are necessary for effective data curation. First, we show that individual-level signals are blind to how samples jointly affect training: synthetic datasets with different compositions can be indistinguishable under individual-level influence yet differ sharply under group-level influence, and curating by the latter yields better downstream performance, particularly in generative capability. Second, we find that group-level signals matter more as training pipelines become increasingly synthetic: among widely used data curation methods, only those incorporating them improve over baseline, with gains increasing when weights capturing relations among samples are amplified. Finally, we translate these findings into practice -- for model developers under a compute budget, we offer a cheap diagnostic that prioritizes which groups of synthetic data most need group-level estimation, recovering much of the benefit of full group-level scoring at a fraction of the compute cost.

</details>


### [4] [Sequential Functional Structured Tucker Compression for Large Language Model Attentions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00717)
*Jiangfeng Chen,Xinyu Wang,Tianshuo Yan,Hanwei Wu,Xiao-Wen Chang,Yang Zhang,Lei Ding*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FTC 提出顺序结构化 Tucker 压缩框架，联合利用 Q/K/V 多头结构并让近似适配压缩后的表示偏移，输出投影单独处理，无需微调。在七个 6B–32B 模型上于各保留率下取得最低 WikiText-2 困惑度，激进压缩时增益最大，且在 32B 规模仍显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 注意力后训练压缩多将各投影矩阵独立近似，忽略了注意力投影间的共享结构以及先前压缩带来的表示偏移，导致在固定存储预算下压缩质量不佳。

Method: FTC 是一种面向 LLM 注意力的后训练顺序结构化压缩框架。输入为预训练 decoder-only LLM 的注意力模块权重（Q/K/V 投影与输出投影）。处理流程按顺序进行：对注意力投影，在当前已被压缩后的模型表示基础上，联合利用原生的 Q/K/V 多头结构，在固定存储预算约束下进行结构化 Tucker 分解近似；由于前面模块的压缩会改变输入表示，后续模块的近似目标随之适应当前压缩模型，而非静态地独立近似原始矩阵。输出投影被单独处理，以显式适配压缩后变化的 post-attention 表示。整个流程无需微调或基于梯度的恢复。推理时直接加载压缩后的结构权重执行标准注意力计算，不引入额外在线开销。训练目标/优化为在固定存储预算下最小化压缩后模型对当前表示的近似误差与困惑度。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "WikiText-2 困惑度", "value": "在五个现代 GQA 模型、每个测试保留率下均为最低；在激进压缩下增益最大", "setting": "七个 6B 至 32B 参数的 decoder-only LLM，对比多种压缩方法"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 FTC 顺序结构化压缩框架：按顺序逐模块压缩，使后续近似适应当前已压缩模型的表示偏移；在固定存储预算下联合利用原生的 Q/K/V 多头结构进行结构化 Tucker 分解；将输出投影单独处理，以适配压缩后变化了的 post-attention 表示；无需微调或基于梯度的恢复即可完成压缩

Abstract: Post-training compression of LLM attention is often formulated as independent matrix approximation, ignoring both the shared structure among attention projections and the representation shift introduced by earlier compression. We propose FTC, a sequential structured compression framework that adapts the approximation to the current compressed model while jointly exploiting the native Q/K/V head structure under a fixed storage budget. The output projection is handled separately to account for the changed post-attention representation. FTC requires neither fine-tuning nor gradient-based recovery. Across seven decoder-only LLMs from 6B to 32B parameters, FTC achieves the lowest WikiText-2 perplexity among the compared methods at every tested keep ratio on five modern GQA models, with the largest gains under aggressive compression. The improvements transfer to downstream tasks and remain substantial at the 32B scale.

</details>


### [5] [AutoCompact: Learning When to Compact Context in Long-Horizon Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02163)
*Xuan Zhang,Longtao Zheng,Cunxiao Du,Bo An,Xin Dong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AutoCompact 让长程编码智能体把“何时压缩上下文、保留什么工作状态、如何继续”作为策略的一部分来学习：先用 judge 修正智能体的压缩决策与压缩后动作生成训练轨迹，再经 SFT 与任务成功奖励的强化学习联合优化编码与压缩，在 SWE-bench Verified 与 SWE-PolyBench Verified 上分别取得 9.2% 和 5.0% 的绝对通过率提升，并在 256K 与 16K 上下文预算下均保持增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程编码智能体中早期探索信息过期，上下文管理不只是避免溢出，而是需要智能体自己决定何时压缩、保留哪些工作状态以及如何从压缩状态继续。

Method: AutoCompact 的端到端流程围绕编码智能体的策略学习展开。输入为仓库级软件工程任务，智能体在环境中执行代码检查、搜索、编辑与测试的长轨迹。基础智能体在任务上运行，产生包含压缩决策、压缩摘要以及压缩后动作的轨迹；一个 judge 对这些输出进行审查，将有缺陷的压缩决策、摘要和动作替换为修正版本，修正后的输出再在环境中执行，因此每条轨迹从修正后的决策继续推进。这些经过修正的轨迹用于监督微调，使智能体学会在策略内部做出压缩决策。随后通过强化学习以任务成功奖励联合优化编码与压缩行为。推理时，智能体在每个上下文预算下依据学习到的策略决定何时压缩、保留哪些工作状态并从压缩状态继续：在 256K 窗口下不发生溢出，在 16K 窗口下溢出会触发回退压缩。该设计使上下文管理从外部启发式变为可训练的策略组件，从而在长轨迹中保留有效工作状态并提升任务成功率。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "pass rate", "value": "绝对提升 9.2%", "setting": "SWE-bench Verified，相比基座模型"}, {"metric": "pass rate", "value": "绝对提升 5.0%", "setting": "SWE-PolyBench Verified，相比基座模型"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将压缩决策（何时压缩、总结什么、如何继续）作为编码智能体策略的一部分进行学习，而不是固定的外部启发式。；利用 judge 审查基础智能体的压缩决策、压缩摘要和压缩后动作，将有缺陷的输出替换为修正版本后再在环境中执行，使轨迹从修正后的决策继续，从而生成高质量训练轨迹。；先对有修正的轨迹进行监督微调，再通过任务成功奖励联合优化编码与压缩行为（强化学习）。；在多种推理预算下验证压缩策略的稳健性，包括 256K 上下文窗口（从不溢出）与 16K 窗口（溢出触发回退压缩）。

Abstract: Coding agents solve repository-level software engineering tasks through long trajectories of code inspection, search, editing, and testing. As a task progresses, earlier exploration becomes stale, so managing context is more than avoiding overflow: an agent must decide when to compact, what working state to preserve, and how to continue from it. We introduce AutoCompact, which trains a coding agent to make these decisions as part of its policy. To collect training data, we run the base agent on coding tasks and use a judge to review its compaction decisions, summaries, and actions after compaction. Flawed outputs are replaced with corrected ones before being executed in the environment, so each trajectory continues from the corrected decisions. We use these trajectories for supervised fine-tuning, then jointly optimize coding and compaction through reinforcement learning with task-success rewards. Experiments on SWE-bench Verified and SWE-PolyBench Verified show that AutoCompact improves pass rates over the base model by an absolute 9.2\% and 5.0\%, respectively. The improvements hold across all evaluated inference budgets, with a 256K context window that never overflows and with a 16K window whose overflow triggers fallback compaction.

</details>


### [6] [Argo-Bench: Evaluating Data Agents on Enterprise-Scale Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02122)
*Gabriel Tomitsuka,Arman Raayatsanati,Emma Xing,Duke Gand,Joseph J Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Argo-Bench 是一个企业级数据智能体评测框架，模拟纽约食品配送平台，构建 235 张表、75 亿行的 ERP 仓库，包含 210 个需导航、分析与动作的任务，按仿真后果评分。最强模型仅 34.8% 任务达 95 分以上，平均 59.5 分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 text-to-SQL 基准仅评估查询生成且答案常出错，缺乏能在真实企业规模数据仓库中评估智能体端到端数据理解、导航与业务动作后果的评测框架。

Method: Argo-Bench 是一个评测框架，其核心架构包含模拟器、数据仓库导出层、任务集、智能体接口与后果评分器。输入为公开数据、同行评审行业文献与监管 filings，据此模拟纽约市食品配送平台在 2024 年的真实规模状态，包含 8100 万订单、经济模型、欺诈模式与市场激励。模拟器的真值状态被保留，不暴露给智能体。该世界被导出为企业级 ERP 数据仓库，包含 235 张表、75 亿行，schema 仿照 Oracle E-Business Suite，作为智能体唯一可见的数据环境。任务集包含 210 个数据科学与分析任务，要求智能体在仓库中导航、重建事实，然后执行动作（如封禁欺诈账号、分配骑手激励预算、发放补发薪酬）。评分器不直接比对 SQL 结果，而是将智能体动作作用于模拟器，依据动作在模拟世界中的后果进行评分。每个任务都提供可执行参考解，证明仅使用仓库即可完成任务，从而保证可解性。训练目标或优化不适用，因为该工作为评测基准而非模型训练。推理或服务流程为：智能体接收任务，访问 ERP 仓库，执行查询与推理，提交动作，评分器在模拟器中执行动作并计算得分。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "任务成功率（得分≥95）", "value": "34.8%", "setting": "14 个前沿与开源模型中表现最强模型的 Argo-Bench 评测结果"}, {"metric": "平均分", "value": "59.5", "setting": "14 个前沿与开源模型中表现最强模型的 Argo-Bench 评测结果"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 构建包含 210 个数据科学与分析任务的 Argo-Bench 评测框架，覆盖查询、统计分析与业务动作。；基于公开数据、行业文献与监管 filings 模拟纽约食品配送平台，2024 年 8100 万订单，具备真实经济、欺诈模式与市场激励。；导出为 235 张表、75 亿行的 ERP 数据仓库，仿照 Oracle E-Business Suite schema，隐藏仿真器真值状态。；评测超越 text-to-SQL，智能体执行封禁欺诈账号、分配骑手激励预算、发放补发薪酬等动作，并按仿真器中的后果评分。；每个任务提供可执行参考解，证明仅用仓库数据即可求解。

Abstract: Real-world enterprise data science and analytics workflows require reasoning across dozens of tables, performing statistical analyses, and acting on the results. Established text-to-SQL benchmarks evaluate query generation alone, and audits have found their answer keys frequently wrong. Because real enterprise warehouses are too sensitive to release, these benchmarks are built on public datasets where a business event fits in a single table. We introduce Argo-Bench, an evaluation framework comprising 210 data science and analytics tasks. Drawing on public data, peer-reviewed industry literature, and regulatory filings, we simulate a food delivery platform in New York City at true scale, with 81 million orders in 2024, grounded economics, fraud patterns, and marketplace incentives. We export this world to an ERP warehouse of 235 tables and 7.5 billion rows, modeled on the Oracle E-Business Suite schema. The simulator's ground-truth state is withheld from the warehouse the agent sees, so tasks require reconstructing facts by navigating the warehouse before acting on them. Argo-Bench goes beyond text-to-SQL: the agent files actions such as banning fraudulent accounts, allocating courier incentive budgets, or issuing back pay, and the grader scores each by its consequences in the simulator. Every task has an executable reference solution that demonstrates solvability using only the warehouse. The strongest of 14 frontier and open-weight models scores 95 or higher on only 34.8% of tasks and averages 59.5 points. We hope Argo-Bench drives progress toward agents that understand, navigate, and act within real data environments.

</details>


### [7] [Mem++: Non-Destructive Memory for Long-Term Organizational LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02002)
*Ahmad Yehia,Aly O. Abdelkareem,Islam Ahmed,Hesham Omran,Khaled Alashmouny,Christian Claudel,Abduallah Mohamed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Mem++ 提出非破坏性记忆框架，将 LLM Agent 记忆从写入时蒸馏改为读时选择：完整存储每篇文档及日期作者，读取时按问题时间点过滤并融合词汇/语义排序，保留旧版本交由回答模型判断。在 OrgMemBench 上比最强基线高 8.0–13.1 分，比 RAG 高 2.6 分，并在 LoCoMo 取得最佳 LLM-judge 平均分、LongMemEval-S 排名第二。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM Agent 记忆系统在写入时蒸馏（facts、notes、graph edges），提前固定了可回答内容，无法处理同一决定被新文档修订后需要知道某时间点生效版本的问题。

Method: Mem++ 的端到端架构围绕读写分离的非破坏性记忆设计。写入阶段：系统接收组织文档，每篇文档以完整原文存储，并附带其日期与作者等元数据；此阶段不调用任何生成模型，因此不进行事实抽取、摘要或图边构建，原始信息不被压缩或覆盖。读取阶段：针对用户问题，系统首先进行时间过滤，仅检索文档日期不晚于问题所询问时间点的文档；随后对候选文档执行检索排序，融合词汇匹配（lexical）与语义匹配（semantic）两种排序信号，得到最终候选集合。与覆盖旧版本的记忆系统不同，Mem++ 保留所有历史版本文档，并将版本选择权交给下游回答模型：回答模型在给定候选文档的基础上自行判断哪个版本在指定时间点生效并生成答案。该设计通过推迟选择到读时，避免写入时蒸馏造成的信息损失，从而支持随时间变化的组织决策问答。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "OrgMemBench 得分提升", "value": "8.0 到 13.1 分", "setting": "在两个回答模型上与最强记忆系统基线对比"}, {"metric": "OrgMemBench 总体得分", "value": "比 RAG 高 2.6 分", "setting": "使用 gpt-4.1-mini 回答模型"}, {"metric": "LoCoMo 平均 LLM-judge 得分", "value": "最佳平均分", "setting": "LoCoMo 基准，与对比方法相比"}, {"metric": "LongMemEval-S 排名", "value": "第二，仅次于其 entity-graph 变体", "setting": "LongMemEval-S 基准"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 从写入时蒸馏转为读时选择 (read-time selection) 的非破坏性记忆范式；存储每篇文档全文及其日期与作者，写入时不调用任何生成模型；读取时仅检索日期不晚于问题所问时间点的文档，并融合词汇与语义排序；保留旧版本而非覆盖，将版本选择权交给回答模型

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents now take part in organizational work, where many authors record decisions across documents over months. Because a revised decision arrives as a new document rather than an edit, answering a question requires knowing which version held at a given time. However, most memory systems compress the record at write time. By distilling each document into facts, notes or graph edges, these methods fix what can be answered before any question is asked. To address this, we propose Mem++, a non-destructive memory framework shifting from write-time distillation to read-time selection. Mem++ stores every document whole with its date and author, and it calls no generative model at write time. At read time, it retrieves only documents dated up to the time a question asks about and fuses lexical and semantic rankings. Unlike systems that overwrite older versions, Mem++ keeps them and leaves the choice to the answering model. Evaluations on the organizational benchmark OrgMemBench demonstrate that Mem++ surpasses the strongest memory system baseline by 8.0 to 13.1 points across two answering models. With gpt-4.1-mini, it also achieves the best overall score, 2.6 points above RAG. In addition, Mem++ achieves the best average LLM-judge score on LoCoMo and ranks second on LongMemEval-S, behind only its entity-graph variant. Code for benchmark evaluation is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [8] [Universal Byte-Level Encoding: UTF-8/UTF-16 Routing to Reduce Cross-Script Token-Budget Disparities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01984)
*Hyunsik Kim,Youngmoon Jung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出UBE双字母表tokenizer，将1-2字节UTF-8字符保留在UTF-8路径，3-4字节字符路由至UTF-16，从而降低非拉丁字符的合并前token成本。实验显示可减少跨语言token预算差异，在LM质量持平下降低高溢价脚本token数并加快提示处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UTF-8字节级BPE对3-4字节的非拉丁字符存在较高的最坏情况合并前成本（encoding floor），导致跨语言token预算不均，增加请求成本和压缩有效上下文。

Method: UBE是一种修改BPE输入字节表示的分词器。输入Unicode文本先判断每个字符的UTF-8编码长度：若为1-2字节，则直接保留UTF-8字节序列作为BPE的输入字母表；若为3-4字节，则将该字符的Unicode码点重新编码为UTF-16字节序列，再交给BPE。BPE合并规则保持标准不变，因此训练和推理时仅改变字节的呈现方式。最终解码时，根据路由规则逆向恢复原始Unicode码点，保证精确无损还原。该方法降低了3字节BMP字符在无学习合并可用时的多字节回退成本（encoding floor），从而减少高溢价脚本的token数，同时不增加混合文本中已高效英文span的token数。由于仅改变输入表示，UBE可与替代边界策略和形态学表示组合，无需修改模型架构。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "英文字母归一化token计数比率的离散度", "value": "降低", "setting": "跨语言内在评估，Unicode 17审计"}, {"metric": "token计数", "value": "高溢价脚本显著减少，英文轻微减少", "setting": "多语言LM实验，主要多语言设置"}, {"metric": "LM质量", "value": "与BBPE持平", "setting": "多语言语言模型实验"}, {"metric": "提示处理速度", "value": "更快", "setting": "内容匹配的基准测试"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出Universal Byte-Level Encoding (UBE)，双字母表tokenizer：1-2字节UTF-8字符走UTF-8路径，3-4字节UTF-8字符路由到UTF-16表示；不改变BPE合并规则，仅改变BPE看到的字节表示，保持精确解码；降低3字节BMP字符的encoding floor，同时不提高混合文本中已高效英文段的token数；可与替代边界策略和形态学表示组合

Abstract: Byte-level byte-pair encoding (BBPE) tokenizers are attractive for multilingual large language models (LLMs) because they cover all Unicode text. In UTF-8-based BBPE, however, many scripts start from a higher fallback cost than English: when no learned merges can be applied, a multibyte character requires multiple byte-derived symbols. We call this worst-case pre-merge cost the encoding floor. A higher floor can increase token counts and per-request cost and shrink usable context. Changing the text encoding can reduce this gap, but a single global encoding can make already-efficient English spans more expensive in mixed-script text. We propose Universal Byte-Level Encoding (UBE), a dual-alphabet tokenizer that keeps 1-2-byte UTF-8 characters on the UTF-8 path while routing 3-4-byte UTF-8 characters through UTF-16. This lowers the encoding floor for 3-byte Basic Multilingual Plane (BMP) characters in scripts with high token premiums (token counts relative to English) without raising it for already-efficient spans in mixed-script text. UBE changes only the byte representation presented to byte-pair encoding (BPE); the merge rule remains standard, and exact decoding is preserved. UBE also composes with alternative boundary policies and morphology-based representations. In a Unicode 17 audit, UBE exactly round-trips all Unicode scalar values and all inputs in the official normalization, grapheme-break, and emoji test suites. Across intrinsic evaluations, UBE lowers dispersion in English-normalized token-count ratios, reducing cross-lingual token-budget disparity. In multilingual language model (LM) experiments, UBE matches BBPE's LM quality. In the main multilingual settings, UBE reduces token counts most for high-premium scripts and slightly lowers English token counts, yielding more usable context under fixed token budgets and faster prompt processing in content-matched benchmarks.

</details>


### [9] [The Asymptotics of Language Model Alignment with Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01828)
*Haricharan Balasundaram,V. Arvind Rameshwar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 理论分析论文，研究在输出序列具有记忆性时，KL约束RL与best-of-n对齐方法的渐近等价性，并给出有限长度下KL散度为零的完整刻画，为LLM对齐算法提供理论支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在语言模型输出序列具有记忆性（马尔可夫性）时，KL约束强化学习与best-of-n对齐方法产生的分布是否渐近接近，以及有限长度下KL散度为零的完整刻画。

Method: 论文属于理论分析工作，不涉及具体模型架构或系统实现。其分析框架基于语言模型Q和对齐后模型q的概率分布，研究两种对齐方法：1）KL约束RL，需要已知LM分布并优化期望奖励同时约束KL散度；2）best-of-n采样，仅需从LM采样。论文在m长度token序列为马尔可夫链的假设下，证明当m趋于无穷时两种方法产生的分布渐近接近，并针对有限m（如m=1）刻画了KL散度为零的条件。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将KL约束RL与best-of-n对齐的渐近等价性从i.i.d. token序列推广到马尔可夫token序列。；针对有限长度（尤其是m=1）给出了两种对齐方法KL散度为零的LM分布和奖励函数的完整刻画。

Abstract: Language model (LM) alignment broadly aims to perturb a given LM $Q$ into an aligned LM $q$ such that i) the outputs produced by $q$ and $Q$ are 'close' in probability, ii) $q$ has a higher expected reward than $Q$. Two common techniques for LM alignment are: KL-constrained RL, which requires knowledge of the LM distribution and is computationally expensive, and the best-of-$n$ algorithm, which requires only sampling from the LM. The work of Yang et al. established asymptotic closeness between the distributions produced by the two alignment methods for an $m$--length i.i.d. token sequence output by the LM, in the limit as $m$ increases to infinity. However, the i.i.d. assumption is not representative of practical LMs, whose output sequences often have memory. In this paper, we extend the asymptotic closeness result to the case when the $m$--length token sequence outputted by the LM is Markovian. Further, for finite-length output sequences -- particularly, when $m=1$ -- we provide a complete characterization of LM distributions and reward functions for which the KL-divergence between the distributions produced by the two alignment methods is zero -- a question first posed in Yang et al.

</details>


### [10] [Cross-Lingual Alignment for Decoder-Only Models using MoE Routers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01921)
*Lucas Bandarkar,Clark Peng,Ahmed Haj Ahmed,Aditi Khandelwal,Nanyun Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种新颖的跨语言对齐方法，利用MoE路由器的输出进行对比学习，避免了对解码器-only LLM隐藏状态直接对齐的困难。在四个开源MoE模型上进行持续预训练实验，证明了该方法能改善跨语言表示对齐并提升多语言性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cross-lingual representation alignment is difficult in decoder-only LLMs due to varying tokenization. Existing contrastive learning methods applied on hidden states are not directly applicable, and the paper aims to achieve effective cross-lingual alignment without auxiliary hidden-state losses.

Method: The proposed method operates on decoder-only MoE language models. During continual pre-training, for parallel or comparable multilingual text, the model processes each language separately. The MoE routers in each layer produce a distribution over experts for each token. These router outputs are pooled (e.g., averaged) across all tokens in a sequence to obtain a sequence-level representation. A contrastive loss, such as InfoNCE, is then applied to these sequence-level router representations to pull together representations of semantically equivalent sequences across languages and push apart non-equivalent ones. This routing loss is added as an auxiliary objective during training, alongside the standard language modeling loss. The method does not require modifying the model architecture. The intuition is that router outputs are naturally coarse-grained and more stable for sequence-level pooling than hidden states, which vary with tokenization. Experiments on four open-source MoE models show that optimizing this routing loss not only directly aligns router outputs but also aligns the underlying hidden representations, improving multilingual performance on a diverse evaluation suite.

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Using the outputs of mixture-of-experts (MoE) routers as the target for cross-lingual contrastive alignment instead of hidden states.；Leveraging router outputs for token pooling, enabling reliable sequence-level cross-lingual comparisons.；Demonstrating that the routing loss indirectly aligns underlying hidden representations across languages.

Abstract: Cross-lingual contrastive learning has been a core component of multilingual encoder training, but the ability to explicitly align representations is not possible in decoder-only LLMs because of varying multilingual tokenization. However, a growing amount of research suggests that even in LLMs, higher cross-lingual representational alignment leads to improved cross-lingual transfer. In this paper, we propose a novel approach to reimagine cross-lingual contrastive learning given the architectural constraints of modern LLMs. Rather than applying an auxiliary alignment loss on hidden states, we propose using the outputs of the mixture-of-experts (MoE) routers as the target for alignment. Router outputs lend themselves better to pooling over many tokens, enabling more reliable cross-lingual comparisons at the sequence-level. Controlled continual pre-training experiments on four open-source MoEs show that incorporating this routing loss also aligns the underlying hidden representations across languages. Most importantly, this loss improves multilingual performance on our diverse evaluation suite, demonstrating the potential of cross-lingual MoE router alignment.

</details>


### [11] [Which LLM to pick? Online Active Model Selection for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01592)
*Alessandro Turrin,Patrik Okanovic,Torsten Hoefler,Nezihe Merve Gürel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 ONLINE LLM PICKER，首个面向流式数据的 LLM 主动模型选择框架：在有限标注预算下挑选信息量最大的 prompt 进行标注，从而可靠识别最佳或近最佳候选模型。在 10 个数据集、130+ 模型上最高节省 71.67% 标注成本，并将在线生成 regret 最多降低 2.51 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实流式数据中 benchmark 只能近似真实性能，而 oracle 标注成本高、难以规模化；如何在在线流式场景下以有限标注预算可靠识别候选 LLM 中最优模型，并尽早用于后续生成以降低 regret。

Method: ONLINE LLM PICKER 的输入是一个任意 query 流以及一组候选 LLM；系统维护一个有限的 oracle 标注预算。框架在在线过程中对每个到达的 prompt 决定是否请求标注：通过主动选择策略挑选信息量最大的 prompt 进行标注，以在预算约束下最大化区分候选模型的能力。标注反馈用于更新对候选模型在流上表现的估计，并据此识别当前最佳或接近最佳的 LLM。被选中的模型随后用于处理流中未标注的后续 prompt，进行序列生成；系统持续观察并更新，目标是最小化相对于事后最优模型的 regret。论文在 10 个数据集、超过 130 个语言模型上评估了标注成本节省、最佳模型识别可靠性以及在线生成 regret 降低。摘要未披露更细的模块结构、训练目标、服务硬件或延迟数据。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "标注成本节省", "value": "最高 71.67%", "setting": "10 个数据集、超过 130 个语言模型、多任务在线主动模型选择"}, {"metric": "regret 降低", "value": "最高 2.51x", "setting": "使用返回模型对未标注 prompt 进行流式序列生成"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出首个面向 LLM 在线场景的主动模型选择框架 ONLINE LLM PICKER；在任意 query 流与有限标注预算下，主动选择信息量最大的 prompt 进行标注以识别最佳 LLM；将模型选择与在线序列生成/regret 最小化连接，验证能提前识别最佳或接近最佳模型

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to process streaming data, with practitioners relying on benchmarks to select the best model even though these signals only approximate real performance. While oracle annotations can provide reliable feedback, they are often costly and difficult to obtain at scale. To address this challenge, we propose ONLINE LLM PICKER, the first framework for active model selection for LLMs in online settings. Given an arbitrary stream of queries and a limited annotation budget, ONLINE LLM PICKER selects the most informative prompts for annotation to identify the best LLM among candidate models. Across multiple tasks including 10 datasets, for over 130 language models, we show that ONLINE LLM PICKER saves annotation cost by up to 71.67% while reliably identifying the best or near-best model for the stream. We also show that using the returned model for sequential generation on unannotated prompts across the stream reduces regret by up to a factor of 2.51x, indicating that ONLINE LLM PICKER can identify the best or near-best model well before processing all streaming prompts.

</details>


### [12] [GAW-PO: Preference Optimization with Gradient-Aligned Token Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01511)
*Andreea Dutulescu,Stefan Ruseti,Mihai Masala,Traian Rebedea,Mihai Dascalu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GAW-PO 提出一种梯度对齐的 token 级重加权方法改进 DPO，对被拒响应中与偏好方向对齐的 token 减弱负惩罚、冲突 token 保留强惩罚。在 11 个数学/推理/编程/问答基准上平均超越 DPO 0.97 分、超越最强基线 0.65 分，并在 β 减小时比 DPO 更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO 等偏好优化方法在响应级别施加偏好监督，导致被拒响应中的所有 token（包括对偏好响应有用的行为）都承受负梯度，缺乏 token 级信用分配。

Method: GAW-PO 在 DPO 训练框架上增加 token 级梯度对齐重加权。输入为偏好数据对（偏好响应 y_w 与被拒响应 y_l）。标准 DPO 损失在响应级别对 y_l 的所有 token 施加统一负梯度。GAW-PO 在训练中估计每个被拒 token 的梯度与偏好更新方向的对齐程度：若某被拒 token 的梯度与偏好行为方向强对齐（即惩罚它会干扰偏好更新），则降低其负贡献权重；若与偏好方向冲突，则保留较强惩罚。该重加权作用于 DPO 损失的逐 token 梯度上，训练目标仍为 DPO 式偏好优化，但实现了 token 级信用分配。推理路径与标准 DPO 微调后的 LLM 相同，无额外推理开销或模块。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均分", "value": "比标准 DPO 提升 0.97 分", "setting": "11 个基准（数学、推理、编程、问答）"}, {"metric": "平均分", "value": "比最强竞争基线提升 0.65 分", "setting": "11 个基准（数学、推理、编程、问答）"}, {"metric": "鲁棒性", "value": "随着 β 减小，标准 DPO 急剧退化，而 GAW-PO 继续提升", "setting": "DPO 正则化参数 β 扫描"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 GAW-PO，对 DPO 中被拒 token 进行梯度对齐重加权：与偏好更新方向强对齐的 token 受到更弱的负惩罚，冲突 token 保留更强惩罚；证明梯度对齐加权在 β 减小时比标准 DPO 更鲁棒，标准 DPO 急剧退化而 GAW-PO 继续提升

Abstract: Most preference optimization methods, such as Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), apply preference supervision at the response level, although autoregressive language models are optimized token by token. As a result, all tokens in a rejected response contribute to the negative training signal, including tokens that may encode behavior that is useful for the preferred response. We introduce GAW-PO, a gradient-aligned token reweighting method for DPO that estimates, for each rejected token, whether penalizing it would interfere with the preferred update directions. Tokens whose gradients are strongly aligned with the preferred behavior receive a weaker negative contribution, while conflicting tokens retain a stronger penalty. Our method achieves the highest average performance among the evaluated preference-optimization methods, improving by 0.97 points over standard DPO and 0.65 points over the strongest competing baseline across 11 benchmarks spanning mathematics, reasoning, coding, and question answering. We further show that gradient-aligned weighting is substantially more robust to aggressive preference optimization: as the DPO regularization parameter $\beta$ decreases, standard DPO degrades sharply, whereas GAW-PO continues to improve. These results suggest that accounting for the interaction between rejected-token updates and preferred behavior provides an effective form of token-level credit assignment for preference optimization.

</details>


### [13] [Auditing Web Agent Evaluation on WebArena-Lite: Human Review of Outcomes and Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01491)
*Chengguang Gan,Zimeng He,Yoshihiro Tsujii,Ken-ichiro Kobayashi,Hiroki Itoh,Kotaro Funakoshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 对WebArena-Lite全部165个任务进行人工审计，发现自动评估器存在5.45-8.49个百分点的假阴性，并验证MASM和Guide Text能提升任务成功率，揭示最终分数不足以反映网页智能体真实行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网页智能体评测仅依赖规则或LLM评估器检查最终结果，缺乏对任务完成度的人工验证和失败轨迹的详细分析，导致最终分数无法完整反映智能体行为。

Method: 论文未提供端到端智能体架构的详细说明。MASM维护显式执行状态，用于追踪任务进展；Guide Text提供任务相关的程序性指导。两者在智能体执行过程中辅助决策。评测时，对每个任务在6种条件下运行，然后人工审查轨迹，保留原始分数、纠正假阴性并定位首个关键错误。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "成功率恢复百分点（人工审查纠正的假阴性）", "value": "5.45至8.49个百分点", "setting": "GPT-5.5的四种设置下，人工审查纠正自动评估器遗漏的成功"}, {"metric": "纠正后成功率", "value": "从34.55%提升至38.18%", "setting": "25步预算下，使用MASM时加入Guide Text"}, {"metric": "评估器分数", "value": "从13.90%提升至18.80%", "setting": "未训练的Qwen3.5 9B模型上，加入MASM"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 对WebArena-Lite全部165个任务在6种评测条件下进行人工审计，保留原始分数并纠正自动评估器的假阴性；定位首个关键错误并分析整个轨迹的进展；提出记忆与分析支持机制（MASM），维护显式执行状态；引入引导文本（Guide Text）提供任务相关程序性指导；通过102条失败轨迹的人工审查，归纳出常见失败模式（滚动循环、未完成探索、过早回答、无效动作、不完整表单流程）

Abstract: Web agents are an important application of large language models, yet their evaluation often depends on rule based or language model evaluators that inspect only the final outcome. Human verification of task completion and detailed analysis of failed trajectories remain limited. We audit all 165 WebArena Lite tasks under six evaluation conditions built from GPT 5.5 and an untrained Qwen3.5 9B model. The audit retains the original score, corrects false negatives from the automatic evaluator, identifies the first consequential error, and examines progress across the trajectory. We also study a Memory and Analysis Support Mechanism (MASM), which maintains explicit execution state, and Guide Text, which provides task relevant procedural guidance. Across four GPT 5.5 settings, human review recovers 5.45 to 8.49 percentage points of success missed by the evaluator. With a 25 step budget, Guide Text raises corrected success with MASM from 34.55% to 38.18%. On the untrained Qwen3.5 9B model, MASM raises the evaluator score from 13.90% to 18.80%. Review of 102 failed GPT 5.5 trajectories reveals frequent scrolling loops, unfinished exploration, premature answers, invalid actions, and incomplete form workflows. Step level evidence further shows that substantial early progress can coexist with a final failure. These results show why final scores alone provide an incomplete account of web agent behavior and motivate human grounded, trajectory aware verification.

</details>

*Nagham Omar,Mahmoud Jabarin,Maya Rozenshtein,Rom Himelstein,Avi Mendelson,Amit LeVi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGO框架，从多个行为维度（生成一致性、内部激活、置信度等）评估LLM在输入变体下的稳定性，发现常用模型普遍存在泛化不稳定，且跨数据集排名可能反转。对LLM可靠性评估有参考价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM泛化评估通常基于单一提示格式的聚合准确率，无法区分鲁棒性与基准性能，忽略了个体样本在多输入变体下的行为稳定性。

Method: SAGO是一个评估框架，不涉及模型架构修改。其流程为：对于同一输入的不同变体（如改写、格式变化），分别获取模型输出；从生成一致性（输出文本的语义稳定性）、内部激活（隐藏层表示的变化）、置信度（输出概率分布的变化）、响应镜像（输出对输入变体的敏感度）等维度量化行为变化；汇总为稳定性指标，用于比较不同模型或同一模型在不同数据集上的表现。该框架强调在个体样本级别分析变异性，而非仅依赖聚合准确率。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出Stability-Aware Generalization Objective (SAGO)框架，从样本级、多输入变体、多行为维度评估泛化稳定性，而非聚合准确率。；定义了包括生成一致性、内部激活、置信度、响应镜像等在内的多个行为轴，用以捕捉独立失败模式。；实验表明常用LLM普遍存在统计显著的泛化不稳定性，且跨数据集变化可逆转模型排名。

Abstract: Generalization in large language models (LLMs) is the ability to produce consistent and semantically stable outputs when the same input is expressed in different ways. Existing work typically evaluates generalization through aggregate accuracy on a single prompt format, task, or set of variations, which conflates robustness with overall benchmark performance. In this work, we show generalization evaluation at the level of individual examples, across multiple input variants, and across different aspects of model behavior, focusing on variability rather than reducing performance to a score that can be improved through narrow training or other ways that obfuscate generalization evaluation. Following this view, we introduce the Stability-Aware Generalization Objective (SAGO), a framework that measures how much model behavior changes for the same input under different variations and benchmarks, capturing variability across several dimensions including generation consistency, internal activations, confidence, and response mirroring. We show that many commonly used models exhibit statistically significant and consistent generalization instability: no model generalizes uniformly, behavioral axes capture independent failure modes, and cross-dataset variation can reverse model rankings.

</details>

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 对 137,293 条法语决定性投票（主分析 137,113 场对战，116 个模型；联合估计 127,092 场）进行风格计量学分析，重建用户投票时可见的响应文本。；在控制格式、长度、可读性、词汇多样性和句子结构后，比较原始排名与调整后排名的差异，发现 116 个模型中有 36 个排名变化至少 10 位。；发现加粗使用（联合模型中每标准差胜率优势 +11.0%）和 MATTR 词汇多样性（+16.8%）是跨设定变化最小的两个关联，且加粗关联在多轮对话中显著减小，而 MATTR 关联变化不大。；建议同时发布原始排名和调整后排名作为透明的敏感性分析，而非用调整后排名替代原始排名。

Abstract: LLM arenas turn pairwise human preferences into model rankings. Those preferences may reflect how an answer is presented as well as what it says. We take a stylometric approach to 137,293 decisive French-language votes from the July 2026 Compar:IA release; the primary formatting analysis includes 137,113 battles across 116 models, and the joint estimates use the 127,092 battles with all required measurements. For each battle, we reconstruct the response visible when the user voted. We then compare the raw ranking with rankings adjusted for formatting, length, readability, vocabulary variety, and sentence structure. Presentation is associated with winning, but length, bold text, and lists tend to occur together, making their individual contributions hard to separate. Across the measured features, two associations change least across specifications: bold usage (+11.0% win odds per standard deviation in the joint model) and moving-average type-token ratio (MATTR), a measure of vocabulary variety that is less sensitive to answer length (+16.8%). The bold association is substantially smaller in observed multi-turn conversations, whereas the MATTR association changes little; because users choose whether to continue, this difference is descriptive rather than causal. The full adjustment moves 36 of 116 models by at least ten ranks. Yet comparisons with external benchmarks do not show that adjusted rankings better measure capability. We therefore recommend publishing raw and adjusted rankings side by side as a transparent sensitivity analysis.

</details>

<details>

Motivation: 语言智能体依赖外部harness提供控制决策（调查、修改、停止），如何通过训练让模型内化这些控制责任，在撤除对应支持后仍保持任务性能。

Method: HAT结合显式控制监督与在逐步减弱harness下收集的教师轨迹课程。模型在训练中学习从完全harness支持过渡到仅使用工具，通过课程化退火过程内化控制决策（如是否调查、是否修改、何时停止）。实验评估了9B和35B模型在SWE-QA和SWE-QA-Pro上，每个检查点在四种部署harness下测试。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 正式定义harness internalization目标：在撤除支持后承担指定控制责任并维持性能。；提出HARNESS ANNEALING TRAINING (HAT)，结合显式控制监督与基于逐步减弱harness的教师轨迹课程。；在SWE-QA和SWE-QA-Pro上对9B和35B模型进行实验，并在四种部署harness下评估每个检查点。

Abstract: Language agents rely on external harnesses to track state, organize workflows, and verify answers. Beyond providing tools and information, these harnesses supply control decisions about what to investigate, whether to revise, and when to stop. Training on successful harness-supported trajectories can improve task performance while leaving these decisions dependent on runtime intervention. We ask whether harness-supported experience can also teach the model to make these decisions, allowing the division of control to change as the model learns. We call this objective harness internalization: learning to assume specified control responsibilities while retaining task performance after the corresponding support is withdrawn. We introduce HARNESS ANNEALING TRAINING (HAT), which combines explicit control supervision with a curriculum over teacher trajectories collected under progressively weaker harnesses. Experiments with 9B and 35B models on SWE-QA and SWE-QA-Pro evaluate every checkpoint under four deployment harnesses. Selected annealed checkpoints operating with tools alone achieve scores close to those of their respective starting checkpoints deployed with the full harness. The benefits vary with model scale and deployment configuration, and further annealing does not uniformly improve performance. These findings suggest that harness-supported experience can help reduce the runtime control required by a trained agent.

</details>

*Zirui He,Haiyan Zhao,Jingyu Hu,Yinghao Wu,Chenxi Yuan,Yingcong Li,Yandong Bai,Mengnan Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HeadEdit 是一种无需梯度、无需更新参数的 LLM 行为校准方法：从成对完成中提取低秩行为子空间，并利用 prompt 坐标对 unembedding 矩阵做词表级校正，实现隐式自适应 steering。在三个任务、三个模型家族共九个设置上均获提升，推理开销可忽略且无系统性的通用能力损失，并揭示与梯度对齐的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法把行为错误当作计算问题，少有人验证所需行为是否已在线性可解码的表示中；如何在不更新参数的情况下校准LLM行为（拒绝、工具调用、抵抗错误用户声明）。

Method: HeadEdit 面向语言模型的行为校准：输入为 prompt，模型首先经过常规前向计算得到最终隐藏状态；同时利用成对完成（paired completions，即期望行为与不期望行为的对比样本）提取低秩行为子空间。推理时，对当前 prompt 的最终隐藏状态在该行为子空间中计算其坐标，进而生成一个词表级的校正量，作用于 unembedding 矩阵（输出嵌入/解嵌入层），从而调整输出 logits 实现隐式自适应 steering。整个过程无需梯度、无需参数更新，也不需人工指定目标 token，仅通过输出端校正改变下一 token 分布。论文还分析了该方法与基于梯度的偏好对齐之间的联系，表明低维表示可部分预测偏好调优对未见 prompt 输出 logits 的影响，且从模型学习到的子空间在调优后可复用而无需重新提取或重新调优。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "实验设置通过率/成功率", "value": "9/9 实验设置均获得提升（三个任务 × 三个模型家族）", "setting": "拒绝、工具调用、抵抗错误用户声明三类任务，三个模型家族，无在线A/B"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 从成对完成中提取低秩行为子空间；利用每个prompt在子空间中的坐标生成词表级校正，实现隐式自适应steering；无需手动指定目标token、无需梯度或参数更新；揭示与基于梯度的对齐方法的联系，并显示子空间可复用于调优后模型

Abstract: Alignment does not eliminate behavioral errors in language models. Models may still refuse benign requests, call unnecessary tools, or yield to false user claims. Current methods mitigate such errors as a computation problem, and rarely explore if the desired behavior is already encoded in the model's representation. Motivated by the observation that behavior-relevant information remains linearly decodable from the final hidden state even when the resulting logits produce the undesired behavior, we introduce HeadEdit, a gradient-free method that calibrates model behavior through the unembedding matrix. HeadEdit extracts a low-rank behavioral subspace from paired completions and uses each prompt's coordinates within it to generate a vocabulary-wide correction, thereby implementing implicitly adaptive steering without manually specified target tokens or parameter updates. HeadEdit improves all nine experimental settings across three tasks and three model families, with negligible inference overhead and no systematic loss of general capabilities. It also reveals a connection to gradient-based alignment. HeadEdit's low-dimensional representation partly predicts how preference tuning changes output logits on unseen prompts. The subspace learned from the model can also be reused after tuning, improving performance without re-extracting or retuning. These results show that HeadEdit provides a practical, lightweight, and interpretable way to calibrate model behavior through the unembedding matrix.

</details>

*Yihua Zhu,Qianying Liu,Weixu Qiao,Xuan Ren,Weiwei Xu,Wenbo Li,Wei Wang,Ruijia Chen,Xinmiao Luan,Yin Luo,Hao Huang,Xiang Zheng,Hidetoshi Shimodaira*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FAULT提出一种自诊断引导的终端信用重分配方法，将自然语言诊断的错误转化为步级信用，解决智能体RL中同结果组无信号与信用模糊的问题，在ALFWorld、WebShop等长时程任务上取得显著提升，并在Search-based QA上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长时程多步任务中，仅依赖终端结果奖励的智能体强化学习存在两个信用分配问题：同一结果组内无学习信号；终端奖励只提供轨迹级反馈，难以定位导致失败的中间决策。

Method: FAULT在智能体强化学习框架中引入自诊断模块，与策略共同训练。输入为多步交互轨迹，策略生成动作，自诊断器对中间决策和错误进行自然语言诊断。FAULT首先检查诊断证据的可靠性，然后将诊断出的错误转化为步级信用，并以终端结果作为锚点进行信用重分配。训练过程中，策略与自诊断器协同进化：策略根据步级信用更新，自诊断器根据任务结果进行改进；错误成本根据近期任务结果在线更新，学习相对错误代价。推理或部署时使用训练后的策略执行任务，自诊断器仅用于训练阶段提供信用信号，不增加额外的推理开销。该方法解决了终端奖励稀疏和轨迹级信用模糊的问题，通过细粒度信用分配提升长时程任务的学习效率。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "信号覆盖率", "value": "95%", "setting": "ALFWorld，对比GRPO的41%与GiGPO的72%"}, {"metric": "任务性能提升", "value": "在两个模型规模上均取得显著提升", "setting": "长时程ALFWorld与WebShop任务；在短时程Search-based QA上保持竞争力"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出FAULT（Self-Diagnosis-guided Terminal Credit Redistribution），将诊断出的错误转化为步级信用，并以终端结果锚定。；自诊断器与策略协同进化，错误成本根据近期任务结果在线更新。；通过诊断证据检查与错误成本学习，从同结果组中恢复学习信号，实现更精准的步级信用定位。

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a powerful approach for training large language model agents on multi-step tasks, yet reliance on terminal outcome rewards creates two credit-assignment problems, particularly in long-horizon tasks. First, same-outcome rollout groups provide no learning signal from terminal rewards. Second, terminal rewards provide only trajectory-wide feedback, making it difficult to identify which decisions caused a failure. Recent work supplements terminal rewards with finer-grained information from trajectory analysis, such as natural-language reflections on intermediate decisions and errors. However, natural-language diagnoses are difficult to use directly for credit assignment: their error claims may be unreliable, and they do not quantify how much each error should affect learning. We propose Self-Diagnosis-guided Terminal Credit Redistribution (FAULT), which turns diagnosed errors into explicit step-level credit anchored by terminal outcomes. FAULT checks diagnostic evidence and learns relative error costs from task outcomes. During training, the policy and self-diagnoser co-evolve, while error costs are updated online from recent outcomes. On ALFWorld, FAULT recovers learning signals from same-outcome groups, reaching 95% signal coverage versus 41% for GRPO and 72% for GiGPO, while better localizing credit to specific error steps. Across two model scales, FAULT delivers strong. improvements on the long-horizon ALFWorld and WebShop tasks while remaining competitive on short-horizon Search-based QA.

</details>

*Shuming Shi,Xiang Zhang,Hao Yu,Wenbo Fei,Changjian Wang,Zhan Wang,Guoqing Pang,Guangye Yu,Quan Lu,Ning Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 CNF 词表构建与 MCE 最小代价编码的分词方案，在 250K 词表下相比 o200k_base 和 qwen250k 压缩率提升 26%/30%，1M 词表下 token 效率提升超 60%，词表利用率从 52.9% 升至 96.9%，且 1.8B/8B 模型下游性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流 LLM 依赖 BPE 等分词器，相同文本产生不同长度的 token 序列，影响推理成本；如何训练更高效的分词器并在编码时取得全局最优分割，以提升压缩率、降低推理时间，同时保持下游性能。

Method: 论文提出 CNF 词表构建与 MCE 编码两阶段方法。CNF 训练阶段：首先直接统计训练语料中所有有效子串的出现次数，构建一个原始词表；然后使用 MCE 算法对训练语料进行分词，统计每个 token 的实际使用情况，在过滤步骤中剔除低频或无效 token，得到最终词表。MCE 编码阶段：对输入文本，定义每个文本段的代价函数（代价通常与 token 长度、频次等相关），将文本分割问题形式化为全局最小化总代价的优化问题，通过动态规划等算法求解最优分割边界，从而产生 token 序列。该编码过程不依赖 BPE 的合并列表，也不依赖 UnigramLM 的 token 概率，因此可应用于由 BPE、UnigramLM、CNF 等任意方法构建的词表。训练目标为最小化语料整体编码代价并提升词表利用率；推理时直接使用 MCE 进行编码，得到更短序列以降低推理时间。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "压缩率提升", "value": "26%", "setting": "250K 词表，英文网页文本，相比 o200k_base 分词器"}, {"metric": "压缩率提升", "value": "30%", "setting": "250K 词表，英文网页文本，相比 qwen250k 分词器"}, {"metric": "token 效率提升", "value": "超过 60%", "setting": "1M 词表，英文网页文本，相比 BPE 分词器"}, {"metric": "词表利用率", "value": "从 52.9% 提升到 96.9%", "setting": "1M 词表，英文网页文本"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 Counting and Filtering (CNF) 词表构建方法：直接统计有效子串构建原始词表，再基于 MCE 分割语料后的实际 token 使用进行过滤得到最终词表。；提出 Min-Cost Encoding (MCE) 文本编码算法：定义文本段的代价函数，全局最小化整体分割代价来确定最优分词。；MCE 不依赖 BPE 的合并列表或 UnigramLM 的 token 概率，可适配多种词表来源。；CNF-MCE 组合实现更高 token 效率、更强可扩展性（可扩展至 1M 词表）和更低的依赖。

Abstract: Mainstream large language models rely on a tokenizer to encode text into a token sequence. Different tokenizers may yield token sequences of substantially different lengths for the same text. With a fixed model architecture, shorter token sequences correspond to lower inference time. We propose a tokenizer training approach named Counting and Filtering (CNF) and a text encoding algorithm called Min-Cost Encoding (MCE). MCE defines a cost function over a text segment, and determines the best segmentation by globally minimizing the overall segmentation cost. CNF builds a raw vocabulary by directly counting valid substrings, and then constructs the final vocabulary through a filtering step based on actual token usage when segmenting the training corpus with MCE. The CNF-MCE conbination offers several advantages over BPE, including higher token efficiency, greater scalability, and lower dependency. Across six text categories and two vocabulary-size groups, CNF-MCE consistently achieves better compression than the evaluated BPE tokenizers. With a 250K vocabulary, CNF-MCE increases compression rate by 26% and 30% on English web text over the o200k_base and qwen250k tokenizers. Experiments scaling the vocabulary to 1M entries on English web text demonstrate sustained improvements over BPE, with a token efficiency improvement of over 60% and vocabulary utilization rising from 52.9% to 96.9%. The MCE algorithm does not depend on a merge list (as in BPE) or token probability (as in UnigramLM), making it applicable to a wide range of vocabularies, including those built from BPE, UnigramLM, CNF, and others. Language models trained from scratch at the 1.8B and 8B scales achieve comparable average performance to models using the BPE tokenizers across 11 benchmarks. These results demonstrate that CNF-MCE can improve token efficiency significantly while maintaining competitive downstream performance.

</details>


### [20] [AgSpec: Pushing the Limits of Retrieval-Based Speculative Decoding in Coding Agent Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01108)
*Sumin Lee,Sukmin Cho,Suengjae Lim,Youngjin Kwon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AgSpec improves retrieval-based speculative decoding for coding-agent pipelines by adding session/workspace/global corpora, indexing files in the agent's emission format, and adapting per-agent draft lengths offline-capped and online-updated. It reports up to 4.37x throughput at batch size 1 and 4.76x at batch size 16 over autoregressive decoding, outperforming five retrieval drafters and EAGLE-3 in most settings.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Retrieval-based speculative decoding is underused in coding-agent pipelines because reusable text is missing from standard corpora or stored in a format differing from agent emissions, and fixed draft lengths ignore that accept length varies across agents and drifts over turns.

Method: AgSpec is a retrieval-based speculative decoding framework for coding agents. It builds three corpora: session (retaining the ongoing session trajectory), workspace (opened files indexed in the agent's emission format), and global (previously seen text). At each decoding step, a retrieval engine proposes draft token continuations copied from these corpora. Draft length is controlled per agent: an offline-profiled cap bounds the maximum draft length, while online adaptation adjusts the length based on verification feedback from the target model. The target model verifies drafted continuations and accepts matching tokens, producing throughput gains without changing output distribution. This design addresses the corpus-mismatch and draft-length-drift problems of prior retrieval-based drafters in agentic coding pipelines.

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "generation throughput", "value": "up to 4.37x over autoregressive decoding", "setting": "batch size 1, two repository-level multi-agent coding benchmarks"}, {"metric": "generation throughput", "value": "up to 4.76x over autoregressive decoding", "setting": "batch size 16, two repository-level multi-agent coding benchmarks"}, {"metric": "acceptance / generation quality comparison", "value": "outperforms five retrieval-based drafters and EAGLE-3 in most evaluated settings", "setting": "repository-level multi-agent coding benchmarks and non-repository/non-multi-agent benchmarks"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Multi-source retrieval over session, workspace, and global corpora, retaining the ongoing session trajectory；Indexing opened files in the agent's original emission format to improve draft-token matches；Offline-profiled per-agent draft-length cap combined with online adaptation from verification feedback

Abstract: Retrieval-based speculative decoding (SD) drafts tokens by copying continuations from existing text, which suits coding agents that repeatedly reproduce code, logs, and earlier attempts. Yet existing methods fall short in agent pipelines: much of the reusable text is missing from their corpora or stored in a form that differs from what the agent emits, and their draft lengths ignore that accept length varies across agents and drifts over turns. We present AgSpec, a framework that supplies the corpus and draft-length policies that existing retrieval engines lack in coding-agent pipelines. AgSpec retrieves from session, workspace, and global corpora, retaining the ongoing session trajectory and indexing opened files in the agent's emission format. It bounds each agent's draft length with an offline-profiled cap and adapts the length online from verification feedback. On two repository-level multi-agent coding benchmarks, AgSpec outperforms five retrieval-based drafters and EAGLE-3 in most evaluated settings, raising generation throughput over autoregressive decoding up to 4.37$\times$ at batch size 1 and 4.76$\times$ at batch size 16. AgSpec also remains effective on benchmarks without a repository or a multi-agent pipeline, showing that its gains generalize to coding agents broadly.

</details>

*Yu Mao,Lei Yu,Zining Zhu,Yusheng Zheng,Haohang Li,Freda Shi,Yutong Yin,Zhaoran Wang,Jingcheng Niu*

TL;DR: 该研究提出随机奖励强化学习可以作为一种探测 LLM 可达能力的方法，解释为何虚假奖励也能提升模型性能。通过在 OLMo 检查点上进行实验，作者发现了训练响应过程中的三个不同阶段，并讨论了该方法与基于可解码性探测的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机奖励强化学习为何能提升 LLM 性能？这揭示的是模型内在机制、数据污染，还是模型的可达能力（reachability）？

Method: 未披露具体模型架构细节。根据摘要，该方法使用 OLMo 系列检查点，在不同预训练阶段（早期、后期、中期训练）进行强化学习训练。训练过程采用两种奖励：正确性奖励（correctness reward）和随机奖励（random reward）。通过比较不同检查点在相同 RL 设置下的性能提升，分析模型当前状态的可达性。此外，还进行了数字掩码监督微调（number-masked SFT）实验，以对比 RL 与 SFT 的效果模式。

Conclusion: 将随机奖励 RL 解释为一种探测模型可达能力（reachability）的工具，而非单纯的训练技巧；通过 OLMo 检查点在不同训练阶段（预训练早期、预训练后期、中期训练）的实验，发现三种不同的 RL 响应机制；对比数字掩码 SFT 实验，表明该现象不依赖于特定 RL 机制，具有跨方法的普遍性；将随机奖励 RL 与基于可解码性的探测（probing）联系起来，讨论了标签泄漏（label leakage）问题

Abstract: We connect the spurious-reward paradox to a model's reachability and propose random-reward reinforcement learning (RL) as a useful tool for the probing enterprise, addressing a decade-long debate over what probing performance actually reveals about a model. There are two prevailing explanations for the surprising finding that even random rewards can improve the performance of large language models (LLMs): one attributes the gains to particular mechanisms within RL training; the other to data contamination. Our results motivate a different view: spurious-reward RL can probe a model's reachability, or what further training can attain from its current state under specified constraints, beyond what is reflected in its current performance. Two OLMo checkpoints with the same accuracy on synthetic arithmetic (3.5%), for example, reach 8.5% and 55% in their best runs under the same correctness-rewarded RL. Examining OLMo checkpoints across pre-training and mid-training reveals three distinct regimes of training response: early on, RL produces little improvement even when correct answers are rewarded; later in pre-training, rewarding correct answers becomes effective while random rewards remain weak; and, upon entering mid-training, even random rewards can produce large gains. A similar ordering appears in a number-masked supervised fine-tuning (SFT) analysis of these checkpoints, suggesting that the pattern is not specific to a particular RL mechanism. Moreover, RL with random rewards offers a distinctive perspective on what training can make an LLM do, since its reward signal supplies no information about which answers are correct. By asking what training can attain without correctness feedback, it addresses the label-leakage side of a central problem in decodability-based probing: whether a successful probe reveals the model's capabilities or learns the task itself.

</details>

*Jungseob Lee,Sugyeong Eo,Seongtae Hong,Seungyoon Lee,Chanjun Park,Jaehyung Seo,Heuiseok Lim*

TL;DR: 提出方向性标签蒸馏：让冻结教师在其已知方向上为候选答案打分并选最优作为学生训练目标，避免教师生成答案的方向性错误传播；在父母-子女事实任务上，学生开放域准确率比先验校正反向标签提升13-15点，且学生几乎复现所选标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教师模型可能在某个关系方向上能回忆事实，却无法在反向生成答案，因此直接用教师生成答案作蒸馏目标会把这种方向性局限传递给学生，导致学生标签质量差。

Method: 方法以冻结的教师LLM与学生模型构成两阶段流程。输入是关系事实（如父母-子女关系）与查询模板。第一阶段标签构造：教师不在请求方向生成答案，而是在它已知的方向上对一组候选答案打分；候选由生成答案或从姓名列表中检索得到，并可用先验校正分数以抵消姓名先验；得分最高的候选被选为训练目标。第二阶段学生训练：学生以这些方向性验证后的高置信标签进行开放域问答训练，使标签质量成为学生准确率的主要驱动。评估时分别使用未筛选查询与不插入正确答案的候选检索设置，并通过词法相似度将生成答案匹配到固定姓名列表以统计标签复现；实验隐藏评估儿童的forward facts，比较known-direction标签与prior-corrected reverse标签训练的学生开放域准确率。

Conclusion: 方向性标签蒸馏：用冻结教师在它已知的方向上对候选答案打分，而非在请求方向生成答案；以最优得分候选作为学生训练目标，避免传播教师生成错误；先验校正的评分机制，用于消除姓名先验带来的方向偏置；发现更优方向取决于事实本身而非模板，并在被挖掘的以父母为显著实体的事实上发生方向反转；将生成答案通过词法相似度匹配到固定姓名列表以评估标签复现

Abstract: Knowledge distillation aims to transfer the factual knowledge of large language models to smaller models for efficient deployment. Yet a teacher may recall a relation in one direction while failing to generate the answer in the reverse direction. Distillation from its generated answers can therefore propagate this directional limitation to the student. The same teacher can nevertheless recognize such an answer by scoring the relation in the direction it knows. We introduce directional label distillation, in which frozen teachers score candidate answers in that known direction and the best-scoring candidate becomes the student's training target. On facts about parents and their children, known-direction scoring yields more accurate labels than scoring the requested direction, even after tuned corrections for name priors. With prior-corrected scores, the better direction depends on the facts rather than the template, and reverses on mined facts whose notable entity is the parent rather than the child. With the evaluated children's forward facts withheld, students trained on known-direction labels improve open-ended accuracy on their trained queries by 13 to 15 points over students trained on prior-corrected reverse labels. After generated answers are matched to a fixed name list by lexical similarity, students reproduce nearly all selected labels. Their accuracy largely follows label quality. The label advantage holds on unscreened queries and when candidates are retrieved without inserting correct answers. Our findings show that directional verification mitigates the transfer of errors from teacher-generated answers to students by providing more accurate training targets. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>

TL;DR: 论文探索连续扩散语言模型的最佳文本嵌入潜在空间，发现缩放同族嵌入模型可提升生成性能，但原始T5Gemma-2嵌入过于判别性导致扩散采样脆弱。通过将T5Gemma-2蒸馏到学生编码器，以教师解码概率为软标签，构建更连通的潜在空间，使中等DLM在OpenWebText上达到Gen. PPL 17.8，优于GPT-2-M。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续扩散语言模型的最佳潜在空间嵌入是什么，以及如何使文本嵌入更具扩散性（diffusibility），避免生成时因嵌入空间过于判别性而采样到无效嵌入。

Method: 连续扩散语言模型将文本编码为连续嵌入，在潜在空间进行扩散生成。输入文本经编码器（如T5Gemma-2）得到连续嵌入，扩散模型在该嵌入空间进行去噪生成，再解码回文本。论文首先替换不同嵌入模型（T5、T5Gemma-1、T5Gemma-2）作为潜在空间，发现同族放大嵌入模型可提升生成质量。原始T5Gemma-2嵌入过于判别性，导致扩散采样难以落入有效嵌入。为此，提出蒸馏方法：教师为原始T5Gemma-2，学生编码器学习教师解码器的概率分布作为软标签，通过拉近替代词嵌入的距离，同时保持原始编码-解码机制，使学生嵌入形成更连通、更易扩散的潜在空间。训练目标为匹配教师解码概率，推理时使用学生编码器生成嵌入并输入扩散模型生成文本。

Conclusion: 系统搜索不同嵌入模型（T5、T5Gemma-1、T5Gemma-2）作为连续DLM的潜在空间，发现按同族缩放嵌入模型能显著提升生成性能。；指出原始T5Gemma-2嵌入过于判别性，使生成对不完美采样脆弱，导致连续扩散无法到达有效嵌入。；提出将T5Gemma-2蒸馏到学生编码器，以教师解码概率作为软标签，拉近替代词嵌入，形成更连通、更具扩散性的潜在空间。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) offer a promising alternative to autoregressive (AR) language generation. Recent advances in continuous DLMs, which apply latent diffusion to continuous text embeddings, raise a practical question: which embedding makes the best latent space, i.e., the most diffusible? To answer this, we search through different embeddings and find that scaling the embedding model to stronger ones within the same family (T5 to T5Gemma-1 to T5Gemma-2) greatly improves generative performance. But the raw T5Gemma-2 embeddings are still not optimal. They are so discriminative that even the embeddings of plausible alternative words are separated, which makes the generation vulnerable to imperfect sampling. Consequently, continuous diffusion often fails to reach any of them and ends up at an invalid embedding instead. To address this, we distill T5Gemma-2 into a student encoder that learns the teacher's decoded probabilities as soft labels. Learning from such soft labels makes the student pull the alternative embeddings closer while maintaining the encoding-decoding mechanism. The distilled embeddings form a more connected and diffusible latent space, improving over the vanilla T5Gemma-2 embeddings. As a result, our medium-sized DLM achieves Gen. PPL 17.8 (against real-text PPL 15.4) at real-text entropy on OpenWebText, outperforming GPT-2-M on Gen. PPL.

</details>

*Chao Li,Shigeng Wang,Anbang Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文深入研究了学习式训练后量化（PTQ）中的优化不平衡问题，发现MSE重建损失导致各量化阶段损失幅度差异巨大，进而引发梯度不均匀。作者提出使用RMSE及其变体作为即插即用替代，通过隐式梯度归一化解耦优化强度与损失尺度，在多种LLM和量化设置下显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习式PTQ中MSE重建损失导致各量化阶段损失幅度差异巨大，进而引发梯度幅度和参数更新严重不均，即优化不平衡问题，限制了量化性能。

Method: 论文针对学习式训练后量化（PTQ），采用顺序量化框架：将预训练LLM划分为一系列单位（如Transformer块），逐阶段量化。每个阶段通过梯度下降优化辅助量化参数（缩放因子、旋转矩阵、裁剪阈值、适配器等），目标是最小化重建损失。论文系统分析了从第一阶段到最后阶段优化过程的演变，发现使用MSE时重建损失幅度在不同阶段差异巨大（称为重建损失尺度），导致梯度幅度高度不均匀，优化强度不平衡。作者提出用RMSE及其变体（样本、通道、令牌、元素级）替代MSE，通过隐式梯度归一化使优化强度与损失尺度解耦，从而均衡各阶段优化。推理时使用量化后的低比特模型，无需额外训练资源。未披露具体模型规模、训练数据、硬件等信息。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 系统性地揭示并定义学习式PTQ中的优化不平衡现象：重建损失尺度在不同阶段间差异显著，MSE将其转化为不均匀的梯度幅度。；提出一般性原则：各阶段的优化强度应与重建损失尺度解耦。；从理论上证明样本、通道、令牌、元素级别的RMSE变体通过隐式梯度归一化自然实现该原则，且作为即插即用替代显著优于MSE。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) methods typically use sequential quantization that partitions a pre-trained LLM into a series of units (e.g., transformer blocks), with one unit quantized at each stage. State-of-the-art PTQ methods are predominantly learning-based, optimizing auxiliary quantization parameters (e.g., scaling factors, rotation matrices, clipping thresholds, and adapters) via gradient descent to minimize a reconstruction loss. A common practice is to use mean squared error (MSE) as the reconstruction loss function, yet its induced optimization behavior remains largely unexplored. In this work, we take a holistic view of sequential quantization and systematically investigate how optimization evolves from the first quantization stage to the last, aiming for a deep understanding of optimization in learning-based PTQ schemes. Through extensive empirical studies spanning representative learning-based PTQ methods, LLM families, model scales, architectures, quantization settings and various tasks, we consistently uncover Optimization Imbalance: reconstruction loss magnitudes vary dramatically across stages, accompanied by highly uneven gradient magnitudes and parameter updates under MSE. We term the cross-stage range of loss magnitudes the reconstruction loss scale, and reveal that MSE translates the unexpectedly large reconstruction loss scale into highly uneven gradient magnitudes, which in turn lead to uneven optimization strength across quantization stages. This finding suggests a general principle for improving learning-based PTQ: optimization strength across stages should be decoupled from the reconstruction loss scale. Theoretically, we show that root mean squared error (RMSE) variants defined at the sample, channel, token, and element levels naturally realize this principle through implicit gradient normalization, outperforming MSE significantly as a drop-in replacement.

</details>


### [25] [Role-aware Heuristic Episodic Attention for Conversational LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00958)
*Wanyang Hong,Zhaoning Zhang,Yi Chen,Libo Zhang,Baihui Liu,Linbo Qiao,Zhiliang Tian,Dongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出REA（Role-aware Heuristic Episodic Attention）上下文管理框架，通过指令记忆与情节记忆分离策略，解决多轮对话中LLM的指令丢失与信息遗忘问题。在Long-MT-Bench+上，judge score从6.32提升至7.36（相对16.5%），平均延迟降低2.91倍，并在1.7B-7B参数骨干及中英文角色扮演任务上取得聚合增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮对话中，随着轮次增长，LLM会丢失持久性指令与相关信息，表现为注意力污染、稀释和漂移。

Method: REA框架将上下文分为指令记忆与情节记忆。指令记忆识别并存储全局约束，将其保留在专用前缀中，确保持久指令不随对话增长而丢失。情节记忆保留用户输入，并对模型回复进行压缩；同时采用启发式检索策略，为每个历史轮次选择原始文本、压缩表示或直接省略，以平衡信息保留与上下文长度。整体通过角色感知策略动态管理上下文，减少注意力污染、稀释和漂移。未披露训练目标、优化方法及具体推理服务流程。

Abstract: Large language models often lose track of persistent instructions and relevant information as multi-turn conversations grow. We study this cumulative contextual decay through three related failure modes: attention pollution, dilution, and drift. We propose REA (Role-aware Heuristic Episodic Attention), a context-management framework that assigns different persistence and representation policies to instructions and episodic interactions. Instructional Memory retains identified global constraints in a dedicated prefix. Episodic Memory preserves user inputs and compresses model replies, while heuristic retrieval selects raw text, compressed representations, or omission for each historical turn. On Long-MT-Bench+, REA improves the judge score from 6.32 to 7.36 on a 10-point scale, a 16.5% relative gain over the Vanilla baseline, and reduces average latency by 2.91$\times$. Additional evaluations show aggregate gains on three backbones spanning 1.7B-7B parameters and on Chinese and English role-playing tasks. These results support role-aware context management as a practical approach to maintaining conversational continuity and instruction adherence.

Abstract: We propose ReHoPER, an inference-only, zero-shot method that improves large language models' reasoning by generating and answering intermediate questions along multiple paths before the final answer. It iteratively plans a horizon of candidate intermediate questions, selects one to answer, and replans from the updated history. ReHoPER is task-agnostic, using the same generic instructions across datasets and models without labeled data or task-specific prompt design. Across multiple datasets, including iLLC, a new controlled benchmark for compositional reasoning, ReHoPER outperforms strong baselines, with the largest gains in the most compositional settings. Our implementation and the iLLC generator are publicly available to support future work.

Abstract: As large language models are increasingly deployed across diverse downstream tasks, efficient task adaptation has emerged as a central challenge. In response, a wide range of task adaptation methods have been proposed, spanning parameter-efficient fine-tuning, in-context learning, and embedding-injection approaches. However, these lines of work have largely evolved within individual paradigms, leaving their cross-paradigm relationships and trade-offs underexplored, especially for recently emerging embedding-based adaptations. This survey presents a unified framework that categorizes task adaptation methods by where and how task information is encoded: model weights, input prompts, or injected task embeddings. We provide a comprehensive taxonomy that integrates these paradigms, analyze their key strengths and limitations to explain how different adaptation paradigms have evolved, clarify relationships across paradigms, and highlight open problems for future research.

</details>

*Yufa Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究LLM如何在上下文中定位事实，提出序数寻址假说：模型按事实提及顺序而非实体名称来寻址。通过隐藏状态差分得到序数向量，验证了事实地址的有序性、可操控性、低秩性和涌现性，在Qwen、Gemma、Llama上跨1.5B至32B参数一致。该发现深化了对LLM上下文推理机制的理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型如何根据问题在上下文中定位到具体事实：是按实体名称还是按事实的提及顺序进行寻址？

Method: 论文未提出新的模型架构，而是对现有Transformer语言模型（Qwen、Gemma、Llama系列，参数范围1.5B至32B）进行机制分析。方法：给定上下文中的事实列表（如“Alice eats an apple. Bob eats a pear.”），对同一上下文分别提出询问不同事实的问题，记录模型隐藏状态的变化。对大量列表的该变化取平均，得到序数向量（ordinal vector）。该向量被假设指向事实在上下文中的提及顺序（第一、第二等）。通过将序数向量加到关于新列表第一个事实的问题上，可引导模型回答第二个事实，验证其可操控性。通过分析事实地址子空间的秩，发现其低秩且第一个提及的事实最易到达。进一步分析跨层分布，发现事实地址在中间偏后层共享，且在预训练早期即形成。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出序数寻址假说：每个提及顺序在模型状态中有一个事实地址，所有上下文共享，问题将状态移动到其询问事实对应的地址。；通过隐藏状态差分定义序数向量，并发现其指向按提及顺序排列的事实地址。；验证事实地址具有有序性、可操控性（steering）、低秩性和涌现性（跨模型规模与预训练早期出现）。

Abstract: Human reasoning depends on how objects are related within propositions. \textit{How do relations organize the language representations of contextual contents?} We give an LLM a list of facts in its context (e.g., \emph{Alice eats an apple. Bob eats a pear.}) and measure how its hidden state changes when the question switches from what Alice eats to what Bob eats. Averaged over many lists, this change is a steering vector, which we call the \emph{ordinal vector}. It points to a fact by its \emph{order of mention}, the order in which the facts were stated in the context. We find that LLMs represent the fact a question asks about by its order of mention, not by the name the question contains. We state this as the \textit{ordinal addressing hypothesis}: each order of mention has a \emph{fact address} in the model's state, shared by all contexts, and a question moves the state to the fact address of the fact it asks about, while the context supplies what that fact says. Across Qwen, Gemma, and Llama, fact addresses are (1) \emph{ordered by mention}: query states are organized by the order of facts, not of names, even when one fact has multiple subjects; (2) \emph{steerable}: added to a question about the first fact of a new list, the ordinal vector makes the model answer with the second fact of that list; (3) \emph{low-rank}: they span a low-rank subspace in which the first-mentioned fact is the easiest to reach, surprisingly similar to human recall; and (4) \emph{emergent}: they are shared in late-middle layers, hold from 1.5B to 32B parameters, and form early in pretraining. Language models reach a stated fact by where it was mentioned, deepening our understanding of LLM reasoning.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 通过干预数据中分布性不确定性与断言性不确定性来源，系统分离并控制训练和上下文中的不确定性影响；发现内部概率与语言化概率均同时受分布和断言不确定性影响，且二者的对齐程度超出独立追踪同一来源的预期；提出语言化概率可以作为探测模型内部概率分布的有效代理

Abstract: Large language models carry an internal notion of uncertainty in their sampling distribution, i.e., the probabilities they place on generating one answer rather than another. They can also be asked to state a confidence, in words or as a number: a verbalized uncertainty. Prior work suggests that internal probabilities track relative frequencies in the training data, and that verbalized probabilities track explicit probabilistic assertions in the training data. However, we do not know whether these two readouts are aligned, except when frequencies and probabilistic assertions in the training data happen to align. This limits our understanding of when we can use verbalized uncertainties as a proxy for either training data frequencies, or a model's internal distribution. We resolve this gap by systematically exploring how LLMs probability readouts are impacted by training and in-context data, via intervening on the underlying uncertainty sources in the data. We find that both internal and verbalized probability readouts are impacted by both distributional and asserted uncertainty in the training data. Further, we find that verbalized and internal probabilities are aligned beyond what would be expected by independently tracking the same uncertainty sources, suggesting that verbalized probabilities can be used to probe a model's internal distribution.

</details>

*Andrei Marchenko,Viacheslav Bezrukov,Oleg Kashurin,Inessa Fedorova,Dmitry Bocharov,Yuliana Shakhvalieva,Maria Tikhonova,Valerii Ternovskii*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出循环语言模型的实用训练配方，将训练预算从7.7T token降至310B token，1.4B LoopLM在12个基准上超越同参数密集模型（GSM8K +14、MATH +10、DROP +22），并给出将预训练密集模型转换为循环模型的最小化方法（仅一个输入混合标量和平滑出口损失），在Qwen3-1.7B上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环语言模型通过重复应用共享层块来增加有效深度，但现有大规模训练方案需要数万亿token的多阶段训练，且循环本身的收益难以与数据和训练差异分离。本文旨在建立实用、计算高效的循环语言模型训练配方。

Method: 循环语言模型（Looped LM）通过重复应用一个共享的层块来增加有效深度。输入表示与标准Transformer类似，但中间的共享块被多次重复执行。训练流程包括预训练和高质量中间训练，并采用学习率预热以及更强的出口门正则化来稳定循环训练，无需多阶段调度。出口门控制何时退出循环。在将预训练密集模型转换为循环模型时，仅添加一个可学习的输入混合标量，并采用平滑的出口损失，没有任何步骤特定的参数。推理时，模型可以多次应用共享块，通过出口门决定计算深度。该架构在相同参数量下比密集模型有更强的推理能力，并在相同推理计算量下以更少参数达到更大密集模型的性能。
