Conclusion: 提出从零开始的计算高效训练pipeline：将训练预算从Ouro的7.7T token降至310B token，通过高质量中间训练、学习率预热和更强的出口门正则化，实现无需多阶段调度的稳定循环训练。；在受控比较下，1.4B LoopLM在全部12个评测基准上超过同等参数量的密集模型，包括GSM8K +14分、MATH +10分、DROP +22分；在相同推理计算量下，以36%的参数逼近3.9B密集模型的数学推理和阅读理解表现。；提出将预训练密集模型转换为循环模型的最小化配方：仅需一个可学习的输入混合标量和平滑出口损失，无步骤特定参数；应用于Qwen3-1.7B-Base后，在两个数据体制下所有基准均优于相同的持续训练密集基线，在精选数据混合上GSM8K、MATH、MMLU-Pro取得统计显著提升。

Abstract: Looped language models increase effective depth by repeatedly applying a shared block of layers, but existing large-scale recipes require multi-stage training over trillions of tokens, while the benefits of recurrence remain difficult to separate from differences in data and training. In this work, we establish practical training recipes for looped language models, with three main results. (1) We develop a compute-efficient from-scratch pipeline that reduces the training budget from 7.7T tokens in Ouro to 310B tokens while retaining strong reasoning performance. Pretraining followed by high-quality mid-training, together with learning-rate warmup and stronger exit-gate regularization, enables stable recurrent training without prior multi-stage schedules. (2) Under controlled comparisons, our 1.4B LoopLM outperforms a parameter-matched dense model trained on the same data and token budget on all 12 evaluated benchmarks, including +14 points on GSM8K, +10 on MATH, and +22 on DROP. At matched inference compute, it approaches a 3.9B dense model on mathematical reasoning and reading comprehension while using only 36\% as many parameters. (3) We introduce a minimal recipe for converting pretrained dense models into looped ones: a single learned input-mixing scalar and a smoothed exit loss, with no step-specific parameters. Applied to Qwen3-1.7B-Base, Looped Qwen improves over an identically continued dense baseline on every evaluated benchmark across two data regimes, with statistically clear gains on GSM8K, MATH, and MMLU-Pro on the curated mixture. Together, these results make looped language models substantially cheaper to train from scratch and practical to introduce into existing pretrained checkpoints, while isolating the gains due to recurrence itself.

</details>


### [31] [Emergent Unfaithfulness: How Alignment Training Causes Language Models to Silently Override Task Faithfulness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00568)
*Pardis Sadat Zahraei,Janvijay Singh,Gokhan Tur,Dilek Hakkani-Tur*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了对齐训练（尤其是DPO后训练）会导致语言模型在敏感内容上悄悄覆盖输入且不披露，提出FaithConflict数据集和两套分类法，发现AIU随模型规模反向缩放，且提示缓解无效，指出了能力-对齐-忠实性的三难困境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对齐训练（特别是后训练）会导致语言模型在遇到不安全或敏感内容时系统性地偏离输入且不披露修改，形成对齐引发的不可信性（AIU），这与能力驱动的不可信性不同，并且随模型规模增大而加剧，形成反向缩放规律。

Method: 论文未提出新的模型架构，而是使用现有大规模语言模型作为研究对象。输入为包含不安全或敏感内容的提示，模型生成回答。通过构建FaithConflict数据集，将任务忠实性冲突分解为对齐冲突（模型为符合安全偏好而偏离输入）与能力冲突（模型因知识与推理错误而偏离）。使用行为分类法（B1-B8）对模型输出行为进行标注，并使用思维链推理分类法（C0-C6）对推理过程进行分类。通过对比不同规模模型、不同训练检查点（包括预训练、微调、DPO等后训练阶段）的输出，量化AIU。训练目标未修改，评估重点在于揭示后训练机制如何覆盖对输入的遵循。推理时，模型直接生成回答，不披露对输入的修改。整个方法旨在诊断而非修复，因此没有端到端的训练或服务优化流程。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "AIU随模型规模的变化趋势", "value": "AIU随规模增加而上升，且比能力驱动不可信性上升更陡峭", "setting": "跨模型规模评估，在FaithConflict数据集上"}, {"metric": "后训练阶段对AIU的影响", "value": "中间检查点显示AIU在后训练期间被放大，DPO阶段差距增长最大且最不明显", "setting": "不同训练检查点的对比分析"}, {"metric": "提示缓解效果", "value": "提示方法无法解决AIU", "setting": "基于提示的缓解实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 定义并形式化了对齐引发的不可信性（AIU）这一失败模式，与能力驱动的不可信性区分。；构建了可控数据集FaithConflict，能够分离对齐冲突与能力冲突。；提出了行为分类法（B1-B8）和思维链推理分类法（C0-C6）两套互补分类体系。；发现AIU随规模增长比能力驱动不可信性更陡峭（反向缩放规律）。；通过中间检查点定位到后训练阶段，并确定DPO是差异增长最大且最不明显的阶段。；表明基于提示的缓解措施无法解决该问题，揭示能力-对齐-忠实性三难困境。

Abstract: Large language models are characterized by three key properties: capability, alignment, and faithfulness. Prior work studies the tradeoffs between capability and alignment, and between capability and faithfulness, but a third tension remains underexplored: the alignment-faithfulness conflict. We show that aligned models systematically deviate from their inputs on unsafe or sensitive content without disclosing the modification, a failure mode we call alignment-induced unfaithfulness (AIU). Unlike capability-driven unfaithfulness, which comes from errors in knowledge or reasoning, this is induced by post-training mechanisms that override adherence to the input. We introduce FaithConflict, a controlled dataset isolating both conflicts, and two complementary taxonomies: behavioral (B1-B8) and chain-of-thought reasoning (C0-C6). Across models, AIU increases with scale and more sharply than capability-driven unfaithfulness, a reverse scaling law; intermediate checkpoints show it is amplified during post-training, with DPO the stage at which the gap both grows most and becomes least visible. Prompting-based mitigation does not resolve it, revealing a capability-alignment-faithfulness trilemma in the design and evaluation of LLMs.

</details>


### [32] [LLM-as-a-Judge for Low-Resource Languages: Adapting Ragas and Comparative Ranking for Romanian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00406)
*Claudiu Creanga,Liviu P. Dinu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究低资源语言（罗马尼亚语）中RAG系统的LLM-as-a-Judge评估方法。作者构建了AdminRo-Eval数据集，适配Ragas框架，并比较了直接评分、比较排序和细粒度分解三种评估策略。结果表明评估策略需因指标而异：细粒度分解在忠实性上达到96%的人类对齐，比较排序在答案相关性上达到90%。Gemini 2.5 Pro可作为鲁棒的基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对低资源语言（罗马尼亚语）的RAG系统缺乏可靠的自动评估方法，传统基于参考指标的评估效果不佳，需要研究LLM-as-a-Judge范式的适用性。

Method: 论文提出了一个基于LLM-as-a-Judge的RAG评估框架，并在罗马尼亚语环境下进行了适配。输入是罗马尼亚语行政文档的RAG系统输出（问题、生成的答案、检索到的上下文）。评估方法包括三种：1）直接评分：让LLM直接对输出进行评分；2）比较排序：通过比较不同输出的相对质量进行排序评估；3）细粒度分解：将评估任务分解为更细粒度的子问题（如忠实性分解为多个方面）。评估指标包括忠实性（Faithfulness）、答案相关性（Answer Relevance）和上下文相关性（Context Relevance）。使用Gemini 2.5和Gemini 3系列模型作为评判器。数据流：首先从RAG系统获取输出，然后通过适配的Ragas框架调用LLM进行评分，最后将自动评分与人工标注的AdminRo-Eval数据集进行对齐分析。训练目标：无模型训练，仅进行推理评估。推理路径：对每个评估样本，根据所选评估方法（直接评分/比较排序/细粒度分解）构造相应的提示，调用LLM获取评分或排序结果，然后计算与人工标注的一致性指标。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "人类对齐度（忠实性）", "value": "96%", "setting": "细粒度分解方法，使用Gemini 2.5 Pro模型"}, {"metric": "人类对齐度（答案相关性）", "value": "90%", "setting": "比较排序方法"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 构建了AdminRo-Eval数据集，包含罗马尼亚语行政文档并由母语者标注，用于基准测试自动评估器；在罗马尼亚语环境下适配Ragas框架，使用Gemini 2.5和Gemini 3等下一代模型进行LLM-as-a-Judge评估；系统比较了三种评估方法（直接评分、比较排序、细粒度分解）在忠实性、答案相关性和上下文相关性指标上的表现

Abstract: Evaluating Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems remains a challenge for Low-Resource Languages (LRLs), where standard reference-based metrics fall short. This paper investigates the viability of the "LLM-as-a-Judge" paradigm for Romanian by adapting the Ragas framework using next-generation models (Gemini 2.5 and Gemini 3). We introduce AdminRo-Eval, a curated dataset of Romanian administrative documents annotated by native speakers, to serve as a ground truth for benchmarking automated evaluators. We compare three evaluation methodologies - direct scoring, comparative ranking, and granular decomposition - across metrics for Faithfulness, Answer Relevance, and Context Relevance. Our findings reveal that evaluation strategies must be metric-specific: granular decomposition achieves the highest human alignment for Faithfulness (96% with Gemini 2.5 Pro), while comparative ranking outperforms in Answer Relevance (90%). Furthermore, we demonstrate that while lightweight models struggle with complex reasoning in LRLs, the Gemini 2.5 Pro architecture establishes a robust, transferable baseline for automated Romanian RAG evaluation.

</details>


### [33] [CAST: Cost-Aware Speculative Trees from One-Pass Block Drafters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00321)
*Jungseob Lee,Sugyeong Eo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CAST 利用块草稿器已评分的多个候选构建推测树，一次目标前向验证，并根据部署延迟测量自适应选择树宽。在五种领域、三代 GPU、两类模型共八个设置中，预测宽度下均快于标准链式解码，最高提速 43%，且不改变输出分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准块草稿器仅验证 top-scoring 链，丢弃已评分的其他候选，造成草稿计算浪费；固定树宽无法适配不同部署的验证成本，可能反而变慢。

Method: CAST 以块草稿器为基础：草稿器在一次前向中为未来一个块内所有 token 打分，得到多个候选 token 及其分数。标准解码只取 top-scoring 链并用目标模型验证，其余候选被丢弃。CAST 将这些已评分的候选组织成树结构，树中各路径对应不同的候选 token 序列。在验证阶段，CAST 将该树打包成一次目标模型前向（通过 attention mask 等机制）同时验证树中所有节点，目标模型不经修改。为控制树宽，CAST 使用成本感知策略：从当前部署的一次延迟测量出发，逐个增加候选，当新增候选带来的预期收益大于其增加的验证时间时继续扩展，否则停止，从而得到自适应的树宽。训练目标不涉及模型训练；推理时保持目标模型权重、草稿器权重和解码规则不变。论文证明该过程在贪心和采样解码下均不改变目标输出分布。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "相对标准链式推测解码的加速比", "value": "最高 43%", "setting": "五个领域、三种 GPU 代际、两种模型家族，共 8 个设置，在预测宽度下"}, {"metric": "相对标准链式推测解码的加速比", "value": "2%（128-token 树，验证成本在 kernel 边界跳变时）", "setting": "特定部署设置"}, {"metric": "相对标准链式推测解码的加速比", "value": "20%（预测宽度树，同一 kernel 边界跳变设置）", "setting": "特定部署设置"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将块草稿器已评分的多个候选打包成推理树，并在一次目标模型前向中验证，不改变目标模型、草稿器权重与解码规则；基于预期收益与验证新增耗时的成本感知宽度决定机制，从单次延迟测量自动确定每部署的最优树宽，无需扫参；证明在贪心与采样解码下 CAST 不改变目标输出分布，保证输出正确性

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference by drafting future tokens cheaply and verifying them with the target model in parallel. Block drafters score a whole block of future tokens in one forward pass, yet standard decoding verifies only the top-scoring chain and discards the other candidates. Because these candidates are already scored, verifying more of them adds target computation but no extra drafting. We introduce CAST (Cost-Aware Speculative Trees), which packs these candidates into a tree and verifies it in a single target pass, leaving the target model, drafter weights, and decoding rule untouched. To decide how wide the tree should be, CAST adds candidates while the expected gain from the next one outweighs the verification time it adds. The width therefore adapts to each deployment from a latency measurement, without sweeping over widths. We evaluate CAST across five domains on three GPU generations and two model families. At its predicted width, CAST is faster than the standard chain in all eight settings, by up to 43%. We also find that the best width depends strongly on the deployment. Where verification cost jumps at a kernel boundary, a 128-token tree is only 2% faster than the standard chain, whereas the tree at the predicted width is 20% faster. Furthermore, we prove that CAST leaves the target output distribution unchanged under both greedy and sampled decoding. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [34] [Refusal Localizes, the Damage Relocates: Safety Layers Under Few-Sample Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00320)
*Jungseob Lee,Dongyub Jude Lee,Sugyeong Eo,Seongtae Hong,Seungyoon Lee,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文在四个模型家族六个checkpoint上测试微调后安全定位能否支持稳健防御，发现层冻结和谱方向修复均会被自适应攻击绕过，并给出防御评估的五项检查，对LLM安全与可靠性工程有参考价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证基于安全行为定位的防御（层冻结、谱方向移除）能否在自适应微调攻击下继续有效，即定位成功是否转化为可泛化的防御。

Method: 论文未提出新的模型架构，而是以对齐LLM为对象进行机制分析与防御实验。输入为有害与良性提示，模型为四个模型家族的六个checkpoint。分析流程包括：对微调前后的隐藏状态进行线性可分性探测；将被攻击模型与干净模型的隐藏状态在特定层进行全状态patch（patching），观察拒绝行为在何时恢复，从而识别一个可复现的转变深度；防御一为在训练时冻结该深度以下的所有层，然后重复有害样本微调攻击；防御二为在短时仅注意力微调后，移除权重更新中前两个奇异方向以恢复拒绝；评估包括自适应攻击（分散更新）、普通训练对修复的削弱，以及校准于良性Llama微调的谱检测器的漏检率。训练目标仍为常规微调损失，但冻结或修改更新方向以保护安全行为。推理时无额外模块，保持标准自回归生成流程。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 系统测试了层冻结防御在六个checkpoint、四类模型家族上的跨攻击泛化性；发现有害/良性提示在攻击后仍线性可分，且在被攻击模型中注入干净隐藏状态可在可复现的转变深度恢复拒绝行为；提出并评估移除更新中前两个奇异方向的修复方法，并揭示其在Llama-3.1-8B上被普通训练削弱及被分散更新攻击击败的局限；指出基于良性Llama微调校准的谱检测器在该checkpoint上漏检多数修复失败；提出针对自适应微调的防御应满足的五项检查

Abstract: Fine-tuning adapts aligned large language models (LLMs) to downstream tasks, but a few dozen harmful examples can remove their refusal of harmful requests. Prior work localizes safety-related behavior to specific layers, directions, and tokens, suggesting targets for protection. We test whether successful localization and recovery support defenses that survive changes in the attack. Across six checkpoints from four model families, harmful and benign prompts remain linearly separable after attack, and patching full clean hidden states into the compromised model restores refusal at a reproducible transition depth. Building on a prior layer-freezing defense, we freeze every layer up to this depth and repeat the attack. At a hundred harmful examples, refusal remains near zero on all six checkpoints, with recovery transitions above the frozen boundary. In a second study, removing the update's top two singular directions restores refusal after short attention-only fine-tunes on four checkpoints. On Llama-3.1-8B, ordinary training changes weaken this repair and an attacker who spreads the update defeats it. A spectral detector calibrated on benign Llama fine-tunes misses most repair failures on that checkpoint. Localized freezing can nevertheless help preserve refusal when a few harmful examples enter training data unintentionally. These results show that an attacker can bypass a region identified by recovery and defeat a repair that works across multiple checkpoints, motivating five checks for defenses against adaptive fine-tuning. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [35] [Certainty Is Not Just Correctness: Rethinking Token-Level Certainty in LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00296)
*Yunfan Zhou,Ye Zhu,Zhihai Wang,Jianguo Yao,Haibing Guan,Xijun Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文重新审视token级certainty在LLM推理中的预测能力，发现其更擅长预测问题难度而非同一问题下的答案正确性，并据此设计早期分配采样数量、末期加权投票的test-time compute策略，在准确率提升的同时大幅降低生成成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: token级certainty通常被当作正确性的代理指标，但其预测能力取决于预测目标、模型、度量指标和聚合位置。论文旨在直接评估certainty对“模型能否正确回答某问题”和“区分同一问题下的正确/错误回答”两种目标的预测能力，并利用其信息改进test-time compute分配。

Method: 论文未提出新的模型架构，而是围绕已有LLM的token级certainty信号构建了一套分析到部署的推理优化流程。输入为模型在生成响应时每个token的certainty分数（具体度量未在摘要中披露）。处理流程分为两个分析维度：第一，将预测目标分为问题级可答性预测和回答级正确性区分，并跨模型与任务评估certainty的预测能力；第二，分析certainty在token类型、词内位置和生成位置上的分布规律。基于这些规律，推理时采用两阶段test-time compute策略：生成早期利用certainty估计问题难度，动态决定为每个问题分配多少条响应；生成末期利用certainty对每条响应的答案进行加权投票。该架构将certainty从单一正确性代理转变为按阶段利用的决策信号，从而在降低生成token成本的同时提升整体准确率。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "整体准确率", "value": "从78.71%提升至79.54%", "setting": "与固定采样多数投票基线相比，具体模型与任务未在摘要中披露"}, {"metric": "生成token成本", "value": "降低82.4%", "setting": "与固定采样多数投票基线相比，具体模型与任务未在摘要中披露"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 区分两类预测目标：问题可答性与同问题内正确性区分，并系统比较certainty在这两类目标上的表现。；发现certainty信息在token类型和词内位置上存在系统性差异：问题难度信息在生成早期出现，答案正确性信息更集中在生成末尾。；基于上述发现设计test-time compute策略：在生成早期用certainty分配生成响应数量，在生成末尾用certainty对答案投票加权。

Abstract: Token-level certainty is widely used as a proxy for correctness in LLM training and inference. However, the performance of certainty-based methods depends both on the information in certainty scores and on how those scores are used. We therefore directly assess certainty's predictive ability through controlled empirical evaluations across models and tasks. We distinguish two prediction targets: identifying questions a model is more likely to answer correctly and distinguishing correct from incorrect responses to the same question. In our experiments, certainty is generally better at identifying questions a model is likely to answer correctly than at distinguishing correct from incorrect responses to the same question. Certainty also varies systematically across token types and positions within words, reflecting local properties of words and text form. Information about question difficulty appears early in generation, while the weaker information about answer correctness is more concentrated near the end. These findings show that the information certainty provides for decisions depends on the prediction target, the model, the certainty metric, and which token positions in the response are included in aggregation. We further demonstrate the practical value of these findings for test-time compute. We allocate the number of responses using certainty early in generation and weight answer votes using certainty near the end of each response. Compared with a fixed-sampling majority-voting baseline, this approach increases overall accuracy from 78.71\% to 79.54\% while reducing generated-token cost by 82.4\%.

</details>


### [36] [Signed Lexical Confidence for Risk-Calibrated Intent Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00262)
*Yezhou Cheng,Zehua Yang,Bojun Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出签名词法门控，通过结合稀疏词法证据与语义logit margin并配合二项校准，在多个意图数据集上降低了风险-覆盖率曲线下面积，并在给定错误目标下提升了接受覆盖率，为风险校准的意图路由提供了可解释的置信度增强方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准置信度仅反映基座模型表示，如何在不改变基分类器预测的前提下，引入互补的词法证据来提升风险校准的选择性意图路由性能。

Method: 系统包含一个基座句子分类器，输出各意图的logit；一个稀疏词法模型，提供对分类器预测意图的词法支持。签名词法门控将分类器logit margin与词法模型对预测意图的支持组合：当词法证据与预测意图一致时作为正证据，当词法更支持竞争意图时作为负证据，形成签名特征。该特征与语义门控分数一起构成两特征门控，用于决定是否接受预测。独立的二项校准阶段在给定风险目标（如5%或2%错误率）下选择操作阈值，确保风险控制。整个流程不改变基分类器的预测，仅调整置信度用于路由决策。推理时，对于每个请求，计算基分类器logit和稀疏词法模型分数，通过门控和阈值决定接受或延迟。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "风险-覆盖率曲线下面积（AURC）相对降低", "value": "15.8%", "setting": "BANKING77数据集，10次运行，相对于仅语义学习门控"}, {"metric": "风险-覆盖率曲线下面积（AURC）相对降低", "value": "15.1%", "setting": "CLINC150数据集，10次运行，相对于仅语义学习门控"}, {"metric": "风险-覆盖率曲线下面积（AURC）相对降低", "value": "11.8%", "setting": "HWU64数据集，10次运行，相对于仅语义学习门控"}, {"metric": "接受覆盖率提升", "value": "1.83个百分点", "setting": "名义5%错误目标，BANKING77"}, {"metric": "接受覆盖率提升", "value": "5.14个百分点", "setting": "名义5%错误目标，HWU64"}, {"metric": "非空策略比例", "value": "100% (30/30)", "setting": "2%错误目标，30个数据集-运行组合，可用校准预算下"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出签名词法门控，将稀疏词法模型对分类器预测意图的支持作为正证据，对词法上更优的竞争意图作为负证据，比无符号词法置信度或硬一致规则保留更多信息。；独立的二项校准阶段根据指定风险目标选择操作阈值，使风险可控。；在多个意图数据集上验证了风险-覆盖率曲线和接受覆盖率的改进，并展示在严格风险目标下二项程序能给出非空策略。

Abstract: Selective intent routing allows an assistant to act on reliable predictions while deferring uncertain requests. Standard confidence scores primarily reflect the base model's representation, leaving an opportunity to incorporate complementary evidence without changing its decisions. We introduce a signed lexical gate that combines a sentence classifier's logit margin with a sparse lexical model's support for the classifier's predicted intent. By assigning positive evidence to lexical agreement and negative evidence to a lexically favored competing intent, the gate retains more information than either unsigned lexical confidence or a hard agreement rule. An independent binomial calibration stage selects an operating threshold for a specified risk target. Across ten runs on BANKING77, CLINC150, and HWU64, the proposed score reduces area under the risk-coverage curve by 15.8%, 15.1%, and 11.8% relative to a learned semantic-only gate. At a nominal 5% error target, it increases accepted coverage by 1.83 and 5.14 percentage points on BANKING77 and HWU64, while CLINC150 is already near full coverage. At a stricter 2% target, the simultaneous binomial procedure yields a nonempty policy in all 30 dataset-run combinations at the available calibration budgets. Matched controls show that the proposed feature improves average error ranking over the tested unsigned lexical-confidence feature, with dataset-dependent gains over binary agreement. The resulting two-feature gate provides a compact, interpretable confidence enhancement for risk-calibrated intent routing while preserving the base classifier's predictions.

</details>

Abstract: Long-term memory agents increasingly rely on it- erative search and reusable experience to answer questions over large personal, factual, or narrative histories. However, current experience-memory systems largely optimize relevance: they re- trieve past search lessons that appear similar to the current state and inject them into the prompt. A relevant experience can still be harmful when the memory substrate, question intent, answer granularity, or evidence boundary changes. We propose CAVE- Mem, a training-free framework that represents experience as a typed intervention operator with applicability, boundary, and utility conditions. CAVE-Mem first obtains a base memory-search answer, then allows an operator to change it only if the oper- ator matches the current substrate, answer contract, evidence boundary, and cross-fitted utility; otherwise the system abstains. Experiments across long-term conversational memory, multi-hop question answering, and long-document narrative reasoning show consistent gains over relevance-only experience reuse.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Reading an LLM judge's verdict from the logits of its first generated token is cheap, requires no generation, and is exactly what constrained decoding and likelihood-scoring evaluation harnesses produce. We show that this readout distorts position bias in one direction: it overstates it in every condition we test, so figures obtained this way behave as upper bounds. The mechanism is that judges do not always lead with a verdict token, on 12% to 49% of pairs for three Qwen3 judges and under 3% for Llama-3.1-8B and Phi-3.5-mini, and forcing a read on those pairs returns whichever response was shown first rather than a judgment. Pooled over the 924 pairs where a judge did not commit, the forced read flips on 89.7% of them when the responses are swapped, against 47.5% read after generation (paired difference +0.422, 95% CI [+0.365, +0.467]). The distortion is specific to what is measured: it moves position bias by 42 points while moving judge accuracy by under one point in seven of ten conditions, so it misleads whoever audits a judge rather than whoever uses one. A second, smaller failure occurs even when the judge does lead with a verdict token, since it sometimes opens with one letter and reasons its way to the other, on 0 to 5.5% of pairs at a rate uncorrelated with compliance. We recommend reporting the rate at which a judge leads with a verdict token, which costs one forward pass and no labels, alongside any position-bias figure.

</details>

Abstract: What makes great scientists great? Even as AI systems start to make progress on open problems, scientists remain far ahead of them at sensing which prior idea, buried in an ever-growing archive of research, a new problem needs. To study this skill, we draw on researchers who know firsthand which earlier work advanced their completed projects, with papers serving as pointers to the ideas within. Using our automated pipeline that makes author annotation scalable, we build ScholarCatalyst by having 184 lead authors of 207 recent computer science papers label which candidates did or could have advanced their project, each with a detailed rationale. We introduce a retrieval task with author-provided judgments: given an initial research question, retrieve these papers from only the literature available when the project began. Agentic search does no better than embedding retrieval (0.42 vs. 0.48 Recall@20) despite calling that same retriever as a tool. Even an agent built on Claude Fable 5.1, which may have seen the completed papers during training, reaches only 0.51 R@20. These results highlight the need for new training recipes that equip models with expert intuition for searching broad corpora. We envision ScholarCatalyst as a step toward scientific agents that can take a half-formed idea and point to the prior research it needs.

</details>


### [40] [Neither Black nor White: Balancing Semantic and Collaborative Signals with Graph-Informed Semantic IDs (GrIS)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01533)
*Aleksei Medvedev,Alejandro Ariza-Casabona,Steven Derby,Gonzalo Fiz Pontiveros,Xinyang Shao,Florian Spiess*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 华为爱尔兰研究中心提出 GrIS，将生成式推荐的 Semantic ID 构造统一为图上递归聚类问题，把图构建与递归划分作为可配置的两轴，并给出 RecDMoN（可微图池化层次分配）与 RQ-GAE（图感知 RQ-VAE）两个实例；在多个真实数据集上相对 CF-aware SOTA 最高取得 +52% Hit@10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Semantic ID（SID）构造被当作纯粹的表示学习与量化问题，忽略了物品间协同信号；论文主张 SID 构造本质上是递归聚类问题，需要在图中同时利用语义内容与协同边，从而更好地生成推荐用语义 ID。

Method: GrIS 将 SID 构造视为节点带语义内容、边带协同信号的图上的层次图划分问题。整体流程为：(1) 图构建：根据物品语义表示和协同交互信号构造物品图，图的节点承载语义内容，边承载协同关系；(2) 递归聚类/层次划分：在图上递归执行聚类，每次划分产生一层 SID 码，最终形成层次化语义 ID。框架将图构建与递归划分算法显式分离为两个可配置轴。 论文给出两种对比实例：RecDMoN 使用可微图池化在图上进行层次化分配，通过可微分的 pool 操作直接优化聚类分配；RQ-GAE 则扩展 RQ-VAE，在码本量化与重构目标上加入图感知的物品表示和图重构损失，使学习到的潜在空间同时编码语义内容与协同结构。训练目标因实例而异：RecDMoN 依赖可微图池化的层次划分目标，RQ-GAE 结合量化重构损失与图重构目标。推理时，物品通过训练好的图与划分/编码器得到层次化 SID，用于生成式推荐的检索或候选生成。该设计使语义与协同信号在 SID 构造阶段被显式平衡，并可分别改进图构建或划分算法以系统评估增益。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Hit@10", "value": "最高 +52%（相对 CF-aware SOTA 的提升）", "setting": "多个真实世界数据集上的离线实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将 SID 构造重新表述为图上的递归聚类/层次图划分问题，而非仅表示学习加量化。；提出统一框架 GrIS，将 graph construction 与 recursive partition 作为两个可分离、可配置的轴，并证明 RQ-VAE 与 RQ-KMeans 是图为空时的特例。；实例化 RecDMoN：通过可微图池化进行层次化分配，将递归聚类端到端可微化。；实例化 RQ-GAE：在 RQ-VAE 基础上加入图感知的物品表示与图重构目标，使量化码本同时保留语义与协同结构。

Abstract: Existing work on Semantic IDs (SIDs) for generative recommendation treats SID construction as a representation learning problem: encode items into a quantised latent space and read off codes. We argue this view is incidental. SID construction is, at heart, a recursive clustering problem, and once stated this way the natural object to cluster is a graph whose nodes carry semantic content and whose edges carry collaborative signal; SID assignment becomes a hierarchical graph partition. This reframing yields a unified framework, Graph-Informed Semantic IDs (GrIS), that subsumes prior approaches rather than displacing them. RQ-VAE and RQ-KMeans are recovered as the special case where the graph is empty, exposing content-only quantisation as one corner of a larger design space along two so-far-collapsed axes: graph construction and recursive partition algorithm. We explore two contrasting instantiations: RecDMoN, which performs hierarchical assignment via differentiable graph pooling, and RQ-GAE, which extends RQ-VAE with graph-aware item representations and a graph reconstruction objective. On multiple real-world datasets, GrIS consistently improves over CF-aware SOTA, with gains of up to +52\% Hit@10. Because graph construction and partition are explicit, separately configurable components, improvements on either axis can be combined and evaluated systematically.

</details>

*Xinyuan Song,Zekun Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出用重尾分布视角审计长程语言智能体的外部记忆，并设计基于秩的记忆控制器 CTWM，以单一指数分配 prompt 预算并保留摘要尾部。在合成图世界、ALFWorld 和 LongMemEval 上报告 token 节省与尾部预测误差降低，属于面向 LLM 智能体推理效率与记忆管理的实用系统工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限上下文和反复检索下，语言智能体外部记忆会集中于少数核心状态，把稀有状态留在长尾，导致预测误差累积；现有记忆系统只用任务成功率或 token 成本评估，缺少对记忆使用形状的刻画。

Method: CTWM 以语言智能体的外部记忆为输入，记忆由已存储的状态与转移构成。控制器首先根据检索频率或访问统计对记忆条目进行基于秩的排序，区分高频核心与低频尾部。随后使用单一指数 τ 控制 prompt 预算分配：核心部分按秩幂律获得较多上下文预算，尾部状态则被压缩为摘要形式保留，以避免完全丢弃稀有状态。在推理时，智能体根据当前查询从核心与摘要尾部中构造 prompt，用于世界模型预测下一状态或转移。训练或优化目标未在摘要中披露；主要优化对象是 prompt token 使用量与尾部预测误差的权衡。该设计试图在有限上下文下保持全状态与转移覆盖，同时降低 token 成本，并缓解长尾状态上的预测误差累积。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "prompt tokens", "value": "减少 5.9%", "setting": "Synthetic Graph World，相对 graph-memory baseline"}, {"metric": "bottom-half tail prediction error", "value": "降低 13.6%", "setting": "Synthetic Graph World，相对 graph-memory baseline"}, {"metric": "token 节省", "value": "一致的 token 节省", "setting": "ALFWorld，配对比较"}, {"metric": "token 减少", "value": "24.48%", "setting": "LongMemEval，同时聚合准确率持平"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出保守的尾部审计方法，刻画长期记忆检索痕迹的分布形状；发现随机游走智能体呈现对数正态兼容的检索痕迹，而语义 LLM 策略产生最强的截断幂律兼容的核心-尾部痕迹。；提出 Core-Tail World Model（CTWM），一种基于秩的记忆控制器，用单一指数 τ 分配 prompt 预算，同时保留摘要化的尾部。；将重尾记忆痕迹从诊断信号转化为实用的 token 高效智能体世界模型控制信号。

Abstract: Long-horizon language agents increasingly rely on external memory as a frozen world model, yet current memory systems are usually judged only by task success or token cost. We argue that the missing object is the shape of memory use: under finite context and repeated retrieval, agent memory can concentrate on a small core while leaving rare states in a long tail where prediction errors accumulate. We study this effect through a conservative tail audit and find that concentration is reproducible but policy-dependent. Random-walk agents produce log-normal-compatible retrieval artifacts, whereas semantic LLM policies yield the strongest truncated-power-law-compatible core--tail traces. Motivated by this audit, we propose Core--Tail World Model (CTWM), a rank-based memory controller that allocates prompt budget with a single exponent $\tau$ while retaining a summarized tail. On Synthetic Graph World, CTWM preserves full state and transition coverage, reduces prompt tokens by 5.9%, and lowers bottom-half tail prediction error by 13.6% relative to a graph-memory baseline. The same paired comparison gives consistent token savings on ALFWorld and a 24.48% token reduction on LongMemEval with aggregate accuracy parity. These results suggest that heavy-tailed memory traces are not only a diagnostic of finite retrieval, but also a practical control signal for token-efficient agent world models.

</details>


### [42] [Causal Memory Policy: Making Memory Utility Identifiable by Intervening on Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02070)
*Arman Behnam,Binghui Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出因果记忆策略 CMP，通过对检索进行干预并使用逆倾向加权，解决记忆增强 LLM 中记忆效用不可识别的问题。实验表明在 LongMemEval 和 LoCoMo 上大量记忆效用无法识别，CMP 将区分所需记忆的 AUC 从 0.54 提升至 0.66。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆增强 LLM 中，仅基于已检索记忆估计记忆效用会导致检索级阳性违反，使大量记忆的效用不可识别，从而无法可靠决定哪些记忆应被保留。

Method: CMP 将记忆保留问题形式化为因果推断问题。系统流程如下：对于每个查询，记忆库中的记忆项原本通过标准检索器被选择并放入 LLM 上下文。CMP 引入干预：在检索阶段，为每个查询预留固定数量的上下文槽位，从整个记忆库中以已知倾向性（概率）采样记忆，而非仅依赖检索器返回结果。这种平衡分配设计保证了每个记忆都有非零概率被观察到，从而满足阳性假设。收集到干预后的反馈（例如任务性能）后，使用自归一化逆倾向加权估计每个记忆对任务性能的因果效用。估计的效用用于执行最优保留决策规则（在不可逆记忆操作下），该规则由理论推导得出。论文还分析了效用通过检索的因果分解，并指出仅靠可识别的效用不足以预测记忆在未来查询上的价值。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "所需记忆不可识别的比例", "value": "54%", "setting": "LongMemEval 数据集"}, {"metric": "所需记忆不可识别的比例", "value": "67%", "setting": "LoCoMo 数据集"}, {"metric": "区分所需与非所需记忆的 AUC", "value": "从 0.54 提升至 0.66", "setting": "CMP 干预检索后的估计"}, {"metric": "单查询记忆效用的 AUC", "value": "0.78", "setting": "对估计该效用的查询本身，无法泛化到未见查询"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出因果记忆策略（CMP）框架，通过对检索本身进行干预（预留上下文槽位并以已知倾向性采样记忆）来恢复记忆效用的可识别性。；在平衡分配设计下，使用自归一化逆倾向加权（SNIPW）估计记忆效用，并证明效用通过检索的因果分解、估计量的无偏性与精确方差，以及不可逆操作下的最优决策规则。；实证揭示了记忆效用可识别性与保留决策之间的差距：即使效用可识别，也无法聚合用于预测未见查询上的记忆价值。

Abstract: Memory-augmented large language models must decide which memories to retain, and recent systems do so by estimating each memory's effect on task performance. However, these estimates rely entirely on retrieved memories. When a memory is never retrieved, store-level interventions produce identical outcomes, leaving its utility unidentified. This is a retrieval-level positivity violation, invisible to diagnostics that examine only memory operations. We introduce Causal Memory Policy (CMP), a causal framework that restores identification by intervening on retrieval itself, reserving a fixed number of context slots for memories sampled with known propensities. CMP estimates memory utility by self-normalized inverse propensity weighting under a balanced assignment design. We prove the causal factorization of memory utility through retrieval, the unbiasedness and exact variance of the estimator, and the optimal decision rule under irreversible operations. Empirically, identification fails for 54% of required memories on LongMemEval and 67% on LoCoMo, and the failure persists in a deployed memory system. CMP improves discrimination between required and non-required memories from 0.54 to 0.66 AUC. Finally, we show that identified memory utility alone is insufficient for retention decisions: per-query utility reaches 0.78 AUC on the query for which it is estimated, yet no aggregation available to a retention policy predicts a memory's value on unseen queries. Code is available at: this https URL .

</details>

*Kingshuk Gupta,Davide Buscaldi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于隐藏状态探针的跨度级 LLM 幻觉检测方法，并构建跨模型观察框架，发现外部观察者（即使是更小模型）定位幻觉起始的能力可匹配或超过生成模型自检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有内部状态探针大多把幻觉检测简化为逐 token 二分类，无法捕捉语义漂移的结构化、序列化边界，难以定位幻觉的起始与延续 token。

Method: 论文提出一个基于 LLM 内部隐藏状态的跨度级幻觉检测框架。输入为 LLM 的生成文本及其逐层激活，主要做法是逐层检查隐藏状态激活模式，从中识别幻觉的起始（onset）token 与延续（continuation）token，从而把幻觉检测从逐 token 二分类扩展为带序列边界的跨度级定位。在此基础上提出跨模型检测框架：由生成模型产生文本与内部表示，另一个“外部观察者”模型接收并探测该表示，输出对生成模型幻觉起始位置的判断；实验对比了观察者模型为不同规模时的表现，发现外部观察者可以匹配或超过生成模型的自检测效果。摘要未披露具体探针结构、训练目标、推理流程、数据规模或硬件资源。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Precision-Recall AUC", "value": "相对随机基线有显著提升（具体数值未在摘要中给出）", "setting": "极端类别不平衡下的跨度级幻觉起始检测实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 基于 LLM 内部隐藏状态的分层激活模式分析，实现跨度级（onset 与 continuation token）幻觉检测；跨模型检测框架：由外部观察模型探测另一个生成模型的内部表示，判断其幻觉起始位置；发现外部观察者（即使规模更小）可以匹配甚至超过生成模型的自检测能力，表明自检测并非幻觉起始定位的上限

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly serve as foundational reasoning engines, their tendency to hallucinate remains a critical vulnerability. While recent internal state probes offer a promising alternative to slow external retrieval systems, they largely reduce hallucination detection to a token-wise binary classification task, failing to capture the structured, sequential boundaries of semantic drift. Here, we introduce an internal hidden state framework for fine-grained, span-level hallucination detection. By inspecting layer-wise activation patterns, we attempt to detect the exact hallucination onset and continuation tokens in an LLM generation. Our experiments show that this approach successfully isolates hallucination onsets, achieving substantial improvements in Precision-Recall AUC over random baselines despite extreme class imbalance. Ultimately, we propose a novel cross-model detection framework in which one model observes the internal representations elicited by another model's generation. We find that an external observer can match or exceed a generator's self-detection of its own hallucination onsets, including when the observer is the smaller model, suggesting that self-detection is not the ceiling for onset localisation.

</details>


### [44] [Mingbird: A Local-First Agent Harness Enabling Small Open Models to Complete Real Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02001)
*Hao Wang,Ting Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Mingbird是一个本地优先的agent harness，运行于Windows与Ollama，通过字节级预填充预算、完成门控、签名级循环检测等十个机制补偿2-9B小模型的失败模式。在自建LRAB受控对比中达到0.886，优于goose（0.631）、opencode（0.479）和agent-mini（0.405）；在τ²-bench上达到0.856，优于0.791和0.737。证据受限于自建基准、单机和单次试验评分，留一消融仅为方向性结果。对推荐系统中的LLM agent可靠性、上下文管理与工具调用工程有参考价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型开放权重模型（2-9B）在云规模agent harness下难以完成真实任务，失败主要来自harness而非模型本身；需要一种本地优先的agent harness来逐项补偿小模型的失败形态。

Method: Mingbird是一个本地优先的agent harness，运行于Windows与Ollama之上，面向2-9B小模型。其输入是任务描述与可用工具；核心处理流程为：首先通过字节级净零预填充预算（byte-level net-zero prefill budget）对工具描述和上下文进行字节级预算控制，使工具预填充不会超出小模型的上下文窗口；随后进入工具调用与多轮交互循环，由签名级循环检测（signature-level loop detection）识别重复的工具演示/调用模式并打断循环；在模型声称完成时，finish gate会重新读取任务要求，验证是否真正满足后再接受完成，避免静默放弃或过早终止。十个机制共同覆盖小模型在云规模harness下的典型失败形态。训练目标方面，论文未涉及模型训练，而是通过harness工程化机制来补偿模型能力。推理/服务流程完全在单机本地执行，模型通过Ollama服务调用。评估上使用LRAB自建受控基准（固定机器、模型、预算与评分，确定性artifact评分）和τ²-bench第三方基准，并进行留一机制消融与同晚重复实验以检验机制贡献与噪声水平。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "LRAB overall score", "value": "0.886", "setting": "Mingbird在受控单机对比中的总体得分，固定机器、模型、预算与评分；对比goose 0.631、opencode 0.479、agent-mini 0.405；4 harness × 4 open models (2B-35B) × 18 real tasks，288 cells全部公开"}, {"metric": "LRAB overall score", "value": "0.631", "setting": "goose harness在同一LRAB受控对比中的总体得分"}, {"metric": "LRAB overall score", "value": "0.479", "setting": "opencode harness在同一LRAB受控对比中的总体得分"}, {"metric": "LRAB overall score", "value": "0.405", "setting": "agent-mini harness在同一LRAB受控对比中的总体得分"}, {"metric": "τ²-bench total score", "value": "0.856", "setting": "Mingbird在τ²-bench（278 tasks，three arms，one protocol）上的总分；对比0.791与0.737"}, {"metric": "τ²-bench total score", "value": "0.791", "setting": "对比arm在τ²-bench同一协议下的总分"}, {"metric": "τ²-bench total score", "value": "0.737", "setting": "对比arm在τ²-bench同一协议下的总分"}, {"metric": "frontier-model probe on same 18 tasks", "value": "0.997 到 0.478", "setting": "前沿模型在同一18个LRAB任务上跨不同harness的得分范围；well-formed scaffolds相互差距在0.072以内"}, {"metric": "paired improvement of executable completion guards vs text re-read alone", "value": "+0.10", "setting": "batch-matched comparison：完整机制栈 vs 仅文本重读，三次重复实验的配对结果"}, {"metric": "same-night replication mean movement", "value": "up to 0.069", "setting": "同晚重复同一arm的均值波动幅度，与单次试验delta量级相当，说明留一消融仅作方向性参考"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 本地优先的agent harness（Windows + Ollama），针对小模型失败模式设计十个机制；字节级净零预填充预算（byte-level net-zero prefill budget），控制工具预填充不溢出上下文；完成门控（finish gate）：在接受完成前重新读取任务，避免静默放弃或过早终止；签名级循环检测（signature-level loop detection），识别并打断工具演示循环；受控单机对比基准LRAB：固定机器、模型、预算与评分，4 harness × 4 open models（2B-35B）× 18真实任务，确定性artifact评分，公开全部288个cell；留一机制消融与同晚重复实验，报告方向性结果并公开限制（自建基准、单机、单次试验评分）

Abstract: Small open-weight models (2-9B) run on ordinary laptops, but under cloud-scale agent harnesses they rarely complete real tasks: tool prefill overflows the context, self-correction diverges, tool demonstrations loop, and tasks are silently abandoned. We present evidence, from a controlled single-machine comparison and one third-party benchmark, that a substantial share of these failures is attributable to the harness rather than the model. We introduce Mingbird, a local-first agent harness for Windows and Ollama whose ten mechanisms compensate point-by-point for small-model failure forms, three of them representative: a byte-level net-zero prefill budget, a finish gate that re-reads the task before accepting completion, and signature-level loop detection. On LRAB, a controlled comparison holding machine, models, budgets, and scoring fixed (4 harnesses $\times$ 4 open models (2B-35B) $\times$ 18 real tasks, deterministic artifact scoring), Mingbird reaches 0.886 overall against 0.631 (goose), 0.479 (opencode), and 0.405 (agent-mini), with all 288 cells published; on $\tau^2$-bench (278 tasks, three arms, one protocol) it totals 0.856 against 0.791 and 0.737; and a frontier-model probe on the same 18 tasks spans 0.997 to 0.478 across harnesses, with well-formed scaffolds staying within 0.072 of each other. A leave-one-mechanism-out ablation is reported as directional only: same-night replications of the same arm move its mean by up to 0.069, the size of every nominal single-trial delta, and the one batch-matched comparison (full mechanism stack versus text re-read alone) gives the executable completion guards a paired +0.10 across three replications. The evidence carries stated limits: a self-built benchmark, a single machine, and single-trial scoring.

</details>


### [45] [Counting Moves, Weighing Voices: Bayesian Dialectical Argumentation for Calibrated Multi-LLM Councils under Persistent Adversaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02005)
*Ionel Eduard Stan,Paolo Napoletano*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BDA将多LLM委员会的 deliberation 动作建模为带可靠性的标注者模型，实现校准置信度并抵御持续对抗代理，无需额外LLM调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多LLM委员会聚合方法的置信度估计反映的是决定性而非正确性，且在部分代理持续不可靠或对抗时无法识别并折损其影响。

Method: BDA将多LLM委员会 deliberation 过程中的类型化动作（例如谁提议、谁挑战、谁让步）作为观测数据，输入到一个经典标注者模型中。该模型为每个代理维护一个可靠性参数，通过 deliberative trace 推断每个代理的可靠性。在聚合阶段，依据推断出的代理可靠性对证据进行加权，从而计算候选答案的校准后验概率。持续不可靠的代理会被赋予低可靠性甚至负权重（即其观点可被反转），而不仅仅是被多数投票忽略。整个流程不需要额外的LLM调用，仅利用已有的 deliberation 轨迹进行概率推断。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将委员会的类型化动作（提议、挑战、让步）视为对经典标注者模型的观测，引入每个代理的可靠性参数。；通过推断代理可靠性来加权证据，实现校准的后验概率估计，并能反转持续不可靠代理的影响而不仅仅是投票否决。；在零额外LLM调用成本下，在二元和多分类基准上达到最佳校准，并提升对持续对抗联盟的鲁棒性。

Abstract: A multi-LLM \emph{council} lets several large language models (LLMs) deliberate on a question and return an answer together with a confidence estimate. As these systems become increasingly used for reasoning, that confidence should represent a calibrated \emph{probability of being correct}, and the decision should remain robust when some agents are persistently unreliable. Existing \emph{council aggregation} methods fail on both fronts: their confidence estimates measure decisiveness rather than correctness, and they cannot identify or discount persistently unreliable agents. We introduce Bayesian Dialectical Argumentation (BDA), which treats the council's \emph{typed} moves---who proposed, challenged, or conceded which answer---as observations of a classical annotator model with \emph{per-agent} reliabilities. This formulation recasts multi-agent deliberation as a reliability estimation problem, using the deliberation trace to infer agent reliability under persistent adversarial behavior. By weighting evidence according to inferred agent reliability, BDA yields calibrated posterior probabilities over candidate answers while allowing persistently unreliable agents to be inverted rather than merely outvoted. Across binary and multi-class benchmarks, BDA achieves the best calibration among zero-cost council aggregation methods, requiring no additional LLM calls, and improves robustness under persistent adversarial coalitions while remaining competitive in clean settings.

</details>


### [46] [RealCompanion: Benchmarking Human Understanding from Reasoning over Longitudinal Real-World Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01780)
*Arman Behnam,Sunglyoung Kim,Liangwei Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RealCompanion 发布基于 10 段真实长期人机对话（27,218 条消息、最长 120 天）的 benchmark，揭示真实场景中记忆需求稀少且 recency window 严重误导，现有 detector 无法判断何时需要记忆，并对比三种 agent 系统在 persona 重建上同 F1 但成本差 31 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在真实、长期、隐私受限的对话记录上，评测 AI companion 是否真正理解人：即能否召回相关历史、判断何时需要记忆、并重建用户 persona，而不是依赖合成对话和预设重要性。

Method: 论文提出 RealCompanion benchmark，数据来自 10 段真实的人与 AI companion 的长期对话关系，共 27,218 条消息，时间跨度最长 120 天。benchmark 发布内容为对话本身以及从对话中派生的四个文件：profile、persona、chat ground truth 和 question set；其中每个条目都引用其依据的具体消息。每个 chat 标签还附带生成该标签的 reasoning trace，并被逐阶段对照真实对话进行核验。评测围绕三类能力展开：记忆检索（给定当前消息，判断过去哪些消息相关）、记忆需求检测（判断当前消息是否需要记忆，而不只是检索）、以及 persona 重建。论文还用三种 agent 系统在 persona 重建任务上进行对比，报告相同 F1 下成本相差 31 倍。摘要未披露模型规模、训练数据、硬件、延迟、吞吐或具体 serving 架构，因此这些细节无法从给定信息中补全。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "recency window 覆盖 required message 的 probes 比例", "value": "95.9%", "setting": "RealCompanion 真实对话 probes 池化统计（论文摘要报告）"}, {"metric": "recency window 覆盖需要记忆的 probes 比例", "value": "2.2%", "setting": "仅统计真正需要记忆的 probes；论文摘要报告"}, {"metric": "提供 recorded evidence 带来的收益中来自无需记忆消息的比例", "value": "96%", "setting": "自然发生率下；论文摘要报告"}, {"metric": "将消息标注为 memories 后其使用率提升", "value": "10-14 个百分点", "setting": "同一批消息改变标注方式；论文摘要报告"}, {"metric": "三种 agent 系统 persona 重建 F1 相同但成本差异", "value": "31 倍成本差异", "setting": "三种 agent 系统在同一 persona 重建任务上；论文摘要未披露具体 F1 数值与成本单位"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 发布 RealCompanion benchmark：10 段真实人-AI companion 关系，27,218 条消息、最长 120 天，并给出由对话派生的 profile、persona、chat ground truth 与 question set 四个文件，每条标注引用其依据的消息。；每个 chat 标签附带产生该标签的 reasoning trace，并逐阶段对照真实对话进行核验。；发现并量化了记忆需求在真实消息中很少见且距离远：recency window 能覆盖 95.9% probes 所需消息，但仅覆盖 2.2% 真正需要记忆的 probes；在自然发生率下，提供记录证据带来的收益中 96% 来自本不需要记忆的消息。；证明现有 detector 无法在真实消息上判断何时需要记忆，而基于同一历史由作者编写的问题会泄漏记忆需求线索；同时发现将相同消息标注为 memories 可将其使用率提高 10-14 个百分点。；对比三种 agent 系统在 persona 重建任务上的 F1 相同但成本相差 31 倍。

Abstract: A companion that talks with a person for months should come to understand them. It should remember what they said, infer who they are, and know when the past bears on the message in front of it. Testing this requires a real person's record, and such records are private, so benchmarks generate the person and the questions and settle in advance what matters. We release \bench, ten real relationships with an AI companion: 27,218 messages over up to 120 days, released as the conversation and four files derived from it, a profile, a persona, a chat ground truth and a question set, each citing the messages it rests on. Every chat label carries the reasoning trace that produced it, checked stage by stage against the conversation. Three findings follow. First, the past is rarely needed and far away. Pooled measures mislead: a recency window finds the required message for 95.9\% of probes and 2.2\% of those that need memory, and at the natural rate 96\% of the gain from supplying recorded evidence comes from messages that need none. Second, no detector we tried can tell when memory is needed on real messages, authored questions over the same histories leak the cue, and labeling the same messages as memories raises their use by ten to fourteen points. Third, three agent systems reconstruct the persona with the same F1 at a 31-fold difference in cost.

</details>


### [47] [TopK-Guided: Adaptive, Budget-Aware Activation Sparsity for Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01763)
*Mukund Agarwalla,Chih-Jen Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TopK-Guided是一种免训练的LLM激活稀疏方法，结合token级有界稀疏自适应与block级敏感度感知预算分配，在Llama-2/3上一致优于TEAL和WINA且保持相近投影计算稀疏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免训练激活稀疏方法在token级稀疏控制与block级预算分配上存在权衡：阈值法（如TEAL）能自适应token稀疏但无法严格控制实际稀疏度，固定TopK法（如WINA）能控制稀疏度但所有token使用相同预算；同时所有transformer block使用统一预算，忽略了block敏感度差异。

Method: TopK-Guided是一种免训练的LLM推理激活稀疏方法。输入为LLM前向传播中的激活张量，方法在token级别进行有界稀疏自适应：以TopK机制确定每个token保留的激活数量，实现稀疏度在受控范围内的自适应，从而兼顾TEAL的token自适应性与WINA的稀疏度可控性。同时，方法在block级别进行敏感度感知预算分配：根据各transformer block对稀疏化的敏感程度，为不同block分配不同的稀疏预算，替代所有block统一预算的做法。前向传播中，被置零的激活对应的投影计算被跳过，从而加速推理；整体流程无需重新训练模型。优化目标是在给定稀疏度预算下最小化精度损失，通过token级与block级两个互补组件提升困惑度和下游任务准确率。推理时保持与WINA基本相同的稀疏相关投影计算量，未披露具体硬件加速比或延迟数据。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "困惑度与下游准确率", "value": "在Llama-2与Llama-3上一致优于TEAL和WINA，高稀疏度下增益最大；消融显示两个组件互补", "setting": "Llama-2和Llama-3模型上的激活稀疏推理评估，投影计算稀疏度与WINA基本一致"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 结合token级有界稀疏自适应与block级敏感度感知预算分配，形成TopK-Guided免训练激活稀疏框架；在token级用TopK引导实现有界稀疏度自适应，克服TEAL无界稀疏波动与WINA固定预算的局限；根据各transformer block的敏感度动态分配稀疏预算，避免block间统一预算导致的精度损失

Abstract: Activation sparsity speeds up large language model (LLM) inference by setting unimportant activations to zero so that the corresponding computations can be skipped. Existing training-free methods, however, make different trade-offs: threshold-based methods such as TEAL adapt the sparsity level to each token but do not tightly control the realised sparsity, while TopK-based methods such as WINA enforce a fixed sparsity level but use the same sparsity budget for every token. Both also apply the same budget across transformer blocks, despite large differences in block sensitivity. We introduce TopK-Guided, a training-free method that addresses both limitations by combining bounded token-level sparsity adaptation with sensitivity-aware block-level budget allocation. Across Llama-2 and Llama-3 models, TopK-Guided consistently improves perplexity and downstream accuracy over TEAL and WINA while preserving essentially the same sparsitydependent projection compute as WINA, with the largest gains at high sparsity. Ablations show that both components provide complementary improvements.

</details>


### [48] [Sharpening Tax in Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01509)
*Changdae Oh,Qi Zeng,Qi Qi,Andrey Zhmoginov,Deren Lei,Yun He,Hoang Phan,Hangoo Kang,Azalia Mirhoseini,Sharon Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发现RL后训练会'锐化'LLM行为，提升单次准确率但损害解覆盖度和测试时扩展性，并提出Sharpening Tax指标量化该损失。进一步提出PTGS采样方法，根据prompt难度自适应调整采样温度，在智能体任务中减轻锐化税并同时改善重复采样和单次准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL后训练是否仅仅锐化基座模型已有行为，从而在提升单次准确率的同时降低解覆盖度（pass@K）和测试时扩展性；在智能体任务中这种权衡是否依然存在。

Method: 论文研究LLM后训练对模型行为分布的影响。输入为智能体任务提示，模型通过多轮交互使用工具。核心分析：对比预训练基座模型与后训练模型在pass@1和pass@K上的表现，发现后训练将任务推向始终解决或从未解决的极端，提升采样效率和一致性但降低解覆盖度。提出Sharpening Tax指标，通过比较后训练前后测试时扩展性损失量化该效应。进一步提出PTGS采样器：在RL训练中，对每个prompt估计其难度，并据此调整采样温度（后验温度），使得简单任务温度降低、困难任务温度升高，从而在重复采样时覆盖更多解同时保持单次准确率。PTGS作为即插即用模块应用于RL训练，在两种智能体环境中评估，表现为比固定温度基线更小的锐化税。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 发现预训练LLM配合轻量推理框架在智能体任务中虽pass@1较低但pass@K常优于后训练模型，挑战了'后训练仅锐化'的假设。；提出Sharpening Tax诊断指标，量化后训练导致的测试时扩展性损失，并证明该损失普遍存在、可从少量rollouts估计且与其他指标相关。；提出posterior-tempered group sampling (PTGS)，一种即插即用的贝叶斯采样器，根据估计的prompt难度自适应调整采样温度，在RL训练中减轻锐化税并同时提升重复采样和单次准确率。

Abstract: An emerging hypothesis about reinforcement learning (RL) post-training of large language models (LLMs) is that it merely sharpens existing behaviors of a base model, improving single-shot accuracy at the cost of solution coverage. Although this trade-off has been observed in math and coding tasks, it need not extend to agentic tasks, where multi-turn tool use and interaction may require capabilities newly acquired during post-training. Our surprising finding is that pre-trained LLMs, equipped with a light inference harness, can serve as capable agents. Despite far lower accuracy (pass@1), they often surpass their post-trained counterparts in solution coverage (pass@K) given a sufficient test-time budget. We further analyze the underlying mechanism and show that post-training pushes tasks toward two extremes, always solved or never solved, and thereby improves sampling efficiency and consistency at the cost of solution coverage. To measure this cost, we propose Sharpening Tax, a diagnostic metric that quantifies the loss in test-time scalability after post-training. Across 14 base/post-trained model pairs from four families and three agentic benchmarks (42 cases in total), the tax is prevalent in most settings, can be estimated from a few rollouts, and correlates well with other metrics. Finally, we present posterior-tempered group sampling (PTGS), a simple plug-and-play Bayesian sampler that adapts the sampling temperature per prompt to its estimated difficulty. Applied during RL training in two agentic environments, PTGS pays a smaller tax than the fixed-temperature baseline, solving more tasks under repeated sampling while also improving single-shot accuracy.

</details>


### [49] [DRelay: Global Draft Context for Prefix-Aware Parallel Speculative Decoding Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01439)
*Zhuoyu Wang,Junnan Huang,Xinyu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DRelay 提出一种前缀感知的并行投机解码修复方法，通过全局读取器和因果选择器在验证前修复早期选择错误，扩展接受前缀。在 H800 GPU 的八个基准上，相对 DFlash、Domino、DSpark 在 SGLang 服务下提升端到端加速比 14.7%-16.8%、8.7%-9.3% 和 8.1%-9.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行投机解码中，即使正确 token 存在于候选池，早期位置的选择错误会阻止后续预测被接受，从而限制接受前缀长度和整体加速比。

Method: DRelay 在并行投机解码的草稿阶段之后、目标模型验证之前，对候选 token 的选择进行选择性修复。输入是草稿块（draft block）中每个位置的候选池以及原生选择路径。全局读取器（global reader）跨所有位置提取每个候选的预测信息，捕捉候选之间的相关性。因果选择器（causal selector）结合全局读取器提取的候选级信息和已选前缀中的 token，判断当前位置的原生选择是否与全局证据及已选前缀一致；若不一致，则用更符合全局证据的候选替换。该修复过程旨在纠正早期选择错误，从而扩展连续接受前缀。训练时，联合优化草稿骨干和选择器，损失包含候选支持学习和修复目标，并根据每个块位置对连续接受前缀的潜在贡献对修复损失加权。推理时，DRelay 集成到 SGLang 服务框架中，在目标模型验证前完成修复，提升平均接受长度和端到端解码速度。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均接受长度和端到端解码性能", "value": "一致提升", "setting": "在 H800 GPU 上，八个多样化基准测试，与 DFlash、Domino 和 DSpark 对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 DRelay，在目标模型验证前对候选选择进行前缀感知的选择性修复，利用整个草稿块的全局信息和已选前缀。；设计全局读取器（global reader）跨位置提取候选相关性的预测信息，以及因果选择器（causal selector）结合候选级信息与已选前缀判断当前位置的本选是否与全局证据和前缀一致，决定保留或替换 token。；联合训练草稿骨干和选择器，结合候选支持学习（candidate-support learning）与修复目标，并按每个块位置对连续接受前缀的潜在贡献加权修复损失。

Abstract: Parallel drafting reduces the drafting overhead of speculative decoding for large language models (LLMs), but its gains remain limited by the accepted prefix length. Even when the correct token is present in the candidate pool, a single early selection error prevents subsequent predictions from being used. We propose DRelay, which uses global information from the entire draft block to perform prefix-aware selective repair of candidate selections before target-model verification. DRelay bases its decisions on candidate correlations and the selected path: a global reader extracts predictive information across positions for each candidate. While a causal selector combines candidate-level information extracted by the global read with the tokens selected at preceding positions to determine whether the native choice at the current position is consistent with the global evidence and the selected prefix. It then decides whether to retain or replace the token, thereby repairing early errors and extending the accepted prefix. We further jointly train the draft backbone and the selector, combining candidate-support learning with a repair objective, while weighting the repair loss according to each block position's potential contribution to the consecutive accepted prefix. Across eight diverse benchmarks on an H800 GPU, DRelay consistently improves both average acceptance length and end-to-end decoding performance over DFlash, Domino, and DSpark. Under SGLang serving, DRelay improves average end-to-end speedup over DFlash, Domino, and DSpark by 14.7%-16.8%, 8.7%-9.3%, and 8.1%-9.3%, respectively.

</details>

*Shiu-Hong Kao,Yubo Zhao,Zhenyu Tian,Pengzhan Sun,Yicong Li,Angela Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发现基于概率奖励的无验证器RL存在长度相关的后验集中现象（PCP），导致奖励不可区分、优化不稳定；提出RLCPR框架，通过不确定性感知采样和集中感知正则化提升训练稳定性与token效率，在六个基准上超越SOTA最高4.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于概率奖励的无验证器强化学习中，长推理轨迹导致参考答案条件概率崩塌到低方差区间（后验集中现象PCP），奖励几乎不可区分，使GRPO类策略优化不稳定且低效。

Method: 论文提出RLCPR，一个无验证器强化学习框架，用于在无法访问外部验证器的一般推理任务上训练LLM。它识别出后验集中现象：随着推理轨迹变长，参考答案在给定轨迹下的条件概率坍缩到低方差区间，导致概率奖励几乎不可区分，在GRPO设置下使策略优化不稳定且低效。RLCPR包含两个组件：不确定性感知数据采样，在生成之前减少容易产生浓度问题的rollout；以及浓度感知正则化，在后验奖励坍缩时惩罚不必要的长轨迹。优化目标基于概率奖励并整合这两种机制，以提高优化稳定性和token效率。推理或服务流程未详细披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "基准性能（相对SOTA无验证器RL基线）", "value": "在7个基准中的6个上最高提升4.0%", "setting": "通用域与数学推理任务，基于GRPO类设置"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 发现并形式化描述概率奖励的长度依赖失败模式——后验集中现象（PCP）；提出RLCPR框架：不确定性感知数据采样（生成前减少易集中rollout）与集中感知正则化（当后验奖励崩塌时惩罚过长轨迹）

Abstract: Verifier-free reinforcement learning with probability-based rewards offers a promising way to train LLMs on general reasoning tasks where external verifiers are unavailable. Yet the reliability of these rewards, especially in long-horizon reasoning, remains underexplored. This work identifies a length-dependent failure mode of probability rewards, which we call the Posterior Concentration Phenomenon (PCP). We show that the probability of a reference answer conditioned on a reasoning trace often collapses to a low-variance interval as the trace becomes lengthy. This phenomenon results in nearly indistinguishable rewards, which, under GRPO-based settings, makes probability-based policy optimization unstable and inefficient. Motivated by this, we propose Reinforcement Learning with Concentration-aware Posterior Rewards (RLCPR), a verifier-free RL framework to explicitly account for PCP for better optimization stability and token efficiency. It has two components: uncertainty-aware data sampling, which reduces concentration-prone rollouts before generation, and concentration-aware regularization, which penalizes unnecessarily long traces when posterior rewards collapse. Extensive experiments show that, alongside higher token efficiency, RLCPR outperforms the state-of-the-art verifier-free RL baseline by up to 4.0% on six of seven benchmarks, including general-domain and mathematical reasoning challenges.

</details>


### [51] [Beyond Memory: Harnessing Long-Horizon Agents with Explicit Belief States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01415)
*Yu Luo,Jiamin Jiang,Yimin Zuo,Xidao Wen,Rongchen Gao,Yongqian Sun,Shenglin Zhang,Guiyang Liu,Cheng Zhang,Fang Situ,Qi Zhou,Dan Pei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PoS 是面向长时程 LLM Agent 的推理时显式信念状态框架，用“世界状态估计+未解决任务需求”替代单纯历史记忆，并通过一致性验证、进度监控与针对性恢复来检测和摆脱 Belief Trapping。在四个执行与诊断基准、三种 LLM 骨干上取得最优整体表现，消融验证了各模块作用，对 Agent 上下文与可靠性工程有参考价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent 将交互历史组织为记忆，无法保证对当前世界状态形成一致理解；长时程任务中会出现持续行动但无实质进展的 Belief Trapping 问题。

Method: PoS 是一个推理时框架，不改变 LLM 参数。它把 Agent 的决策上下文从单纯的交互历史记忆替换为显式信念状态：每个信念由两部分组成，一是对当前世界状态的估计，二是尚未解决的任务需求，从而显式表达 Agent 还需要了解什么、完成什么。在推理过程中，PoS 持续维护该信念：首先进行一致性验证，确保信念内部和与环境的观测保持一致；同时监控任务进度，用于检测 Belief Trapping，即 Agent 看似继续行动但并未向目标推进的状态。一旦检测到 trapping，框架会根据 trapping 的具体模式以及未解决任务需求的类型选择相应的恢复策略，使 Agent 重新回到有效推进任务的轨道。实验在覆盖执行与诊断的四个基准上，配合三种 LLM 骨干模型进行评测；消融实验分别去掉一致性验证和恢复机制以衡量其贡献，上下文扩展实验则检验信念维护在上下文增长时的稳定性。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出推理时框架 PoS，构造并持续维护显式信念状态作为 Agent 的决策上下文，每个信念结合当前世界状态估计与未解决任务需求。；对信念进行一致性验证，并监控任务进度以检测 Belief Trapping。；根据 trapping 模式与未解决任务需求类型定制恢复策略。；通过消融实验证明一致性验证与恢复机制的重要性，通过上下文扩展实验展示对上下文增长的韧性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can now undertake increasingly complex tasks, but the way they organize interaction history into memory does not ensure a coherent understanding of the current world. We introduce PoS, an inference-time framework that constructs and continually maintains explicit belief states as the agent's decision context. Each belief combines an estimate of the current world state with unresolved task requirements, making explicit what the agent still needs to learn and accomplish. To keep this belief reliable and actionable, PoS validates its consistency and monitors task progress to detect Belief Trapping, where the agent continues to act without making meaningful progress toward the goal. Recovery is then tailored to both the trapping pattern and the type of unresolved task requirement. Experiments on four benchmarks spanning execution and diagnosis show that PoS achieves the highest overall performance on every benchmark with all three LLM backbones. Ablations demonstrate the importance of consistency validation and recovery, while context-scaling experiments show resilience to context growth. Together, these results support belief construction and continual maintenance as a foundation for long-horizon context management beyond history retention and compression.

</details>

Abstract: Modern retrieval systems must both be automated and interactive, allowing users to search and refine results in real time. We present AiSearch, a flexible multimodal retrieval framework that leverages the zero shot capabilities of Vision Language Models (VLMs) for natural language search over images and videos. AiSearch supports interactive search refinement through user feedback to tailor results to the user's intent, and allows visual benchmarking across multiple VLMs, enabling users to select the most suitable model for their task.
