*Scott Geng,Yufei Zhang,Joseph Lee,Jerry Li,Marjan Ghazvininejad,Pang Wei Koh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Gacha Decoding 通过将多样性建模为指令遵循问题并引入外部RNG，显著提升LLM生成多样性，且样本效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成多样性方法依赖token熵，导致多样性与模型能力之间存在张力，高质量模式覆盖不足。

Method: Gacha Decoding 是一种推理时方法。核心流程：在生成过程中，利用大语言模型的指令遵循能力，结合外部RNG工具提供的随机性，引导模型识别并生成响应空间中不同的模式。具体地，将多样性视为指令遵循问题，通过外部随机源决定生成方向，模型根据指令生成不同模式的高质量输出。训练目标不涉及模型参数更新，完全在推理阶段实现。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Vendi score", "value": "up to 2.4x", "setting": "equal quality, across open-ended domains"}, {"metric": "number of high-quality modes reached", "value": "over an order of magnitude fewer samples (11.0x)", "setting": "same number of high-quality modes, across domains"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将多样性建模为指令遵循问题，而非依赖token熵。；利用外部RNG工具注入随机性，结合模型指令遵循能力来识别并实现响应空间中的不同模式（“planning with dice”）。；推理时方法，随底层模型指令遵循能力提升而持续改善多样性。

Abstract: We introduce Gacha Decoding, an inference-time method for eliciting diverse language model generations that scales with model capability. Across open-ended domains (in-the-wild chat, creative writing, planning for image generation, and protein design), Gacha Decoding significantly outperforms existing generation diversity approaches at equal quality (up to 2.4x Vendi over the next-best prior approach), reaching the same number of high-quality modes with over an order of magnitude fewer samples (11.0x) and discovering novel modes that no other approach surfaces. Our key insight is to treat diversity as an instruction-following problem: rather than relying on the LM's token entropy, we combine its instruction-following capability with randomness from an external RNG tool to scalably identify and realize distinct modes of the response space. This approach of "planning with dice" enables Gacha to invert the long-observed tension between diversity and model capability. As the underlying LM becomes a better instruction follower, diversity under Gacha Decoding consistently improves--even as its token entropy and diversity under prior approaches decline. Together, our results highlight that instruction following, rather than token entropy alone, can drive generation diversity.

</details>


### [54] [HHR: Hierarchical Hash Retrieval for Efficient LLM Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01230)
*Lianjun Liu,Tiantian Zheng,You Huang,Weiqi Yan,Mingte Qiu,Huazhong Liu,Xiaofeng Zhu,Yunshan Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HHR提出分层哈希检索框架，通过GKR和LHP解决汉明距离与注意力相关性不匹配问题，在LongBench上平均分提升1.10，128K上下文下解码加速3.30倍、端到端加速2.83倍，代码开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 哈希检索中汉明距离与注意力相关性不匹配，导致低logit键的假阳性检索和高logit键的假阴性遗漏，降低长上下文推理中稀疏注意力检索的精度。

Method: HHR是一个粗到细的框架，用于长上下文LLM推理中的哈希检索稀疏注意力。输入为查询（Q）和键（K），首先通过GKR进行粗粒度路由：GKR为每个注意力头学习正交变换，重新分布特征幅度，从而导出页级的logit上界，用于剪枝低logit的关键页，保留可能重要的候选键。然后，LHP进行细粒度检索：为每个头学习一个投影空间，将键和查询投影到该空间并二值化为哈希码，使得汉明距离与真实Query-Key相关性排序对齐，从而准确选出高logit键。最终在选出的稀疏键子集上执行注意力计算。训练目标使GKR的边界尽可能紧且不遗漏高logit键，同时使LHP的哈希距离排序与注意力logit排序一致。推理时，先通过GKR快速筛选页级候选，再通过LHP在候选内进行细粒度键选择，仅对选中的键计算注意力，从而降低计算量并加速解码。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "LongBench平均分", "value": "提升1.10分", "setting": "在多个LLM和基准上与现有方法对比"}, {"metric": "解码加速", "value": "最高3.30倍", "setting": "上下文长度128K，Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct"}, {"metric": "端到端加速", "value": "2.83倍", "setting": "上下文长度128K，Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 分层哈希检索（HHR）：粗到细框架，逐步提高检索精度；Geometry-Aware Key Routing (GKR)：学习逐头正交变换重新分配特征幅度，得到更具区分度的页级logit边界以剪枝低logit键；Learned Hash Projection (LHP)：学习逐头投影空间，使汉明距离与真实Query-Key相关性排序对齐，实现细粒度检索；结合GKR和LHP抑制假阳性并恢复假阴性，提升哈希稀疏注意力的保真度

Abstract: Efficient long-context inference is essential for large language models (LLMs), yet it poses a severe computational bottleneck. Hash-based retrieval offers an efficient alternative by encoding queries and keys into binary codes and using Hamming distance for key selection. However, this leads to a critical mismatch between Hamming distance and attention relevance. Query-Key logits depend jointly on directional similarity and feature magnitudes, whereas hash binarization discards magnitude information, causing both false-positive retrieval of low-logit keys and false-negative omission of high-logit keys. To address these failures, we propose Hierarchical Hash Retrieval (HHR), a coarse-to-fine framework that progressively improves retrieval accuracy through Geometry-Aware Key Routing (GKR) and Learned Hash Projection (LHP). GKR learns a head-wise orthogonal transformation to redistribute feature magnitudes and derive more discriminative page-level logit bounds, enabling effective pruning of low-logit keys while preserving important candidates. LHP then learns a head-wise projection space that aligns Hamming distance with the true Query-Key relevance ranking for fine-grained retrieval. By combining GKR and LHP, HHR suppresses false positives and recovers false negatives, substantially improving the fidelity of hash-based sparse attention. Extensive experiments across diverse LLMs and benchmarks demonstrate that HHR achieves superior performance over existing methods. For example, on LongBench, HHR improves the average score by 1.10 points and, at a context length of 128K, achieves up to a 3.30x decoding speedup and a 2.83x end-to-end speedup for Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct. The code is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [55] [DeFA: Dependency-Guided Failure Attribution for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01256)
*Bo Deng,Xinlei Zheng,Yi Wei,Kang Zhou,Chongyang Tao,Renzhao Liang,Xuanren Chen,Lifan Guo,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DeFA 提出依赖引导的 LLM Agent 失败归因框架：通过事件依赖图与失败传播图定位决定性错误、责任 Agent 与错误类别，并用分段摘要支持长轨迹。在 Who and When 文本基准上取得最高责任 Agent 与精确步骤准确率，并验证多模态适用性；其诊断反馈还能提升 Trace2Skill 下游任务准确率 6-15 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent 执行中错误与其可见后果相隔多步，如何同时理解步骤内容与步骤依赖，从而定位决定性错误、责任 Agent 与错误类别。

Method: DeFA 是一个依赖引导的 Agent 失败归因框架。输入为 LLM Agent 的执行轨迹。首先，框架结合协议关系与语义依赖，构建覆盖整条轨迹的事件依赖图。其次，识别可能违反任务要求的事件，并沿依赖图追踪这些事件的来源与后续影响，构造失败传播图。然后，利用步骤证据以及各步骤在失败传播中的角色，定位决定性错误、责任 Agent 和错误类别。为支持长轨迹，DeFA 将执行过程分段，诊断当前段时使用该段的详细内容，并拼接其他段的摘要，使局部诊断获得全局执行上下文。摘要中未披露具体训练目标、优化方式、推理硬件与延迟。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "责任 Agent 准确率、精确步骤准确率", "value": "在所有评估 backbone 上取得最高（具体数值未在摘要披露）", "setting": "Who and When 与 Who and When Pro 文本子集"}, {"metric": "失败模式准确率", "value": "在 taxonomy-aligned 方法中最高（具体数值未在摘要披露）", "setting": "Who and When Pro"}, {"metric": "下游任务准确率", "value": "相比原生 pipeline 提升 6-15 个百分点", "setting": "使用 DeFA 诊断反馈进行 Trace2Skill 技能演化"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 构建融合协议关系与语义依赖的事件依赖图（event dependency graph），覆盖整条轨迹。；识别可能违反任务要求的事件，并追踪其来源与后续影响，构建失败传播图（failure propagation graph）。；结合步骤证据与步骤在失败传播中的角色，联合定位决定性错误、责任 Agent 和错误类别。；面向长轨迹的分段处理：用当前段详细内容加其他段摘要，使局部诊断获得全局上下文。；将诊断反馈用于 Trace2Skill 中的技能演化，提升下游任务准确率。

Abstract: Errors in LLM agent executions and their visible consequences can be separated by many steps, making decisive-error localization a matter of understanding both step content and step dependencies. We introduce DeFA, a dependency-guided framework for agent failure attribution. DeFA first combines protocol relations and semantic dependencies into an event dependency graph spanning the trajectory. It then identifies events that may violate task requirements and traces their sources and subsequent effects to construct a failure propagation graph. Finally, DeFA uses step evidence and the steps' roles in failure propagation to identify the decisive error, responsible agent, and error category. To support long trajectories, DeFA partitions executions into segments and combines the current segment's detailed content with summaries of the other segments, giving local diagnosis access to global execution context. Across Who and When and the Who and When Pro text subset, DeFA achieves the highest responsible-agent and exact step accuracy with all evaluated backbones, and the highest failure-mode accuracy among taxonomy-aligned methods on Pro. Further experiments on image and video trajectories demonstrate its applicability to multimodal failure attribution. Ablations support the contributions of segmentation, the event dependency graph, and the failure propagation graph. Using DeFA's diagnostic feedback for skill evolution in Trace2Skill improves downstream task accuracy by 6-15 percentage points over the native pipeline, showing that the diagnoses can also support agent improvement on subsequent tasks.

</details>


### [56] [Dependency-Aware Reward Shaping for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01207)
*Ziyi Chen,Yan Zhang,Jianhui Wei,Daoan Zhang,Zuozhu Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DARS提出依赖感知奖励塑形，通过谓词依赖图对智能体强化学习中的步骤进行细粒度信用分配，在ALFWorld、WebShop、Search-R1、AIME等任务上优于GiGPO、OmniOPD等基线，并支持蒸馏标注器以降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于终端成功/失败奖励的强化学习中，失败的轨迹中每一步的总未来奖励均为零，导致无法区分哪些步骤对任务进展有贡献、哪些步骤建立在未被修正的错误之上，从而缺乏有效的步骤级信用分配。

Method: DARS将任务进展表示为谓词，谓词之间由前提依赖关系连接成图。标注器对每个步骤标记其验证、使无效或修复的谓词。对于已验证的谓词，根据其与最近被破坏前提的图距离进行折扣，距离越远信用越低；与任何破坏前提无关的独立谓词则不受影响。当某步骤修复了错误时，修复操作会基于任何残留错误更新谓词权重；被无效化的谓词必须重新验证才能重新获得信用。最后通过一个固定势函数将上述标注转换为带符号的每步奖励。该奖励和标注接口具有通用性，可与GiGPO、ARPO/AEPO等多种推理和智能体训练方法集成，无需改变其rollout策略或优化器。在推理阶段，可用蒸馏的8B标注器替代API标注器，在ALFWorld上达到与API标注器相当的效果，避免了对外部前沿评判模型的依赖。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "ALFWorld成功率提升", "value": "最高提升10个百分点", "setting": "与相同预算和训练框架下的GiGPO对比"}, {"metric": "WebShop任务分数", "value": "提升", "setting": "与基线对比"}, {"metric": "Search-R1 QA准确率", "value": "提升", "setting": "与基线对比"}, {"metric": "AIME24/25准确率", "value": "与AEPO基于熵的训练互补", "setting": "使用Python解释器"}, {"metric": "无工具推理任务表现", "value": "超过OmniOPD", "setting": "1.7B和4B模型，受控对比实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将任务进展表示为带前提依赖关系的谓词图，通过依赖图距离对已验证谓词进行折扣，独立谓词不受影响；引入标注器标记每步验证、无效或修复的谓词，修复操作根据残留错误更新权重，无效谓词需重新验证才能恢复信用；使用固定势函数将标注转换为带符号的每步奖励，形成通用的奖励与标注接口，可无缝集成GiGPO、ARPO/AEPO等方法而不改变其rollout策略或优化器；验证蒸馏的8B标注器在ALFWorld上可匹配API标注器，使DARS无需前沿评判模型即可高效运行

Abstract: When training large language models with reinforcement learning, terminal rewards provide little guidance about which steps matter. Common methods for assigning step credit overlook that work built on uncorrected mistakes is wasted while independent work remains valid. With only a final success/failure reward, every step in a failed episode has zero total future reward, even when it made progress. We propose Dependency-Aware Reward Shaping (DARS), which represents task progress as predicates linked by prerequisite relations and assigns step-level credit over the dependency graph. An annotator marks which predicates each step verifies, invalidates, or repairs. Verified predicates are discounted according to graph distance from the nearest broken prerequisite, while independent predicates are unaffected. Repairs update these weights based on any errors that remain; invalidated predicates need re-verification to regain credit. A fixed potential converts these annotations into signed per-step rewards. A common reward and annotation interface allows DARS to integrate with a range of reasoning and agentic training methods, such as GiGPO and ARPO/AEPO, without changing their rollout strategies or optimizers. Across five task families and models from 1.5B to 8B, DARS improves success by up to 10 points over GiGPO trained with the same budget and harness (ALFWorld), raises the WebShop task score and Search-R1 QA accuracy, complements AEPO's entropy-based training on AIME24/25 with a Python interpreter, and exceeds OmniOPD in controlled tool-free reasoning comparisons at 1.7B and 4B. Ablations show that step-level credit, dependency attenuation, and graph topology each contribute. On ALFWorld, a distilled 8B annotator matches the API annotator, enabling DARS to run efficiently without a frontier judge. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [57] [Pay for the Fault, Not the Flow: Label-Free In-Flow Multi-Agent Workflow Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01017)
*Xuehang Guo,Haoyu Wang,Shengyu Chen,Zach Chen,Wei Cheng,Qingyun Wang,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出InFlowOp，用无标签的统一成本度量同时优化LLM多智能体工作流的任务分解粒度、智能体分配与运行中故障修正，并发布Braid多智能体协调基准；在多领域多骨干下相对单智能体基线提升最高+11.97%，流内优化贡献+9.64%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在没有参考答案、评分结果或训练好的评估器的情况下，端到端优化LLM多智能体工作流，包括任务分解粒度、智能体分配以及运行中的故障修正。

Method: InFlowOp将工作流构建与执行统一在一个无标签成本函数之下。输入是复杂任务与一个可用的专家智能体池；成本度量同时考虑智能体能力与子任务需求的匹配程度以及智能体运行开销。在构建阶段，InFlowOp依据该成本双向确定任务分解粒度并完成智能体到子任务的分配，即分解粒度和分配不是固定模板，而是由成本最优性驱动。在执行阶段，工作流运行时若发现某个子任务未成功，InFlowOp不再依赖参考答案、评分结果或训练好的评估器来定位故障，而是使用同一成本度量选择最廉价的修正动作，从而在同一优化信号下兼顾工作流的构建与运行。论文还引入Braid基准，用于评估需要多智能体协调、超出单智能体能力的任务。整体而言，架构围绕成本驱动的任务分解、分配与故障修正形成闭环优化。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "相对单智能体基线的性能提升", "value": "最高 +11.97%", "setting": "多种领域与骨干模型下的评测"}, {"metric": "流内优化带来的性能提升", "value": "+9.64%", "setting": "多种领域与骨干模型下的评测"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出无标签的统一成本度量，同时权衡智能体能力与子任务需求的匹配度以及智能体运行开销；在构建阶段双向决定任务分解粒度与智能体分配，由成本驱动而非固定模板；在执行阶段用同一成本以最廉价动作修正故障，使构建与运行共享同一优化信号；提出Braid基准，其任务需要多智能体协作才能完成，超出单智能体能力

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly construct multi-agent workflows that decompose a complex task and assign specialist agents from a pool. However, building such a workflow well remains challenging: how finely to divide the task, which agent to trust with each subtask, and when to create a new specialist are all critical decisions a workflow constructor needs to settle up front. Thus, whether each subtask succeeds remains unknown until the workflow runs. Yet, improving a workflow is costly. Locating a fault usually requires a reference answer, a graded outcome, or a trained assessor, and the fix is applied to the whole workflow through re-execution, re-search, or retraining. We propose InFlowOp, which prices every decision in one label-free cost that weighs how well an agent's competence meets what a subtask demands against how much that agent takes to run. Before execution, InFlowOp bidirectionally determines the granularity of task decomposition and agent assignment following from the cost rather than from a fixed template. During execution, InFlowOp corrects a fault with the cheapest move via the same cost that serves the workflow both as it is built and as it runs. Facing the workflow-level evaluation challenge, we introduce Braid, a benchmark whose tasks require multi-agent coordination beyond single-agent capability. Across various domains and backbones, InFlowOp outperforms single agent baselines by up to $+11.97\%$, achieving $+9.64\%$ with in-flow optimization. Our project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [58] [RISED: RubrIcs for agentic multi-environment Selection and sElf-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00979)
*Jingtan Wang,Sirajul Salekin,Young mok Jung,Javier Movellan,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low,Manjot Bilkhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RISED 利用跨环境共享的鲁棒性评分（rubrics）同时指导在线数据选择、自蒸馏监督和 rollout 生成，旨在提升单一 LLM 智能体在多环境联合训练中的表现。论文报告在多个模型骨干上取得最高平均通过率，但未披露机构、在线部署或大规模实验细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Training a single LLM agent jointly across diverse interactive environments suffers from environment-level or local-scalar-reward data selection that ignores cross-environment relationships, and from coexisting all-failure/all-success rollout groups that provide no group-relative reward contrast.

Method: The proposed RISED architecture operates during multi-environment reinforcement learning of an LLM agent. Rollouts are generated across diverse interactive environments. An LLM judge tags each rollout using a predefined rubric vocabulary shared across environments, producing a behavioural profile per rollout. These profiles guide selection of training data that matches the overall behavioural composition of the mixed-environment batch while penalizing overlap with already-selected data. Positive rubrics, which describe desired behaviours, are provided as privileged context to an on-policy self-distillation teacher, adding token-level supervision. Negative rubrics, describing undesired behaviours, are used to steer subsequent rollout generation away from recurring failure modes. The system thus integrates data selection, self-distillation, and controlled rollout generation in a unified rubric-driven training loop. No further architectural details, objectives, or serving flow are disclosed in the abstract.

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "mean pass rate across environments", "value": "highest among compared methods", "setting": "across model backbones"}, {"metric": "individual environment ranking", "value": "first or second in every individual environment", "setting": "across model backbones"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Using a shared predefined rubric vocabulary across environments to tag each rollout with an LLM judge.；Selecting training data based on rubric profiles to align with the mixed-environment batch behavioural composition while limiting overlap with already-selected data.；Repurposing positive rubrics as privileged context for an on-policy self-distillation teacher to supply token-level supervision.；Using negative rubrics to guide subsequent rollout generation away from recurring failure modes.；Combining these components into the RISED framework for multi-environment agent training.

Abstract: Training a single LLM agent jointly across diverse interactive environments has attracted increasing attention as a route to generalist agents. Existing curriculum and data-selection strategies often allocate training at the environment level or prioritize local reward-based signals, without explicitly considering relationships between current rollouts across environments for prompt-group selection. Meanwhile, as environments are learned at different rates, all-failure and all-success rollout groups can coexist within a batch, leaving those data without group-relative reward signals. Both challenges highlight limitations of relying solely on scalar rewards in multi-environment RL: they provide limited information about cross-environment relationships and no within-group reward contrast when rewards are identical. This motivates richer textual feedback, such as rubrics describing rollout behaviours, to guide learning. Beyond rubrics' usage as reward, we repurpose rubrics to guide both online data selection and policy supervision. An LLM judge tags each rollout using a predefined rubric vocabulary shared across environments. The resulting profiles guide the selection of data that aligns with the overall behavioural composition of the mixed-environment batch while limiting overlap with already-selected data. Available positive rubrics (describing desired behaviours) provide privileged context for an on-policy self-distillation teacher, supplying additional token-level supervision, while negative rubrics (describing undesired behaviours) guide subsequent rollout generation away from recurring failure modes. Together, these components form RISED. Across model backbones, RISED achieves the highest mean pass rate across environments and ranks first or second in every individual environment. Rubric-based analysis of RISED can further characterize the behavioural changes accompanying these gains.

</details>


### [59] [PG-SFT: Balancing Capability Acquisition and Retention in Offline Agent Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00949)
*Ronghua Li,Zi Liang,Zhishan Li,Shinan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 PG-SFT，用 agent 轨迹的 turn-level information gain 调节监督强度，以缓解离线 agent 微调中的能力退化，在评测基准上以目标任务的轻微下降换取分布漂移和广泛能力退化的大幅降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在离线 agent 轨迹的监督微调中，逐 token 模仿会损害基座模型的通用推理、工具调用、代码生成等既有能力，如何平衡新能力获取与旧能力保留。

Method: 论文以离线 agent 轨迹上的监督微调流程为基础：输入为基座语言模型与带有多轮推理/动作的 agent 轨迹数据。标准 SFT 对推理与动作 token 逐 token 施加交叉熵监督。PG-SFT 在此流程上引入 turn-level information gain 作为「特权」信号，用以衡量每个回合轨迹相对于基座行为的偏离/信息增量，并据此调整监督强度：信息增益高、真正需要习得的回合获得更强监督，而可能引发不必要分布漂移的回合被弱化或抑制。对比基线包括标准 SFT、施加 KL 惩罚以约束分布漂移、以及限制更新幅度的方法。训练目标仍为监督式学习，但监督权重随回合信息增益自适应变化；推理/服务路径与常规 SFT 后模型一致，未披露额外推理开销。论文未披露具体模型规模、数据规模、硬件或延迟信息。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "分布漂移与广泛能力退化", "value": "PG-SFT 相比标准 SFT 大幅降低分布漂移与非目标任务能力退化，但目标任务性能略有下降", "setting": "论文自设的 agent 轨迹 SFT 实验，多基准对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 利用 agent 轨迹的 turn-level information gain 作为监督强度调节指标；提出 Privilege-Guided SFT（PG-SFT），按需在何处、以多强程度偏离基座行为施加监督；系统对比标准 SFT、KL 惩罚约束分布漂移、限制更新幅度等基线在能力获取-保留权衡上的表现

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on offline agent trajectories is the standard approach for training specialized tool-using agents, but forcing models to imitate reasoning and actions token by token may harm other capabilities (e.g., general reasoning, tool calling, code generation) of the base model. In this work, we focus on studying \emph{how to better balance the trade-off between acquiring new capabilities and preserving existing ones during agent trace SFT}. By comparing several baselines in our setup, standard SFT improves the target benchmark while lowering several non-target benchmark scores; meanwhile, simply constraining distributional drift using KL penalty or limiting the update magnitude did not avoid this regression trend. Motivated by recent token-wise adaptive learning objectives, this work proposes \textbf{Privilege-Guided SFT (PG-SFT)} to leverage turn-level information gain of agent trajectories as an indicator to adjust supervision strength. PG-SFT yields a more favorable observed trade-off on the evaluated benchmarks, substantially reducing distributional drift and broad capability degradation at the cost of slight degradation in target-task performance. Our findings suggest that balancing the acquisition--retention trade-off depends not only on whether the model is anchored to its base behavior, but also on where and how strongly supervision should depart from that behavior.}

</details>


### [60] [OR for AI That Does OR: Routing LLMs up the Escalator inside the OSCAR Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00912)
*Jinzhi Bu,Haixin Tang,Huanan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OSCAR是一个利用模拟器、编码器和审查器协作的LLM框架，通过认证的决策示例验证优化模型改进，并将多LLM调用建模为序贯决策，在五个基准问题上达到95%-100%准确率，且成本远低于Codex和Claude Code。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何低成本、本地化地验证LLM生成的优化模型改进，并在不同价格和能力的LLM之间分配调用尝试。

Method: OSCAR由Simulator、Coder、Reviewer三个模块组成。Coder使用LLM将业务描述翻译成优化模型代码；Simulator基于已标注的决策示例（可行与不可行）对候选模型进行认证和比较，当LLM解释与标签冲突时遵循标签；Reviewer审查代码和模型确保约束和目标正确。系统将寻找下一次认证改进的过程建模为序贯决策：在未观测的难度下，选择调用哪个LLM以及何时停止。在简化已知先验设置下，成本顺序升级（先调用便宜模型，不满意再升级到更贵模型）被证明最优；对一般菜单推导出免先验的竞争性保证。推理时支持两个小型开源LLM本地部署（单GPU）或云端部署，以平衡预算和保密需求。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "准确率", "value": "95%到100%", "setting": "五个基准问题，使用两个小型开源LLM，在报告设置下"}, {"metric": "单次尝试准确率", "value": "平均29%和48%", "setting": "两个小型开源LLM各自单次尝试"}, {"metric": "平均token成本", "value": "Codex和Claude Code分别是OSCAR的3.1倍和5.8倍", "setting": "每个问题五次运行"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: OSCAR框架：模拟器、编码器、审查器三个模块协作，模拟器基于标注决策示例认证，持续搜索超越可行性的改进。；将寻找下一次认证改进的搜索建模为未观测难度下的序贯决策问题：调用哪个LLM以及何时停止。；在已知先验简化设置下，给出成本顺序升级最优的条件；对一般菜单，推导出免先验的竞争性保证。

Abstract: Large language models can translate business descriptions into optimization models, but executable code may misrepresent constraints or objectives. A solver can then return an optimal solution to the wrong problem. Even when the solution satisfies the intended operating rules, a better plan may exist. For organizations that repeatedly use optimization modeling, an LLM-based framework should produce accurate formulations at low cost and, ideally, run locally. We study how to verify improvements and allocate attempts across LLMs that differ in price and capability. We develop OSCAR (Optimization modeling by Simulator, Coder, And Reviewer), which uses an offline Simulator certified against labeled decision examples to compare candidates and continues searching beyond feasibility. We model the search for the next certified improvement as sequential decisions under unobserved difficulty: which LLMs to call and when to stop. In a simplified known-prior setting, we give conditions under which cost-ordered escalation is optimal. For general menus, we derive a prior-free competitive guarantee. On five benchmark problems, OSCAR achieves 95% to 100% accuracy at the reported settings using two small open-weight LLMs, each deployable locally on a single GPU. Their single-attempt accuracies average 29% and 48%. In five runs per problem, Codex and Claude Code incur average token costs 3.1 and 5.8 times OSCAR's, respectively. OSCAR supports open-weight models locally or in the cloud, depending on budget and confidentiality requirements. Firms should maintain labeled decision examples of feasible and infeasible decisions to clarify plain-language operating rules. OSCAR follows these labels when an LLM's interpretation conflicts with them. As LLM capabilities and prices change, OSCAR's simple operating rules and adjustable settings help firms adapt their model choices and benefit from these advances.

</details>


### [61] [MemFit: Efficient Long-Term Agentic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00872)
*Mitchell Piehl,Muchao Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MemFit 提出 LLM 长期记忆系统：逐轮原文 append-only 写入、以片段摘要建索引、无 LLM 的词法+语义多路召回加 cross-encoder 重排，在 LoCoMo、MemGallery、LongMemEval-S 上取得 SOTA，并将记忆构建时间与成本降低数倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 长期记忆系统依赖 LLM Agent 组织与整合记忆，写入成本与延迟高；而压缩式方法会丢失表层细节。如何在保持细节与检索质量的同时显著降低记忆构建的时间与成本。

Method: MemFit 采用 append-only 存储：对话每一轮以原文逐字写入，插入过程不调用 LLM，因此写入近乎即时。索引层不替换原始轮次，而是以片段摘要作为轮次的索引表示。检索阶段完全无 LLM，采用多路策略：同时利用词法匹配信号与语义匹配信号生成候选，并在 caption 增强的 episode 上使用 cross-encoder 进行重排；该流程同时覆盖纯文本与多模态设置。论文在 LoCoMo、MemGallery、LongMemEval-S 上验证，报告 SOTA 性能并显著降低记忆构建时间与成本。摘要未披露具体模型规模、训练数据、硬件、延迟与吞吐数值。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "state-of-the-art 性能（具体数值未在摘要披露）", "value": "SOTA", "setting": "LoCoMo、MemGallery、LongMemEval-S 三个基准"}, {"metric": "记忆构建时间与成本降低数倍", "value": "数倍（several-fold）", "setting": "与依赖 LLM 调用的现有记忆系统对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: append-only 逐轮原文存储，插入过程无需 LLM 调用，实现近即时写入；用片段摘要（segment summaries）为轮次建立索引，而非用摘要替换原始轮次，兼顾压缩与保真；无 LLM 的多路检索策略：融合词法信号与语义信号；在 caption 增强的 episode 上做 cross-encoder 重排，并同时支持文本与多模态设置

Abstract: Long-term memory systems for large language models (LLMs) have gained popularity for extending reasoning capabilities across applications. Current memory systems rely on LLM agents to organize and consolidate memory, resulting in costly, inefficient write operations. To address this limitation, we propose MemFit, a long-term memory system for conversational agents that reduces the cost and latency of memory operations. Unlike existing systems that rely on expensive LLM calls for memory construction or discard surface-level details through compression, MemFit stores each turn verbatim in an append-only store with near-instantaneous, LLM-free insertion, indexing turns with segment summaries rather than replacing them. Additionally, MemFit uses an LLM-free, multi-path retrieval strategy that combines lexical and semantic signals with cross-encoder reranking over caption- augmented episodes in both textual and multimodal settings. Empirical results on three widely used benchmarks, LoCoMo, MemGallery, and LongMemEval-S, show that MemFit achieves state-of-the-art performance while reducing memory construction time and cost several-fold, providing a scalable and efficient solution for persistent agentic memory.

</details>


### [62] [Sapien: A Stateful Policy Engine for Autonomous AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00797)
*Corinn Tiffany,Wen Zhang,Eugene Bagdasarian,Lillian Tsai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Sapien 是面向自主 AI Agent 的状态化安全策略引擎，用带状态化谓词的扩展正则表达工具调用序列，支持延迟策略生成与作用域语义检查。实验显示其效用接近无约束 agent，在被完全劫持时可在 AgentDojo 排除 93-95% 攻击、Toolathlon 排除 62-85% 攻击，明显优于工具白名单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多轮任务中的上下文安全防御假设动作的有效性只由当前任务决定，忽略了多步任务中动作有效性依赖于 agent 已经执行和学到的历史状态，导致无法表达和执行状态相关的安全策略。

Method: Sapien 是一个状态化策略引擎，用于对自主 AI Agent 的工具调用进行上下文安全强制。其端到端架构包括：（1）策略表示层：策略由扩展正则表达式构成，描述允许的工具调用序列；正则表达式上附加状态化谓词，使策略可以引用 agent 已执行动作与已获取信息等历史状态。（2）策略生成模块：支持延迟策略生成（deferred policy generation），即并非在任务开始前一次性生成完整策略，而是在需要时根据当前任务上下文生成策略，以适应多步任务中不断变化的状态。（3）策略强制模块：在 agent 每次发起工具调用前，将调用与当前累积状态一起匹配到策略的正则表达式与状态化谓词，判断该调用序列是否仍处于允许集合内；若不允许则阻止执行。（4）语义检查模块：作用域语义检查（scoped semantic checks）在特定调用范围内对动作内容做语义层面的合法性校验，弥补纯语法序列匹配的不足。整体数据流为：agent 执行任务产生历史状态 → 延迟生成/更新策略 → 工具调用请求结合历史状态进入策略匹配与语义检查 → 允许则执行并更新状态，拒绝则阻断。论文未披露具体模型规模、训练数据、训练硬件或推理延迟等资源细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "AgentDojo 攻击排除率", "value": "93-95%", "setting": "即使 agent 被完全劫持，策略仍能排除的攻击比例"}, {"metric": "Toolathlon 攻击排除率", "value": "62-85%", "setting": "长时程任务，约为工具白名单的两倍"}, {"metric": "效用保持", "value": "与无约束 agent 相差仅几个百分点", "setting": "在有策略约束下的任务效用对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 Sapien 状态策略引擎，用扩展正则表达式表达允许的工具调用序列；支持状态化谓词（stateful predicates），可根据 agent 历史状态判断动作是否合法；引入延迟策略生成（deferred policy generation），在需要时再生成策略而非一次性生成；引入作用域语义检查（scoped semantic checks），在特定调用范围内做语义合法性校验

Abstract: Contextual security defenses prevent AI agents from taking rogue actions by synthesizing a task-specific policy and enforcing it on the agent's tool calls. In multi-step tasks, however, which actions are valid often depends on what the agent has already done and learned. We present Sapien, a policy engine for enforcing stateful contextual policies. A Sapien policy specifies permitted tool-call sequences using a regular expression extended with stateful predicates, deferred policy generation, and scoped semantic checks. We show that Sapien stays within a few percent of an unconstrained agent's utility. Even if the agent is fully hijacked, Sapien's policies rule out 93-95% of attacks on AgentDojo and 62-85% on Toolathlon (twice as many as tool allowlists on long-horizon tasks).

</details>


### [63] [Backdoor Containment via Expert Quarantine and Shutdown in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00663)
*Jianwei Li,Min-Seon Kim,Jung-Eun Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出QES，一种基于MoE-like路由与LoRA专家的后门遏制策略：训练时允许后门形成但将其路由到可隔离专家，部署时仅需将隔离专家路由权重置零即可常数时间禁用后门。实验覆盖两个任务、三类攻击、四个模型系列，ASR从100%降至0-10%，下游效用常保持或轻微下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后门LLM在良性输入上表现正常、在隐藏触发下产生攻击者指定输出；现有防御要么在训练前/中抑制后门学习，要么在训练后/推理时净化已形成的后门，缺乏一种允许后门形成但可部署时低成本无害化的策略。

Method: QES在Transformer语言模型基础上增加路由的专家特定LoRA分支与轻量路由器，形成MoE-like结构。输入为中毒数据集；训练时通过辅助路由目标（regularization-steered）引导触发条件行为进入指定隔离专家，同时保持良性能力分布在其他专家中。部署时无需触发筛查或权重更新，仅将隔离专家的路由权重置零，即可常数时间禁用该路径。该设计使后门行为被局限在可关闭组件内，从而在保留良性效用的同时实现后门遏制。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Attack Success Rate (ASR)", "value": "从100%降至0-10%", "setting": "两个任务、三类攻击、四个模型系列中的大多数设置"}, {"metric": "下游效用", "value": "经常保持或仅轻微受影响", "setting": "同上实验设置"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出第三种后门防御范式“learn, but channel”：允许训练中形成后门，但将其路由到可隔离、可关闭的专用组件。；提出Quarantined Expert Shutdown (QES)：在Transformer LLM上引入路由的专家特定LoRA分支和轻量路由器，通过辅助路由目标将触发条件行为吸引到指定隔离专家。；部署时缓解仅为单一常数时间操作：将隔离专家的路由权重置零，无需触发筛查或再更新模型权重。

Abstract: Backdoored large language models (LLMs) can behave normally on benign inputs while producing attacker-specified outputs under hidden triggers. Existing defenses span four stages--prior-training, in-training, post-training, and inference-time--and share one of two underlying strategies: either suppress backdoor learning (by filtering poisoned data or interrupting its acquisition during optimization) or learn, then purify (by repairing model weights or gating inputs after a fully backdoored model has formed). We propose a third strategy, learn, but channel: allow backdoor formation during training but route it into a designated, quarantined component that can be disabled at deployment. To this end, we propose Quarantined Expert Shutdown QES, a computationally efficient containment strategy built in a regularization-steered MoE-like setting. Specifically, given a poisoned dataset, QES augments a Transformer-based language model with routed expert-specific LoRA branches and lightweight routers, and uses auxiliary routing objectives to attract trigger-conditioned behavior into a designated expert while preserving benign capability elsewhere. At deployment, mitigation reduces to a single constant-time operation: zeroing the quarantined expert's routing weight, without trigger screening or further updating model weights. Empirically, our methods reduce the attack success rate ASR from 100% to 0-10% on most settings across two tasks, three attacks, and four model families, while downstream utility is often preserved or only modestly affected. These results establish learn, but channel as a previously unexplored regime for backdoor containment in generative LLMs.

</details>

*Xisen Jin,Jingheng Li,Zhenglun Chen,Junyi Du,Xiang Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReLiveGym是一个面向长生命周期LLM智能体的诊断性评测环境，通过按时间顺序回放数周的真实新闻、市场和社交媒体流，考察智能体在动态环境中的稀疏行动、时机把握与持续适应能力。作者在八个基础模型上系统研究模型选择与harness设计（尤其是行动时机机制）的影响，并评估事后反馈持续学习的作用，提出模型选择、行动触发机制与反馈利用是长生命周期智能体设计的关键维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时程智能体工作多在静态环境中评估，无法刻画智能体在真实动态环境中数天至数周无人值守运行、在正确时机行动并随时间适应环境的需求。

Method: ReLiveGym是一个诊断性评测环境，而非单一模型架构。其输入是按时间顺序回放的真实世界新闻、市场和社交媒体数据流，模拟数周时间跨度；智能体需要在这些流上稀疏地行动。环境提供多种任务，覆盖不同的时间敏感性、推理强度和重复频率。评测围绕两类设计维度展开：其一是基础语言模型的选择（共八个基础模型），其二是harness设计，特别是智能体决定何时行动的触发机制。此外还评估基于事后反馈的持续学习如何影响性能并缓解失败模式。评测结论指出模型选择、行动时机机制和反馈使用是长生命周期智能体设计的重要考量，但摘要未披露具体模块、训练目标或服务流程细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 构建ReLiveGym诊断性评测环境，按时间顺序回放真实世界的新闻、市场和社交媒体流，模拟数周时间跨度；任务覆盖不同时间敏感性、推理强度和重复频率，系统考察模型选择与harness设计的影响；识别出'智能体何时行动'（行动时机机制）是长生命周期任务的关键harness设计轴；评估基于事后反馈的持续学习对性能提升和失败模式的作用

Abstract: As large language model (LLM) agents become widely adopted, they are increasingly deployed for tasks that require persistent monitoring or recurring actions (e.g., market analysis). These agents are expected to operate unattended for days or weeks, act at the right timing, and adapt to the dynamic environment over time. These challenges are not fully captured in the existing long-horizon agent work, as they often consider a static environment that is not temporally changing. We introduce ReLiveGym, a diagnostic evaluation environment of long-lived tasks in which agents act sparsely over simulated weeks of chronologically replayed real-world news, market, and social-media streams. The tasks span diverse levels of time sensitivity, reasoning intensity, and recurrence. Across eight base language models, we investigate how model choice and harness design affect agent performance on such long-lived tasks. Our results show that how agents determine when to act arises as an important harness-design axis for long-lived tasks; and that the optimal design varies across tasks and sometimes model choices as well. We also evaluate how continuous learning from hindsight feedback affects performance and addresses failure modes observed in these long-lived tasks. These findings indicate model choice, action timing mechanism, and use of feedback as important considerations in the design of long-lived agents. Code: this https URL

</details>


### [65] [Agent Evaluation Reliability: More Tasks Won't (Always) Fix An Agent Leaderboard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00651)
*Michael Hardy,Ruhana Azam,Anka Reuel,Mykel Kochenderfer,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出贝叶斯方差分解框架，对 22 个 agent 基准的排行榜做可靠性分析，区分信号与噪声，指出可靠性取决于具体结论：固定系统排序可靠（0.935-0.994），底层模型排序可靠性显著更低（0.148-0.841）；单纯增加任务最多只能提升 0.097 的模型排名可靠性；池化多样基准可在同预算下把跨任务排名可靠性从 0.44 提升至 0.75，并最多降低 83% 成本。结论是评测设计应跟随目标结论并优先解决主导性不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 agent leaderboard 的排名既受模型影响也受评测条件（scaffold、任务集）影响，导致可靠性依赖于具体结论：能可靠排序已部署系统的评测未必能可靠排序底层模型。需要量化并诊断限制排名可靠性的不确定性来源。

Method: 本文并非模型架构论文，而是评测可靠性分析框架。其‘架构’为统计推断流程：输入为来自 Holistic Agent Leaderboard 与 Harbor Index 的 22 个 agent 基准的稀疏、不均衡排行榜数据（每个模型-scaffold-任务组合的得分或成功/失败结果）。首先形式化测量目标（claim），区分‘排序已部署的固定模型-scaffold 系统’与‘排序底层模型’两类结论。随后构建贝叶斯方差分解模型，把观测分数的变异分解为信号（与目标结论相关的性能差异）与噪声（与目标无关但会改变排名的变异，如 scaffold 选择、任务抽样）。基于该分解计算可靠性指标：固定系统间可靠性、底层模型可靠性、以及 scaffold 间可靠性（scaffold 是否保持模型排序）。进一步引入投影分析：在给定任务预算下模拟增加任务数或池化多个基准对可靠性的影响，从而给出评测预算应投向哪类不确定性来源的建议。没有训练目标或推理服务路径；优化目标是最大化目标结论下的排名可靠性估计。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "固定模型-scaffold 系统排名可靠性", "value": "0.935-0.994", "setting": "对 22 个基准（Holistic Agent Leaderboard 与 Harbor Index）应用贝叶斯方差分解"}, {"metric": "底层模型排名可靠性", "value": "0.148-0.841", "setting": "同上，测量底层模型排序时可靠性显著更低"}, {"metric": "无限增加同类任务对模型排名可靠性的提升上限", "value": "0.097", "setting": "当不确定性主要由有限的 scaffold 覆盖主导时"}, {"metric": "跨多样 agentic 任务的排名可靠性（池化前）", "value": "0.44", "setting": "固定任务预算下的投影可靠性"}, {"metric": "跨多样 agentic 任务的排名可靠性（池化后）", "value": "0.75", "setting": "相同任务预算下池化多个基准的投影可靠性"}, {"metric": "评测成本降低", "value": "最多 83%", "setting": "池化多样基准以进行跨任务排名时，投影成本下降"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 为稀疏、不均衡的 agent leaderboard 提出贝叶斯方差分解框架，将总变异拆分为与目标结论相关的信号和会改变排名的无关噪声；针对不同测量目标（固定模型-scaffold 系统 vs 底层模型）分别定义并计算可靠性，并引入 scaffold 间可靠性衡量 scaffold 是否保持模型排序；量化‘增加任务数量’这一常见补救手段的上限：在 scaffold 覆盖受限主导不确定性时，无限增加同类任务对模型排名可靠性的提升最多仅 0.097；提出并验证跨基准池化（pooling diverse benchmarks）策略，在相同任务预算下提升跨任务排名可靠性，并量化可降低的评测成本

Abstract: Agent evaluations are increasingly used to compare LLMs and inform deployment decisions, yet ranks can reflect not only the model but also the effects of the evaluation conditions such as the scaffolds or tasks. This makes reliability claim-dependent: an evaluation that reliably ranks deployed systems may not reliably rank underlying models. We ask which conclusions current agent evaluations reliably support and what additional evaluation would improve them. We develop a Bayesian variance-decomposition framework for sparse, imbalanced agent leaderboards and apply it to 22 benchmarks from the Holistic Agent Leaderboard and Harbor Index. The framework separates signal, performance differences relevant to the intended claim, from noise, irrelevant variation that can still change rankings. We find: (1) Reliability depends on the measurement goal. Fixed model-scaffold systems are ranked reliably (0.935-0.994), while underlying-model reliability is substantially lower (0.148-0.841). (2) Scaffold choice can change conclusions. Inter-scaffold reliability measures whether scaffolds preserve model rankings, showing that scaffold effects vary substantially across evaluations. (3) More tasks cannot resolve all uncertainty. Even infinitely many similarly constructed tasks improve model-ranking reliability of a benchmark by at most 0.097 when uncertainty is dominated by limited scaffold coverage. (4) Pooling diverse benchmarks can improve cross-task rankings at lower cost. For rankings across diverse agentic tasks, pooling benchmarks raises projected reliability from 0.44 to 0.75 at the same task budget and can reduce projected cost by up to 83\%. Evaluation design should follow the intended claim: identify what a score or ranking should mean, diagnose what limits its reliability, and spend evaluation budget on the sources of uncertainty that matter.

</details>


### [66] [When Harnesses Lose the Signal: Causal Evaluation of Recovery in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00372)
*Shuyao Xiao,Shengling Wang,Xuan Chen,Ke Chao,Ming Cui,Feifei Qian,Chaoyang Mei,Fanlin Meng,Ziming Yu,Junxi Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将LLM智能体的错误恢复视为因果决策问题，区分恢复的挽救与危害，并提出轻量策略CIR选择性干预，在ALFWorld长程任务上使Qwen3-14B成功率提升3个百分点，且不破坏观测正确的轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的恢复操作通常仅用平均任务成功率来评估，掩盖了同一操作既可能挽救失败轨迹、也可能破坏原本成功轨迹的双重效应；需要因果地分离恢复的收益与危害，并决定何时干预才有价值。

Method: 论文将LLM智能体的恢复过程形式化为因果决策问题：智能体在外部harness中执行动作，环境返回观测，恢复操作（recovery）可能被触发。为评估恢复，从同一执行状态出发进行对照——有恢复与无恢复——以分离挽救（rescue）与破坏（harm），并观察恢复价值随执行时间的变化。基于此，提出Causal Intervention Router (CIR)：一种轻量策略，利用恢复前可获得的信息（如当前状态、历史执行信息等）决定是否执行恢复干预。CIR在长程ALFWorld任务上与Qwen3-14B结合，将成功率从70.33%提升至73.33%。实验还设置控制条件，以排除仅由环境新观测带来收益的解释。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "success rate", "value": "70.33% -> 73.33% (+3.00 percentage points)", "setting": "long-horizon ALFWorld tasks with Qwen3-14B; all evaluated trajectories with correct observations left untouched"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将智能体恢复建模为因果决策问题：从相同执行状态出发比较有无恢复的结果，区分rescue与harm，并研究恢复价值随时间的变化。；提出Causal Intervention Router (CIR)，一种使用恢复前可用信息判断干预是否值得的轻量策略。；通过额外控制实验表明，恢复收益不能仅由环境返回的新观测解释。

Abstract: Large language model agents rely on external harnesses to pass information between the model and its environment and to recover from execution errors. Yet recovery is usually judged only by average task success. This hides an important tension. The same operation can rescue a failing trajectory or disrupt one that would otherwise succeed. We frame recovery as a causal decision problem. Starting from the same execution state, we compare what happens with and without recovery, separate rescue from harm, and study how the value of recovery changes over time. We then introduce the Causal Intervention Router (CIR), a lightweight policy that uses information available before recovery to decide when intervention is worthwhile. On long-horizon ALFWorld tasks with Qwen3-14B, CIR raises success from 70.33% to 73.33%, a gain of 3.00 percentage points. It leaves all evaluated trajectories with correct observations untouched. Additional controls show that the benefit of recovery cannot be explained solely by the new observation returned by the environment. These results provide a practical way to evaluate recovery and apply it selectively.

</details>


### [67] [Gradient-Aligned Pair Selection for Personalized Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00061)
*Ruoming Jin,Xinyu Li,Hao Zhou,Jianfeng Zhu,Ruixin Guo,Feodor Dragan,Lei Xu,Haixun Wang,Yang Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GAP-DPO 从梯度几何角度重新审视个性化偏好优化中的偏好对选择，提出效用感知、几何对齐的迭代选择算法，并在个性化文本生成基准上优于标准 DPO 变体，为个性化 LLM 对齐提供了新的优化视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在个性化 LLM 偏好优化中，如何选择偏好对（preference pairs）以最大化用户效用，避免现有基于启发式（如似然极端值）的选择方法与显式用户效用解耦导致个性化退化。

Method: GAP-DPO 将个性化偏好优化建模为几何对齐问题。输入为用户特定偏好数据及初始 LLM；算法迭代执行：(1) 基于当前策略生成或重新生成偏好对，控制分布偏移（epoch-wise regeneration）；(2) 计算期望用户效用梯度与 DPO 更新方向之间的一阶交互，评估偏好边际与效用梯度的方向对齐程度；(3) 优先选择方向对齐的偏好对，使 DPO 更新从误差纠正偏向强化式更新；(4) 使用选定的偏好对执行 DPO 更新。训练目标结合标准 DPO 损失与效用感知的对齐选择策略。推理时直接使用训练后的策略模型进行个性化生成。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "风格保真度、偏好对齐、生成质量", "value": "持续优于标准 DPO 变体（未提供具体数值）", "setting": "个性化文本生成基准实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 从一阶梯度交互角度形式化个性化偏好学习为几何对齐优化问题，分析期望用户效用梯度与 DPO 更新方向的关系；揭示在 off-policy 采样下，当偏好边际与效用梯度方向对齐时，DPO 更新从纯误差纠正信号转变为强化学习式更新；提出 GAP-DPO：效用感知、几何对齐的迭代偏好对选择算法，并通过 epoch 级重新生成控制分布偏移

Abstract: Personalizing large language models (LLMs) requires aligning generation behavior with user-specific preferences rather than aggregate quality. While Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) provides a stable framework for preference learning, its effectiveness in personalized settings critically depends on how preference pairs are selected. Existing approaches typically rely on heuristic criteria, such as likelihood-based extremes, which decouple optimization from explicit user utility and can lead to degraded personalization. We formalize personalized preference learning as a geometry-aligned optimization problem by analyzing the first-order interaction between gradients of expected user utility and DPO update directions. Our analysis reveals that, under off-policy sampling, the DPO update transitions from a purely error-corrective signal to a reinforcement-like update when preference margins are directionally aligned with utility gradients. This perspective exposes pair selection as a geometric decision that governs whether preference optimization advances or hinders personalization. Motivated by this insight, we propose GAP-DPO (Geometry-Aligned Preference DPO), an iterative algorithm that performs utility-aware, geometry-aligned pair selection while controlling distribution shift via epoch-wise regeneration. Experiments on personalized text generation benchmarks show that GAP-DPO consistently improves stylistic fidelity, preference alignment, and generation quality compared to standard DPO variants. Together, our results establish gradient alignment as a unifying principle for personalized preference optimization and demonstrate that pair selection is an intrinsic component of the optimization geometry rather than a heuristic preprocessing step.

</details>


### [68] [Measuring the Microtask Eligibility Gap: When Is an Off-the-Shelf SLM Enough for an Agent Harness?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00025)
*Jundong Hu,Shekar Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文评估现成小语言模型在智能体框架微任务上的表现，发现16个配置无一满足预定义阈值，量化未改善资格，且缺口与模型规模而非精度更相关。提出基于对数概率阈值的诊断方法，并建议将SLM置于满足阈值的基线之后，例如使用4B重排序器对BM25短名单重排，获得+0.047的NDCG提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现成的小语言模型（SLM）是否满足工业实践中定义的任务阈值，以及量化是否会改变这一结论。

Method: 论文提出一个基于微任务的评估框架：输入是智能体框架中的特定请求（如shell命令、工具选择查询、记忆写入内容、历史轮次排序请求），输出是模型的文本响应（如批准/拒绝、工具名称、记忆条目、排序结果）。每个微任务有固定的提示模板、自动评估指标、预先指定的阈值τ（锚定于廉价非LLM基线）以及CI感知的资格规则（配置的置信界必须超过τ）。作者在FP16、贪心解码、单一冻结提示、无微调条件下扫描Qwen3 0.6/1.7/4/8B，并在Llama-3.x上复现。为了诊断失败原因，他们使用对数概率的决策阈值分析（任务1、3、4直接使用，任务2通过上下文长度/级联探针），将失败分为能力缺陷和可通过调整解码阈值解决的四种状态。此外，研究4-bit量化（RTN/GPTQ/AWQ）对硬标签任务（T1/T3）的影响，以及对诊断/窗口化任务（T2/T4）的鲁棒性，发现量化损害随模型规模变化，但未使任何配置达到资格。最后，论文提出一个实用建议：将SLM作为满足CI阈值基线的后备，仅在基线不满足阈值时调用SLM，例如在BM25短名单上使用4B重排序器，该重排序器优于BM25但自身未达到资格。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "通过资格规则的配置数", "value": "0/16", "setting": "Qwen3 0.6/1.7/4/8B 在FP16、贪心解码、单一冻结提示、无微调，4个微任务上"}, {"metric": "通过资格规则的配置数（Llama-3.x）", "value": "0/12", "setting": "Llama-3.x 系列模型，在相同基准上"}, {"metric": "通过资格规则的配置数（提示词变体）", "value": "0/112", "setting": "原始提示加上每个单元格3个中性改写，共112个配置"}, {"metric": "4B重排序器相对BM25的NDCG增益", "value": "+0.047 [0.020, 0.073]", "setting": "4B模型对BM25短名单进行重排序，未达到资格规则"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 构建了包含4个微任务的基准，每个任务有固定提示、自动指标、预指定阈值τ和CI感知的资格规则。；发现资格缺口：在16个配置（4任务×4模型）中，0个通过资格规则。；提出基于对数概率的决策阈值诊断方法，将失败分为能力缺陷和可通过调整解码阈值解决的失败（4种状态）。；量化（4-bit RTN/GPTQ/AWQ）带来的损害取决于模型大小，但未使任何配置达到资格；缺口更多与模型规模相关而非精度。；在Llama-3.x上复现（12/12不合格），且对锚点选择和提示措辞具有鲁棒性（0/112合格）。；建议将SLM放置在满足CI-backed阈值的基线之后，仅在基线不满足阈值时使用SLM；例如4B重排序器在BM25短名单上超越BM25（+0.047 [0.020, 0.073]），但自身未达到资格。

Abstract: Agent harnesses increasingly want to run small language models (SLMs) on the microtasks around a frontier large language model (LLM) planner: auto-approving shell commands, writing memory, selecting tools, ranking past turns. We ask whether off-the-shelf SLMs meet practitioner-defined thresholds and, when they fail, why, and whether quantization changes the answer. We build a benchmark of 4 such microtasks with fixed prompts and automatic metrics, each with a pre-specified threshold $\tau$ anchored to a cheap non-LLM baseline and a CI-aware eligibility rule (a configuration passes only if its confidence bound clears $\tau$). Sweeping Qwen3 0.6/1.7/4/8B at their best (FP16, greedy, one frozen prompt, no tuning), we find an eligibility gap: 0 of 16 (4 tasks $\times$ 4 models) configurations pass (verified by checking the raw outputs and parser behavior). A logprob decision-threshold diagnostic (T1/T3/T4; T2 via a context-length/cascade probe) separates the failures into capability deficits and failures that can be addressed by changing the decoding threshold (4 regimes). Quantization to 4-bit (RTN/GPTQ/AWQ) does damage that depends on model size and moves no configuration into eligibility (certified on the reconstructable hard-label tasks T1/T3, diagnostic/windowed robustness on T2/T4), so the gap tracks model size more than precision; it replicates on Llama-3.x (12/12 ineligible) and is robust to the anchor choice (a $\tau$-sweep) and to prompt wording (0/112 eligible across the original plus 3 neutral paraphrases per cell). The practical implication: place SLMs behind a baseline that meets the CI-backed threshold, and use the SLM only where the baseline fails to meet the threshold; e.g. a 4B re-ranker over a BM25 shortlist beats BM25 ($+0.047$ [0.020, 0.073], without itself certifying eligibility).

</details>

### [69] [Not All Is Lost: Repairing Lossy User Preference States of Personalization Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01270)
*Parthiv Chatterjee,Dhiraj Golhar,Ummesalma Diwan,Sourish Dasgupta,Manjunath Joshi,Tanmoy Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究个性化编码器压缩用户历史时丢失可用证据的问题，提出REPAIR方法，在冻结编码器和任务头的前提下，通过学习坐标空间比较缓存表示与当前偏好状态，聚合时间步上的纠正证据以修复状态，在多个推荐数据集上一致提升十二个宿主的排序指标，并在个性化生成任务上显著提升偏好响应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化编码器将不断演化的交互历史压缩为偏好状态时，会丢失缓存表示中仍存在的可用证据，导致任务头性能下降，即存在可恢复性差距。

Method: REPAIR将缓存表示与当前偏好状态在一个紧凑的学习坐标空间中进行比较。输入包括冻结编码器为历史各个时间步缓存的表示以及当前偏好状态。该模块在扩展历史、近期交互和局部爆发三个尺度上解析纠正证据，然后选择哪些模式在哪些时间步上贡献，并将这些贡献聚合为修正量，加到当前偏好状态上，最后送入任务头。编码器宿主修复复用现有前向计算的缓存表示，不重新编码历史。训练时仅训练REPAIR，保持编码器和任务头冻结；推理时同样只使用REPAIR产生的校正状态。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "MRR", "value": "3.96 MRR points (提升)", "setting": "Mamba4Rec on MovieLens，仅训练REPAIR，编码器与任务头冻结"}, {"metric": "MRR", "value": "0.19 MRR points (提升)", "setting": "Mamba4Rec on MovieLens，仅微调任务头"}, {"metric": "MRR, nDCG@10", "value": "十二个代表性推荐宿主全部提升", "setting": "MovieLens, PENS, MIND, Amazon Reviews 2023；编码器与任务头冻结，仅训练REPAIR"}, {"metric": "两个已报告的加权PerSEval变体", "value": "最高提升25.23%", "setting": "个性化生成任务IMPerSumm，评估对用户偏好的响应性"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出REPAIR，在紧凑的学习坐标空间中比较缓存表示与当前偏好状态，识别可纠正证据。；通过扩展历史、近期交互和局部爆发模式三个尺度解析纠正证据，并选择性地聚合添加到偏好状态中。；编码器宿主修复：直接复用冻结编码器的现有前向计算缓存表示，无需重新编码历史。；仅训练REPAIR，保持编码器和任务头冻结，即可持续提升十二个代表性推荐宿主的MRR和nDCG@10。

Abstract: Personalization encoders compress evolving interaction histories into preference states used to rank items or condition text generation. A task head operating only on this state can miss useful evidence that remains in the frozen encoder's cached representations for individual timesteps. We study this recoverability gap and propose REPAIR, which compares cached representations with the current preference state in a compact learned coordinate space. It resolves corrective evidence over extended history, recent interactions, and localized bursts. It then selects which patterns at which timesteps contribute and adds their aggregate correction to the state before the task head. Encoder-host repair reuses representations from the existing forward computation without re-encoding the history. Across MovieLens, PENS, MIND, and Amazon Reviews 2023, training only REPAIR improves MRR and nDCG@10 for all twelve representative recommendation hosts while both encoder and task head remain frozen. Head-only finetuning of the same hosts yields smaller gains. For example, Mamba4Rec on MovieLens gains 3.96 MRR points, compared with 0.19 from head-only finetuning. Rank and temporal diagnostics support a compact, host-dependent corrective structure. In personalized generation, IMPerSumm improves the two reported weighted PerSEval variants, which assess responsiveness to user preference, by up to 25.23%. These results support post-compression state correction and distinguish the availability of preference evidence from its downstream use.

</details>


### [70] [Adapter Thickets: Splitting an RLVR Budget Beats Concentrating It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00991)
*Jonathan Williams,Esin Tureci Karthik R. Narasimhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示RLVR后训练与多数投票直接组合的缺陷：单策略被锐化后错误高度相关，多数投票反而可能低于未训练基座模型（最多低4.8个百分点），且训练中投票精度先升后降（最多降7.0个百分点）。作者提出adapter thicket，将同等数据和训练预算拆给K个独立LoRA适配器，在16个(model, K)设定中全部优于完整训练适配器，K>=4时接近或超过基座模型，并随投票数增加优势扩大。对推荐/搜索系统中的多专家路由、候选生成与测试时扩展具有参考价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RLVR后训练流程训练单一策略后再进行多次采样投票，会因策略锐化导致采样错误高度相关，从而损害多数投票的测试时扩展效果；核心问题是如何在固定RLVR数据与训练预算下，同时保留单样本精度提升并避免多数投票性能退化。

Method: 该工作以RLVR后训练与测试时多数投票的组合为对象。输入为问题集，每个问题恰好抽取160个补全；标准流程先用完整RLVR数据与训练预算训练一个策略（以LoRA适配器形式附加到1.5B-8B基座模型），再对每个问题采样多个补全并做多数投票。作者发现该组合有损：RLVR锐化策略，使采样结果越来越倾向于犯相同的错误，投票只能纠正投票者不共享的错误，因此错误相关性随训练上升，多数投票精度早期达峰后下降。为缓解集中训练，论文提出adapter thicket：将同一RLVR数据和训练预算拆分为K份互不相交的随机分片，分别训练K个LoRA适配器；推理时对每个问题用各适配器采样补全并汇合投票。训练目标仍为可验证奖励的强化学习，未引入新的损失函数；关键设计是数据分片与适配器数量K。实验在1.5B-8B模型上比较完整训练适配器、thicket、早停单适配器和未训练基座模型，并研究K值与投票数16到160的影响。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "单样本准确率", "value": "训练单个LoRA适配器在全额RLVR预算下，在所有测试模型上均提升1.5B-8B模型的单样本准确率", "setting": "1.5B-8B模型，每个问题恰好160个补全，全额RLVR预算训练单个LoRA适配器"}, {"metric": "多数投票准确率", "value": "四个模型中有三个，完整训练适配器的多数投票低于未训练基座模型，最多低4.8个百分点", "setting": "1.5B-8B模型，每个问题160个补全，多数投票"}, {"metric": "多数投票准确率下降幅度", "value": "多数投票精度峰值后下降最多7.0个百分点", "setting": "训练过程中记录多数投票精度，每个问题160个补全"}, {"metric": "多数投票准确率（thicket）", "value": "在全部16个(model, K)设定中，thicket投票胜过完整训练适配器；K>=4时保持在基座模型0.8个百分点以内或更高", "setting": "16个(model, K)设定，每个问题160个补全，多数投票"}, {"metric": "多数投票准确率（早停控制）", "value": "单个适配器在thicket成员的步数处停止训练，是小K时强控制，与thicket匹配；K>=8时thicket保留更多RLVR单样本增益，并在8个设定中的6个中胜过该控制", "setting": "每个问题160个补全，多数投票，8个K>=8设定"}, {"metric": "多数投票领先幅度", "value": "从16票到160票，thicket相对完整训练适配器的领先幅度从1.3个百分点扩大到3.3个百分点", "setting": "投票数量从16到160，每个问题160个补全"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 指出RLVR与多数投票组合的损失性：单适配器训练提升单样本精度，但可能使多数投票低于未训练基座模型（最高低4.8个百分点）；诊断集中训练是主因：投票者错误相关性随训练上升，多数投票精度早期达到峰值后下降最多7.0个百分点；提出adapter thicket：将相同数据和训练预算拆分为K个LoRA适配器，每个在互不相交的随机数据分片上独立训练，再采样投票；验证thicket在多设定下优于完整训练适配器，并分析K、投票数量与训练步数对效果的影响

Abstract: Majority voting over sampled completions is the workhorse of test-time scaling, and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is the workhorse for making each completion better. The standard pipeline composes the two: train one policy with RLVR, then sample it many times and vote. We show that this composition is lossy. A vote can only overturn mistakes that its voters do not share, and RLVR sharpens a policy so that its samples increasingly make the same mistakes. With every method drawing exactly $160$ completions per problem, training a single LoRA adapter on the full RLVR budget raises single-sample accuracy on every model we test ($1.5$B-$8$B). Yet on three of four models it leaves the majority vote below that of the untrained base model, by up to $4.8$ points. The damage builds during training: voter errors grow steadily more correlated, and the majority vote accuracy peaks early before falling by up to $7.0$ points. The cause is concentration, not RLVR itself. We split the same data and training budget across $K$ LoRA adapters, each trained on its own random disjoint shard, and call the result an adapter thicket. Thickets out-vote the fully trained adapter in all $16$ (model, $K$) settings, and for $K{\geq}4$ they stay within $0.8$ points of the base model or above it. A single adapter stopped early, at a thicket member's step count, is a strong control that matches thickets for small $K$. For $K{\geq}8$, thickets keep more of RLVR's single-sample gain and out-vote this control in six of eight settings. The cost of concentration also grows with the number of votes: from $16$ to $160$ votes, the thicket's lead over the fully trained adapter widens from $1.3$ to $3.3$ points. When the plan is to sample and vote, an RLVR budget is better spent broad than deep.

</details>


### [71] [Permutation-Robust Decision Modeling with Candidate-Independent Block-Causal Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01601)
*Guy Amit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对决策模型在单序列中评分多个候选时的排列敏感问题，提出候选独立块因果注意力：保留上下文与候选内部因果性，阻断跨候选信息流并重置候选位置。在多种LLM骨干上降低排列敏感性并保持决策质量，代码与模型已公开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准因果交叉编码在单序列中为多个候选动作打分时，候选得分依赖序列化顺序而非底层决策问题，导致排列敏感性。

Method: 输入为单个序列中编码的可变大小候选动作集合。标准因果交叉编码允许所有token互相注意，使得候选得分受序列化顺序影响。提出的候选独立块因果注意力保持共享上下文内以及每个候选内部的因果计算：共享上下文token可以因果地关注先前上下文；每个候选内部也进行因果计算；但阻断任何跨候选的信息流，并重置每个候选的位置编码。因此每个候选得分仅取决于共享上下文和自身内容，与候选间排列无关。训练目标为决策质量（未具体说明），在Gemma 3 1B、Qwen3 1.7B、Qwen3 4B上进行实验和消融。代码在项目仓库公开，Qwen3-4B模型工件在Hugging Face公开。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "排列敏感性", "value": "持续降低", "setting": "与标准因果注意力和互补不变基线对比，在Gemma 3 1B、Qwen3 1.7B、Qwen3 4B骨干上"}, {"metric": "决策质量", "value": "保持竞争力", "setting": "同上"}, {"metric": "更大训练数据下的行为", "value": "在Qwen3-4B上使用显著更多训练数据进行了进一步研究", "setting": "Qwen3-4B study"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出候选独立块因果注意力（candidate-independent block-causal attention），在共享上下文中保留因果计算，同时阻断候选之间的信息流并重置候选位置；通过消融表明候选隔离是降低排列敏感性的主要来源，位置重置完成对称性

Abstract: Decision models often score a variable-sized set of candidate actions encoded in a single sequence. This setting is increasingly relevant for System 1 components inside generative systems, where candidates may be proposed or ordered differently across runs. Standard causal cross-encoding is expressive, but it can make a candidate's score depend on serialization order rather than on the underlying decision problem. We introduce candidate-independent block-causal attention, which preserves causal computation within the shared context and each candidate while blocking cross-candidate information flow and resetting candidate positions. We compare this architecture with standard causal attention and complementary invariant baselines across Gemma 3 1B, Qwen3 1.7B, and Qwen3 4B backbones. Candidate-independent attention consistently reduces permutation sensitivity while retaining competitive decision quality; ablations indicate that candidate isolation is the primary source of the effect, with position resetting completing the intended symmetry. A larger Qwen3-4B study further examines the behavior of the proposed architecture with substantially more training data. Code is available at the \href{ this https URL }{\textcolor{blue}{project repository}}, and the \href{ this https URL }{\textcolor{blue}{Qwen3-4B model artifact}} is available on Hugging Face.

</details>


### [72] [FedFit: Federated Fine-Tuning of LLMs via Vector-Bank Parameterization and Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01537)
*Hang Zou,Chao Zhang,Yuzhi Yang,Yu Tian,Samson Lasaulce,Mérouane Debbah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedFit提出用不相交共享向量库参数化与交替优化解决联邦LLM微调中的通信开销和LoRA聚合困境，配合块状量化与误差反馈，在Qwen2.5上实现最高100倍压缩且保持困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中LLM微调的通信开销巨大，且LoRA在联邦场景下存在Sum-of-Products（SoP）精确聚合与Product-of-Sums（PoS）通信高效之间的“聚合困境”。

Method: FedFit面向联邦LLM微调场景，客户端在本地数据上更新LoRA适配器。为避免传输完整高维适配器矩阵，算法引入两个不相交的全局共享向量库，每个适配器矩阵A或B由对应向量库中的向量组合重建，从而显著减少需要通信的参数数量。在聚合阶段，采用交替优化调度：先进行解耦的单库更新阶段，此时各客户端只更新一个向量库，服务器可以准确聚合（对应SoP）；然后进行联合更新阶段，同时更新两个库，但聚合时使用残差谱聚合机制对由PoS引入的误差进行修正，缓解聚合困境。此外，每个客户端对要传输的向量执行块状量化并利用误差反馈补偿量化误差，进一步压缩通信。训练目标为最小化微调损失（如语言建模损失），理论分析给出了算法的收敛保证。推理时，客户端根据全局向量库重建LoRA适配器并合并到基础模型中，无需额外服务器推理组件。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "压缩比", "value": "最高提升至100倍", "setting": "在Qwen2.5模型上的大量实验，与标准联邦LoRA方法对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 不相交共享向量库参数化：用两个紧凑且不相交的全局向量库重建高维适配器矩阵，大幅降低通信量；交替优化调度：在解耦单库更新（支持精确聚合）和由残差谱聚合机制修正的联合更新之间循环，解决SoP与PoS冲突；块状量化与客户端误差反馈，进一步压缩传输向量；为整个算法建立理论收敛保证

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables privacy-preserving fine-tuning of Large Language Models (LLMs), yet the massive communication overhead remains a critical bottleneck. Furthermore, applying Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) in FL faces a fundamental "aggregation dilemma" between the accurate Sum-of-Products (SoP) and the communication-efficient Product-of-Sums (PoS) implementations. To tackle these challenges, we propose FedFit. First, to significantly reduce communication overhead, we introduce a disjoint shared vector-bank parameterization that reconstructs high-dimensional adapter matrices from two compact and disjoint global vector banks. Second, to address the aggregation dilemma, we devise an alternating optimization schedule. By cycling between decoupled single-bank updates (which allow for accurate aggregation) and joint updates corrected by a Residual Spectral Aggregation mechanism, we resolve the conflict between SoP and PoS. Additionally, we integrate blockwise quantization with client-side error feedback to further compress the transmitted vectors. Furthermore, we establish theoretical convergence guarantees for the proposed algorithm. Extensive experiments on Qwen2.5 models demonstrate that FedFit achieves perplexity performance comparable to standard federated LoRA methods, while providing compression ratios up to 100x higher.

</details>


### [73] [Asynchronous LLM Post-Training: Group-Mass Capping and Convergence Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01896)
*Qijia He,Ruinan Jin,Jun Luo,Shaofeng Zou,Yingbin Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究异步LLM后训练中陈旧rollout的收敛性问题，提出GMC-GRPO方法，通过组质量封顶最小化偏差界，并证明比TIC-GRPO有更好的延迟依赖，在Qwen3模型上展现出对陈旧rollout的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步强化学习用于LLM后训练时，陈旧rollout（由早期策略生成）会引入偏差，影响收敛性和稳定性；现有理论对陈旧度如何影响收敛以及如何缓解缺乏理解。

Method: GMC-GRPO是一种用于异步LLM后训练的强化学习算法。其输入为异步生成的rollout轨迹，这些轨迹可能由较旧策略产生，带有陈旧性。核心流程：1）对轨迹级重要性加权估计器，分析其梯度估计的二阶矩与偏差；2）在共享共同二阶矩保证的加权估计器类中，通过最小化基于比率的偏差界，引入组质量封顶（group mass capping）机制，对组级别的权重进行重缩放或封顶以控制偏差；3）使用该加权估计器进行GRPO风格的策略梯度更新。训练目标为最大化期望回报，同时显式控制陈旧性带来的偏差。推理/服务流程未在摘要中详述，但训练时异步收集rollout，可能使用多步延迟。收敛性分析给出了步长、组大小、比率阈值等超参数与收敛速率的理论关系。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "性能（最佳稳定基线比较）", "value": "在大rollout延迟下取得最优性能", "setting": "Qwen3模型和推理基准上的实验，与稳定基线对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 推导了GRPO风格异步算法的收敛界，显式刻画了梯度估计器二阶矩与偏差之间的权衡；对于轨迹级重要性加权估计器，一旦二阶矩被一致控制，延迟通过裁剪或重缩放引入的偏差进入界中。；提出组质量封顶GRPO（GMC-GRPO），在共享共同二阶矩保证的加权估计器类中最小化基于比率的偏差界。；建立了异步GMC-GRPO的收敛保证，证明与TIC-GRPO相比，当ε→0时，四阶延迟项的阈值依赖从O(ε^{-4})改进到O(ε^{-2})，其中1+ε是比率阈值。；在局部策略重叠下，调优步长后，延迟相关项按G^{-2/5}下降，G为组大小；对于固定的行为策略和当前策略，组重缩放引入的偏差随G→∞消失，而轨迹级裁剪的偏差可能持续。

Abstract: Asynchronous reinforcement learning (RL) improves the efficiency of large language model post-training but introduces stale rollouts generated by earlier policies. Theoretical understanding of how this staleness affects convergence and how to mitigate its impact remains limited. We derive a convergence bound for GRPO-style algorithms that explicitly characterizes the tradeoff between the gradient estimator's second moment and bias. For trajectory-level importance-weighted estimators, our analysis shows that once the second moment is uniformly controlled, delay enters the bound through the bias introduced by clipping or rescaling. Guided by this insight, we propose a novel group mass capping GRPO (GMC-GRPO) method, which minimizes a ratio-based bias bound within a class of weighted estimators sharing a common second-moment guarantee. We establish convergence guarantees for asynchronous GMC-GRPO and show that, compared with TIC-GRPO, it improves the threshold dependence of the fourth-order delay term from $O(\epsilon^{-4})$ to $O(\epsilon^{-2})$ as $\epsilon\to0$, where $1+\epsilon$ is the ratio threshold. Under local policy overlap, the delay-dependent term decreases as $G^{-2/5}$ after tuning the step size, where $G$ is the group size. For fixed behavior and current policies, the bias introduced by group rescaling also vanishes as $G\to\infty$, whereas the bias from trajectory-wise clipping can persist. Experiments across Qwen3 models and reasoning benchmarks demonstrate improved robustness to stale rollouts, with GMC-GRPO achieving the best performance among stable baselines under large rollout delays.

</details>


### [74] [Selection-Based Structured Reasoning: Toward Efficient Multimodal Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01892)
*Feiyu Gavin Zhu,Xiaoyu Zhu,Jiqi Yang,Rui Yang,Arnab Kumar Mondal,Yancheng Wang,Xinke Deng,Jean Oh,Reid Simmons,Joerg Liebelt,Xiang Kong,Zhongyu Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SSR 将多模态搜索智能体的推理从自由生成改为从预定义自然语言推理候选中选择，无需辅助头，并用共享 KV cache 的 teacher-forced prefilling 并行评分。在七个多模态搜索基准上，2B/4B 模型每轮推理延迟降低超过 90%，总推理延迟降低 28-54%，成功率与同规模领先搜索智能体相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小模型多模态智能体自由形式推理冗长、对动作指导有限且推理成本高；需要一种更高效、可并行评分的结构化推理方式。

Method: SSR 将多模态搜索智能体每一轮的动作前推理建模为选择问题。输入包括当前多模态上下文和历史交互；系统预先指定一组可复用的自然语言高层推理候选。每轮中，模型不生成自由文本推理，而是基于当前上下文对每个候选计算似然，选择最可能的推理候选作为该轮的结构化推理。该选择过程不需要额外的辅助任务头，直接复用语言模型的下一个 token 概率。为实现高效评分，SSR 采用 teacher-forced prefilling：在共享的上下文 KV cache 基础上，对候选及其内部 token 进行并行评分，从而在候选内部与候选之间并发计算 token 似然。选中的推理随后指导动作生成，形成“结构化推理选择—动作生成”的循环。训练方面，论文在多种强化学习目标和监督微调下评估 SSR，表明该框架可兼容不同优化目标。推理时，由于推理被限制在有限候选集合内，且可通过并行预填充评分，避免了长自由推理的逐 token 生成，显著降低每轮推理延迟和每问题总推理延迟，同时保持与同规模搜索智能体相当的成功率。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "每轮推理延迟", "value": "降低超过 90%", "setting": "在七个多模态搜索基准上，使用 2B 和 4B 模型，多种 RL 目标与 SFT 下"}, {"metric": "每问题总模型推理延迟", "value": "降低 28-54%", "setting": "在七个多模态搜索基准上，使用 2B 和 4B 模型，多种 RL 目标与 SFT 下"}, {"metric": "平均成功率", "value": "与同规模领先搜索智能体相当", "setting": "在七个多模态搜索基准上，2B 和 4B 模型"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将推理重构为选择而非开放式生成，使用预定义、可复用的自然语言高层推理候选。；每轮根据当前上下文从候选推理中选择，无需辅助任务头。；利用 teacher-forced prefilling 和共享上下文 KV cache 对候选进行并行 token 似然评分，实现跨候选与候选内并行计算。

Abstract: Multimodal agents commonly generate free-form reasoning before each action. For small models, limited model capacity can result in lengthy reasoning that provides little useful guidance for action generation while incurring substantial inference cost. To address this challenge, we introduce Selection-based Structured Reasoning (SSR), a framework that reformulates reasoning as selection instead of open-ended generation. SSR represents recurring high-level reasoning as pre-specified, reusable natural-language candidates. At each turn, the model selects from these reasoning candidates based on their likelihoods given the current context, without requiring an auxiliary task head. Using pre-specified reasoning traces enables parallel scoring, where teacher-forced prefilling computes token likelihoods concurrently within and across candidates using a shared context KV cache. We evaluate SSR on seven multimodal search benchmarks using 2B and 4B models. Across multiple reinforcement learning objectives and supervised fine-tuning, SSR delivers significant efficiency gains without sacrificing task performance. SSR achieves an average success rate competitive with leading search agents of the same scale, while reducing per-turn reasoning latency by over 90% and total per-question model inference latency by 28-54%. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [75] [Pooling Helps, Learned Weighting Hurts In-Context: Decomposing Group Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01831)
*Michael Fore,James Mason Inder,Mrishika Nair,Praneetha Vaddamanu,Sharlina Keshava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文分解Chronos-2中的分组注意力为V/O均匀池化与Q/K学习加权，通过推理时编辑注意力矩阵发现均匀池化在多变量与ICL预测中普遍有益，而学习加权在ICL中常常有害，为注意力机制设计提供了新见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究分组注意力机制中跨变量注意力（Q/K学习加权与V/O均匀池化）分别在多变量预测与上下文学习中的作用，并确定哪种路径带来收益。

Method: 论文以时间序列预测模型Chronos-2中的分组注意力机制为研究对象。该机制在每个固定补丁索引处对一组变量进行注意力计算，同时支持多变量预测和上下文学习预测。每个注意力头被分解为两个功能路径：V/O路径，将组内变量投影为加权摘要；Q/K路径，根据查询和键决定权重。作者通过在推理时编辑注意力矩阵α来分离这两条路径：将α设为均匀分布相当于仅保留V/O池化，强制Q/K权重均匀。通过比较均匀池化与学习加权的效果，在20个传感器网络配置上评估多变量预测和ICL预测的表现，并通过逐层隔离发现仅在第一块中均匀化α即可提升所有ICL配置。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 在推理时通过编辑注意力矩阵α，将头部内的V/O池化路径与Q/K加权路径分离进行分析；发现均匀池化（无Q/K加权）在20个传感器网络配置中的18个上带来正面效果；发现学习加权（Q/K）对多变量预测有益或可忽略，但在10个传感器网络ICL配置中的8个上显著有害，导致4个配置比单变量推理更差；发现仅在第一块中将α均匀化即可改善所有测试的ICL配置

Abstract: Group attention, introduced by the time series forecasting model Chronos-2, attends over the variates of a group at a fixed patch index and serves both multivariate (MV) and in-context learning (ICL) forecasting. Rather than evaluating this cross-variate attention design as a whole, we ask which part of the mechanism earns the benefit and probe its applicability to both MV and ICL regimes. By editing the attention matrix $\alpha$ at inference we separate the two pathways a head comprises: V/O, which projects a weighted summary of the group, and Q/K, which decides the weights. Uniform pooling (V/O without any Q/K weighting) is positive on 18 of our 20 sensor-network configurations, while the learned weighting (Q/K) splits by group type: its contribution is positive or negligible for MV, but materially degrades 8 of the 10 sensor-network ICL configurations, leaving 4 of them worse than univariate inference. By isolating the impact of different layers, we find that uniforming $\alpha$ in the first block alone improves every ICL configuration we test.

</details>


### [76] [TACO: Ternary Absolute-max Column-wise One-sparse Optimizer for LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02199)
*Jichao Jiang,Cristian McGee,El Houcine Bergou,Hanqin Cai,Aritra Dutta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TACO 提出一种三值绝对最大列一稀疏优化器，通过每列仅保留最大幅值元素的符号，在维度归一化 1→1 算子范数下实现精确最速下降，将优化器状态内存较 AdamW8bit 减少 174×，峰值训练内存减少 2.9×，并在单张 80GB H100 上支持 30-32B 模型的全参数微调，精度与运行时间持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全参数微调大语言模型时，优化器状态内存开销巨大，限制了单卡可训练模型规模；现有方法或压缩状态、或改变更新几何导致性能下降。

Method: TACO 是面向 LLM 全参数微调的优化器。输入为模型参数梯度（二维权重矩阵）。对于每个二维权重矩阵的每一列，TACO 计算该列中绝对值最大元素的符号，仅保留该符号作为更新方向，其余元素置零，从而每列仅有一个非零三值（-1/0/1）更新。该更新是在维度归一化的 1→1 算子范数下的精确最速下降方向，保留了梯度的一阶信息。优化器状态方面，TACO 仅维护每列的一小部分低精度梯度分量，持久状态远小于 AdamW8bit 的密集一阶/二阶矩。训练流程：前向/反向传播得到梯度后，按列提取最大幅值符号并更新参数，无需存储全量优化器状态。在 OPT-13B 等模型上，持久优化器状态从 AdamW8bit 的 27.7 GB 降至 0.16 GB，峰值训练内存从 80.6 GB 降至 27.5 GB，准确率与运行时间相当。该设计使单张 80GB H100 能够全参数微调 30-32B 参数模型。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "持久优化器状态内存", "value": "27.7 GB → 0.16 GB（减少 174×）", "setting": "OPT-13B 全参数微调，对比 AdamW8bit"}, {"metric": "峰值训练内存", "value": "80.6 GB → 27.5 GB（减少 2.9×）", "setting": "OPT-13B 全参数微调，对比 AdamW8bit"}, {"metric": "可微调模型规模", "value": "30-32B 参数模型可在单张 80GB H100 上全参数微调", "setting": "多个模型家族与任务"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: TACO 优化器：在维度归一化的 1→1 算子范数下，每列仅保留最大幅值元素的符号，实现精确最速下降方向，同时保留一阶梯度信息。；三值/列一稀疏优化器状态表示：每列仅存储少量低精度梯度分量，将持久优化器状态降至极低。；理论：在维度归一化的 1→1 算子范数下推导精确最速下降更新，推广了 Muon 的算子范数视角。；工程实现：支持单张 80GB H100 全参数微调 30-32B 模型，保持与 AdamW8bit 相当的准确率和运行时间。

Abstract: Full-parameter fine-tuning of large language models (LLMs) incurs substantial optimizer state memory overhead, limiting the model sizes that fit on modern GPUs. Existing approaches either compress optimizer state, abandon first-order gradients, or change the update geometry while retaining dense state. The recently introduced Muon optimizer reduces optimizer memory through matrix-valued updates. Still, its geometry differs from AdamW and can lead to performance degradation when fine-tuning AdamW-pretrained models. To reduce optimizer memory without sacrificing accuracy or computational efficiency in LLM fine-tuning, we propose Ternary Absolute-max Column-wise One-sparse optimizer, or TACO, which follows Muon's operator-norm steepest-descent view but takes the geometric route further. TACO computes the exact steepest-descent direction under a dimension-normalized $1\to1$ operator norm by selecting the sign of the largest magnitude entry in each column of two-dimensional weight matrices. This retains first-order gradients while making optimizer state memory nearly negligible. Our practical TACO optimizer maintains only a small set of low precision gradient components per column, reducing persistent optimizer state by $174\times$ relative to AdamW8bit (from 27.7 GB to 0.16 GB) and peak training memory by $2.9\times$ (from 80.6 GB to 27.5 GB) on OPT-13B, while achieving comparable accuracy and runtime. TACO further enables full-parameter fine-tuning of 30-32B-parameter models on a single 80 GB H100 GPU across multiple model families and tasks.

</details>


### [77] [Decoding Looped Transformers Better for (Almost) Free](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02185)
*Weihao Liu,Huangjie Zheng,Tianrong Chen,Rohit Dilip,Richard He Bai,Yizhu Jiao,Yuyang Wang,Ruixiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoopCD 是一种训练无关的对比解码方法，利用循环式 Transformer 早期循环的弱预测与最终循环的强预测构造弱-强对，在 logit 或隐藏状态空间对比。在四个循环模型家族上稳定提升 AIME 2024、HumanEval 等指标，并可在循环深度减半时匹配全深度基线，降低前向 FLOPs 22.5%–48.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环式 Transformer 在每个 recurrent loop 都产生可解码为同一 next token 的中间表示，但标准解码只使用最终循环的预测，丢弃了早期循环状态，导致计算冗余且解码质量未充分利用递归结构。

Method: 方法作用于循环式 Transformer：模型由共享块重复执行 T 次 recurrent loop 构成，每个循环产生一个隐藏表示并可直接解码为下一个 token。标准解码只取最后循环的输出分布。LoopCD 训练无关，将早期循环视为计算量较少的弱预测器、最终循环视为强预测器，二者天然对齐于同一目标 token 形成弱-强预测对。LoopCD-Logits 在 logit 空间对比最终预测与某个较早循环的预测分布来调整 token 选择，需要额外一次输出前向；LoopCD-Hidden 直接在隐藏状态空间对比，不增加输出前向开销。推理时按循环顺序执行共享块，取最终循环与选定早期循环的预测/隐藏表示，经对比准则得到最终 token，并可在减少循环次数时用该对比信号补偿深度下降带来的质量损失。论文未披露训练目标、数据或更细的模块交互细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "AIME 2024 pass@1", "value": "61.88% -> 73.33%", "setting": "Ouro-2.6B-Thinking，全递归深度，LoopCD-Logits"}, {"metric": "HumanEval pass@1", "value": "22.56% -> 31.71%", "setting": "Huginn，全递归深度，LoopCD-Hidden"}, {"metric": "forward FLOPs 降低", "value": "22.5% - 48.2%", "setting": "循环深度减半后仍匹配或超过全深度未引导基线"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出无需训练的对比解码框架 LoopCD，利用同一模型早期循环作为弱预测、最终循环作为强预测，自动构造对齐的弱-强预测对。；LoopCD-Logits 在 logit 空间对比最终预测与早期循环预测，仅需额外一次输出前向。；LoopCD-Hidden 在隐藏状态空间进行对比，零输出开销。；证明可在减半循环深度下仍匹配或超过全深度未引导基线，从而降低前向 FLOPs。

Abstract: Looped Transformers achieve parameter efficiency by repeatedly executing a shared block across recurrent loops. Each loop yields an intermediate representation decodable for the same next token, yet standard decoding discards earlier states. Because earlier loops embody less computation, recurrence inherently supplies aligned weak-and-strong prediction pairs without auxiliary models or external training. We introduce LoopCD, a training-free contrastive decoding framework that guides token selection by contrasting the final prediction with an earlier recurrent pass, operating either in logit space with one extra output pass (LoopCD-Logits) or in hidden-state space with zero output overhead (LoopCD-Hidden). Across four looped Transformer families, LoopCD delivers substantial, consistent gains at full recurrent depth: LoopCD-Logits raises Ouro-2.6B-Thinking's AIME 2024 pass@1 from 61.88% to 73.33%, while LoopCD-Hidden lifts Huginn's HumanEval pass@1 from 22.56% to 31.71%. Crucially, these performance gains enable halving the number of recurrent loops while still matching or exceeding full-depth unguided baselines, reducing forward FLOPs by 22.5% to 48.2%. By transforming intermediate recurrent states into effective guidance signals, LoopCD achieves superior decoding quality while substantially reducing inference compute.

</details>


### [78] [SoftServe: A Scalable Quasi-Newton Method for Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02182)
*Joohwan Ko,Tetiana Parshakova,Diana Cai,Robert M. Gower*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SoftServe是一种可扩展的准牛顿优化方法，通过正定曲率估计和Kronecker分解，结合Newton-Schulz迭代，在136M参数模型上取得优于Adam等基线的损失，适用于深度学习训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准牛顿方法在深度学习中受限于非凸性和巨大参数量，难以应用。

Method: SoftServe是一种准牛顿方法家族，旨在克服深度学习中的非凸性和大参数量挑战。它从Berglund等人(2025)的变分目标推导正定曲率估计，即使存在负曲率。开发了对角线和Kronecker分解变体，通过构造保持正定性，并利用稳定的耦合Newton-Schulz迭代进行矩阵运算，避免昂贵的矩阵分解，采用GPU友好的矩阵乘法。该方法无需线搜索或临时曲率修正，可直接应用于大规模神经网络训练。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "损失值", "value": "通常低于基线", "setting": "在病态问题上，包括循环网络、深度自编码器、物理信息神经网络和136M参数物理信息扩散模型，与Adam、Muon、SOAP对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 从变分目标推导正定曲率估计，即使存在负曲率；对角和Kronecker分解变体，构造上保持正定且可扩展至大规模神经网络；使用稳定的耦合Newton-Schulz迭代替代昂贵的矩阵分解，实现GPU友好的矩阵乘法

Abstract: Quasi-Newton (QN) methods have long been among the most effective methods for large-scale unconstrained convex optimization. Two obstacles have limited their use in deep learning: non-convexity and enormous parameter sizes. We introduce SoftServe, a family of QN methods designed to overcome these obstacles without line searches or ad hoc curvature corrections. SoftServe derives positivedefinite curvature estimates from the variational objective of Berglund et al. (2025), even in the presence of negative curvature. We develop diagonal and Kroneckerfactored variants that preserve positive definiteness by construction and scale to massive neural networks. Finally, SoftServe relies on the stable coupled Newton-Schulz iteration for the required matrix operations, replacing costly matrix decompositions with GPU-friendly matrix multiplications. SoftServe excels on problems that are severely ill-conditioned, including tasks such as recurrent networks, deep autoencoders, physics-informed neural networks, and a 136M-parameter physics-informed diffusion model, often achieving lower losses than established baselines including Adam, Muon, and SOAP.

</details>


### [79] [From Gradients to Capabilities: Understanding Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02179)
*Siqi Zhu,Suozhi Huang,Kaixuan Zhang,Yuheng Yang,Zhanyang Jin,Yihang Sun,Jiaxuan You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了多教师在线策略蒸馏中教师信号对参数更新的影响，发现损失平均、Adam一阶矩和BF16舍入等因素显著影响蒸馏效果，并给出量化实验证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多教师在线策略蒸馏（MOPD）中教师信号如何影响学生模型的参数变化尚不明确。

Method: 论文以Qwen3-1.7B为学生模型，四个领域教师模型从相同初始化出发经RL训练。研究对比了梯度、优化器更新和任务学习曲线，并补充SmolLM3-3B诊断。核心分析聚焦于损失平均方式（token平均、响应平均、全局token平均）对教师信号加权的影响，Adam优化器一阶矩对参数更新差异的平滑作用，BF16与FP32权重更新的差异，以及top-64词表交集KL梯度与全词表梯度的匹配程度及其对数学任务准确率的影响。未涉及在线服务或推荐系统级架构。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 系统分析损失平均方式对教师信号的影响，发现token平均偏向长响应，等化域贡献仍保留域内加权。；揭示Adam一阶矩减少参数更新差异，教师间余弦相似度0.83，平均规则间0.96，尽管原始梯度不同。；发现BF16舍入隐藏微小变化：约97%的FP32主权重与初始化不同，但仅7-11%的BF16权重不同。；证明top-64交集KL梯度与Qwen全词表梯度高度匹配，但对任务表现的影响取决于平均方式：在响应平均下数学准确率比采样token策略梯度高2.6分，在全局token平均下低2.1分。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) aims to combine the strengths of RL-trained teachers in a single student, but how teacher signals affect parameter changes remains underexplored. We study Qwen3-1.7B with four domain teachers trained with RL from the same initialization as the student, comparing gradients, optimizer updates, and task learning curves, with additional SmolLM3-3B diagnostics. We find that several factors influence teacher signals. First, loss averaging implicitly weights responses: token averaging favors longer responses, and equalizing domain contributions retains this weighting within domains. Second, Adam's first moment reduces differences in parameter updates: the cosine similarity is 0.83 between teachers and 0.96 between averaging rules, despite differences in raw gradients. Third, BF16 rounding hides small changes: about 97\% of FP32 master weights differ from initialization, but only 7--11\% of BF16 weights do. Finally, the top-64 intersection KL gradient closely matches Qwen's full-vocabulary gradient, but the effect on task performance depends on averaging: mathematics accuracy is 2.6 points higher than with sampled-token policy-gradient (PG) under response averaging and 2.1 points lower under global token averaging.

</details>


### [80] [Local Support Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02126)
*Assaf Ben-Kish,Akarsh Kumar,James Glass,Raja Giryes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出Local Support Learning (LSL)，一种无需旧数据的持续学习框架，通过标准适配器与基于GMM的门控配合，使更新仅作用于当前训练分布，从而在7B LLM上有效缓解灾难性遗忘，保留预训练与多阶段微调能力，并展现良好的内存/计算效率与扩展潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大模型顺序学习新任务时，避免灾难性遗忘且无需访问旧任务数据，同时保持预训练与已微调能力。

Method: LSL将模型更新分解为两部分：一个标准的权重适配器（如LoRA）以及一个门控函数。适配器按常规训练以最小化当前阶段损失，但门控函数控制适配器是否作用于某个输入激活。门控基于当前阶段训练数据激活分布拟合的高斯混合模型（GMM），计算输入激活在GMM下的似然；当似然高时（当前任务分布内）门控打开，允许适配器修改；当似然低时（旧任务分布外）门控关闭，适配器不生效，从而保留原模型行为。训练仅在当前阶段数据上进行，无需旧数据。推理时，输入经过原模型与门控判断，若门控开启则加入适配器输出，否则使用原模型输出。目标是在学习新能力的同时，由于更新仅局限于当前数据分布的支持集，因此对旧任务性能影响最小。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将灾难性遗忘视为权重矩阵输入空间中的几何问题，揭示梯度更新在保留旧能力方面是次优的。；提出Local Support Learning (LSL)通用框架：标准权重适配器负责学习新任务，GMM门控函数仅在当前任务的激活分布上启用适配器，使更新局部化。；利用高斯混合模型的似然在远离训练数据时快速衰减的特性，设计门控自然在旧任务数据上关闭。

Abstract: We explore catastrophic forgetting in the context of large pre-trained models. By considering forgetting as a geometric problem in the input space of each weight matrix, we uncover a natural retention objective under which updates produced by gradient-based optimizers are suboptimal. Following this observation, we propose Local Support Learning (LSL), a general-purpose framework that augments gradient-based training for retention of prior capabilities without access to prior data. During a new learning phase, LSL pairs two components with distinct roles: a standard weight adapter, trained as usual to minimize the loss, and a gating function that enables the adapter only on input activations from its own training distribution, making the update local to that distribution. The key challenge is that this gate must route data from all learning phases while training only on data from the current one. We address this with a gate based on a Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM), whose likelihood decays rapidly away from its training data, giving it a natural tendency to stay closed on data from prior phases. We show that this post-training approach can resolve forgetting in LLMs of up to 7 billion parameters, retaining both pretrained and finetuned capabilities across multiple training phases, while being efficient in memory and compute, robust to hyperparameter choice, and showing scaling potential.

</details>
