### [81] [Learn the Directions, Normalize the Gains: Post-Training Normalization for LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02067)
*Zailong Tian,Yanzhe Chen,Zhuoheng Han,Houfeng Wang,Lizi Liao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现 LoRA 更新存在“适配失衡”——少数奇异方向主导增益分配，提出无需校准数据与再训练的 LoRA-Norm：谱重平衡加核范数恢复，在保留学习方向的同时重新分配增益；双骨干三任务上平均优于谱剪枝与梯度引导编辑，且无推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA 学习到的更新被少数奇异方向主导，导致适配增益分配不平衡；即使学对了方向，也不能保证适配增益被合理平衡，从而损害目标任务外的能力。

Method: LoRA-Norm 是作用于已训练 LoRA 适配器权重的后处理流程。输入为训练完成的低秩适配矩阵（含其奇异值分解），流程分两步：(1) spectral rebalancing——对适配器更新的奇异值施加固定非线性变换，重新分配各奇异方向的增益，使少数主导方向不再独占更新幅度，但不改变学习到的方向（奇异向量）；(2) nuclear-norm restoration——在重新平衡后恢复原始总谱质量（核范数），避免整体更新幅度被归一化步骤改变。二者结合在保留原始学习方向的条件下调整增益分布。该流程不需要校准数据、不需要再训练，仅在训练后一次性修改权重，因此推理路径与原始 LoRA 完全一致，无额外延迟或显存开销。论文用双骨干、三任务实验验证其在目标特化与通用能力保持上的平均提升。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均目标任务特化性能与通用能力保持", "value": "在两个骨干模型、三个适配任务上平均超越所评估的后处理谱剪枝与梯度引导编辑配置", "setting": "两种骨干模型、三种适配任务的离线实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 识别出 adaptation imbalance：少数奇异方向主导更新，增益分配敏感；提出 LoRA-Norm 后训练归一化方法，包含 spectral rebalancing（奇异值的固定非线性变换）；提出 nuclear-norm restoration，保持原始总谱质量；无需校准数据、无需额外训练、无推理开销；区分“平衡适配器增益”与“均衡响应”两个不同目标

Abstract: While Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) enables efficient task specialization, its learned updates can compromise capabilities beyond the target task. We identify \textbf{adaptation imbalance}: a few singular directions dominate the trained update, leaving its performance sensitive to how gains are allocated. We argue that \textbf{learning where to adapt does not ensure that adaptation gains are well balanced}. This motivates \textbf{LoRA-Norm}, a post-training normalization method that retains learned directions while rebalancing their gains. LoRA-Norm combines spectral rebalancing, a fixed nonlinear transformation of singular values, with nuclear-norm restoration, which preserves the original total spectral mass. It requires no calibration data or additional training and introduces no inference overhead. Across two backbones and three adaptation tasks, LoRA-Norm improves average specialization and capability retention, outperforming the evaluated post-hoc spectral pruning and gradient-guided editing configurations on both measures. Stronger functional equalization brings no consistent additional gains, revealing that balancing adapter gains and equalizing their responses are distinct objectives.

</details>


### [82] [FastCI: Efficient GPU-Intensive CI for LLM Training Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01967)
*Tianshuo Qiao,Naiqian Zheng,Xiaopeng Liu,Shuguang Wang,Diandian Gu,Xuanzhe Liu,Xin Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FastCI 是字节跳动为 LLM 训练框架设计的 GPU 密集型 CI 优化框架，通过运行时证据驱动的测试选择与剪枝、高风险测试优先排序、以及验证范围外的负载优化，在已部署 CI 流水线上实现 CI 延迟降低 77.5%、GPU 资源使用降低 63.9%，同时保持并提升修改代码覆盖率 3.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 训练框架的 CI 依赖 GPU 密集型测试（完整模型训练或评估），随框架快速演进，CI 本身成为开发效率的瓶颈。如何在保证测试覆盖与缺陷暴露能力的前提下，降低 CI 延迟与 GPU 资源占用。

Method: FastCI 是用于 LLM 训练框架 GPU 密集型 CI 的优化框架，其流程包含以下阶段：首先利用运行时证据（runtime evidence）选择受变更影响的测试，并剪枝那些在等价上下文中执行已变更代码的测试，以减少无效的 GPU 计算；随后对高风险测试进行优先级排序，使潜在失败在 CI 早期暴露，缩短反馈时间；最后沿各测试预期验证范围之外的维度优化测试工作负载，在不损害测试目标的前提下压缩执行开销。该框架已集成到字节跳动 LLM 训练框架的 CI 流水线中，与已部署的 CI 流水线相比，在真实 CI 工作负载上实现 CI 延迟降低 77.5%、GPU 资源使用降低 63.9%、修改代码覆盖率保留提升 3.2%。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": [{"metric": "CI 延迟", "value": "降低 77.5%", "setting": "与当前部署的 CI 流水线对比，在字节跳动 LLM 训练框架的 CI 工作负载上评估"}, {"metric": "GPU 资源使用", "value": "降低 63.9%", "setting": "与当前部署的 CI 流水线对比，在字节跳动 LLM 训练框架的 CI 工作负载上评估"}, {"metric": "修改代码覆盖率保留", "value": "提升 3.2%", "setting": "与当前部署的 CI 流水线对比，在字节跳动 LLM 训练框架的 CI 工作负载上评估"}]}

Conclusion: 利用运行时证据选择受影响的测试，并剪枝在等价上下文中执行变更代码的测试；对高风险测试进行优先级排序，使潜在失败更早暴露；沿各测试预期验证范围之外的维度优化测试工作负载

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) keep growing in size and complexity, their training frameworks evolve at a rapid pace as well. Therefore, continuous integration (CI) is critical for maintaining the quality and stability of these frameworks. However, unlike traditional software, CI for LLM training frameworks relies on GPU-intensive tests, which usually involve complete model training or evaluation. This leads CI itself to become a new bottleneck for fast-paced development. In this paper, we introduce FastCI, a framework that improves the efficiency of CI for LLM training frameworks. FastCI leverages runtime evidence to select affected tests and prune tests that execute changed code in equivalent contexts. Then FastCI prioritizes high-risk tests to expose potential failures earlier, and optimizes test workloads along dimensions outside the intended validation scope of each test. Evaluated on the CI workload of our LLM training framework, FastCI reduces the CI latency by 77.5% and the GPU resource usage by 63.9%, while improving the modified code coverage retention by 3.2%, compared with the currently deployed CI pipelines. FastCI has now been integrated into the CI pipelines of our LLM training framework at ByteDance.

</details>


### [83] [Learning to Predict Distributions over Weight Updates for Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01934)
*Azal Ahmad Khan,Keshav Ramji,Tahira Naseem,Ali Anwar,Ramón Fernandez Astudillo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出query-conditioned的分布化Hypernetwork，仅利用输入query生成LoRA权重更新的分布，通过可微蒙特卡洛近似端到端训练，并引入采样权重更新的测试时扩展方式。实验表明分布均值优于确定性超网络，采样权重提升性能且优于token采样基线，权重更新可跨query迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何仅利用LLM的输入query作为自适应信号，在测试时生成参数适配器（LoRA），并进一步建模参数更新的分布以支持测试时扩展。

Method: 论文提出query-conditioned分布化Hypernetwork架构，用于在测试时根据输入query生成LoRA参数适配器。具体而言，超网络以输入query作为条件信号，输出LoRA权重更新的点估计或分布参数；分布化变体通过可微蒙特卡洛近似定义端到端损失，并探索回归与凸组合等多种分布参数化方式。训练目标是最大化生成正确权重更新的似然（或等价损失），从而学习条件分布。推理时，既可使用分布均值作为确定性适配器，也可通过采样多个权重更新获得多个适配模型，实现一种新的测试时扩展形式；采样权重数量增加时性能提升，且优于对应的token采样自适应基线。此外，生成的权重更新可跨query迁移，表明超网络捕获了可复用的自适应结构。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出query-conditioned Hypernetwork用于LoRA估计，仅用输入query生成自适应权重。；提出分布化Hypernetwork，可输出LoRA参数的概率分布而非点估计。；设计可微蒙特卡洛近似的端到端损失函数，并探索回归与凸组合等分布参数化方式。；提出一种新的测试时扩展方式：通过采样权重更新生成多个适配模型，而非仅采样token序列。；发现生成的权重更新可跨query迁移，表明超网络学到了可复用的自适应结构。

Abstract: Hypernetworks have recently shown success in dynamically adapting the parameters of Large Language Models (LLMs) at runtime based on signals such as task descriptions or additional demostrations. Here we ask: how much adaptation signal can be obtained using only the input query to an LLM?. To answer this, we study query-conditioned Hypernetworks for LoRA estimation. Further, we introduce distributional Hypernetworks, able to produce not only point estimates of parameter adaptors, but also a distribution over possible LoRAs. For this we propose a simple end-to-end loss using a differentiable Monte Carlo approximation and explore multiple distribution parametrizations including regression and convex combination variants. Results show that even using the mean of the learned distribution can outperform deterministic hypernetworks. Crucially, the learned distribution enables a different form of test-time scaling: instead of spending additional compute only by sampling more token sequences from a fixed model, we sample weight updates, yielding multiple adapted models for the same query. Performance improves as more weight samples are considered and remains stronger than corresponding token-sampling adaptation baselines. Finally, we find that generated updates can transfer across queries, suggesting that the hypernetwork learns reusable structure in how the model should adapt. Together, these results show that query-conditioned distributions over weight updates can support both adaptation and test-time scaling.

</details>


### [84] [Same Reward, Different Skills: When Multimodal RL Learns to Look](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01908)
*Haocun Ye,Xinlong Jiang,Qile Chen,Bingyu Wang,Teng Zhang,Shubai Chen,Tingyu Wu,Zhenkun Zheng,Yiqiang Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究多模态RLVR中视觉信号是否真正被学习，提出视觉可解性规则，并在7B模型上将目标发现准确率从0.425提升至0.875，通过多项控制实验定位增益来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在RLVR训练中，图像虽然出现在提示中，却不一定构成有效的学习信号；论文旨在解决多模态强化学习是否真正学习“看”的问题，并提出视觉可解性作为奖励设计规则。

Method: 论文基于视觉语言模型进行强化学习后训练，采用GRPO算法，奖励函数包含正确性和格式奖励。训练数据为反事实坐标场景，问题固定且目标不被命名，模型必须从图像中定位目标才能正确回答。论文通过控制实验分析学习信号来源：将测试图像替换为灰色画布、用灰色画布训练、将学习技能迁移到独立构建的grounding任务、以及在相同图像上添加可回答训练问题的字幕。这些控制用于证明视觉可解性规则的必要性。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "目标发现准确率", "value": "0.425 → 0.875", "setting": "7B模型，标准GRPO与正确性/格式奖励，在比训练场景更密集的held-out反事实坐标场景上"}, {"metric": "真实图像增益恢复比例", "value": "约1/2（3B），约4/5（7B）", "setting": "盲训练模型在测试时提供图像，与真实图像训练对比"}, {"metric": "测试图像替换为灰色画布后的发现率", "value": "0", "setting": "7B模型，标准GRPO训练后，测试时图像替换为灰色画布"}, {"metric": "灰色画布训练在测试时使用真实图像的增益", "value": "几乎没有", "setting": "匹配step 30且四种随机种子，训练时使用灰色画布"}, {"metric": "添加可回答训练问题的字幕后的增益削减", "value": "近三分之二", "setting": "相同图像、奖励和预算下，加入字幕"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出视觉可解性设计规则：视觉证据必须对正确答案必要，且任务仍可学习。；构建反事实坐标场景：问题固定、目标不被命名，正确回答必须从图像中定位目标。；发现并量化盲训练模型在真实图像上可恢复部分增益，以及延长真实图像训练可能侵蚀grounding而基准分数仍上升的现象。；通过灰色画布替换测试图像、灰色画布训练对照、独立grounding任务迁移、以及添加字幕对照，系统定位增益来源。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) improves vision-language benchmark scores even without visual information during training. With images at test, blind-trained models recover roughly half of the real-image gain at 3B and nearly four fifths at 7B. Prolonged real-image training can erode grounding while benchmark gains persist. Both findings expose the same gap: an image in the prompt is not an image in the learning signal. Our design rule, visual resolvability, asks that visual evidence be necessary for a correct answer and that the task remain learnable. We test it on counterfactual coordinate scenes in which the question stays fixed and the target is never named, so a correct answer requires finding the target in the image. With standard GRPO and correctness-and-format rewards, a 7B model raises its accuracy at finding the target (discovery) from 0.425 to 0.875 on held-out scenes denser than any it trained on, and it improves on question types it never trained on. Two controls locate the source of the gain. Replacing test images with gray canvases drops discovery to zero; training on gray canvases instead, at matched step 30 and in each of four seeds, yields essentially none of the gain even when the model is then tested with real images. The learned skill carries over to grounding tasks built independently of the training corpus. A caption that answers the training question, added to the same images, reward and budget, cuts the gain by nearly two thirds. Changing what reward requires changes what RL learns.

</details>


### [85] [Evidence-Gated Research: Statistically Controlled Model Adoption in Adaptive Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01751)
*Yifan Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Evidence-Gated Research 统计采纳层，在路径依赖的自适应模型搜索中冻结挑战者、构建 anytime-valid 证据并组合持久 e-value，经在线控制器决策以控制错误采纳 FDR；在 5,000 条轨迹闭环基准中 development-only e-LOND 的持久 FDR 为 0.621，而审计的 EGR 变体未观察到持久误采纳路径，Stagewise EGR 在 600 对回放中以 56.1% 更少决策证据保持固定 anytime 决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在路径依赖的自适应模型搜索中，如何对模型替换这一控制点做统计控制，避免无证据支持的替换污染后续未提出的假设，同时保持 anytime-valid 证据的效率。

Method: EGR 是一个统计采纳层，作用于移动现任者（moving-incumbent）的自适应模型搜索流程。输入是每个搜索轮次中产生的挑战者模型及其在预声明环境集合上的决策证据。主要模块按处理顺序为：1）冻结模块：在决策证据揭示之前先固定挑战者，阻断后续证据对候选生成假设的污染；2） anytime-valid 证据构建模块：在预声明环境上按时间顺序构建始终有效的证据序列；3）证据路由模块：以可预测的方式将证据导向尚未解决的组件，避免证据被任意复用；4）e-value 组合模块：维护一个持久的候选 e-value，累积跨轮次的证据强度；5）在线控制器：接收该 e-value，据此决定是否采纳挑战者。训练/优化目标不是模型参数训练，而是对声明全环境采纳目标的错误发现率（FDR）控制；在条件有效性与可预测性条件下，即使早期采纳会改变后续挑战者，该过程仍控制 FDR。推理/服务路径即在线搜索循环：每轮生成挑战者、冻结、构建证据、路由、组合 e-value、控制器决策，采纳后更新现任者并继续生成后续候选。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "persistent FDR", "value": "0.621 (development-only e-LOND)", "setting": "5,000-trajectory closed-loop benchmark；审计的 EGR 变体在该有限运行中未观察到持久误采纳路径"}, {"metric": "decision evidence usage reduction", "value": "56.1%", "setting": "matched replay over 600 challenger--incumbent pairs, Stagewise EGR, representative threshold，保持 fixed-anytime alternative crossing decisions"}, {"metric": "evidence-efficiency pattern reproduction", "value": "qualitative reproduction", "setting": "three-environment public-data study and 40,000-sample controlled neural benchmark"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Evidence-Gated Research (EGR) 统计采纳层，冻结挑战者后再揭示决策证据；构建跨预声明环境的 anytime-valid 证据，并按可预测方式路由到未解决组件；组合持久候选 e-value 并传递给在线控制器；在条件有效性/可预测性条件下控制声明全环境采纳目标的 FDR；Stagewise EGR 在保持固定 anytime 替代穿越决策的同时减少决策证据使用

Abstract: Adaptive model search is path dependent: once a challenger is adopted, it becomes the reference from which later candidates are generated. A statistically unsupported replacement can therefore alter hypotheses that have not yet been proposed. We introduce Evidence-Gated Research (EGR), a statistical adoption layer for moving-incumbent search. EGR freezes each challenger before decision evidence is revealed, builds anytime-valid evidence across a predeclared set of environments, routes evidence predictably toward unresolved components, composes a persistent candidate e-value, and passes that e-value to an online controller. Under explicit conditional-validity and predictability conditions, the resulting procedure controls false discovery rate for the declared all-environment adoption target even though earlier adoptions change later challengers. In a 5,000-trajectory closed-loop benchmark, development-only e-LOND attains persistent FDR 0.621, whereas no persistent false-adoption path is observed for the audited EGR variants in that finite run. In matched replay over 600 challenger--incumbent pairs, Stagewise EGR preserves fixed-anytime alternative crossing decisions while using 56.1% less decision evidence at the representative threshold. A three-environment public-data study and a 40,000-sample controlled neural benchmark reproduce the evidence-efficiency pattern. These results identify model replacement as a distinct statistical control point in adaptive model development.

</details>


### [86] [Invent a Dataset: Measuring dataset generation abilities with zero seed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01674)
*Shivalika Singh,Andrija Djurisic,Gbemileke Onilude,Sudip Roy,Sara Hooker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该技术报告提出 Invent-A-Dataset，一个零数据设定下从数据集描述生成大规模后训练数据集的提示系统。与五个前沿模型 API 相比，在八类任务、最大 20K 样本上质量相对提升 17%、多样性相对提升 19%，多样性优势随规模扩大而增大（20K 样本时相对提升 37%），并带来下游后训练模型性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在没有任何种子数据的零数据场景下，如何从数据集描述自动生成大规模、高质量、高多样性且能有效提升下游后训练模型性能的数据集。

Method: Invent-A-Dataset 是一个基于提示的系统，输入为数据集描述，输出为大规模、真实且可用于后训练的数据集。系统首先解析数据集描述，据此生成用于指导数据合成的提示；随后调用生成模型（论文中与 Anthropic、Google、OpenAI、DeepSeek、Zai 五个前沿模型 API 进行对比）生成候选样本。生成的数据集在八种任务类型上进行评估，数据集规模从 200 样本扩展到 20K 样本，以考察质量和多样性随规模的变化。评估指标包括生成质量与样本多样性，并进一步通过下游后训练实验验证：使用生成数据对各后训练模型架构进行微调，比较不同生成器微调后的模型性能排名。论文未披露具体的模型参数规模、训练硬件、训练时间或推理延迟等信息。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "生成质量", "value": "相对提升 17%", "setting": "与五个前沿模型 API 对比，八类任务，数据集规模最大 20K 样本"}, {"metric": "样本多样性", "value": "相对提升 19%", "setting": "与五个前沿模型 API 对比，八类任务，数据集规模最大 20K 样本"}, {"metric": "多样性优势随规模变化", "value": "200 样本时持平，20K 样本时相对提升 37%", "setting": "数据集规模从 200 到 20K 样本的缩放对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出零数据设定下的 Invent-A-Dataset 提示式系统，实现从数据集描述到大规模后训练数据集的自动生成；在八类任务、最大 20K 样本规模上与五个前沿模型 API 进行系统对比评估；揭示生成数据多样性优势随训练数据集规模扩大而增大的规律，从 200 样本时的持平到 20K 样本时的相对提升 37%；验证生成数据在下游后训练中的一致性增益，Invent-A-Dataset 微调在多种后训练模型架构上排名更高

Abstract: Building datasets remains one of the most manual and brittle parts of AI development. In this technical report, we focus on the most extreme but also most prevalent setting real world practitioners face: a zero data regime. Here, practitioners don't have any data for the capability they want to learn. We introduce Invent-A-Dataset which is a prompt based system to go from dataset description to realistic and large scale post-training datasets. We evaluate Invent-A-Dataset against five frontier model APIs including Anthropic, Google, Open AI, DeepSeek, Zai. Across eight task types and dataset sizes up to 20K samples, Invent-A-Dataset significantly outperforms with both the highest quality (17% relative gains) while simultaneously producing the most diverse samples (19% relative gains). Its diversity advantage widens with scale of training dataset size (from parity at 200 samples to 37% relative gains at 20K samples). This translates into considerable downstream training gains, resulting in far more performant post-trained models. Invent-A-Dataset fine-tune consistently ranks higher compared to other generator fine-tunes across different post-trained model architectures.

</details>


### [87] [Action-On-Item Preference Flow: A Shared Event Schema for Predictive and Generative Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01375)
*Parthiv Chatterjee,Kashish Kanjaria,Vashisth Purani,Sourish Dasgupta,Tanmoy Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 Action-On-Item 共享事件模式与 PerTIDE，用统一历史更新机制跨电影/新闻/对话领域构建用户状态，并支持预测与生成式个性化；在 PENS 与 MIND 上取得高于控制组的 MRR 增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同领域交互历史（电影、新闻、对话）的动作与输出异构，能否学习一个可复用的历史更新机制，并用同一编码器同时支持预测式与生成式个性化消费

Method: 输入为用户在电影、新闻、对话等领域的交互历史，每条交互被表示为 action-on-item：将交互角色映射为统一角色嵌入，并与内容嵌入配对。PerTIDE 按顺序处理：先通过动作门控控制不同动作对状态更新的贡献；再维护三条不同时间尺度的状态空间轨迹，分别捕捉不同持久性的用户记忆；随后进行轨迹融合得到统一用户状态；最后通过命令条件读出（command-conditioned readout）生成预测或生成式输出。训练目标是在不同领域的历史上共享更新参数，使同一历史编码器服务于下一新闻预测与个性化标题生成两类消费者。理论部分给出了对原生标签重命名的不变性、项目嵌入扰动下的有界状态变化以及池化训练界，为跨源复用提供条件。推理时，同一编码器将不同领域历史映射为共享更新机制下的用户状态，并由预测头或生成头消费。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "MRR", "value": "提升 15.23 点", "setting": "受控 PENS-to-MovieLens 实验，冻结的源训练核心对比相同结构随机核心，在拟合相同目标消费者后"}, {"metric": "MRR", "value": "领先 4.12 点", "setting": "MIND 数据集上 PerTIDE 对比同输入三分支状态空间控制模型"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Action-On-Item 共享事件模式：将交互角色映射后与内容嵌入配对，使共享更新参数可作用于不同用户状态；Multi-Timescale State Hypothesis (MTSH)：规定证据如何进入、持久化与消费的统一假设；PerTIDE 实现：动作门控、三条状态空间轨迹、融合与命令条件读出；证明对原生标签重命名的不变性、项目嵌入扰动下有界状态变化，以及兼容条件下的池化训练界

Abstract: A user's movie, news, and dialogue histories differ in their native actions and outputs, yet each interaction supplies evidence that can update user memory. We study whether these histories can train one reusable update mechanism. An action-on-item schema pairs a mapped interaction role with a content embedding, allowing shared update parameters to operate on separate user states. We establish invariance to native relabeling, bounded state changes under item-embedding perturbations, and a pooled-training bound under explicit compatibility conditions. The Multi-Timescale State Hypothesis (MTSH) specifies how this evidence enters, persists, and is consumed; PerTIDE implements it with action gating, three state-space traces, fusion, and command-conditioned readout. On PENS, the same history encoder supports both next-news prediction and personalized headline generation. In a controlled PENS-to-MovieLens experiment, a frozen source-trained core exceeds an identically structured random core by 15.23 MRR points after fitting the same target consumer. On MIND, PerTIDE retains a 4.12-point MRR advantage over a same-input three-branch state-space control. Action, readout, and trace interventions identify complementary contributions to these gains. Together, the theory and experiments support learning history updates across compatible sources and reusing them through predictive and generative consumers.

</details>


### [88] [Mixture-Trained Merging for Unified Multi-Objective Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01238)
*SeongHyeon Kim,Chaeyun Jang,Seungyoo Lee,Jiyeon Ham,Yunju Bak,Boseop Kim,Juho Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MTM 通过让每个分支在目标偏置数据混合上训练，使多目标专家在权重空间可合并，并用合并评估反馈与 qNEHVI 多目标贝叶斯优化迭代选择混合与合并系数，从而在代码、数学、指令跟随与 think/non-think 控制上优于朴素合并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序多目标后训练会纠缠目标并导致训练顺序、数据比例、调度和停止标准敏感；简单权重空间合并单目标专家又会造成领域能力下降或 think/non-think 模式坍缩。

Method: MTM 面向统一多目标语言模型。输入是多个目标（代码、数学、指令跟随、think/non-think 控制）的数据。方法不采用顺序单目标后训练，而是为每个分支构造目标偏置的数据混合，使每个分支在训练中接触跨目标交互，从而在合并时处于兼容的权重空间区域。与训练同时，MTM 使用合并模型评估作为低成本反馈信号来选择分支数据混合，避免直接做昂贵的数据混合消融。流程迭代进行：在一轮中，先根据全局选择的合并系数提升基础模型（合并多个分支），再利用领域偏好系数，在保持其他目标不退化约束下，精炼每个分支的混合比例。为在超出单纯形网格搜索的大空间中寻找合并系数，MTM 采用 qNEHVI 多目标贝叶斯优化。最终合并后的统一模型在代码、数学、指令跟随和 think/non-think 行为分离上优于朴素合并，且在单目标合并坍缩处仍能保留行为区分。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 Mixture-Trained Merging (MTM)，每个分支用目标偏置数据混合而非单目标数据训练，增强跨目标交互并使分支在合并时兼容；用合并模型评估作为低成本信号选择分支数据混合，避免昂贵的数据混合消融；迭代式流程：每轮用全局选择的合并系数提升基础模型，并在保持其他目标约束下用领域偏好系数精炼各分支混合；为超越单纯形网格搜索，采用 qNEHVI 多目标贝叶斯优化进行缩放；将合并失败归因于不兼容的权重空间几何：单目标专家漂移到不同参数区域，插值落入共享低损失盆地之外

Abstract: Unified language models are increasingly expected to combine heterogeneous capabilities, such as mathematics, code, instruction following, and controllable thinking behavior, within a single set of parameters. A common solution is sequential post-training on multiple objectives, but this entangles all objectives along one optimization trajectory and makes the final model highly sensitive to training order, data ratios, schedules, and stopping criteria. Weight-space merging offers a modular alternative, but naive merging of single-objective experts often fails: domain capabilities degrade sharply, or think/non-think modes collapse into one dominant behavior. We attribute both failures to incompatible weight-space geometry: experts trained on single objectives drift to distant regions of parameter space, placing their interpolations outside any shared low-loss basin. We propose Mixture-Trained Merging (MTM), which trains each branch on an objective-biased data mixture rather than a single objective, exposing it to cross-objective interactions and making branches compatible at merge time. MTM uses merged-model evaluations as a low-cost signal for selecting branch mixtures, avoiding expensive data-mixture ablations. The procedure is iterative: each round promotes the base model using globally selected merge coefficients and refines each branch mixture using domain-preferred coefficients under constraints that preserve other objectives. To scale beyond simplex grid search, MTM uses qNEHVI-based multi-objective Bayesian optimization. Across code, mathematics, instruction following, and think/non-think control, MTM outperforms naive merging and preserves behavioral separation where single-objective merging collapses, suggesting that effective unified models require branches trained to be mergeable.

</details>


### [89] [Looping Beyond Twice: A Scalable Recipe for Looped Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01153)
*Di He,Pengxiang Li,Da Chang,Qingyan Meng,Lu Yin,Shiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文诊断了循环 MoE 在深层循环下方差增长与专家选择崩溃两大问题，提出 LOOM：通过缩放残差、循环注入输入嵌入、每循环独立路由器和循环残差实现稳定且多样化的循环计算。在 100M–1.7B 模型上稳定扩展至 9–12 次循环，700M 近等 FLOPs 下 5 次循环将困惑度从 18.36 降至 16.54，1.7B 模型 9 次循环将困惑度从 9.62 降至 7.77，零样本准确率从 42.4% 提升至 47.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 FLOPs 匹配比较下，循环 Transformer/MoE 的收益随循环次数快速递减甚至退化，导致此前工作通常只采用两次循环，无法将循环扩展到更深层数。

Method: LOOM 基于循环 Transformer 与 Mixture-of-Experts 结构。输入序列首先经过嵌入层，在每次循环开始时将输入嵌入重新注入，以缓解深层循环中的表示漂移。每个循环复用共享的 Transformer 块，但引入每循环独立的路由器，为不同循环选择不同的专家集合，从而避免专家选择崩溃并增加计算多样性。残差更新被缩放以限制隐藏状态方差随循环次数的增长，维持循环稳定性。循环残差将早期循环的输出向前传递并累加，使每一轮循环在保持状态稳定的同时贡献新的计算。训练目标为标准语言建模损失，在 100M–1.7B 规模上验证模型可稳定扩展至 9–12 次循环。推理时按循环次数重复应用共享块与对应路由器，推理硬件、延迟、吞吐与成本未披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "困惑度 (perplexity)", "value": "18.36 → 16.54", "setting": "700M 模型，近等 FLOPs，5 次循环 vs 非循环基线"}, {"metric": "平均零样本准确率", "value": "38.84% → 39.53%", "setting": "700M 模型，近等 FLOPs，5 次循环 vs 非循环基线"}, {"metric": "困惑度 (perplexity)", "value": "9.62 → 7.77", "setting": "1.7B 模型，60B tokens，9 次循环 vs 非循环基线，未做 FLOPs 匹配"}, {"metric": "平均零样本准确率", "value": "42.4% → 47.7%", "setting": "1.7B 模型，60B tokens，9 次循环 vs 非循环基线，未做 FLOPs 匹配"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 诊断出循环 MoE 难以扩展的两个障碍：隐藏状态方差随循环累积增长导致表示漂移，以及专家选择崩溃（路由在不同循环中反复选择相同专家）。；提出 LOOM，通过缩放残差更新以限制方差增长，并在每次循环重新注入输入嵌入来稳定循环状态。；通过每循环独立路由器（per-loop routers）和循环残差（Looping Residual）前传早期输出，使不同循环激活不同专家、增加计算多样性。；在 100M–1.7B 模型上实现稳定扩展至 9–12 次循环，并给出近等 FLOPs 与非等 FLOPs 条件下的性能结果。

Abstract: Looped Transformers introduce recurrent depth as a new scaling axis for LLMs: by repeatedly applying shared Transformer blocks, they increase effective depth without increasing parameter count. However, the benefits of looping remain unclear for large MoE LLMs under FLOPs-matched comparisons. The main reason is that the gains from additional iterations diminish quickly and can even turn into degradation, so the extra FLOPs spent on looping yield little substantial improvement. Consequently, prior work typically settles on two loops. We identify two main obstacles to scaling looped MoE. First, looping inherits and amplifies the curse of depth: hidden-state variance grows with each iteration as residual updates accumulate, which destabilizes deep recurrence and causes representations to drift. Second, looped MoE suffers from expert selection collapse: routers repeatedly select the same experts across loops, so extra iterations add computation without adding computational diversity. Guided by this diagnosis, we propose LOOM, built on a single principle: each loop should contribute new computation while keeping the recurrent state stable. LOOM stabilizes recurrence by scaling residual updates to bound variance growth and re-injecting the input embedding at every loop, and diversifies it through per-loop routers that engage different experts and a Looping Residual that carries earlier outputs forward. Experiments across 100M-1.7B models show stable scaling to 9-12 loops. Under near-iso-FLOP, the 700M model performs best at 5 loops, reducing perplexity from 18.36 to 16.54 and improving average zero-shot accuracy from 38.84% to 39.53% over the non-looped baseline. Without FLOP matching, the 1.7B model trained on 60B tokens peaks at 9 loops, reducing perplexity from 9.62 to 7.77 and improving average zero-shot accuracy from 42.4% to 47.7%. Code is available this https URL .

</details>


### [90] [Learning Rate Transfer for Hybrid Transformer-SSM Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01172)
*Jimin Seo,Gyubok Lee,Yeonsik Jo,Kiwoong Yoo,Yeongoon Kim,Minhae Oh,Jin Woo Koo,Suhwan Kim,Nakyung Lee,Minsik Seol,Idris Nechnech,Jaehyeon Kim,Giho Lee,Jungwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究混合 Transformer-SSM 架构的学习率迁移，发现在实际简化 ZOH Mamba 实现下，原始 μP 处方可在 8 倍宽度范围和 4-32 深度下实现近零学习率迁移差距，即使该架构不满足 μP 的理论条件。机制归因于全局更新-权重不变性与 AdamW 逐参数归一化的局部平衡，且迁移至 Nemotron-H 生产模型，为大规模混合模型预训练提供实用指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合 Transformer-SSM 架构中，理论上基于无限宽度和增长状态尺寸的 SSM 学习率缩放规则，与实际使用固定状态尺寸的简化 ZOH Mamba 实现之间存在差距；本文旨在探究在这种实际条件下学习率迁移行为及其机制。

Method: 论文研究的混合架构由 Transformer 块和简化 ZOH Mamba 块（State-Space Model）组成，具体比例可变。模型采用 μP（最大更新参数化）进行初始化与学习率缩放，优化器使用 AdamW。输入为序列 token，依次经过混合层：Transformer 块执行自注意力与前馈网络，SSM 块执行状态空间递推。训练目标为标准的自回归语言建模。关键发现是：尽管 SSM 操作不满足 μP 的 Tensor Programs 可表示性条件，且所有测试的参数化均未通过 μP 的坐标检查诊断，但仅使用原始 μP 处方即可在宽度 256-2048 范围内实现学习率近乎不变，这归因于两个条件：全局更新-权重不变性由 μP 的初始化与 LR 缩放保证，局部逐组件平衡由 AdamW 的逐参数归一化提供。该不变性在深度 4-32、不同序列长度、批大小和 Transformer-SSM 比例下均成立，并成功迁移至 Nemotron-H 生产混合模型。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "最优学习率宽度不变性范围", "value": "宽度 256-2048 下不变（8倍宽度范围）", "setting": "混合 Transformer-SSM 架构，深度 4-32，十亿参数规模，使用简化 ZOH Mamba，原始 μP 处方"}, {"metric": "学习率迁移差距", "value": "近零", "setting": "上述宽度范围内，无需额外调参即可迁移最优学习率"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 发现混合架构在宽度 256-2048、深度 4-32、十亿参数规模下，使用原始 μP 处方可获得近零学习率迁移差距；提出混合架构学习率迁移的两条件分解：全局更新-权重不变性（由 μP 初始化与 LR 缩放实现）和局部逐组件平衡（由 AdamW 逐参数归一化提供）；验证了宽度不变性在深度、序列长度、批大小及 Transformer-to-SSM 比例上的鲁棒性，并迁移至 Nemotron-H 生产模型

Abstract: We study learning rate (LR) scaling for hybrid architectures combining Transformer and State-Space Model (SSM) blocks, a class adopted by several recent production language models. In particular, we focus on the gap between the theoretical scaling rules derived for SSMs under zero-order-hold (ZOH) discretization at infinite width with growing state size, and the field-standard practical implementations using simplified-ZOH Mamba at fixed state size. Surprisingly, in this practical regime hybrid architectures achieve a near-zero LR transfer gap across widths 256-2048 and depths 4-32 up to billion-parameter scale using only the original $\mu$P prescription, even though SSM operations fall outside its Tensor Programs representability conditions and every parameterization we test fails the standard coordinate-check diagnostic of $\mu$P correctness. We attribute this to a two-condition decomposition of LR transfer in hybrid architectures: a global update-to-weight invariance, enforced by $\mu$P's initialization and LR scaling; and a local per-component balance, provided by AdamW's per-parameter normalization. Our observations show that the optimal LR is invariant to width up to 8$\times$, that this width invariance holds across depth, sequence length, batch size, and Transformer-to-SSM ratio, and that it transfers to Nemotron-H, a production hybrid outside our custom architecture set. We hope these findings fill the gap between theoretical scaling rules and practical hybrid implementations, and stimulate further research toward bridging it.

</details>


### [91] [Does Scaling Reinforcement Learning Really Require More Training?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01133)
*Bangji Yang,Jiajun Fan,Hongba Ma,Ruihan Guo,Ge Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SURGE 提出一种从固定 RL 训练历史中扩展策略集的方法，通过谱域融合锚点和捐赠者检查点，在不增加训练或推理计算下提升推理准确率并减少推理 token，在 1.5B 数学和 7B 代码任务上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习训练得到的最终策略可能弱于训练历史中的中间检查点，且标准做法需要更多训练或更多推理计算来提升推理能力。如何在不延长训练、不增加单次推理计算的前提下，从已有RL训练历史中释放更强策略。

Method: SURGE 利用同一 RL 训练历史中的两个检查点：一个高精度锚点（anchor）和一个产生更短响应的竞争性捐赠者（donor）。它将每个检查点参数表示为相对于共享初始化的变化量，然后对锚点的更新进行谱分解，保留其主导成分，并融入捐赠者的互补成分。给定锚点更新保留比例的目标值，SURGE 直接从权重确定块大小，无需评估候选策略。最终融合后的模型作为单一策略部署，在基准测试中准确率高于两个输入检查点，且推理 token 数少于锚点。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "AIME24 accuracy", "value": "54.17%", "setting": "DeepSeek 1.5B mathematical-reasoning history"}, {"metric": "AIME24 native maximum accuracy", "value": "50.83%", "setting": "DeepSeek 1.5B mathematical-reasoning history"}, {"metric": "HumanEval+ accuracy", "value": "83.7%", "setting": "OLMo 7B coding history"}, {"metric": "HumanEval+ baseline accuracy", "value": "82.8%", "setting": "OLMo 7B coding history"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出policy-space scaling概念：从固定RL历史中扩展可部署策略集，不增加训练或推理计算。；SURGE方法：通过谱分解融合同一次RL运行中的高精度锚点检查点和生成更短回复的捐赠者检查点，保留锚点主导更新并融入捐赠者互补成分。；无需测试候选策略，根据权重自动确定块大小以保留锚点更新的固定目标比例。；实验证明融合策略在基准准确率上超过两个输入检查点，且使用比锚点更少的推理token。

Abstract: Scaling reasoning typically spends more compute on reinforcement learning (RL) or on inference. We show that a completed RL training history can yield policies stronger than the checkpoints visited by its optimizer. We call this policy-space scaling: expanding the deployable policy set accessible from a fixed RL history, without extending training or increasing per-query inference computation. We instantiate it with SURGE (Scaling Up RL Gradient-free via Eigenspace fusion). SURGE combines two checkpoints from the same RL run: a high-accuracy anchor and a competitive donor that generates shorter responses. It expresses both checkpoints as changes from their shared initialization, then spectrally decomposes the anchor's update to retain its dominant component and incorporate the donor's complementary component. With a fixed target for how much of the anchor update to retain, SURGE determines the block size from the weights without testing candidate policies. We evaluate two 1.5B mathematical-reasoning histories, DeepSeek and Nemotron, and one 7B coding history, OLMo. SURGE improves benchmark-average accuracy over both input checkpoints while using fewer reasoning tokens than the anchor. It reaches 54.17% on DeepSeek AIME24 against a measured native maximum of 50.83%, and 83.7% on OLMo HumanEval+ against 82.8%. These gains exceed the observed training curves. Geometric controls support the importance of RL-update structure beyond weight displacement or token reduction alone. Each constructed model runs as a single policy. Our findings identify stored RL history as a reusable scaling resource: the capability available from a training run need not end at its best checkpoint.

</details>


### [92] [Match the Distribution, Not the Compute: Post-Training Multi-Token Prediction Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00888)
*Prachi Badarayani,Aidan Jay,Chenghui Zhou,Dayquan Julienne,Yuan Gao,Tianwei Chen,George Zerveas,Ishmam Zabir,Xiren Zhou,Chris Quirk,Xia Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究在冻结的Qwen3-8B推理模型上仅通过约2.5B token的轻量后训练训练K=3链式MTP草稿头，即可在数学/代码/知识基准上达到或超过联合训练的MiMo-7B的预期加速，MTP训练token减少10^3-10^4倍；并提出链感知验证松弛（+12-16%加速）与自适应MTP头数量控制器（挽回11-14%加速损失）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 所有开放MTP模型都在预训练阶段联合训练草稿头，草稿质量被预训练固定；论文探究能否仅在冻结推理模型上用轻量后训练达到同等吞吐加速，并优化服务时系统（验证规则与草稿长度控制）。

Method: 系统以冻结的Qwen3-8B为骨干语言模型，在其上附加K=3个链式多token预测（MTP）头，每个头负责预测草稿序列中的下一个token，从而在一次前向传播中草拟多个后续token。草稿token随后经过验证步骤：原始验证规则要求草稿token与骨干模型的token分布一致，以保证输出分布不变。论文提出链感知的验证规则松弛，允许草稿token相对骨干分布有有界的漂移，以换取更高的接受率和预期加速。后训练阶段：在目标模型生成的思维链数据（约2.5B token）上，仅对MTP头使用普通交叉熵损失进行训练，骨干保持冻结；这与现有MTP模型在预训练全程联合训练头与骨干的做法不同。推理服务流程：自适应控制器在运行时根据当前上下文动态决定启用多少个MTP头（在固定的最大草稿长度约束下），以平衡草稿成本与接受收益，挽回因固定草稿长度造成的加速损失。整体目标是在不修改骨干分布、不重新预训练的前提下，通过轻量后训练与推理期调度获得与联合训练相当的吞吐加速。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "预期加速（与联合训练MiMo-7B对比）", "value": "达到或超过MiMo-7B的预期加速", "setting": "冻结Qwen3-8B + K=3链式MTP头，后训练约2.5B token，数学/代码/知识基准"}, {"metric": "MTP训练token消耗", "value": "减少10^3-10^4倍", "setting": "后训练约2.5B token vs MiMo-7B MTP联合预训练（数万亿token）"}, {"metric": "预期加速提升", "value": "+12%到+16%", "setting": "链感知验证规则松弛，每个基准，保持任务准确率"}, {"metric": "加速损失挽回", "value": "最多11-14%", "setting": "自适应控制器动态选择MTP头数量，固定最大MTP草稿长度"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 在冻结的Qwen3-8B上仅用约2.5B token的普通交叉熵后训练K=3链式MTP头，即可达到或超过联合训练的MiMo-7B的预期加速，MTP训练token消耗少10^3-10^4倍；提出链感知的草稿token验证规则松弛，允许相对骨干模型token分布的有界漂移，在保持任务准确率的同时每个基准提升预期加速+12%到+16%；提出自适应控制器，在推理时动态选择启用的MTP头数量（固定最大草稿长度），可挽回最多11-14%的加速损失

Abstract: Multi-token prediction (MTP) improves the throughput of autoregressive generation by enabling the language model to draft multiple next tokens per forward pass, while a verification step over draft tokens ensures that token distribution of the backbone is preserved. Every open MTP-family release (MiMo-7B, DeepSeek-V3, Qwen3) trains its heads jointly with the backbone over the full pretraining run of tens of trillions of tokens, thus setting the drafter quality at pretraining time. We ask whether a lightweight post-training pass on target-generated chain-of-thought is enough to reach the same expected throughput speedup on a frozen reasoning model, and study how a serving-time system built on such a checkpoint can be optimized. We present three findings. 1) On a frozen Qwen3-8B with $K{=}3$ chained MTP heads, we show that a post-training recipe with plain cross-entropy on $\approx\!2.5$B tokens reaches or exceeds the expected speedup of jointly trained MiMo-7B on math, coding and knowledge benchmarks. Our post-training recipe utilizes $10^3$-$10^4\times$ less MTP-training tokens as compared with joint pre-training of MiMO-7B MTP baseline. 2) We propose a chain-aware relaxation of draft token verification rule that allows a bounded drift from backbone language model token distribution. We show that this relaxation lifts expected speedups by $+12$ to $+16\%$ per benchmark while preserving task accuracy. 3) We propose an adaptive controller that dynamically chooses the number of MTP heads to be engaged at inference time and demonstrate recovery of upto $11$--$14\%$ loss in speedup using fixed maximum MTP draft length.

</details>


### [93] [Cross-Benchmark Transfer from RL on Agentic Coding Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00890)
*Sushant Mehta,Logan Ritchie,Edwin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究对1T参数MoE模型Kimi K2.7 Code进行RL后训练（GSPO），在1,700个编码任务上提升Agent能力，并在六个外部基准上实现跨分布迁移，包括训练后发布的任务集，且轨迹步数缩短24-35%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过RL在专家构建的Agent编码任务上后训练，能否弥补编码Agent在最后一公里上的需求遗漏、测试不足、行为破坏和未经验证假设等失败模式，并且学习到的能力能否跨分布迁移到外部基准和未见过的Agent执行框架。

Method: 论文以Kimi K2.7 Code（1T总参数、32B激活的MoE模型）为基础，采用RL（GSPO算法）进行后训练。输入为专家构建的1,700个agentic编码任务：1,000个仓库级任务，使用隐藏的fail-to-pass测试和pass-to-pass测试进行评分；700个终端任务，由专家编写的隐藏验证器评分。奖励定义为通过目标检查的比例，但如果任何pass-to-pass测试失败则奖励归零。训练时仅更新rank-32的LoRA适配器，进行一个epoch的GSPO优化。推理时模型在三种不同的agent harness下在六个外部基准上评估。论文还通过配对轨迹分析比较基础模型和训练模型在相同任务上的行为，并统计轨迹步数变化。整个流程旨在通过RL让模型学会保留既有行为、全面验证需求，并泛化到训练分布之外的任务。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "pass@1", "value": "60.1 → 64.8", "setting": "SWE-Bench Pro，单epoch GSPO rank-32 LoRA微调后"}, {"metric": "pass@1", "value": "31.0 → 43.4", "setting": "DeepSWE"}, {"metric": "pass@1", "value": "67.4 → 82.0", "setting": "Terminal-Bench 2.1"}, {"metric": "pass@1", "value": "1.4 → 12.1", "setting": "Terminal-Bench 3"}, {"metric": "pass@1", "value": "0.0 → 7.6", "setting": "Terminal-Bench 4"}, {"metric": "pass@1", "value": "5.0 → 25.0", "setting": "SWE-Marathon"}, {"metric": "轨迹长度（Agent步数）", "value": "缩短24-35%", "setting": "DeepSWE和Terminal-Bench 3的中位轨迹"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 仅使用RL（GSPO）在1,700个专家构建的Agent编码任务上对1T参数MoE模型进行后训练，任务包含1,000个带隐藏fail-to-pass和pass-to-pass测试的仓库任务以及700个由专家隐藏验证器评分的终端任务。；设计奖励为通过目标检查的比例，但若任何pass-to-pass测试失败则奖励归零，强制保持现有行为。；验证了跨六个外部基准和三种Agent框架的迁移能力，包括训练数据收集后发布的三个任务集，并发现中位轨迹步数缩短24-35%。；通过配对轨迹分析表明训练后模型新增解决的任务避免了四种失败模式。

Abstract: Coding agents often fail in the last mile: they build most of a feature but drop a requirement, test only the cases their implementation already handles, break behavior that was supposed to stay intact, or validate against an unchecked assumption. We ask whether reinforcement learning (RL) on expert-built agentic coding tasks closes this gap, and whether what the agent learns transfers beyond the training distribution. We post-train Kimi K2.7 Code, a 1T-parameter (32B active) open-weight mixture-of-experts model, with RL alone on 1,700 tasks: 1,000 repository tasks graded by hidden fail-to-pass tests and by pass-to-pass tests of existing behavior, and 700 terminal tasks graded by expert-written hidden verifiers. The reward is the fraction of target checks passed and drops to zero if any pass-to-pass test fails. One epoch of GSPO on a rank-32 LoRA adapter improves pass@1 on each of the six external benchmarks we evaluated, across three agent harnesses: SWE-Bench Pro (60.1 to 64.8), DeepSWE (31.0 to 43.4), Terminal-Bench 2.1 (67.4 to 82.0), Terminal-Bench 3 (1.4 to 12.1), Terminal-Bench 4 (0.0 to 7.6), and SWE-Marathon (5.0 to 25.0). Pooled over the five independent task sets (Terminal-Bench 4 revises Terminal-Bench 3), the improvement is significant (p < 0.001), and it remains significant on the three sets released after the training data was collected (p = 0.004); the model also improves under both harnesses never used in training. Median trajectories on DeepSWE and Terminal-Bench 3 are 24-35% shorter in agent steps. The base model's failed DeepSWE runs are mostly near-misses, and on the tasks the trained model newly solves, paired trajectories show it avoiding each of the four failure modes above.

</details>


### [94] [Clock Diffusion: Efficient Semi-Autoregressive Continuous Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00894)
*Yair Schiff,Omer Belhasin,Roy Uziel,Matan Rusanovsky,Ran Zilberstein,Marianne Arriola,Gilad Turok,Guanghan Wang,Volodymyr Kuleshov,Michael Elad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Clock Diffusion 提出位置相关噪声调度的半自回归连续扩散语言模型，支持变长生成与 KV 缓存，并配套 Cache Grab 高效采样器，在 OpenWebText 扩散似然与 GSM8K 基准上取得优于连续基线及部分离散扩散模型的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续扩散语言模型缺少变长生成与键值缓存支持，且生成质量仍落后于自回归与离散扩散模型，限制了实际作为语言模型使用的效率与效果。

Method: Clock Diffusion 是一种面向离散数据的连续扩散语言模型框架。其核心是通过位置相关噪声调度（position-dependent noise schedules）来参数化模型，从而支持半自回归（SAR）生成。框架包含两个特殊情形：块生成（block generation）与滑动窗口生成（sliding window generation）。输入为离散 token 序列，经嵌入后映射到连续空间；模型以扩散过程对连续表示进行加噪与去噪训练。ClockDLMs 是基于滑动窗口 Clock Diffusion 的高斯扩散语言模型族，训练目标为对数似然的变分下界（diffusion likelihood bound）。解码阶段，模型按块或滑动窗口进行半自回归生成，每个窗口内使用扩散采样；为提升效率，提出 Cache Grab 采样器，利用离散扩散推理加速技术，包括对概率超过置信度阈值的 token 直接提交，以及自推测解码，从而减少采样步数并提升生成质量。虽然论文声称支持键值缓存与变长生成，但具体实现细节（如缓存管理机制、窗口间状态传递方式）在摘要中未展开。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "扩散似然界（diffusion likelihood bound）", "value": "state-of-the-art", "setting": "OpenWebText 数据集，优于 block SAR 离散扩散模型"}, {"metric": "GSM8K 基准表现", "value": "显著优于连续基线，并匹配或超过同类 SAR 离散扩散模型", "setting": "TinyGSM 训练后的 ClockDLMs"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 Clock Diffusion 框架，通过位置相关噪声调度定义半自回归连续扩散语言模型。；实例化 block generation 与 sliding window generation 两种生成策略。；提出 ClockDLMs，基于 sliding window Clock Diffusion 的高斯扩散语言模型族。；提出 Cache Grab 采样器，结合置信度阈值提交与自推测解码以提升推理效率与质量。

Abstract: Recent works on continuous diffusion for discrete data have demonstrated performance on par with comparable discrete diffusion models. However, these continuous counterparts lack key features that are essential to practical use as language models, namely variable-length generation and support for a key-value cache, and they still lag behind the frontier of autoregressive and discrete diffusion quality. In this work, we address these limitations. We do so by introducing a model parameterization that uses position-dependent noise schedules to define semi-autoregressive (SAR) continuous diffusion language models (DLMs). Together with efficient training and sampling algorithms, we call this framework Clock Diffusion, and we present two special cases of our method: block and sliding window generation. We then define ClockDLMs, a family of Gaussian DLMs based on sliding window Clock Diffusion that attain state-of-the-art diffusion likelihood bounds on OpenWebText, even beating the performant block SAR discrete diffusion models. ClockDLMs trained on TinyGSM also substantially outperform continuous baselines on the GSM8K benchmark and match and exceed comparable SAR discrete diffusion models. Finally, building on our parameterization, we propose more efficient samplers that we dub Cache Grab, which adapt techniques from accelerated inference in discrete diffusion, such as committing tokens whose probabilities exceed a confidence threshold and self-speculative decoding, further improving our models' quality and efficiency.

</details>


### [95] [SLIM: Simplex-Lattice Interpolation Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01037)
*Seongcheol Jeong,Masahiro Suzuki,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SLIM提出一种基于单纯形格点插值的模型合并系数优化方法，通过评估单个专家和等权对构建二次代理模型，在有限评估预算下准确预测多专家合并性能，并找到较优合并系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限的基准评估预算下，高效地优化大语言模型合并系数，减少昂贵的基准评估次数。

Method: SLIM框架首先通过经典混料设计选择一组系数点，包括单个专家模型（对应单纯形顶点）和等权重的两两混合模型（对应边的中点）。对这些选定点进行基准评估，获得聚合性能。然后，利用这些评估数据拟合一个二次多项式代理模型，该模型能够近似表示整个单纯形上任意合并系数对应的性能。接着，在代理模型上进行优化，寻找最优的合并系数，整个过程不再需要额外的基准评估。最终，使用优化后的系数对专家模型进行加权合并，得到最终模型。该方法的核心是通过结构化采样和二次曲面拟合，在极少的评估次数下准确预测未见过的多专家混合性能。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "预测保真度", "value": "优于随机设计（包括正则化拟合）", "setting": "两种模型架构上，有限评估预算下的匹配预算对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 使用经典混料设计构造合并系数单纯形上的聚合性能二次代理模型；仅需评估单个专家及等权对即可确定代理模型，最小化测量次数；在代理模型上直接优化合并系数，无需进一步的指标评估

Abstract: Optimizing merging coefficients for large language models can require many costly benchmark evaluations. We propose \textbf{Simplex-Lattice Interpolation Merging (SLIM)}, which constructs a quadratic surrogate of aggregate performance on the coefficient simplex using a classical mixture design. Evaluations of individual experts and equal-weight pairs determine the surrogate with the minimum number of measurements needed to identify a general quadratic on this domain. SLIM then optimizes the surrogate without further target-metric evaluations. Experiments on two model architectures demonstrate accurate prediction of unseen multi-expert mixtures and competitive merge performance under limited evaluation budgets. Matched-budget comparisons show that structured evaluation points improve prediction fidelity over random designs, including those using regularized fitting.

</details>


### [96] [Kernelized Activation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01062)
*Laziz U. Abdullaev,Minh-Hieu Pham,Bach Do,Khoat Than,Tan M. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KAS将激活干预统一到再生核希尔伯特空间，通过核评估实现输入自适应的非线性干预，理论涵盖DiM为特例，在越狱LLM和图像风格控制任务上表现优于或持平现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活干预方法（如Difference-in-Means）使用单一的、输入无关的干预向量，表达力有限且忽略了激活空间的局部几何结构，难以实现精细、自适应的模型行为控制。

Method: KAS将激活干预视为在再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）中的优化问题。输入是模型在某一层的激活表示（例如Transformer的隐藏状态）。框架假设存在源参考集（代表不需要的属性）和目标参考集（代表期望的属性），每个参考集由若干激活向量组成。对于待干预的激活x，KAS通过核函数k(·,·)计算x与所有源/目标参考集元素的相似度，并基于这些核评估隐式地求解一个优化问题，得到干预后的激活x'。该优化问题的目标是最小化x'与目标参考集的核加权距离，同时最大化与源参考集的核加权距离，从而将x'推向目标流形并远离源流形。解可以表示为参考集元素的线性组合，系数由核矩阵求逆或求解线性系统得到。由于核函数的作用，干预方向是输入依赖的，即每个激活根据其在表示空间中的相对位置获得不同的非线性调整，形成非线性的干预场。在线性核下，该解退化为经典的Difference-in-Means向量，因此KAS是DiM的严格泛化。在推理时，对于每个待干预的激活，只需计算它与参考集元素的核值，然后应用预计算的系数，即可得到修改后的激活，无需训练或梯度更新。整个流程是训练自由的，适用于多种生成模型和任务。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "任务性能（越狱LLM、图像风格控制）", "value": "超过或持平现有方法", "setting": "标准激活干预基准任务"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将激活干预提升到再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS），通过核评估隐式地定义依赖于激活的干预得分，无需显式构造特征映射。；提出KAS框架，将干预建模为优化问题，其解可通过源参考集和目标参考集之间的核评估闭式得到，实现对每个激活的局部自适应调整。；证明DiM是KAS在线性核下的一个特例，并展示更丰富的核函数能够实现几何感知的干预。

Abstract: Activation steering provides a simple, training-free mechanism for controlling attributes of generative models such as sentiment, style, and helpfulness. However, standard approaches such as Difference-in-Means apply a single input-independent steering vector across all activations, limiting expressivity and ignoring the local geometry of the activation space. We propose Kernelized Activation Steering (KAS), a unifying framework that lifts activation steering into a reproducing kernel Hilbert space. KAS formulates steering as an optimization problem expressed purely via kernel evaluations, yielding an implicit, activation-dependent steering score without constructing explicit feature maps. Unlike DiM, KAS induces locally adaptive steering: each activation is modified according to its relative position with respect to source and target reference sets, producing a nonlinear steering field over the representation space. Importantly, DiM is recovered as a special case under a linear kernel, while richer kernels enable geometry-aware interventions. Across standard activation steering tasks, including jailbreaking LLMs and image style control, KAS outperforms or is on par with the existing methods.

</details>


### [97] [SHARPO: Segment-Level Credit Assignment for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00838)
*Xinchen Du,Zhengze Zhou,Wenhui Zhu,Han Yu,Sen Na,Rohit Jain,Alborz Geramifard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SHARPO提出段级信用分配，用段内教师-学生log概率差生成有界乘子重加权GRPO优势，在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct上于ALFWorld与WebShop优于GRPO等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程多步智能体RL中轨迹级稀疏奖励难以对单个决策进行信用分配，一个局部错误即可导致任务失败，需要更细粒度的信用分配机制。

Method: SHARPO以group relative策略优化GRPO为基础，训练LLM在环境中进行多步交互。环境交互被切分为若干面向环境的段（segment），每段包含若干token。受on-policy self-distillation启发，模型在每段内同时充当教师与学生（同策略自蒸馏），计算段内教师分布与当前策略的log概率差，该差值作为该段的信用信号。信用信号被转换为一个落在有界范围内的乘子，作用在该段的GRPO优势上；同一段内所有token共享同一乘子，因此信用可在不同段之间变化，而段内保持一致。最终以被重加权后的优势进行策略梯度更新，从而在保持GRPO组内相对比较框架的同时，把轨迹级回报细化为段级信用。推理时按常规策略采样执行环境动作，无需额外教师模型在线部署（摘要未披露推理开销）。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "基准得分（未在摘要中给出具体数值）", "value": "优于GRPO、SDAR、RLSD、StepOPSD等基线", "setting": "Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct，ALFWorld与WebShop基准"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出段级（segment-level）信用分配机制SHARPO，在环境交互段粒度上细化GRPO；借鉴on-policy self-distillation (OPSD)，计算段内教师-学生log概率差作为信用信号；该信号生成有界乘子作用于GRPO优势，同一段内所有token共享乘子，使跨段信用差异化；在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct上结合ALFWorld与WebShop验证优于GRPO、SDAR、RLSD、StepOPSD

Abstract: Agentic reinforcement learning (RL) trains a large language model (LLM) to act over long, multi-step interactions. However, a single localized error can cause task failure, while trajectory-level rewards provide limited guidance for assigning credit to individual decisions. To address this limitation, we introduce Segment-level Hindsight Advantage Reweighting for Policy Optimization (SHARPO), a credit-assignment mechanism that refines Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) at the level of environment-facing segments. Inspired by the existing on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) method, SHARPO computes teacher-student log-probability gaps within each segment and uses the resulting signal to compute a bounded multiplier on the GRPO advantage. This multiplier is shared by all tokens within the segment, allowing credit to vary across different segments. With Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, SHARPO outperforms existing baselines on the ALFWorld and WebShop benchmarks, including GRPO, SDAR, RLSD, and StepOPSD.

</details>


### [98] [AnyJev Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00831)
*Jiamu Zhang,Tianze Yang,Yucheng Shi,Evan Chen,Zixiang Nie,Kelly Wan,Liangjie Hong,Ninghao Liu,Liang Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AnyJev 把指令微调 LLM 的单次 prefill 在答案位置受限读出为类型化决策概率，并无需梯度地修正标签先验与选项顺序两类偏差：对 K 个选项循环旋转的 log 概率取平均，使 20 选项任务的顺序翻转率从 0.33 降到 0.14/0.18，并在 11/11 个模型上提升准确率；再用无标注状态上的停止规则把平均旋转次数降到 7.3（共 18），在 vLLM 上把每秒决策数提升 2.2 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从指令微调 LLM 的单次前向 prefill 中直接读出类型化决策（限制答案位置 next-token 分布到选项 token）时，存在两个系统性缺陷：对某些标签存在与输入无关的先验偏好，以及对选项在列表中的位置存在顺序偏好，导致决策不可靠。

Method: AnyJev 的端到端流程如下：输入是一个提示（包含任务指令、上下文与候选选项列表）以及一个固定的选项 token 集合。模型为预训练指令微调 LLM。第一步，对提示做一次 prefill 前向，取答案位置的下一个 token 分布，并将其限制（restrict）在选项 token 上，从而得到归一化后的类型化决策概率向量，而不是自由文本。第二步，针对标签先验缺陷：在无标注输入集合上统计各选项 token 的平均分布，得到标签先验，再把目标样本的 log 概率减去（除以）该先验的对数，抵消「无论输入如何某些标签概率更高」的偏差。第三步，针对位置顺序缺陷：构造选项列表的 K 个循环旋转（cyclic rotation），对每个旋转分别做一次 prefill 与受限读出，然后在 log 概率空间对 K 个旋转结果取平均，得到与选项排列顺序无关的决策概率；这使顺序翻转率下降、准确率上升。第四步，因为完整 K 次旋转需要 K 次 prefill，引入停止规则：按某种顺序逐次读取旋转，累计的平均决策与「读满全部旋转后的决策」做一致性比较，当在无标注状态上的差异低于阈值时提前停止。阈值在一个无标注切分上选择，并在第二个无标注切分上对不一致率给出经验上界（0.008）；实际服务部署时只在一个切分上选阈值并做界，平均读取 7.3 次旋转，从而在 vLLM 上把每秒可服务的决策数提高 2.2 倍。训练目标不涉及梯度：全部校正都是推理期的无参数后处理。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "顺序翻转率 (order-flip rate)", "value": "从 0.33 降至 0.14（20 选项任务一）", "setting": "两个 20 选项任务，K 个循环旋转平均，无梯度更新"}, {"metric": "顺序翻转率 (order-flip rate)", "value": "从 0.33 降至 0.18（20 选项任务二）", "setting": "两个 20 选项任务，K 个循环旋转平均，无梯度更新"}, {"metric": "准确率 (accuracy)", "value": "在 11 个模型上全部提升（11/11）", "setting": "两个 20 选项任务，循环旋转平均"}, {"metric": "决策读取次数 / 旋转次数", "value": "平均读到 7.3 次旋转（共 18 次）；另一设定下 10.6/18，并在四个单元格中的两个上验证 0.008 的差异上界", "setting": "无标注状态上选择停止阈值，一个切分选阈值、另一个切分做界"}], "online_gains": [{"metric": "服务吞吐（每秒决策数）", "value": "提升 2.2 倍", "setting": "vLLM 上的 serving run，使用在单一无标注切分上选择并做界的停止规则"}]}

Conclusion: 无梯度、无参数更新的标签先验校正：用无标注输入估计标签先验并在 log 概率上相除；选项顺序偏差消除：对选项列表的 K 个循环旋转平均 log-probability，得到顺序不变的决策概率；面向服务成本的停止规则：在无标注状态上选择阈值，使旋转次数从 K 降到约 7.3-10.6，并给出在第二个无标注切分上的差异上界（0.008）

Abstract: A typed decision is a choice among a fixed set of options, returned as a probability rather than as text. Systems that need typed decisions today use models trained for that purpose. This report describes AnyJev, which reads a typed decision from one prefill of a pretrained instruction-tuned language model. The readout restricts the next-token distribution at the answer position to the option tokens. It has two defects: the model assigns higher probability to some labels whatever the input, and to some positions in the option list. AnyJev corrects both with no gradient steps and no parameter changes: it divides out a label prior estimated from unlabelled inputs, and it averages log-probabilities over the K cyclic rotations of the option list. On two 20-option tasks the rotations lower the order-flip rate from 0.33 to 0.14 and from 0.33 to 0.18, and raise accuracy on 11 of 11 models on both. Reading every rotation requires K prefills. A stopping rule selected against the full-rotation decision on unlabelled states cuts that. Selecting the threshold on one unlabelled split and bounding its disagreement on a second, it reads 10.6 rotations of 18 at a verified 0.008 bound on two of four cells; selected and bounded on one split, as our serving run did, it reads 7.3 and serves 2.2 times as many decisions per second on vLLM. The code is open source.

</details>


### [99] [CARM: Cancellation-Aware Response Masking for LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02039)
*Yafei Zhang,Songshuo Lu,Sicong Liao,Zhi Chen,Yaohua Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CARM提出了一种新的序列级掩码方法，通过取绝对值的对数比来防止序列级概率比抵消，从而更有效地控制LLM强化学习中的离策略问题。在数学推理和代码生成任务上，CARM相比几何平均掩码在AIME等基准上平均提升3.13个百分点，在代码基准上提升2.88个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列级响应掩码使用长度归一化的几何平均令牌概率比，其有符号的对数比在不同位置上可能相互抵消，从而隐藏了双向策略漂移，导致掩码决策不准确。

Method: CARM是一种序列级响应掩码方法，用于LLM强化学习后训练中控制离策略程度。在每次策略更新后，对采样得到的响应，计算每个令牌的新旧策略概率比的对数（log-ratio）。与传统的长度归一化几何平均掩码（即取平均对数比）不同，CARM先对每个令牌的对数比取绝对值，然后计算这些绝对值的平均值，作为掩码分数。该分数用于决定该响应是否参与优化。通过取绝对值，CARM防止了正负对数比在序列级别相互抵消，从而更准确地检测双向策略漂移。论文还从理论上证明了被接受的响应满足一个联合界：超出预设概率比区间的令牌比例和平均对数距离受控。该方法在数学推理和代码生成任务上进行了实验验证，并展示了优于几何平均掩码的性能。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "mean@16", "value": "up to +3.13 percentage points", "setting": "averaged over AIME 2024/2025/2026 and BeyondAIME, compared to geometric-mean masking"}, {"metric": "average pass@1", "value": "+2.88 points", "setting": "across four code benchmarks, compared to the strongest evaluated baseline"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出Cancellation-Aware Response Masking (CARM)，对每个令牌的对数比先取绝对值再平均，防止相反概率变化相互抵消。；证明了被接受的响应满足一个联合界，限制了采样令牌比率超出预设区间的比例及其平均对数距离。；在数学推理和代码生成任务上验证了CARM的有效性，相比几何平均掩码在AIME 2024/2025/2026和BeyondAIME上平均提升最高3.13个百分点，在四个代码基准上平均pass@1提升2.88个百分点。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed the rapid adoption of reinforcement learning (RL) in large language model (LLM) post-training, with substantial gains in mathematical reasoning and code generation. In practical systems, however, policy updates and differences between rollout and training engines can make sampled responses off-policy. Sequence-level masking addresses this mismatch by deciding whether an entire response should contribute to optimization. A common masking rule uses the length-normalized geometric mean of sampled token probability ratios. Its signed log-ratios can cancel across positions, concealing substantial bidirectional policy drift. We propose \emph{Cancellation-Aware Response Masking} (CARM), a sequence-level mask that takes the absolute value of each token log-ratio before averaging, preventing opposing probability changes from canceling. We prove that accepted responses satisfy a joint bound on the fraction of sampled-token ratios outside a prescribed band and their mean log-distance beyond its boundaries. Experiments on mathematical reasoning and code generation show that CARM improves mean@16 averaged over AIME 2024/2025/2026 and BeyondAIME by up to $3.13$ percentage points over geometric-mean masking, and increases average pass@1 across four code benchmarks by $2.88$ points over the strongest evaluated baseline. These findings support CARM as a theoretically grounded and effective method for response-level off-policy control in LLM reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [100] [Training-Aware Target Coverage for Synthetic Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00814)
*Yang Ba,Michelle V. Mancenido,Rong Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于线性理论的合成数据选择方法TATC，用于LLM微调。该方法量化合成数据对目标任务的训练价值，选择有益且能扩展目标覆盖的样本。在数学推理任务上微调Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B-Instruct，GSM8K性能优于对比方法，为合成数据选择提供了原则性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何量化合成数据对目标任务的训练价值，并据此选择合成数据以提升LLM微调效果，避免更多合成数据反而损害模型性能。

Method: TATC方法基于线性理论：假设模型在目标任务上的表现与训练数据的覆盖和噪声有关。该方法首先通过分析训练数据对目标任务的梯度影响，评估每个合成样本的训练效果，选择那些对目标任务有正面贡献的样本；然后，在候选样本中选择能够扩展目标相关方向覆盖的样本，这些方向是当前已有数据未充分表示的。最终，将选出的合成数据与原始数据结合，用于LLM微调。整个过程不涉及复杂的网络架构改动，主要关注数据选择策略。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "GSM8K准确率", "value": "优于替代合成数据选择方法（未披露具体数值）", "setting": "使用Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B-Instruct在数学推理任务上微调，不同选择预算下评估"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出线性理论刻画合成数据在目标覆盖与错误引入之间的权衡，确定合成数据何时有用、应添加多少以及边际价值。；基于理论开发TATC（Training-Aware Target Coverage）合成数据选择方法，识别对目标任务训练有益且能扩展目标相关方向覆盖的候选样本。；在文本和图像数据上验证理论，并在数学推理任务上对Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B-Instruct进行微调，验证TATC的有效性。

Abstract: Synthetic data are increasingly used to scale LLM training, yet more synthetic data do not necessarily produce better models. Useful synthetic data must add information relevant to the target task without introducing errors that offset their benefit, and the value of an example can change as the training set grows. We develop a linear theory that characterizes this tradeoff and determines where synthetic data are useful, how much should be added, and the marginal value of adding one example to an existing set. The analysis shows the conditions when input coverage alone is sufficient and when synthetic errors must also be considered. Guided by these results, we introduce \emph{Training-Aware Target Coverage} (TATC), a synthetic data selection method for LLM fine-tuning. TATC identifies candidates whose training effects are beneficial to the target task and selects among them to expand coverage of target-relevant directions not already represented by the available data. Experiments on text and image data verify the linear theory. With mathematical reasoning tasks, TATC selects synthetic solutions for fine-tuning Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B-Instruct and outperforms alternative synthetic-data selection methods on GSM8K across selection budgets. In summary, we provide a principled approach to synthetic data selection by quantifying and maximizing its value to the target task.

</details>


### [101] [Increasing Width Allows Greedy Layer-wise Training to Rival End-to-End Backpropagation in Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00753)
*Syon Mansur,Joel Zylberberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作研究自监督学习中贪心逐层训练能否媲美端到端反向传播，发现更宽的网络能缩小差距，甚至在浅而宽的网络中贪心训练更优，并将其归因于更有利的表示几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自监督学习下，网络宽度和深度如何影响贪心逐层训练相对于端到端反向传播的有效性，以及局部最优网络在何种架构条件下能学到可比的表征。

Method: 摘要层级信息显示：研究对象为卷积神经网络，在自监督学习框架下训练；对比两种训练范式——端到端反向传播（跨所有层协调参数更新）与贪心逐层训练（逐层局部优化、避免误差的端到端反传）。实验变量为网络宽度与深度。结果模块为：首先在不同宽度/深度配置下测量两种训练方式的表征学习性能；其次对训练所得网络的表征进行几何分析，比较贪心训练与端到端训练所形成表征的几何性质。摘要未披露具体网络结构、目标函数、优化器、数据流或推理服务细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "自监督训练性能", "value": "在相对较浅且非常宽的网络中，贪心逐层训练观察到比端到端反向传播更高的性能（具体数值未在摘要中披露）", "setting": "卷积网络上的自监督学习，对比贪心逐层训练与端到端反向传播"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 系统研究网络宽度与深度对贪心逐层自监督训练与端到端训练性能差距的影响，发现宽度增大可缩小甚至逆转该差距；对训练所得表征进行表示几何分析，发现极宽贪心训练网络具有更有利的表示几何，并提出其作为性能提升的潜在机制

Abstract: End-to-end backpropagation has been the dominant mode of training in deep learning, allowing for the coordination of parameter updates across layers of a neural network. Prior studies have explored alternative -- and, in some cases, simpler -- training mechanisms, showing that they can sometimes achieve performance similar to backpropagation. However, the architectural conditions under which locally optimized networks, which avoid end-to-end backpropagation of error, can learn representations comparable to those learned through end-to-end training remain unclear. We aim to answer this question in the context of self-supervised learning, an important framework for large-scale pretraining in artificial intelligence. Here, we investigate how network width and depth affect the efficacy of greedy layer-wise and end-to-end self-supervised training in convolutional networks. We find that in wider networks, the benefits of end-to-end backpropagation over greedy layer-wise training shrink: in relatively shallow and very wide networks, we even observed higher performance in models trained with greedy layer-wise training. Subsequent analysis of the representations formed by these networks shows that very wide greedy-trained networks exhibit more favorable representational geometry than do networks trained end-to-end with backpropagation. This work shows that width can compensate for restricted credit assignment and identifies differences in representational geometry as a potential mechanism for their improved performance.

</details>


### [102] [A Safe Prototype Is Not a Safety Direction: Reference Dependence and Prompt Confounds in Response-Safety Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01801)
*Sahil Kadadekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 审计基于嵌入的安全评分：正类质心规则并不识别安全方向，AUC接近随机；显式安全减去不安全的参考可提升AUC至0.588-0.738，并在5%假安全阈值下接受更多安全响应，提示标签组成会膨胀未控制评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 响应安全能否仅通过已知安全响应嵌入的均值（正类质心）的余弦相似度来评分？原始正类质心规则未被识别，正类观测仅定位安全类相对于编码器原点的位置，并不确定区分安全与不安全响应的方向。

Method: 论文未提出新的模型架构，而是对现有的基于嵌入的响应安全评分方法进行审计。方法使用冻结的编码器将响应映射为嵌入向量。输入包括响应文本及其对应的提示。核心评分规则包括：(1) 正类质心：计算已知安全响应嵌入的均值作为安全原型，用新响应嵌入与该原型的余弦相似度作为安全分数；(2) 显式参考：利用安全与不安全响应的嵌入差异构建安全方向，即安全减去不安全的参考向量，用新响应嵌入在该方向上的投影或余弦相似度作为分数。论文在多个语料（PKU-SafeRLHF、Aegis、BeaverTails）上使用四个冻结编码器进行实验，采用prompt分组拆分以避免提示泄漏。评估指标为ROC-AUC和验证集校准的5%假安全阈值下的安全响应接受率。此外，进行了完全无标签的留出参考实验和提示标签组成消融，以探究未控制评估的偏差。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "ROC-AUC (safe prototype)", "value": "0.457-0.545", "setting": "在人工标注语料上，四个冻结编码器，prompt分组拆分"}, {"metric": "ROC-AUC (safe-minus-unsafe reference)", "value": "0.588-0.738", "setting": "相同嵌入与拆分"}, {"metric": "ROC-AUC (safe prototype, jury control)", "value": "0.358-0.405", "setting": "陪审团标注控制集"}, {"metric": "ROC-AUC (reference, jury control)", "value": "0.754-0.793", "setting": "陪审团标注控制集"}, {"metric": "safe response acceptance rate at 5% false-safe threshold (PKU-SafeRLHF)", "value": "0.153-0.263 (reference) vs 0.039-0.061 (prototype)", "setting": "验证集校准的5%假安全阈值，四个编码器"}, {"metric": "safe response acceptance rate at 5% false-safe threshold (Aegis)", "value": "0.189-0.291 (reference) vs 0.004-0.045 (prototype)", "setting": "验证集校准的5%假安全阈值，四个编码器"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 系统审计了原始正类质心评分规则，证明其不是安全方向。；提出带显式安全减去不安全参考的对比评分，显著提升AUC和校准阈值下的接受率。；通过prompt分组拆分和prompt标签组成消融，揭示未控制评估的膨胀。；在多个冻结编码器与人工标注/陪审团标注语料上验证。

Abstract: Can response safety be scored by cosine similarity to the mean embedding of known-safe responses? A recent sleeper-agent detector proposes exactly this score, yet the raw positive-centroid rule is not identified: positive observations locate the safe class relative to an encoder origin, but do not determine which direction separates safe from unsafe responses. We audit the rule on two prompt-controlled, human-labeled corpora and one auxiliary jury-labeled source control, using four frozen encoders and prompt-grouped splits. On the human-labeled corpora the safe prototype reaches ROC-AUC 0.457-0.545, with two cells significantly below chance and one above, while an explicit safe-minus-unsafe reference reaches 0.588-0.738 on the same embeddings; on the jury control the prototype is inverted (0.358-0.405) and the reference reaches 0.754-0.793. At validation-calibrated 5% false-safe thresholds, the reference accepts more safe responses on PKU-SafeRLHF (0.153-0.263 versus 0.039-0.061 across encoders) and Aegis (0.189-0.291 versus 0.004-0.045), but not reliably on BeaverTails. A fully unlabeled held-out reference recovers part to most of the referenced ranking, much less when only 5% of the pool is unsafe, whereas 80-634 labeled unsafe responses recover most of it. Prompt-only ablations show that prompt-label composition can inflate uncontrolled evaluations. This is a bounded result about a raw positive centroid, not all one-class methods or safety-specialized guards. A class mean is a location, not necessarily a safety direction; a declared reference with enough unsafe mass identifies orientation.

</details>


### [103] [Stochastic Rounding in Low-Precision Transformer Inference: A Variable-Precision Emulation Study of a Small GPT-2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01889)
*Yohan Chatelain,Pablo de Oliveira Castro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过可变精度随机舍入算法，系统研究了低精度 Transformer 推理中随机舍入 (SR) 与就近舍入 (RN) 在不同操作位点的效果差异。理论分析表明 MLP 对 SR 更鲁棒，而语言模型头对 RN 更优，并在 DistilGPT-2 上验证混合策略可将困惑度降至全精度参考的 1.10 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在低精度 Transformer 推理中，随机舍入 (SR) 与就近舍入 (RN) 的取舍问题：不同操作位置（MLP 与语言模型头）对舍入误差的敏感度不同，需要确定在何处使用何种舍入策略以最小化精度损失。

Method: 论文研究低精度 Transformer 推理中的舍入策略，以 DistilGPT-2 为实验对象。输入为 token 序列，经过嵌入层、多层 Transformer 块（包含自注意力与前馈网络 MLP）以及语言模型头输出 logits。核心操作在于：在固定数值格式下，对各个操作位点（尤其是 MLP 的下投影和语言模型头）分别应用随机舍入 (SR) 或就近舍入 (RN)。为支持任意精度实验，作者扩展 PRISM 向量化舍入库，实现可变精度随机舍入 (VPSR) 算法，该算法在硬件浮点中精确评估舍入决策。分析部分包含两个理论工具：(1) 针对线性投影的概率前向误差界，表明 SR 误差随归约长度 n 以 O(√n u) 增长，而 RN 为 O(n u)；(2) 对输出 softmax 处期望交叉熵损失变化的二阶分解，分解为符号漂移、漂移曲率和 Fisher 加权方差惩罚。这些分析解释了 MLP 噪声主要为均匀 logit 偏移（softmax 对其不变），而头部噪声在词表上非均匀，导致两者对 SR 的响应相反。推理时，MLP 输出采用 t=6 位 SR，头部采用 RN，从而在混合精度配置下达到最优困惑度。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "困惑度倍数 (相对于全精度参考)", "value": "1.15x", "setting": "DistilGPT-2, MLP 使用 SR, t=6 有效位"}, {"metric": "困惑度倍数 (相对于全精度参考)", "value": "2.21x", "setting": "DistilGPT-2, MLP 使用 RN, t=6 有效位"}, {"metric": "困惑度倍数 (相对于全精度参考)", "value": "1.10x", "setting": "DistilGPT-2, 混合精度 (MLP 输出 t=6 使用 SR, 头使用 RN)"}, {"metric": "困惑度降低比例", "value": "28%", "setting": "混合精度配置相比匹配位宽 RN"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 扩展 PRISM 向量化舍入库以支持任意虚拟精度，提出可变精度随机舍入 (VPSR) 算法，并证明其舍入决策可在硬件浮点中精确评估。；为线性投影推导概率前向误差界，证明 SR 误差随归约长度 n 以 O(√n u) 增长，而 RN 为 O(n u)。；提出输出 softmax 处交叉熵损失变化的二阶分解（符号漂移、漂移曲率、Fisher 加权方差惩罚），解释 MLP 与头对 SR 表现相反的原因。；在 DistilGPT-2 上验证混合精度配置：MLP 输出用 t=6 位 SR，头用 RN，困惑度降至全精度参考的 1.10 倍，比匹配位宽 RN 降低 28%。

Abstract: Should low-precision transformer inference use stochastic rounding (SR) or round-to-nearest (RN)? The answer depends on where in the network you look. We isolate this effect by holding the numerical format fixed and varying only the rounding rule at individual operation sites. To enable experiments at freely chosen precisions, we extend the PRISM vectorized rounding library to arbitrary virtual precision via a variable-precision stochastic rounding (VPSR) algorithm, proving that the rounding decision is evaluated exactly in hardware floating point. We develop two analyses providing complementary insight into this site-level trade-off. First, a probabilistic forward-error bound for linear projections shows that SR's error envelope grows as $O(\sqrt{n} u)$ in reduction length $n$, versus $O(n u)$ for RN, a gap that widens rapidly at low precision and is most pronounced in the long multilayer perceptron (MLP) down-projection. Second, a second-order decomposition of expected cross-entropy loss change at the output softmax into signed drift, drift curvature, and a Fisher-weighted variance penalty reveals why the two sites behave oppositely: MLP noise is predominantly a uniform logit shift to which softmax is invariant, so SR's variance is largely discounted; head noise is non-uniform across the vocabulary and is not. On DistilGPT-2 at $t=6$ significand bits, observations match theory: SR in the MLP raises perplexity to 1.15x the full-precision reference, versus 2.21x for RN. At the language-model head, the ordering reverses because SR introduces non-uniform variance, whereas deterministic RN carries none. In a mixed-precision configuration (MLP output at $t=6$), assigning SR to the MLP and RN to the head brings perplexity within 1.10x of the full-precision reference, a 28% reduction over matched-bit RN.

</details>


### [104] [SkillSpec: Consensus-Gated Agent Skill Evolution via Representation Specialization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00704)
*Huancheng Chen,Xiaodi Sun,Zhaoqiong Huang,Shenyang Huang Shreya Singhal,Jingwen Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SkillSpec 提出共识门控演化与表示专业化两阶段框架，让 LLM 智能体在不更新权重的情况下可靠地演化自然语言技能，并按过程依赖与检索需求选择扁平、图或混合结构；在六个基准、三个模型上平均成功率比 SkillOpt 提升 6.89%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言技能作为LLM智能体的程序性记忆，现有方法将技能视为静态产物或单一文档，仅通过聚合验证分数优化文本内容，未显式建模程序性知识的组织、检索与执行结构；论文区分“保留什么知识”与“如何组织知识”两个问题。

Method: SkillSpec 是一个两阶段框架。第一阶段为共识门控演化：输入为当前技能库、任务执行环境与验证任务集；由互补编辑意图生成完整的候选技能（而非仅修改文本片段），每个候选技能在配对评估协议下进行多次重复评估，要求整体成功率有足够提升，且每次重复评估中的聚合配对增益非负，满足共识后才将候选技能提交并加入技能库。第二阶段为表示专业化：从整个优化轨迹中提取信号，包括被接受的候选与被拒绝的候选，计算过程敏感与冗余敏感信号，据此为技能选择扁平、图或混合的表示结构，使程序性知识按其依赖关系与检索需求组织。推理时，智能体根据当前任务从结构化技能库中检索并执行相应技能。优化目标是在不更新模型权重的前提下，可靠地保留可复用任务知识并优化其组织结构。实验在六个基准和三个目标语言模型上进行，平均成功率相比 SkillOpt 提升 6.89%。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均成功率", "value": "比 SkillOpt 提升 6.89%", "setting": "六个基准、三个目标语言模型上取平均"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 共识门控演化：由互补编辑意图生成完整候选技能，仅当配对评估达到共识（总体足够提升且每次重复评估中的聚合配对增益非负）时才提交更新。；表示专业化：利用完整优化轨迹（包括被接受和被拒绝的候选）推导过程敏感与冗余敏感信号，指导选择扁平、图或混合的技能表示结构。

Abstract: Natural-language skills are textual procedural memories through which large language model (LLM) agents retain reusable task knowledge without updating model weights. Existing methods typically treat skills as either static artifacts or monolithic documents optimized using aggregate validation scores as feedback. However, representing a skill as a monolithic document restricts optimization to its textual content, without explicitly modeling the structure through which procedural knowledge is retrieved and executed. We identify a key distinction between learning what knowledge to retain and determining how to organize it: textual updates should first be validated through execution evidence, after which the retained knowledge should be structured according to its procedural dependencies and retrieval requirements. To this end, we introduce SkillSpec, a two-phase framework comprising consensus-gated evolution and representation specialization. In the consensus-gated phase, complementary editing intents generate complete candidate skills. An update is committed only when paired evaluations reach consensus, requiring sufficient overall improvement and non-negative aggregate paired gain in every repeated evaluation. In the specialization phase, signals of process and redundancy sensitivity derived from the full optimization trajectory, including accepted and rejected candidates, guide the selection of a flat, graph, or hybrid this http URL six benchmarks and three target language models, SkillSpec improves average success rate over SkillOpt by 6.89%, averaged across the three models. These results demonstrate that reliable skill evolution and representation specialization address complementary objectives: deciding what knowledge to retain and how to structure it for inference.

</details>


### [105] [LabBook: Harnessing Experimental History for Efficient LLM-Driven Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00675)
*Bo Yuan,Wenqian Ye,Zelin Zhao,Lama Moukheiber,Henry Kautz,Aidong Zhang,Yongxin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LabBook是一个单智能体LLM发现框架，用智能体自维护记忆来同时驱动完整实验日志中的证据检索与新程序生成，把历史保留与上下文选择解耦，在49个Frontier-CS问题上改进质量-成本权衡，并在数学、系统等9个任务上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进化式LLM发现方法从小规模祖先集中生成新程序，上下文可控但会遗漏其他实验的有用证据；而纳入完整实验历史又会产生冗长、冗余的上下文。

Method: 系统为单智能体发现框架。输入为完整的实验日志（全部历史程序及其评估结果）与当前任务。LabBook是由同一智能体在迭代中维护的记忆结构，承担两个角色：(1) 作为检索索引/线索，从完整实验日志中检索与当前问题相关的证据；(2) 作为生成先验，指导新程序的产生。每次迭代时，同一智能体将自身记忆与检索到的证据结合，联合输出下一个候选程序以及更新后的LabBook。该设计将“保留完整历史”与“选择性构造上下文”分离：完整历史始终保留在日志中，而进入模型上下文的是经记忆引导筛选后的证据，从而在避免长上下文冗余的同时不丢失有用实验证据。训练上不依赖显式种群或分支搜索结构，也不需要额外训练模型，属于单智能体迭代式程序发现循环。推理/运行流程即为“检索—生成—评估—记忆更新”的循环。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "质量-成本权衡（observed quality-cost trade-off）", "value": "优于所评估的进化基线", "setting": "49个Frontier-CS问题，两个backbone"}, {"metric": "任务竞争力", "value": "在9个额外任务上保持竞争力", "setting": "数学、系统与启发式设计任务"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: LabBook：由同一智能体维护的记忆，同时承担从完整实验日志中检索相关证据和指导新解生成两个互补角色；将完整历史保留与选择性上下文构建解耦，无需显式种群或分支搜索结构；每轮迭代中同一智能体结合记忆与检索证据，联合产出下一个程序并更新LabBook

Abstract: Evolutionary approaches to LLM-driven discovery often generate new programs from a small set of selected ancestors. This keeps contexts manageable but can omit useful evidence from other experiments, whereas including the full experimental history produces long, redundant contexts. We introduce a simple, single-agent discovery harness built around LabBook, an agent-maintained memory that serves two complementary roles: guiding retrieval of relevant evidence from a complete experimental log and informing the generation of new solutions. At each iteration, the same agent combines its memory with retrieved evidence and jointly produces the next program and an updated LabBook. This separates complete history retention from selective context construction, without requiring an explicit population or branching search structure. On 49 Frontier-CS problems, LabBook improves the observed quality-cost trade-off over the evaluated evolutionary baselines with two backbones, while remaining competitive across nine additional mathematical, systems, and heuristic-design tasks. Code will be released at this https URL .

</details>


### [106] [Pre-training interventions, ex post facto: Grafting model beliefs across checkpoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00767)
*Peter Nutter,Dani Roytburg,Clément Dumas,Jinghua Ou,Shi Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出grafting方法，通过将预训练检查点上训练的SDF适配器权重更新嫁接到后训练模型上，实现无需完整后训练的预训练干预。方法将现实漂移和偏好连贯性损失平均减少超过一半，同时保持目标信念安装强度与后训练SDF相当。实验覆盖最高284B参数模型，支持虚假事实安装、错位模型生物和宪法中训练干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成文档微调（SDF）通常直接应用于后训练模型，导致现实漂移、能力退化，而将SDF混入预训练或中间训练又需要完整后训练流程，迭代慢且昂贵。如何在无需完整后训练的情况下，实现忠实的预训练干预。

Method: 论文提出grafting方法，用于预训练干预。给定一个预训练检查点和一个后训练模型，首先在预训练检查点上使用合成文档微调（SDF）训练一个适配器，学习到权重更新Δθ。然后将该Δθ直接加到后训练模型的权重上，得到嫁接后的模型。这种方法避免了在预训练或中间训练中混入合成文档后必须重新进行完整后训练的问题，同时近似了忠实的预训练干预。其目标是在不重新后训练的情况下，将目标信念安装到模型中，并减少现实漂移和偏好连贯性损失。推理时，嫁接后的模型可直接使用，无需额外适配器。论文在多个模型家族（最高284B参数）上进行了实验，包括安装虚假事实、训练错位模型生物以及应用宪法中训练干预，并与后训练SDF和忠实中训练运行进行比较。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "现实漂移减少", "value": "平均减少超过一半", "setting": "跨模型家族最高284B参数，与直接应用于后训练模型的SDF对比"}, {"metric": "偏好连贯性损失减少", "value": "平均减少超过一半", "setting": "跨模型家族最高284B参数，与直接应用于后训练模型的SDF对比"}, {"metric": "目标信念安装强度", "value": "与后训练模型上的SDF相当", "setting": "跨模型家族最高284B参数"}, {"metric": "与忠实中训练运行的接近程度", "value": "更接近", "setting": "跨模型家族最高284B参数"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出grafting方法：在预训练检查点上训练SDF适配器，并将学得的权重更新加到后训练模型上，近似忠实的中训练干预。；识别并量化“现实漂移”失败模式：SDF应用于后训练模型会使模型将与文档无关的虚构实体当作真实。；证明同一适配器可应用于任何后续检查点，无需后训练，从而以单次微调成本快速迭代预训练干预。

Abstract: Pre-training interventions are critical to alignment research, since beliefs formed during pre-training shape how a model generalizes from later training. One recently popular technique for such interventions is synthetic document fine-tuning (SDF), which aims to alter what the model believes. Ideally, synthetic documents would be mixed into pre- or mid-training, but every change to a pre-training corpus must be followed by a full post-training run before its effect can be measured, making iteration slow and expensive. Common practice instead applies SDF to an already post-trained model. This is known to leave artifacts and degrade capabilities, and, as we show, it makes the model treat fabricated entities unrelated to the documents as real, a failure we call reality drift. We propose grafting: train the SDF adapter on the pre-trained checkpoint, then add the learned weight update to the post-trained model, which approximates the faithful approach while reusing the existing post-training. We demonstrate this by installing false facts, training misaligned model organisms and applying a constitutional mid-training intervention, across model families up to 284B parameters. Grafting installs the target belief as strongly as SDF on the post-trained model while reducing both reality drift and the loss of preference coherence by more than half on average, and it stays closer to a faithful mid-training run. Because grafting requires no post-training, the same adapter can be applied to any later checkpoint, enabling researchers to iterate quickly on pre-training interventions at the cost of a single fine-tuning run.

</details>


### [107] [Make Sparse Rewards Count: Density-Aware Reward Aggregation for Multi-Reward RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00574)
*Tong Zheng,Skylar Zhai,Zhan Cheng,TianMing Sha,Youling Huang,Shuo Zhou,Shaotong Qi,Jingcheng Liang,Xuwei Ding,Pengcheng Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DARA通过分析多奖励RL中优势能量与活跃组密度的关系，提出逆平方根密度校正奖励聚合，在工具调用和数学推理任务上分别最多减少26%和65%的训练步数，同时保持最终性能竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多奖励强化学习中不同行为目标学习进度不均，导致训练效率低，需要一种动态调整奖励贡献的聚合方法。

Method: 该方法在多奖励强化学习（如GDPO）框架下运行。输入为每个rollout组内各奖励的原始奖励值。首先计算每个奖励在组内的相对优势，并累积为“优势能量”（一个batch内奖励平方优势之和）。理论分析表明，在理想GDPO归一化下，优势能量正比于活跃组密度，即该奖励在rollout组中提供非零相对优势的比例。基于此，DARA对每个奖励计算逆平方根密度校正权重，使得活跃频率低的奖励获得更大权重。权重从当前rollout批次动态计算，因此随训练过程自适应变化，无需修改底层策略优化目标（如PPO/GRPO）。训练时，各奖励优势乘以对应的密度校正权重后聚合，用于策略梯度更新。推理阶段无额外模块，与标准RL训练流程一致。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "达到高格式合规所需训练步数", "value": "最多减少26%", "setting": "工具调用任务"}, {"metric": "达到近饱和长度合规所需训练步数", "value": "最多减少65%", "setting": "数学推理任务"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出优势能量（advantage energy）指标，将奖励贡献与活跃组密度（active-group density）关联；基于密度关系推导逆平方根密度校正，自动调整各奖励权重；权重按rollout批次计算，自适应训练中奖励活跃度变化，且不改变底层策略优化目标

Abstract: Multi-reward reinforcement learning trains large language models to satisfy multiple behavioral objectives simultaneously. Reward-wise normalization, as used in GDPO, preserves reward-specific relative information within rollout groups, but different objectives can still exhibit uneven learning progress. We study this behavior through advantage energy, the sum of a reward's squared advantages over a batch. Under idealized GDPO normalization, we show that this energy is proportional to active-group density: the fraction of rollout groups in which the reward provides nonzero relative advantages. This reveals a residual batch-level signal imbalance and provides a basis for calibrating reward contributions. Based on this relation, we propose Density-Aware Reward Aggregation (DARA). We derive an inverse-square-root density correction that gives greater weight to signals from less frequently active rewards. DARA computes its weights from each rollout batch, adapting to changes in reward activity throughout training without modifying the underlying policy optimization objective. Experiments on tool calling and mathematical reasoning show that DARA learns the targeted behaviors faster than GDPO, reaching high format compliance in up to 26% fewer training steps on tool calling and near-saturated length compliance in up to 65% fewer steps on mathematical reasoning, while remaining competitive in final performance. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [108] [Redundancy Meets Synergy: Dependency-aware Expert Selection for MoE via Submodular Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00558)
*Zheng Lin,Shaoke Fang,Yuxin Zhang,Jinfeng Xu,Zihan Fang,Zhe Chen,Wei Ni,Jun Luo,Symeon Chatzinotas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DS-MoE 通过 difference-of-submodular 优化建模专家间的冗余与协同，利用 MM 算法选择紧凑专家子集，在保持性能的同时降低 MoE 内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 模型部署受限于内存瓶颈，现有 Top-k 启发式专家选择忽略专家间依赖关系，导致提取的子集性能次优。

Method: DS-MoE 是一个理论驱动的专家选择框架，用于从预训练的 MoE 模型中提取紧凑的专家子集。输入是完整 MoE 模型的门控网络参数与专家集合。首先，通过分析移除专家子集导致的损失退化的二阶泰勒展开，将专家组合的贡献分解为冗余（重叠表示）和协同（互补误差抵消）两个部分。然后，将专家选择问题建模为 difference-of-submodular (DS) 函数，其中冗余项对应子模函数，协同项对应超模或反子模部分，从而将冗余减少与协同最大化解耦。接着，采用定制化的 majorization-minimization (MM) 算法迭代优化该 DS 目标，该算法通过构造替代函数并逐步最小化，提供可证明的单调性保证，最终高效识别出最优专家子集。选择出的专家子集可直接替换原始 MoE 中的全部专家，实现内存压缩，推理时仅需激活保留的专家，门控网络保持不变。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出基于 difference-of-submodular (DS) 优化的专家选择框架，统一建模冗余与协同效应。；通过损失退化的二阶泰勒展开揭示专家组合的功能二元性（冗余与协同），并将选择目标公式化为 DS 函数。；设计具有单调性保证的 majorization-minimization (MM) 算法高效求解最优专家子集。

Abstract: While Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models effectively scale model capacity through sparse activation, their deployment is often bottlenecked by prohibitive memory requirements. Extracting a compact subset of experts presents a promising solution. However, existing expert selection heuristics predominantly rely on Top-k ranking, which isolates the evaluation of individual experts and ignores the intricate inter-expert dependencies introduced by the MoE gating network. In this paper, we propose DS-MoE, a theoretically grounded framework that redefines expert selection via difference-of-submodular (DS) optimization. By analyzing the second-order Taylor expansion of the loss degradation, we reveal functional duality within expert combinations: redundancy (where experts encode overlapping representations) and synergy (where experts provide complementary error cancellation). To navigate this duality, we mathematically decouple redundancy reduction from synergy maximization by formulating the selection objective as a DS function.
