Furthermore, we devise a tailored majorization-minimization (MM) algorithm with provable monotonicity guarantees to efficiently identify the optimal expert subset. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DS-MoE effectively preserves indispensable expert combinations, achieving superior performance compared to the state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [109] [Denoising Surface: Modeling and Predicting Inference Cost for Diffusion LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00499)
*Haoyu Zheng,Fangcheng Fu,Binhang Yuan,Yongqiang Zhang,Liang Deng,Hao Wang,Yuanyuan Zhu,Xiao Yan,Jiawei Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对扩散大语言模型推理服务中成本预测不准的问题，提出Denoising Workload Surface（DWS），将块自回归生成的二维结构保留为概率表面以加权异质每步成本，并设计粗到细训练方案训练轻量级仅提示预测器。该预测器可在单CPU核心运行且跨硬件迁移。真实服务实验表明，DWS将成本预测误差降低最高2.50倍，引导的最短作业优先调度器将在线聊天机器人端到端延迟降低最高1.92倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理成本代理指标（输出长度、去噪步数）无法准确刻画扩散大语言模型中块自回归生成机制带来的二维执行结构及异质每步成本，导致dLLM服务中请求调度与资源分配缺乏准确的每请求推理成本估计

Method: 论文提出的DWS（Denoising Workload Surface）架构首先观察到块自回归生成机制在输出块和块内去噪步上形成二维执行结构。DWS将这一结构保留为概率表面，用于加权异质每步成本，从而避免传统标量代理（输出长度或去噪步数）的信息损失。然后设计粗到细的训练方案，训练一个轻量级仅提示（prompt-only）预测器来预测DWS：粗阶段可能捕捉整体块级分布，细阶段细化块内步级概率。该预测器仅依赖提示输入，在单CPU核心上高效运行，避免GPU争用。由于DWS将请求相关执行行为与部署相关成本因素解耦，预测器可跨硬件配置迁移而无需重新训练。最后，将预测的DWS用于引导最短作业优先（SJF）调度器，优化在线聊天机器人的请求调度，降低端到端延迟。整个推理路径包括：请求进入→仅提示预测器生成DWS→调度器基于DWS估计成本排序→执行服务模型。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "成本预测误差降低倍数", "value": "最高2.50倍", "setting": "与基于标量的预测器相比，在预测DWS的成本预测任务中"}], "online_gains": [{"metric": "端到端延迟降低倍数", "value": "最高1.92倍", "setting": "真实服务实验，DWS引导的最短作业优先调度器用于在线聊天机器人"}]}

Conclusion: 提出Denoising Workload Surface (DWS)，将块-步二维执行结构保留为概率表面，以加权异质每步成本；设计由粗到细的训练方案，使轻量级仅提示预测器能准确预测DWS；DWS将请求相关执行行为与部署相关成本因素解耦，预测器无需重新训练即可跨硬件配置迁移；DWS引导的最短作业优先调度器，在真实服务实验中降低端到端延迟

Abstract: As diffusion large language models (dLLMs) become more capable, they are moving from research settings to real-world \textit{serving}, where request management (such as scheduling and resource allocation) relies on accurate estimation of per-request inference cost. However, common cost proxies fall short for dLLMs: output length ignores that one forward pass can unmask multiple tokens, and denoising-step count ignores the \textit{heterogeneous} per-step costs. We observe that the block-autoregressive generation mechanism induces a two-dimensional execution structure over output blocks and within-block denoising steps, whereas these proxies collapse it into a scalar, discarding information essential for characterizing the cost. Motivated by this insight, we propose the Denoising Workload Surface (DWS), which preserves this two-dimensional block-step structure as a probability surface to weight the heterogeneous per-step costs. We then design a coarse-to-fine training scheme that enables a lightweight prompt-only predictor to accurately predict the complex DWS. This predictor runs efficiently even on a single CPU core, avoiding GPU contention with the serving model. Since DWS decouples request-dependent execution behavior from deployment-specific cost factors, the predictor transfers across hardware configurations without retraining. In \textit{real-world} serving experiments, DWS reduces cost-prediction error by up to $2.50\times$ over scalar-based predictors, while the DWS-guided shortest-job-first scheduler reduces end-to-end latency by up to $1.92\times$ for online chatbots.

</details>


### [110] [IrekoGPT: Turning Structured Pruning into Post-Hoc Slimmable LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00426)
*Pietro Moriello,Pietro Buzzega,Angelo Porrello,Simone Calderara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IrekoGPT是一种后处理方法，将预训练LLM转换为推理时可调宽度的slimmable模型，通过保留SliceGPT投影矩阵、跨压缩率逐层校准以及无梯度岭回归校正下游线性层，在Llama和Qwen上初步验证了优于朴素PCA剪枝的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何将预训练LLM后处理为可在推理时动态调整宽度的slimmable模型，并避免固定压缩率带来的性能损失。

Method: IrekoGPT采用post-hoc方法处理预训练LLM。首先，基于SliceGPT的框架，但不剪枝其投影矩阵，而是保留这些投影矩阵，使单个模型能够通过选择不同宽度的子网络在推理时调整模型宽度，暴露嵌套的子网络结构。为提高在多个压缩率下的子网络质量，对每一层跨多个压缩率进行校准。最后，使用无梯度岭回归方法校正下游线性层，以恢复因压缩导致的表示偏移。整个过程无需梯度训练，直接应用于预训练模型。推理时，根据需求选择特定宽度的嵌套子网络，实现弹性计算。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "相对naive PCA slimming的改进", "value": "在高压缩率下增益最大（未提供具体数值）", "setting": "Llama与Qwen模型，初步结果"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 保留SliceGPT投影矩阵不剪枝，暴露不同宽度嵌套子网络；跨多种压缩率对每层进行校准以增强鲁棒性；使用无梯度岭回归校正下游线性层

Abstract: We introduce IrekoGPT, a post-hoc method for converting pretrained LLMs into slimmable models whose width can be adjusted at inference time. Building on SliceGPT, we retain its projection matrices without pruning them, allowing a single model to expose nested subnetworks at different widths. We improve robustness by calibrating each layer across multiple compression ratios, and correct downstream linear layers through gradient-free ridge regression. Across Llama and Qwen models, preliminary results show improvements over naive PCA-based slimming, with the largest gains at high compression. Code is available at this https URL

</details>


### [111] [XOR-Trellis: Ultra-Low-Complexity Dequantization and Curvature-Aware Hadamard-Free LLM Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00432)
*Xiaofan Que,Nir Elkayam,Spandan Pyakurel,Shuokai Pan,Dibakar Gope*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: XOR-Trellis面向LLM超低位宽trellis量化，提出硬件友好的低复杂度反量化器与曲率感知路径优化，在不使用Hadamard变换的情况下实现高并行重建，目标缓解反量化推理瓶颈并保持精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Trellis-coded量化在超低位宽下可高维压缩LLM权重，但实际部署面临两个挑战：反量化重建需足够并行吞吐以避免成为推理瓶颈，以及在不使用昂贵Hadamard非相干变换的情况下保持量化精度。

Method: 论文提出XOR-Trellis方法，针对LLM权重的超低位宽trellis-coded量化。输入端对权重进行trellis编码压缩；核心包含两个模块：其一为超低复杂度trellis反量化器，通过结构化、硬件高效的状态到值映射实现高度并行的运行时重建，同时保持多种重建选择以支撑trellis搜索；其二为曲率感知的离散trellis路径优化，将模型敏感度直接嵌入原始坐标空间的目标函数中以优化离散路径，替代传统Hadamard-based非相干处理。训练/优化目标为在曲率感知目标下最小化量化误差，推理时通过并行反量化器快速重建权重供LLM使用。具体模块交互、数据流细节、参数量、训练与推理资源未在摘要中披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 超低复杂度trellis反量化器：采用结构化、硬件高效的状态到值映射，同时保留多样化的重建选择以支持trellis搜索。；曲率感知的离散trellis路径优化目标：直接在原始坐标空间中反映模型敏感度，避免Hadamard-based非相干处理。

Abstract: Trellis-coded quantization enables high-dimensional compression of large language model (LLM) weights at ultra-low bit widths without the exponentially large codebooks required by conventional vector quantization. Practical deployment, however, presents two challenges: reconstructing compressed weights at sufficient parallel throughput to avoid making dequantization an inference bottleneck, and maintaining quantization accuracy without costly incoherence transformations. We address these challenges with two complementary techniques. First, we introduce an ultra-low-complexity trellis dequantizer that uses a structured, hardware-efficient state-to-value mapping while preserving diverse reconstruction choices for trellis search. Second, we reformulate discrete trellis path optimization with a curvature-aware objective that reflects model sensitivity directly in the original coordinate space. Together, these techniques enable high-quality ultra-low-bit trellis quantization with inexpensive, highly parallel runtime reconstruction and without relying on Hadamard-based incoherence processing.

</details>


### [112] [Every Batch Is Its Own Validation Set: Leave-One-Out Gradient Matching for Online Data Selection in LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00436)
*Hongyu Chen,Xinyi Luo,Ming Zhao,Lin Tang,Zihan Xu,Jing Li,Yuxuan Wang,Haoran Deng,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出LOOM，一种基于留一法梯度匹配的在线数据选择方法，用于LLM微调。通过消除样本内梯度匹配的协方差惩罚，LOOM利用梯度信噪比加权选择子集，在四个任务和七个骨干模型上超越全批次训练及现有方法，且无需验证集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于梯度匹配的在线批次选择方法通常无法超越全批次训练，因为样本内梯度匹配存在协方差惩罚，导致目标偏向噪声样本。

Method: LOOM是一种在线批次选择方法，用于LLM微调。其流程为：在每个训练批次中，对于候选批次中的每个样本，在普通反向传播过程中计算Adam预条件度量下的梯度Gram矩阵。然后基于留一法（LOO）目标，将每个样本的权重设为其梯度信噪比（SNR），并使用贪心算法选择加权子集，保证(1-e^{-γ})近似。选择后的子集用于计算更新并微调模型。该方法无需验证集，且能在线进行。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "准确率提升", "value": "2.3 points", "setting": "Llama-3.1-8B模型，四个微调任务"}, {"metric": "准确率提升", "value": "2.4 points", "setting": "Qwen2.5-7B模型，四个微调任务"}, {"metric": "相比样本内梯度匹配器提升", "value": "2.4 points", "setting": "整体比较"}, {"metric": "相比验证引导方法GREATS和OPUS提升", "value": "1.6-2.0 points", "setting": "整体比较"}, {"metric": "标签噪声选择率", "value": "低于基础率的五分之一", "setting": "注入标签噪声实验"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 揭示样本内梯度匹配的协方差惩罚问题，并证明留一法（LOO）目标是对总体梯度误差的无偏估计；推导出LOO目标的最小化权重与梯度信噪比（SNR）成正比，并给出半批次优于全批次的精确条件；实现LOOM方法，在Adam预条件度量下计算Gram矩阵，并使用贪心算法进行加权子集选择，无需验证集

Abstract: Online batch selection fine-tunes a language model on the most useful part of each candidate batch. Selectors that match the gradient of the candidate batch are attractive because they need no held-out data, yet they rarely beat training on the whole batch. We show why. In-sample gradient matching uses every example as part of its own target, so its objective credits each example with its own gradient noise. This is the covariance penalty that makes training error optimistic, now sitting on the diagonal of the gradient Gram matrix: it steers selection toward the noisiest examples and makes the full batch the best solution the objective can reach. The fix costs nothing. For each example, the other candidates form an independent sample of the data distribution, so removing the diagonal turns the matching objective into an unbiased estimate of the update's error with respect to the population gradient. The minimizer of this leave-one-out objective weights examples by their gradient signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), and whenever per-example SNR is heterogeneous enough, half of a batch yields a lower-error update than the whole batch; we give the exact condition. We build \method{} on this principle. It computes the Gram matrix in the metric of the Adam preconditioner during the ordinary backward pass, selects a weighted subset greedily with a $(1-e^{-\gamma})$ guarantee, and uses no held-out data. Across four fine-tuning tasks and seven backbones from 1.5B to 8B parameters, LOOM improves on full-batch training by 2.3 and 2.4 points on Llama-3.1-8B and Qwen2.5-7B, exceeds every in-sample gradient matcher by 2.4 points and the validation-guided GREATS and OPUS by 1.6--2.0, and selects injected label noise at under a fifth of its base rate.

</details>


### [113] [CommunityKV: Efficient Long-Context Decoding via Graph Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00418)
*Joe McKenna,Anastasios Alexandridis,Nathan Susanj,Jing Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CommunityKV 将训练无关稀疏注意力转化为 token 图上的社区检测问题，利用 prefill 已算出的 QK^T 构图并划分语义社区，配合常数时间的新 token 局部社区分配规则，实现流式解码中的高效稀疏检索；在 Qwen3 与 Llama-3.1 上取得最高 1.25 倍（每 query head 一图）及 1.71 倍（query-group 图聚合）的端到端生成吞吐提升且精度相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文解码中自注意力二次复杂度与 KV cache 线性增长的显存搬运开销；现有训练无关稀疏注意力方法依赖语义粗糙的启发式或难以在解码过程中高效更新的聚类。

Method: CommunityKV 在标准 Transformer 的前缀填充（prefill）阶段复用已经计算出的 QK^T 注意力分数作为边权，构造以 token 为节点的图。对该图执行社区检测/图划分，将 token 分成若干个语义连贯的社区，每个社区作为稀疏注意力检索的基本单元。解码阶段，每个新生成的 token 依据局部更新规则在常数时间内被分配到某个社区，从而无需对全图重新划分即可持续维护社区结构。查询时只检索与当前 query 相关的社区，而非全部 token，从而降低 KV cache 的读取量与注意力计算量。作者提供两种图粒度配置：每个 query head 维护一张图，或对多个 query head 做图聚合（query-group graph aggregation）以共享图结构、减少图维护开销。论文在 Qwen3 与 Llama-3.1 模型上、三个长上下文基准上进行评估，属于训练无关（training-free）方法，不涉及额外大规模训练。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将稀疏注意力形式化为 token 图上的社区检测问题，基于 prefill 阶段已计算的 QK^T 分数构图并划分社区，实现语义连贯的 token 组检索；提出对新生成 token 在常数时间内分配社区的局部更新规则，使流式解码全程可稀疏检索而无需全局重划分；支持每个 query head 一张图，并可进一步做 query-group 图聚合以降低图维护开销并提升吞吐

Abstract: Scaling Transformers to long contexts is constrained by the quadratic cost of self-attention and the linear growth of key-value cache memory transfer. Sparse attention mitigates this by retrieving only relevant tokens, but current approaches either require large-scale training or, within the training-free regime, rely on semantically coarse heuristics or expensive clustering that is difficult to update efficiently during decoding. We introduce CommunityKV, a framework that formulates sparse attention as a community detection problem. CommunityKV constructs a token graph from the $QK^T$ scores already computed during standard prefill, and partitions the graph into communities to enable retrieval of semantically coherent token groups. A local update rule assigns newly generated tokens to communities in constant time, enabling sparse retrieval throughout streaming decoding without global re-partitioning. We evaluate CommunityKV on Qwen3 and Llama-3.1 models across three long-context benchmarks. With one graph per query head, CommunityKV delivers up to $1.25\times$ the end-to-end generation throughput of dense attention, while query-group graph aggregation yields up to $1.71\times$ with comparable accuracy.

</details>


### [114] [EchoPress: Query-Agnostic KV Cache Pruning via Virtual Context Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00412)
*Jiawei Lin,Saibo Geng,Thomas Bourgeat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EchoPress 是一种训练无关的 KV Cache 剪枝方法，通过复用标准 prefill 中已有的 query/key 近似重建注意力来评估缓存重要性，仅重建第一个 chunk 做校准，在 LongBench/RULER 上以 1.7-19.6 倍更低的压缩开销匹配 KVzip 精度，并将 prefill 时间最多降低 2.9 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 KV Cache 剪枝方法 KVzip 通过逐块重建上下文来估计重要性，精度高但需要额外的前向传播（额外 prefill），压缩开销大；而学习式近似则依赖模型特定训练，通用性差。论文旨在无损精度下近似 KVzip 的重建重要性评分，从而消除额外前向开销。

Method: EchoPress 是一种无需训练的 KV Cache 剪枝方法，直接利用标准 prefill 阶段已有的计算结果。其处理流程为：1）对输入上下文执行标准 prefill，得到每层的 query 和 key 表示；2）利用这些 query-key 点积近似重建注意力，从而估计各 key-value 对的重要性，替代 KVzip 中逐块重建所需的额外前向传播；3）为校准相对重要性，仅对第一个 chunk 执行一次轻量重建，将其结果作为剩余上下文重要性评分的参考基准；4）根据校准后的重要性分数按给定比例（如 50%-90%）驱逐低重要性的 KV 对，保留高重要性缓存用于后续解码。整个过程在 prefill 阶段完成，无需额外训练或微调，可与任意使用 KV 缓存的 Transformer 模型集成。由于重要性评分来自已有的注意力计算，额外计算量极小，从而在保持任务精度的同时显著降低压缩开销和总 prefill 时延。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "压缩开销降低倍数", "value": "1.7-19.6 倍", "setting": "LongBench 和 RULER，Qwen3-8B 与 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct，驱逐比例 50%-90%，与 KVzip 对比"}, {"metric": "总 prefill 时间降低倍数", "value": "最高 2.9 倍", "setting": "同上"}, {"metric": "任务准确率", "value": "与 KVzip 相当", "setting": "LongBench 和 RULER，驱逐比例 50%-90%"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 分析并揭示 KVzip 的重要性评分主要可由标准 prefill 中已计算的 query-key 注意力近似表示；提出 EchoPress：利用标准 prefill 的 query/key 近似重建注意力，无需额外前向传播、无需训练；仅重建第一个 chunk 以校准剩余上下文的相对重要性，从而将额外开销控制在极低水平

Abstract: KV cache pruning reduces long-context inference memory usage by evicting less important key-value pairs. KVzip estimates importance through context reconstruction: prompting a model to repeat the context chunk by chunk. This achieves strong compression quality at the cost of additional forward passes. Learned approximations reduce this cost but require model-specific training. We analyze how KVzip identifies important cached information and show how to approximate its reconstruction scores using information already computed during prefill. These findings motivate EchoPress, a training-free method that approximates reconstruction attention using queries and keys from standard prefill. For each request, it reconstructs only the first chunk to calibrate importance scores for the remaining context. Experiments on LongBench and RULER with Qwen3-8B and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct show that EchoPress matches KVzip in task accuracy across eviction ratios from 50% to 90%, while reducing compression overhead by a factor of 1.7-19.6 and total prefill time by a factor of up to 2.9. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [115] [Metacognitive Reasoning in Energy Based Models using Instance Based Learning Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00399)
*Tailia Malloy,Prateek Kumar Rajput,Serge Lionel Nikiema,Cleotilde Gonzalez,Tegawendé F. Bissyandé*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出MERITED框架，将实例学习理论（IBLT）与能量基模型（EBM）结合，实现基于不确定性的动态推理计算分配，并开源了191M参数推理EBM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的AI系统无法在生成输出前估计不确定性，也无法根据不确定性动态分配推理计算资源，难以实现类似人类元认知的资源分配过程。

Method: MERITED框架以能量基模型（EBM）为核心推理引擎。EBM通过能量函数对输入与输出进行联合建模，能量值可解释为不确定性或置信度，从而在生成输出前提供不确定性估计。框架引入实例学习理论（IBLT）模块，该模块根据历史经验（实例）和当前不确定性，决定分配给当前推理任务的能量最小化迭代次数或计算步数。具体流程为：输入问题后，EBM计算初始能量状态，IBLT模型基于该状态和过往实例预测所需计算量，然后动态调度EBM的推理迭代，直至达到停止条件。训练目标包括EBM的能量函数优化（如对比散度或最大似然）以及IBLT模型对计算分配策略的学习，后者可能通过监督或强化学习从经验中优化。推理时，系统根据IBLT预测动态分配计算资源，实现元认知控制，避免对所有输入均匀分配计算。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出MERITED框架，将实例学习理论（IBLT）与能量基模型（EBM）结合，实现基于不确定性的元认知计算分配。；训练并开放共享一个191M参数的推理EBM，支持可解释的不确定性建模和动态计算分配。；实现IBLT模型驱动的动态计算分配机制，在保持计算效率的同时实现元认知控制。

Abstract: Metacognition involves reasoning about cognitive processes themselves. An example is in resource allocation where we choose how much time and effort to put into a reasoning task before we begin based on our confidence. Current Artificial Intelligence (AI) systems that rely on Large Language Models (LLMs) cannot estimate their uncertainty about an output without first responding, and cannot dynamically allocate resources to producing an output, making this type of metacognitive process difficult. A recently proposed alternative to classic transformer architectures that addresses these two concerns is the Energy Based Model (EBM) which allows for interpretable uncertainty modeling and dynamic allocation of compute resources. While EBMs can allow for control of these two processes, the actual metacognitive task of determining compute allocation based on uncertainty is not directly addressed. Instance-Based Learning Theory (IBLT) provides an approach to modeling human-like decisions from experience that has previously been applied to predicting human metacognitive reasoning. In this paper we introduce a framework for MEtacognitive Reasoning with Instance-based Learning Theory and Energy Dynamics (MERITED). Grounded in IBLT, this framework allows for control of the computational effort allocated in an EBM to allow for metacognitive control over reasoning effort based on uncertainty while remaining computationally efficient. This work has two main contributions, the training and open weight sharing of a 191M parameter reasoning EBM, and an implementation of the MERITED framework for dynamic compute allocation using an IBL model.

</details>


### [116] [Representation Transitions Reveal Emerging Safety Risks in Multi-Turn LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00400)
*Haoyu Wang,Wei Zhao,Yedi Zhang,Christopher M. Poskitt,Jun Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DART利用LLM智能体内部表示转移的累积信号，通过去噪对比安全方向，实现多轮攻击的运行时检测与归因，在多个模型和基准上显著降低攻击成功率，且开销轻量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮攻击通过组合看似无害的动作产生有害结果，现有孤立评估动作或状态的防御方法难以应对。

Method: DART是一个运行时框架。输入为LLM智能体多轮交互中的内部表示。核心流程：首先，在每一轮上下文更新后，提取智能体的内部表示，计算表示转移。然后，使用去噪的对比安全方向来评估转移是否为有害。具体地，通过将良性流量锚定在零并移除其领先变化方向来去噪安全方向，从而减少良性表示漂移的干扰。若检测到显著的累积表示转移，则触发归因机制识别触发上下文，并施加针对性的提醒干预，以降低攻击成功率。训练目标为对比学习安全方向，优化目标是使有害转移在该方向上投影显著而良性转移接近零。推理时无需辅助模型，计算开销低。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 发现有害行为在智能体内部表示中表现为累积的表示转移，且触发上下文可从同一信号识别；提出去噪对比安全方向，将良性流量锚定在零并移除其主变分方向，无需运行时成本；提出DART运行时框架，检测和归因表示偏移并进行针对性提醒干预

Abstract: Multi-turn attacks on agentic systems can compose individually permissible actions into harmful outcomes, challenging defenses that assess actions or states in isolation. We show that such attacks leave a detectable signature in the agent's internal representations: harmful behavior emerges as an accumulated representation transition across context updates, whose triggering context can be identified from the same signal. We further find that naive aggregation is confounded by benign representation drift, as a contrastive safety direction need not assign zero to benign transitions. We address this by denoising the direction, anchoring benign traffic at zero and removing its leading variation directions, with no runtime cost. These findings motivate DART, a runtime framework that detects and attributes representation shifts and intervenes with targeted reminders. Across six models and two multi-turn benchmarks, DART reduces attack success from 84% to 25% on MT-AgentRisk, catching every attack at a mean false-alarm rate of 12%, and from 97% to 52% on ASEval, at costs in benign non-refusal of 8% and 0%, respectively. On MT-AgentRisk, it outperforms ToolShield, the state-of-the-art multi-turn defense, on all six models: under the same protocol, ToolShield reaches only 55%. Denoising is critical: on ASEval, the undenoised monitor catches only 7%-40% of attacks, while the denoised monitor catches 60%-85%. The same monitor covers single-turn indirect injection without modification and adds only 0.14-0.56 s overhead per monitored step without requiring an auxiliary model, making it a lightweight complement to computation-heavy speculative defenses.

</details>


### [117] [T2SPO: Trajectory-to-Step Policy Optimization for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00388)
*Bo-Wen Zhang,Junwei He,Maoqi Liu,Feiran Li,Song-Lin Lv,Wentao Ma,Rongyi Lin,Shuhan Zhong,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: T2SPO 利用成功轨迹构造剩余距离目标，并用冻结的 TabPFN 回归器在 rollout 各状态估计到成功的剩余距离，以距离变化量作为步级辅助信用，配合 GRPO 提升 LLM 智能体在 ALFWorld、WebShop 上的任务成功率（1.5B/7B）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步交互任务中奖励通常只有最终结果信号，无法指示哪些中间决策真正推进了任务，导致步级信用分配困难。

Method: T2SPO 的输入是智能体与环境交互产生的多步轨迹（状态序列与最终结果）。流程分两阶段：其一，从成功轨迹中抽取剩余距离目标——将轨迹中每个访问状态标注为距离成功的步数/剩余距离，并与该状态的表示配对，形成上下文示例集合；其二，在新 rollout 的每一步，将当前状态表示与成功轨迹示例一起作为上下文，送入预训练的 TabPFN 回归器（参数冻结），回归器输出该状态到成功的剩余距离估计。相邻状态估计值之差即为该步的辅助信用（credit）信号，与任务级结果奖励共同构成策略优化目标（以 GRPO 为基线进行替换/增强）。随着训练推进，新完成的轨迹被加入回归器的上下文，刷新其条件分布，等价于在不更新回归器参数的情况下在线吸收新经验。最终策略由该复合信号驱动更新，用于多步交互任务的决策，推理时直接执行学到的策略，回归器仅在训练阶段用于生成辅助信用。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "overall task success", "value": "报告在 ALFWorld 与 WebShop 上相较 GRPO 一致提升（摘要未给出具体数值）", "setting": "1.5B 与 7B 语言模型，ALFWorld 与 WebShop 环境"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 用成功轨迹构建剩余距离（remaining-distance）监督目标，并与沿途状态表示配对；使用预训练 TabPFN 回归器基于上下文示例，在不更新参数的情况下估计新 rollout 每个状态到成功的剩余距离；以连续状态间剩余距离估计的变化量作为辅助信用信号，与任务级监督共同指导策略学习；训练过程中新完成轨迹不断刷新回归器上下文，实现经验的无参数增量吸收

Abstract: Reinforcement learning enables large language model (LLM) agents to learn multi-step behaviors through interaction with their environments. However, rewards in many interactive tasks reflect only the final outcome, providing limited guidance on which intermediate decisions advance the task. Successful training trajectories contain intermediate states that can provide supervision for subsequent interactions. We introduce Trajectory-to-Step Policy Optimization (T2SPO), a method that uses past interaction trajectories to provide step-level feedback for policy learning. T2SPO derives remaining-distance targets from successful trajectories and pairs them with representations of the states visited along the way. Conditioned on these examples, a pretrained TabPFN regressor estimates the remaining distance to success at each state of a new rollout. Changes in this distance estimate across consecutive states yield auxiliary credit for agent steps alongside task-level supervision. As training proceeds, newly completed trajectories refresh the estimator's context, incorporating new experience without updating its parameters. Experiments with 1.5B and 7B language models on ALFWorld and WebShop show that T2SPO consistently improves overall task success over GRPO.

</details>


### [118] [Forking: Sudden Overfitting Under Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00394)
*Shanbin Yu,Shaoyang Guo,Haoran Zhao,Danni Yu,Ziming Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了数据回放下的泛化失败新现象"forking"：模型出现过编码n-gram记忆分支时，训练和验证损失在epoch边界急剧分离。在NanoGPT和DeepSeek风格Engram模型中复现，揭示了低频上下文主导、n-gram子空间放大等机制，并指出更大训练预算和拥挤表可抑制。对LLM训练稳定性有参考价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据回放场景下，模型出现过拟合记忆分支导致训练和验证损失急剧分离（forking）现象，其机制与缓解条件尚不明确。

Method: 论文在受控的vanilla NanoGPT和DeepSeek风格Engram模型上研究forking现象。NanoGPT是一个标准的自回归Transformer语言模型。Engram是DeepSeek风格模型中的n-gram记忆模块。训练采用数据回放（replay）策略，即重复遍历相同数据。在训练过程中，模型内部形成一个过编码的n-gram记忆分支，该分支在epoch边界导致训练损失和验证损失急剧分离。机制上，重复更新会加强训练中观察到的n-gram延续的概率，同时抑制未见延续的概率，使得未见延续的损失随每次遍历增加。n-gram模块创建了弱交互的上下文特异性子空间，放大了这一效应。低频上下文对损失缺口的贡献最大。更大的训练预算和更拥挤的哈希表可以抑制forking。论文还在短预算、高度重复的监督微调（SFT）和类强化学习（RL-like）训练范式中观察到了forking。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 定义并系统研究了forking现象：在数据回放中，具备过编码n-gram记忆分支的模型在epoch边界出现训练/验证损失急剧分离；在受控vanilla NanoGPT和DeepSeek风格Engram模型中复现了forking；揭示了机制：重复更新强化训练中观察到的延续，抑制未见延续的概率，且n-gram模块创建弱交互的上下文特异性子空间放大该效应；发现低频上下文贡献最大差距，而更大训练预算和拥挤表可抑制forking；在短预算、高度重复的SFT和类RL训练范式中也观察到forking

Abstract: This paper studies forking, a generalization failure discovered in NanoGPT autoresearch. Under data replay, models with an over-encoding n-gram memory branch show a sharp separation of training and validation loss at epoch boundaries, resembling the shape of forks. We study this phenomenon in a controlled vanilla NanoGPT setting and reproduce it in a DeepSeek-style model with Engram. Mechanistically, repeated updates sharpen the continuations observed in training while suppressing the probability of unseen continuations, whose loss grows with each pass. The n-gram module creates weakly interacting context-specific subspaces, amplifying this effect. Low-frequency contexts contribute most of the gap, whereas larger training budgets and heavily crowded tables suppress it. We also observe forking in short-budget, heavily repeated SFT and RL-like regimes. The contributions of this paper are twofold: (1) Forking reveals yet another curious phenomenon in deep learning, in addition to grokking and double descent. (2) Forking is an unexpected and unpleasant by-product of tricks proposed by autoresearch agents. While these agents produce an enormous number of results that seem useful, we should always be careful with their results.

</details>


### [119] [FAER: Auditable Utility-Aligned Trajectory Replay for Language Model Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00385)
*Miaobo Hu,Shuhao Hu,Xiaobo Guo,Xin Wang,Bokun Wang,Tianshu Fu,Daren Zha,Jun Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FAER 提出可审计的全轨迹重放框架，将缓存级反馈与下游学习器效用区分开；FAER-UTILITY 用校准块上的优化器感知虚拟更新构造效用面，在 Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct + GSM8K 上以更低/相当 token 成本取得优于固定选择器、uniform 与 format-feedback 的质量，并记录完整校准与训练开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缓存轨迹重放选择器通常依据格式反馈、置信度、新鲜度或响应长度排序，但缓存级正确性与下游学习器效用是不同目标，存在选择到学习的差距（selection-to-learning gap）；论文要形式化并以可审计方式缩小该差距。

Method: FAER 是一个可审计的全轨迹重放框架，输入为已缓存的训练轨迹（trajectory）及其可观测元数据字段。框架首先定义审计契约：在评估标签加入之前冻结观测字段与重放轨迹，保证选择过程与评估过程可分离、可复查。选择器分为两类。其一是训练无关的固定选择器（fixed selector），不依赖学习器信号，作为协议基线，用于度量缓存级反馈与下游效用之间的差距。其二是学习器感知的 FAER-UTILITY 选择器，在不相交的校准块上拟合，使用可丢弃的优化器感知虚拟更新来构造幅度感知的效用面（magnitude-aware utility surface），从而把「选择哪些轨迹对当前学习器最有帮助」转化为可估计的效用排序，而不是用格式反馈、置信度、新鲜度或长度等代理信号。论文还报告归一化梯度对齐作为基线，用以对照虚拟更新效用面的效果。训练目标是在给定更新次数下最大化下游学习器质量；推理/服务路径为：读取缓存轨迹与冻结元数据，由选择器（固定基线或 FAER-UTILITY）输出重放顺序/子集，再交给学习者进行固定步数更新，随后在 GSM8K 上评测质量。审计契约与成本记录（target-run tokens、GPU-hours）贯穿整个流程，使选择决策和资源开销均可审计。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "quality", "value": "0.6329", "setting": "GSM8K，Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct，固定选择器，128 次更新，匹配学习器研究"}, {"metric": "quality", "value": "0.5482", "setting": "GSM8K，Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct，uniform 选择器，128 次更新"}, {"metric": "quality", "value": "0.6037", "setting": "GSM8K，Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct，format-feedback 选择器，128 次更新"}, {"metric": "metadata-only cross-fitted calibration quality", "value": "0.6476±0.0139（中位数 0.6481；配对 95% 区间 [+0.079,+0.122]）", "setting": "八个随机种子，63,276 target-run tokens，记录完整成本 189,642 tokens 与 3.48 GPU-hours（含校准）"}, {"metric": "FAER-UTILITY quality", "value": "0.6624", "setting": "62,844 target-run tokens，4.26 GPU-hours"}, {"metric": "format-feedback 选中记录的 correctness", "value": "0.6953", "setting": "与固定选择器的 0.3594 相比，体现下游排序不同"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 形式化选择到学习的差距（selection-to-learning gap），区分缓存级正确性与下游学习器效用；提出 FAER 可审计全轨迹重放框架，包含训练无关的固定选择器作为协议基线；提出 FAER-UTILITY 学习器感知选择器，在不相交校准块上拟合；使用可丢弃的优化器感知虚拟更新提供幅度感知的效用面；将归一化梯度对齐作为基线报告，便于对比；审计契约在评估标签加入前冻结观测字段与重放轨迹

Abstract: Replay selectors often rank cached trajectories by format feedback, confidence, freshness, or response length, although cache-level correctness and downstream learner utility are distinct objectives. We formalize this selection-to-learning gap and introduce FAER as an auditable full-trajectory replay framework. Its training-free fixed selector is a protocol baseline; FAER-UTILITY is the learner-aware selector fitted on disjoint calibration blocks. The normalized gradient alignment is reported as a baseline, while a disposable optimizer-aware virtual update supplies a magnitude-aware utility surface. The audit contract freezes observed fields and replay traces before evaluation labels are joined. On GSM8K with Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct, the matched learner study reports quality 0.6329 for the fixed selector, compared with 0.5482 for uniform and 0.6037 for format-feedback under 128 updates. Metadata-only cross-fitted calibration reaches $0.6476\!\pm\!0.0139$ over eight seeds (median 0.6481; paired 95% interval $[+0.079,+0.122]$) at 63,276 target-run tokens; its recorded full cost is 189,642 tokens and 3.48 GPU-hours including calibration. The completed FAER-UTILITY row reaches 0.6624 at 62,844 target-run tokens and 4.26 GPU-hours. Format-feedback selects records with correctness 0.6953, compared with 0.3594 for the fixed selector, despite the different downstream ranking. The completed comparison surfaces report the learner-aware ablation, same-seed gap, policy-optimization rows, and strict zero-shot transfer.

</details>


### [120] [MoRA: MoE Pruning via Router Bias Learning and Expert Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00367)
*Yushuai Sun,Zikun Zhou,Lin Gao,Jun Yu,Wenjie Pei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MoRA提出通过可学习路由偏置和专家近似进行MoE结构化专家剪枝，在三个主流MoE模型上移除25%/50%专家后于九个零样本基准优于SOTA，可降低部署内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构化MoE专家剪枝方法的专家排序准则与模型性能不对齐，或依赖计算昂贵的专家子集搜索，并且通常忽略保留专家之间的路由行为冗余，导致剪枝后模型性能下降。

Method: MoRA是一个结构化MoE专家剪枝框架。输入为MoE模型的各层路由专家池。第一步，为每个专家引入一个可学习的路由偏置，并联合优化这些偏置，目标函数由语言建模损失和路由多样性正则项组成；该正则项鼓励所选专家具有多样的路由偏好，从而在锐化路由概率分布的同时识别对模型性能关键的专家并减少保留专家间的路由冗余。第二步，根据学习到的路由偏置确定要剪枝的专家，执行结构化剪枝。第三步，作为剪枝后增强，引入专家近似机制：利用剩余专家通过仿射变换来近似被剪枝专家的输出，以补偿剪枝造成的性能损失。最终得到内存占用更低的剪枝MoE模型，推理时仅需加载保留的专家。论文未披露具体的训练数据、硬件、时延或吞吐量。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "九个零样本基准上的整体性能", "value": "优于现有最先进剪枝算法", "setting": "在Qwen3-30B-A3B、DeepSeek-V2-Lite、Moonlight-16B-A3B上分别移除25%和50%的路由专家"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 为每个专家引入可学习的路由偏置，并通过最小化语言建模损失与路由多样性正则项来优化，从而锐化路由概率分布，识别对模型性能关键的专家，同时鼓励选择路由偏好多样的专家。；提出专家近似机制作为剪枝后增强：利用剩余专家通过仿射变换近似被剪枝专家的输出，进一步改善剪枝模型性能。；在Qwen3-30B-A3B、DeepSeek-V2-Lite、Moonlight-16B-A3B上各MoE层移除25%和50%的路由专家，并在九个零样本基准上优于现有最先进剪枝算法。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models enable parameter scaling with limited per-token computation by activating only a small subset of experts for each token, but deploying them still requires loading the complete expert pool into memory. Structured expert pruning can effectively reduce the memory usage by removing experts. However, existing pruning methods either use expert ranking criteria that are not well aligned with model performance or rely on effective expert subset searching that is computationally expensive. Moreover, these methods typically overlook the routing-behavior redundancy among the retained experts. In this paper, we propose MoE Pruning via Router Bias Learning and Expert Approximation (MoRA), a framework for structured MoE expert pruning. We introduce a learnable router bias for each expert and optimize these biases by minimizing the language-modeling loss and a routing-diversity regularizer. The learned router biases sharpen the routing probability distributions to identify experts critical to model performance while encouraging the selection of experts with diverse routing preferences. In addition, we introduce an expert approximation mechanism as a post-pruning enhancement. It leverages the remaining experts to approximate the outputs of pruned experts by affine transformation, further improving the performance of the pruned model. We evaluate MoRA on Qwen3-30B-A3B, DeepSeek-V2-Lite, and Moonlight-16B-A3B, removing 25\% and 50\% of the routed experts in each MoE layer. Extensive experiments on nine zero-shot benchmarks show that MoRA outperforms state-of-the-art pruning algorithms. Our code will be released.

</details>


### [121] [Attention Manifolds: Steering or Blocking Language Models by Editing Learned B-Spline Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00257)
*Naveen Mysore*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出注意力流形，用可学习B样条曲面根据query-key交互调制value维度，在LLaMA 3.2上以0.3%参数开销降低困惑度2-2.5点，并实现模型转向与安全阻断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer注意力中，源token向所有接收者发送相同的value向量，query仅决定注意力权重而非提取内容，导致模型无法根据query-key交互动态调整value信息。

Method: 在标准Transformer注意力中，每个value维度d由query维度q_d和key维度k_d通过一个可学习的张量积三次B样条曲面S_d(q_d, k_d)进行调制。该曲面初始化为零，因此初始时对value无影响，保持预训练模型行为。在前向传播中，注意力权重仍由query-key点积计算，但value向量在加权求和前会逐维度乘以S_d(q_d, k_d)的取值。训练时，只有B样条曲面的控制点被更新，基础模型参数冻结，参数增加约0.3%。推理时，曲面作为额外计算步骤，可动态调整每个value维度的贡献。通过反转某层曲面系数，可改变模型输出；将系数设为-1形成'注意力墙'，阻断特定维度value流。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "WikiText-2验证困惑度", "value": "降低2-2.5点", "setting": "LLaMA 3.2-1B-Instruct和3B-Instruct，增加0.3%参数"}, {"metric": "贪心解码输出变化率", "value": "69%（1B）和83%（3B）", "setting": "112个多样提示"}, {"metric": "歧义/多义词输入输出变化率", "value": "94-100%", "setting": "112个多样提示"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出可学习的2D B样条曲面S_d(q_d, k_d)，基于query-key交互调节每个value维度；曲面初始化为零，保持预训练行为不变，实现无损微调；通过反转曲面系数实现模型转向（steering），9/10提示改变输出（KL约0.010）；设置曲面系数为-1创建'注意力墙'，阻断特定维度的value流，初步展示安全导向的流形塑形

Abstract: In standard transformer attention, a source token sends the same value vector to every receiver. The query determines \emph{how much} to attend but not \emph{what} to extract. This work introduces \textbf{attention manifolds}: learned 2D B-spline surfaces $S_d(q_d, k_d)$ that modulate each value dimension based on the query-key interaction. Each surface is a tensor-product cubic B-spline initialized to zero, preserving pretrained behavior. Applied to LLaMA 3.2-1B-Instruct and 3B-Instruct, attention manifolds reduce WikiText-2 validation perplexity by 2--2.5 points with 0.3\% parameter overhead. Across 112 diverse prompts, surfaces change greedy-decoded output for 69\% (1B) to 83\% (3B) of cases, with the strongest effects on ambiguous and polysemous inputs (94--100\% change rate). The surfaces improve output quality: correcting factual errors (\emph{``the CAP theorem has three main components''} $\to$ \emph{``it is impossible to guarantee all three''}), increasing precision (\emph{``impossible to know certain properties''} $\to$ \emph{``impossible to know both position and momentum''}), and adding specificity (a generic quote $\to$ an attributed Saint Augustine citation, consistently at both scales). The learned surfaces are also mechanically editable: inverting a layer's coefficients changes greedy output for 9/10 prompts (KL~0.010), providing a geometric mechanism for model steering. Setting surface coefficients to $-1$ creates ``attention walls'' that block value flow through specific dimensions. In a preliminary experiment, a layer-wide wall redirects an explosive-device prompt from specific instructions to general educational content, suggesting a path toward safety-oriented manifold shaping.

</details>


### [122] [Constant-Memory Recall: Learned Associations in a Fixed Matrix State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00232)
*Samuel Larson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文在固定 32 KiB 循环矩阵状态下用小型 DeltaNet 变体学习 32 个新键值配对的联想召回，三个种子平均准确率达 99.95%，填充至 1,798 token 上下文仍近乎完美；置零首个记忆块则召回消失。参数匹配的向量与 Transformer 基线接近随机，阻碍了内存效率比较。属于固定内存 LLM 记忆机制的探索性学术工作，无公司或生产证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在推理时存储不增长（固定大小循环状态）的前提下，模型能否可靠地存储与召回序列内的新键值配对，以及这种召回如何依赖任务设计与训练。

Method: 模型为小型 DeltaNet 变体：输入为 token 序列中的键值配对与填充文本，键带有固定的、token 专属的偏置（key bias），用于增强键之间的可区分性。循环状态是一个固定大小（32 KiB）的矩阵，作为联想记忆使用，并按记忆块组织。前向过程中，每个 token 以增量式规则（DeltaNet 的 delta 更新）把键值关联写入矩阵状态，随后查询阶段用给定键从矩阵中读出对应值，并在该序列出现的取值集合中做选择（多项选择式召回）。训练目标即让模型记住每条序列中的 32 个新键值配对并在查询时正确取回；通过三个随机种子评估稳定性，并加入填充文本把查询前上下文拉长到 1,798 token 以检验上下文长度是否影响召回。消融通过将第一个记忆块置零来检验该块是否承担召回功能。对照实验采用参数匹配的向量记忆基线与 Transformer 基线，用以评估同等参数量下普通架构是否也能完成该任务。推理路径保持固定内存占用，矩阵状态不随序列长度或配对数量增长（在实验设定范围内）。论文未披露具体学习率、优化器、层数、隐藏维度及解码硬件等细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均准确率", "value": "99.95%（三个训练种子均值）", "setting": "在序列自身取值集合中做选择，32 对新键值配对，32 KiB 循环矩阵状态的小型 DeltaNet 变体"}, {"metric": "召回准确率", "value": "近乎完美（未给出精确数值）", "setting": "填充文本将查询前上下文扩展至 1,798 token 且不增加配对数量"}, {"metric": "召回准确率", "value": "降至随机水平附近（未给出精确数值）", "setting": "将第一个记忆块置零的消融实验"}, {"metric": "48 对配对测试准确率", "value": "接近随机水平", "setting": "探索性实验，使用主训练预算的四分之一，未定位到容量上限"}, {"metric": "基线准确率", "value": "接近随机水平", "setting": "参数匹配的向量基线与 Transformer 基线（Transformer 经额外训练搜索后仍如此）"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 采用小型 DeltaNet 变体，并用固定、token 专属的 key bias 强化键的区分性，从而在固定 32 KiB 循环矩阵状态中完成 32 对新键值配对召回；通过填充文本把查询前上下文扩展到 1,798 token（不新增配对）仍保持近乎完美的召回，验证上下文长度与配对数量的解耦；对首个记忆块置零的消融显示召回完全消失，给出记忆块功能定位的证据；报告参数匹配的向量基线与 Transformer 基线在相同任务上接近随机水平，指出基线失败阻碍了内存效率比较

Abstract: Fixed-size recurrent memory limits storage growth during inference, but successful recall depends on the task and training. We study a small DeltaNet variant with fixed token-specific key biases, trained to remember 32 new key-value pairings per sequence. With 32 KiB of recurrent matrix state, it achieves 99.95% mean accuracy across three training seeds when choosing among the sequence's values. Recall remains near perfect when filler extends the pre-query context to 1,798 tokens without adding pairings. Zeroing the first memory block removes this recall. An exploratory 48-pair test remains near chance after one quarter of the primary training budget and does not locate a capacity limit. Parameter-matched vector and Transformer baselines remain near chance, including the Transformer after additional training searches. This unresolved baseline failure prevents a memory-efficiency comparison.

</details>


### [123] [Format-Aware Fusion for Fast FP4 Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00053)
*Robert Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出格式感知融合，通过协同设计量化生产者、缩放域与消费者布局，实现FP4张量核心的端到端LLM预训练加速。在Llama-3 8B模型160B token训练中，自定义FP4路由达到37.9K tokens/s/GPU，高于bfloat16的18.8K和TE nvfp的27.6K；mxfp配合随机舍入和Hadamard预条件达到37.2K tokens/s/GPU，最终损失仅比bfloat16高2.11%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FP4张量核心在矩阵乘法中加速明显，但缩放计算、操作数打包、布局构建以及保存反向状态的开销可能抵消收益，如何设计端到端的FP4预训练方案以真正获得吞吐提升同时保持训练稳定性。

Method: 该方法采用格式感知融合（format-aware fusion），协同设计量化的三个关键部分：量化生产者（producer）、缩放域（scale domain）和消费者布局（consumer layout）。具体支持三种FP4格式：原生mxfp、全局nvfp以及协作线程数组（CTA）局部nvfp。训练流程基于Llama-3系列8B模型，输出投影使用bfloat16，交叉熵采用编译版本。在反向传播中，对mxfp采用行梯度随机舍入（row-gradient stochastic rounding），并对权重梯度应用固定符号32值Hadamard预条件。训练共进行160B token。评估比较了不同加速器上的bfloat16和Transformer Engine nvfp基线，展示了自定义路由的最高吞吐。训练目标为标准语言建模损失。推理或服务流程未披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "训练吞吐", "value": "18.8K tokens/s/GPU (bfloat16)", "setting": "Llama-3 8B预训练，匹配同加速器"}, {"metric": "训练吞吐", "value": "27.6K tokens/s/GPU (Transformer Engine nvfp)", "setting": "同上"}, {"metric": "训练吞吐", "value": "37.9K tokens/s/GPU (最快自定义路由)", "setting": "同上"}, {"metric": "训练吞吐", "value": "37.2K tokens/s/GPU (mxfp + 随机舍入 + Hadamard预条件)", "setting": "同上，86.3% bfloat16模型FLOP利用率"}, {"metric": "最终训练损失相对bfloat16终点", "value": "2.11% above", "setting": "mxfp配方，160B tokens"}, {"metric": "最终训练损失相对bfloat16终点", "value": "0.87% above", "setting": "Transformer Engine配方+四个最终bfloat16块，27.1K tokens/s/GPU"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 格式感知融合（format-aware fusion）：将每个量化生产者与其缩放域及消费者布局协同设计，支持原生mxfp、全局nvfp和协作线程数组局部nvfp。；针对mxfp的行梯度随机舍入（row-gradient stochastic rounding）和固定符号32值Hadamard权重梯度预条件。；在Llama-3系列8B模型上完成160B token的FP4预训练，并系统比较了不同加速器、不同FP4配方下的吞吐与损失终点。

Abstract: Four-bit floating-point (FP4) Tensor Cores accelerate matrix multiplication, but scale computation, operand packing, layout construction, and saved backward state can erase the gain. We present \emph{format-aware fusion}, which co-designs each quantization producer with its scale domain and consumer layout for native \mxfp{}, global \nvfp{}, and cooperative-thread-array-local \nvfp{}. We evaluate Llama-3-family 8B pretraining through 160 billion tokens using bfloat16 output projections and compiled cross entropy. In matched same-accelerator probes, bfloat16 and Transformer Engine \nvfp{} reach 18.8K and 27.6K tokens/s/GPU, while our fastest custom route reaches 37.9K. \mxfp{} with row-gradient stochastic rounding and fixed-sign 32-value Hadamard weight-gradient preconditioning reaches 37.2K tokens/s/GPU (86.3\% bfloat16 model FLOP utilization) and ends 2.11\% above the raw bfloat16 training-loss endpoint. A Transformer Engine recipe with four final bfloat16 blocks ends 0.87\% above bfloat16 at 27.1K tokens/s/GPU. Downstream rankings differ from training-loss rankings, showing that FP4 outcomes depend jointly on scale contract, operand, and execution path.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [From Rules to Neural Graphs: Scalable Structured Prediction for Patent Prior Art Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01553)
*Nikolai Zenovkin,Sebastian Björkqvist*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: This paper introduces a neural parser for constructing structured invention graphs from long patent texts, replacing brittle rule-based parsers. It adapts biaffine attention with a sliding window to handle >40k tokens efficiently, and distills from 1M rule-parsed documents to surpass the teacher at 3× lower inference cost. The neural graphs improve citation recall in a downstream Graph Transformer retrieval system by 0.5% on short queries and 1.1% on full documents.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Patent documents routinely exceed tens of thousands of tokens, causing most neural retrieval models to truncate inputs and lose effectiveness. Existing graph-based retrieval relies on brittle rule-based parsers to construct invention graphs, which do not generalize and are expensive.

Method: The system consists of two main stages: (1) a neural parser that converts raw patent text into a structured invention graph, and (2) a downstream Graph Transformer retrieval system that uses these graphs for prior art search. The neural parser adapts biaffine attention from dependency parsing: given patent text, it predicts a graph where nodes are invention entities and edges are relations. The biaffine scoring is made local by restricting pairwise attention to a sliding window of size w, reducing complexity from O(n^2) to O(n·w). Because local and global scoring share the same weights, the model can be trained on short sequences and deployed on very long documents (>40,000 tokens) without retraining. The parser is distilled from 1 million documents parsed by an existing rule-based parser (the teacher), and the distilled neural model surpasses the teacher while being 3× faster at inference. The output graphs are then fed into a Graph Transformer retrieval system, which encodes the graph structure and performs retrieval; evaluation shows improved citation recall on both short queries and full documents.

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "citation recall", "value": "+0.5%", "setting": "short queries, downstream Graph Transformer retrieval"}, {"metric": "citation recall", "value": "+1.1%", "setting": "full documents, downstream Graph Transformer retrieval"}, {"metric": "inference cost", "value": "3× lower", "setting": "neural parser compared to rule-based teacher"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: A neural parser that adapts biaffine attention from dependency parsing to directly predict invention graphs from patent text, replacing rule-based parsers.；Local biaffine attention with a sliding window reducing pairwise scoring complexity from O(n^2) to O(n·w), enabling training on short sequences and deployment on documents over 40,000 tokens without retraining.；Distillation of the neural parser from 1 million rule-parsed documents, achieving 3× lower inference cost while surpassing the teacher's quality.；Demonstration that neural graphs improve citation recall by 0.5% on short queries and 1.1% on full documents when integrated into a downstream Graph Transformer retrieval system.

Abstract: Patent search requires processing documents routinely exceeding tens of thousands of tokens. Most neural retrieval approaches operate on truncated inputs, limiting their effectiveness. Graph-based retrieval addresses this by representing each patent as a structured invention graph, but constructing these graphs relies on brittle rule-based parsers. We present the neural parser, which adapts biaffine attention from dependency parsing to predict invention graphs directly from patent text. Our local biaffine attention restricts pairwise scoring to a sliding window, reducing complexity from $O(n^2)$ to $O(n \cdot w)$. Since local and global scoring share the same weights, the model trains on short sequences and deploys on documents exceeding 40,000 tokens without retraining. Distilled from 1 million rule-parsed documents, it surpasses its teacher at 3$\times$ lower inference cost: neural graphs improve citation recall by 0.5% on short queries and 1.1% on full documents in a downstream Graph Transformer retrieval system.

</details>


### [125] [Learning to structure data from user-generated thematic corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01463)
*Elishay Avram,Oren Glickman,Elad Yom-Tov*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出一种基于LLM的迭代框架，从社交媒体等主题语料中自动发现域特定属性模式并抽取结构化数据，无需预定义本体。在5个健康相关Reddit社区上，属性发现与人类标注一致性达61%（接近人工间一致性62%），结构类型分配准确率82%，值抽取F1为0.8。框架还支持使用较小LLM进行值抽取以权衡成本与准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在没有预定义本体的前提下，自动从主题语料（如社交媒体社区）中发现并提取领域特定的属性模式（schema），并填充结构化数据。

Method: 框架采用迭代的LLM驱动流程：输入为目标主题语料（如Reddit社区文本），首先通过LLM诱导候选属性（attribute induction），然后迭代地合并语义重叠的属性（consolidation），并为每个属性分配结构类型（如布尔、数值、字符串等）。这些步骤产生属性模式，形成本体（ontology），随后利用LLM从语料中抽取属性值填充本体。训练目标为无需预定义本体，通过LLM的推理能力自动发现模式。推理时，可以直接使用大LLM或替换为较小的指令微调LLM进行值抽取，并评估准确率-成本权衡。整体流程迭代进行，直到属性集稳定（通常少于10轮）。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "属性发现与人类标注一致性", "value": "61%", "setting": "在5个健康相关Reddit社区上，与人工识别的属性对比"}, {"metric": "结构类型分配准确率", "value": "82%", "setting": "在5个健康相关Reddit社区上"}, {"metric": "值抽取F1", "value": "0.8", "setting": "在5个健康相关Reddit社区上，与人类标注对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出全自动迭代框架，利用LLM逐步发现、合并语义重叠的属性，并赋予结构类型，无需预定义本体。；框架能够使用较小LLM进行值抽取，并评估相对于大LLM的准确率损失，支持准确率-成本权衡。；在健康相关Reddit社区上验证了框架的有效性，属性发现与人类标注者一致性接近人工间一致性。

Abstract: Thematic corpora, such as social media communities, contain unstructured text describing data that could be made structured. These include, for example, personal attributes, behaviors, and experiences mentioned in social media data. Extracting structured data is challenging as relevant attributes are often implicit, domain-dependent, and unknown in advance. We propose a fully automated, iterative framework for discovering and extracting domain-specific attribute schemas without a predefined ontology. Using large language models (LLMs), the framework induces candidate attributes, sequentially consolidates semantically overlapping attributes, and assigns a structural type. These enable creating an ontology and populating it with values from the corpus. The framework also enables the use of smaller LLMs for value extraction with estimable accuracy loss compared to large LLMs. We evaluate the framework on 5 health-related Reddit communities. Discovered attributes achieved 61% agreement with human-identified attributes, close to the 62% agreement between independent annotators. In most cases, the algorithm converges to a stable attribute set in fewer than 10 iterations. Structural type assignment achieves 82% accuracy, and value extraction reaches an F1 score of 0.8 compared to human annotations. Across four LLM families, smaller instruction-tuned models show statistically significant improvements in extraction performance with model scale when evaluated against a high-capacity reference LLM, supporting informed accuracy-cost trade-offs. These results show that attributes comparable to those identified by humans can be discovered automatically, enabling the creation of high-quality structured datasets economically and at scale. By removing the need for predefined ontologies, iterative model-driven schema induction offers a practical and scalable foundation for mining thematic corpora.

</details>


### [126] [Optimizing Effective Training Time for Large-Scale Recommendation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02057)
*Mingming Ding,Ruilin Chen,Yuzhen Huang,Hang Qi,Menglu Yu,San Tan,Damian Reeves,Boris Sarana,Kevin Tang,Satendra Gera,Gagan Jain,Sahil Shah,Vishwa Karia,Fuzail Khan,Yashasvi Makin,Edward Z. Yang,Oguz Ulgen,Jia Chen Ren,Laith Sakka,Mayank Garg,Meet Vadakkanchery,Aici Lin,Wei Sun,Mengjiao Zhou,Shuai Yang,Junqing Zhou,Max Leung,Apoorv Purwar,Musharaf Sultan,John Bocharov,Zhenyu Tang,Vivek Trehan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 面向每日数十亿样本、数千GPU的大规模推荐训练车队，提出ETT%运营框架定位生命周期开销，并通过初始化通信消除与流水线重叠、编译缓存复用、异步检查点、独立模型发布与恢复优化，使平均ETT%提升15.5%、最大负载达85%，车队整体ETT%由约80%升至90%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐训练作业端到端墙钟时间中只有50–60%真正用于新数据训练，生命周期开销（初始化、编译、检查点、发布、恢复等）静默消耗加速器算力，缺少可观测、可归因的度量框架与系统性优化手段。

Method: 论文以ETT%为贯穿全栈的运营度量框架：先对训练作业端到端墙钟时间做埋点，把总时间拆分为有效训练时间与生命周期开销，并将开销归因到独立负责的基础设施组件，同时识别在作业重启之间被重复执行的工作。基于该分析，优化覆盖从初始化、编译、检查点、模型发布到故障恢复的完整训练栈：在训练器初始化阶段做通信消除并在计算与通信之间做流水线重叠，减少启动阶段的串行等待；针对动态shape做专门处理，对autotuning进行剪枝，并复用PyTorch 2的编译缓存，避免重复编译开销；采用异步检查点降低保存对训练的阻塞；将模型发布解耦为独立流程，并压缩恢复路径成本。所有优化在代表性模型与真实训练车队上进行评测。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "ETT%", "value": "平均提升15.5%", "setting": "在代表性模型上评测"}, {"metric": "ETT%", "value": "达到85%", "setting": "最大规模工作负载"}], "online_gains": [{"metric": "Fleet-wide ETT%", "value": "从约80%提升到90%以上", "setting": "优化部署到训练车队后"}]}

Conclusion: 提出Effective Training Time (ETT%)作为运营框架，对丢失时间进行埋点、归因到独立负责的基础设施组件，并暴露作业重启间重复执行的工作；面向训练器初始化的通信消除与流水线重叠优化；动态shape处理、autotuning剪枝与可复用PyTorch 2编译缓存；异步检查点、独立模型发布流程以及降低恢复开销的优化

Abstract: Lifecycle overhead silently consumes accelerator capacity across large-scale recommendation training fleets. Our largest recommendation workloads process tens of billions train- ing examples per day on thousands of GPUs. Before this work, only 50-60% of their end-to-end wall time advanced training on new data. We present a fleet-scale study of this lifecycle overhead and a set of optimizations spanning the full training stack. We use Effective Training Time (ETT%) as an operational framework to instrument lost time, localize it to independently owned infrastructure components, and expose work repeated across job restarts. This analysis guides optimizations like communication elimination and pipeline overlap during trainer initialization; dynamic-shape handling, autotuning pruning, and reusable Py- Torch 2 compilation caches; asynchronous checkpointing; stan- dalone model publishing; and reductions in recovery cost. We evaluate the optimizations on representative models and measure their impacts in our training fleet. ETT% improves on every benchmark, by 15.5% on average, and reaches 85% on our largest workload. Fleet-wide ETT% rose from about 80% to above 90% after deployment.

</details>


### [127] [AgentWebRec: Compact Evidence Fusion over the Agent Web for Personalized Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01705)
*Haoran Qiang,Guannan Liu,Liang Zhang,Junjie Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: AgentWebRec将Agent Web上的推荐建模为任务时证据获取与融合问题，在有限证据预算下决定向哪些用户智能体查询并保留哪些证据，在四个InstructRec数据集上优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在用户智能体网络中，推荐所需证据分散在彼此不透明的智能体中，只能通过有限查询获取，且仅少量证据与当前推荐决策相关，不同智能体返回的响应语义异构。如何在此约束下决定'问什么'与'保留什么'，实现任务时证据获取与融合。

Method: AgentWebRec是一个用户-智能体导向的框架，面向Agent Web上的个性化推荐。输入包括平台提供的物品语义以及目标用户智能体私有记忆中与当前任务相关的证据。流程上，首先以物品语义和本地用户智能体证据为每个用户-物品决策建立基础；当本地证据不足时，条件性地查询邻居用户智能体以获取互补的偏好模式；随后对获取到的分布式证据进行融合，形成最终推荐决策。该框架在获取与融合过程中保持各智能体底层记忆本地化，不将分散的记忆聚合到中心，同时需在有限查询预算下决定向哪些智能体提问以及保留哪些证据。训练目标未在摘要中披露。推理时按需逐步获取证据并融合，属于任务时的决策流程。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "推荐性能（未披露具体指标与数值）", "value": "优于基线推荐器", "setting": "四个InstructRec数据集上的实验，消融验证各证据层贡献互补增益"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将Agent Web上的推荐重构为有限证据预算下的任务时证据获取与融合问题，而非从聚合数据中学习。；提出用户-智能体导向的逐步证据获取与融合框架AgentWebRec，保持各智能体底层记忆本地化。；以平台提供的物品语义和目标用户智能体私有记忆中任务相关证据为决策基础。；当本地证据不足时，条件性查询邻居用户智能体以获取互补偏好模式。

Abstract: LLM-based personal agents are emerging as persistent carriers of user semantics and intermediaries between users and recommendation platforms, maintaining richer user knowledge locally. As agents interact with one another, the conventional \textit{User--Platform} relation evolves into a \textit{User--Agent Web--Platform} information pathway, enabling distributed user-side information to complement item-side information. This new pathway, however, defies conventional recommendation: evidence is scattered across mutually opaque agents and reachable only through bounded queries, only a small portion of it is relevant to the current recommendation decision, and the responses returned by different agents are semantically heterogeneous. We therefore recast recommendation over the agent web as a \emph{task-time evidence acquisition and fusion} problem under a finite evidence budget by deciding what to ask and what to keep, rather than learning from aggregated data. We propose AgentWebRec, a user-agent-oriented framework that progressively acquires and fuses distributed evidence for each user-item decision while keeping underlying agent memories local. It grounds each decision in platform-provided item semantics and task-relevant evidence from the target user agent's private memory, and conditionally queries neighboring user agents for complementary preference patterns when local evidence is insufficient. Experiments on four InstructRec datasets show that AgentWebRec consistently outperforms baseline recommenders, and ablations verify that the evidence layers contribute complementary gains.

</details>


### [128] [CANOPY: Adaptive-Granularity Evidence Compression for Multimodal RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00923)
*Hyojeong Yun,Jueun Kim,Wook-Shin Han*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CANOPY提出一种多模态RAG的检索后自适应粒度证据压缩框架：将条目组织为层次结构，用微调节点编码器和父-子相对细化选择多粒度区域，并以critic触发目标性再检索。在33M异构语料、五个QA基准上提升答案准确率，且在Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct设置中减少读者输入token 14.2-27.7%而不损失准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态RAG中，检索粒度无法决定每个条目内部应保留多少上下文：粗粒度会引入无关内容，均匀细粒度选择会删除理解证据所需的上下文。现有压缩器依赖模态特定机制，缺乏跨异构条目按区域自适应保留范围的通用过程。

Method: CANOPY是一种检索后证据压缩框架。输入为异构检索条目（文本、表格、图像、视频）；每个条目被表示为层次结构，并通过Canonical Projection将区域投影到共享潜在空间，以支持跨模态比较。节点编码器在gold evidence上微调，为层次结构中的每个区域相对于查询的得分进行估计。父-子相对细化利用这些分数选择多个区域，使得在不同粒度上保留足够上下文而避免无关内容，节点级剪枝完全在嵌入/编码器侧完成，无需LLM调用。压缩后，critic评估累积证据是否足以回答问题；若判断不足，则请求目标性后续检索，新检索条目在被加入前也先经过相同的层次化压缩。整个过程在迭代流水线中运行：压缩-批评-再检索-压缩，最终将压缩后的证据输入读者模型。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均答案准确率", "value": "高于评估的检索基线", "setting": "五个QA基准，33M条目异构语料"}, {"metric": "读者输入证据token数", "value": "减少14.2-27.7%", "setting": "unrouted Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct设置，与相同迭代流水线但不压缩相比，答案准确率相当"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出CANOPY（Canonical Projection over Hierarchy）框架，将检索条目表示为层次结构，并对齐到统一表示以实现跨模态自适应粒度压缩。；节点编码器在gold evidence上微调，为区域相对于查询打分；父-子相对细化比较分数，在不调用LLM进行节点级剪枝的情况下选择不同粒度的多个区域。；引入critic判断累积证据是否充分，不充分时请求目标性后续检索，新检索条目先行压缩再加入。

Abstract: Multimodal RAG retrieves text, tables, images, and videos, but choosing a retrieval granularity does not determine how much context to retain within each item. Coarse units include irrelevant content, while uniformly fine selection can remove context needed to interpret the evidence. Existing compressors address this trade-off with modality-specific mechanisms, leaving open a shared procedure for adapting the retained extent region by region across heterogeneous items. We introduce CANOPY (Canonical Projection over Hierarchy), a framework for adaptive-granularity post-retrieval evidence compression. CANOPY represents retrieved items as hierarchies and uses a node encoder fine-tuned on gold evidence to score regions against the query. Parent-relative refinement compares these scores to select multiple regions at different granularities without LLM calls for node-level pruning. Because compression cannot recover evidence that was never retrieved, a critic requests targeted follow-up retrieval when it judges the accumulated evidence insufficient; newly retrieved items are compressed before being added. Across five QA benchmarks over a 33M-item heterogeneous corpus, CANOPY achieves higher average answer accuracy than the evaluated retrieval baselines. Ablations indicate that additional retrieval drives the main accuracy gains on multi-hop QA. In the unrouted Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct setting, compression reduces reader-input evidence tokens by 14.2-27.7% relative to the same iterative pipeline without compression, with comparable answer accuracy.

</details>


### [129] [RPTune: Learned Context Curation for LLM Catalog Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00964)
*Chuxuan Hu,Hejie Cui,Norman Huang,Shubham Kumar Bharti,Wang-Chiew Tan,Sercan Ö. Arık*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: RPTune面向中小商户的目录搜索，提出学习式上下文整理与LLM后训练耦合框架，在7个真实商户数据上显著提升搜索准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全长目录放入LLM上下文后，模型不能有效利用长上下文进行产品选择；如何整理目录上下文并适配LLM以提升搜索准确率。

Method: RPTune是一个端到端框架，包含两个主要组件：学习式目录整理器和LLM后训练。整理器采用编码器-重排器（encoder-reorganizer）架构，以完整目录为输入，通过编码器生成表示，再由重排器对产品进行排序和剪枝，并由下游LLM的反馈进行训练。整理后的目录被用于LLM的后训练，训练目标为上下文相对奖励。在推理时，给定用户查询，系统先使用整理器对目录进行排序和剪枝，然后将整理后的目录与查询一起输入经过后训练的LLM，由LLM选择产品。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "搜索准确率", "value": "提升高达31.4个百分点", "setting": "上下文整理（未指定具体指标和基线）"}, {"metric": "搜索准确率", "value": "平均提升10.3个百分点", "setting": "后训练（未指定具体指标和基线）"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 学习式目录整理器（encoder-reorganizer curator），通过下游LLM反馈来指导产品的排序与剪枝；基于自动生成的、目录接地的监督信号进行LLM后训练；上下文相对奖励（context-relative reward），利用整理后的目录改进后训练效果；端到端框架耦合目录整理与LLM后训练

Abstract: For small merchant businesses (SMBs) whose catalogs fit within a long-context LLM, full-catalog prompting offers a compelling alternative to multi-stage retrieval designed primarily for large marketplaces with millions of items. However, fitting the full catalog into the context window does not ensure that the model can use it effectively, since LLMs do not exploit long contexts uniformly. We therefore study in-context catalog search through two complementary questions: (1) how to curate and present catalogs to the LLM, and (2) how to adapt the LLM for product selection on curated contexts. We propose RPTune, an end-to-end framework that couples learned catalog curation with LLM post-training using automatically generated, catalog-grounded supervision. An encoder-reorganizer curator orders and prunes products guided by downstream LLM feedback, while the resulting curated catalogs in turn improve the effectiveness of LLM post-training with a context-relative reward. We evaluate RPTune on 7 real merchants spanning distinct retail verticals, using 100 complex conversational queries per merchant. RPTune consistently improves search accuracy across both proprietary and open-weight LLMs, with context curation yielding gains of up to 31.4 percentage points and post-training adding a further 10.3 points on average.

</details>


### [130] [Do Multilingual Encoders Produce Language-Consistent Semantic IDs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01139)
*Abhinav Bohra,Anuj Bohra*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文质疑多语言编码器是否足以让同一商品的多语言版本获得语言一致的语义ID。基于 Amazon ESCI 的英/西/日商品数据，实验显示翻译版本语义ID一致性远低于英语改写，且残差量化并未选择性放大语言方向；语言平衡拟合虽使码本更均匀，却进一步降低跨语言前缀一致率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言编码器是否足以让同一商品的不同语言版本获得语言一致的语义ID，以及残差量化是否对翻译引起的表示偏移异常敏感。

Method: 论文并非提出完整新系统，而是围绕生成式检索中的语义ID流程做诊断实验。输入为 Amazon ESCI 商品列表的英语、西班牙语、日语渲染文本。使用多语言 E5 编码器将每个商品文本编码为稠密嵌入。随后通过残差量化将嵌入压缩为离散码序列，即语义ID：每个商品由多个层级的码字组成，前缀码由早期量化层决定。实验比较不同语言渲染的同一商品在SID上的重叠程度，重点考察首码一致率与完整SID不一致率。为区分语言方向效应与一般距离效应，论文构造距离匹配的商品导向对照：选取与翻译版本嵌入距离相近的其他商品，比较其SID不一致率是否与翻译版本相似。训练/拟合层面，论文比较三种量化器拟合数据配置：英语主导拟合、语言平衡拟合、纯英语拟合，观察码本使用均匀度与跨语言前缀一致率的变化。推理路径为：文本→多语言E5嵌入→残差量化→SID码序列；评价指标为不同语言渲染之间SID首码或完整码的一致/不一致比例。结论是多语言暴露和均衡码本使用本身不足以保证语言一致的SID。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "首码一致率", "value": "7.7%", "setting": "英语主导拟合下，日语翻译保留其英语对应商品的第一SID码"}, {"metric": "首码一致率", "value": "89.0%", "setting": "英语主导拟合下，英语改写保留第一SID码"}, {"metric": "首码一致率", "value": "从28.3%降至6.6%", "setting": "西班牙语翻译在语言平衡拟合与英语主导拟合下的第一码一致性对比"}, {"metric": "首码一致率", "value": "67.6%", "setting": "纯英语拟合下，西班牙语翻译保留第一SID码的比例"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 系统量化多语言编码器下翻译版本与英语原文的语义ID一致性，发现日语翻译首码一致率仅7.7%，而英语改写为89.0%。；使用距离匹配的商品导向对照，发现完整SID不一致率与翻译情况接近，没有证据表明量化器会选择性放大语言方向。；比较英语主导拟合、语言平衡拟合与纯英语拟合：平衡拟合使码本使用更均匀，但西班牙语首码一致率从28.3%降至6.6%，纯英语拟合反而保持67.6%。

Abstract: Semantic IDs (SIDs) compress item embeddings into discrete code sequences used in generative retrieval. We ask whether a multilingual encoder is sufficient for different-language renderings of the same product to receive language-consistent SIDs. Using Amazon ESCI listings rendered in English, Spanish, and Japanese, we test whether translations remain close to their English source, whether residual quantization is unusually sensitive to translation-induced movement, and whether multilingual or language-balanced quantizer fitting improves SID agreement. Multilingual E5 places translations measurably apart: under an English-heavy fit, a Japanese translation preserves the first SID code of its English counterpart in only 7.7% of cases, compared with 89.0% for an English rewording. Distance-matched product-directed controls produce nearly the same full-SID mismatch as translation, providing no evidence that the quantizer selectively amplifies language directions. Balancing the fitting mixture makes codebook use more uniform but further reduces cross-lingual prefix agreement: Spanish first-code consistency falls from 28.3% to 6.6%, while an English-only fit preserves it for 67.6% of Spanish translations. These results show that multilingual exposure and balanced codebook use alone do not guarantee language-consistent SIDs.

</details>
